FILE: scripts\inspectSelectorData.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const w = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(p:number) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true) {
    const b=w[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2**s;
    if(!(b&0x80)) return {v,p};
    s+=7;
  }
}

function hex(n:number) {
  return "0x"+n.toString(16).padStart(8,"0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSPECT DATA INITIALIZATION NEAR 0x19534C");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p=8;
let dataFound=false;

while(p<w.length) {
  const id=w[p++];
  const z=uleb(p);
  p=z.p;
  const end=p+z.v;

  if(id===11) {
    console.log("DATA SECTION START :", hex(p));
    console.log("DATA SECTION END   :", hex(end));
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let q=p;
    const count=uleb(q);
    q=count.p;

    for(let seg=0;seg<count.v;seg++) {
      console.log(`DATA SEGMENT ${seg} @ ${hex(q)}`);

      const flags=uleb(q);
      q=flags.p;

      // active segment with memory index 0
      if(flags.v===0) {
        // offset expression
        const op=w[q++];

        if(op===0x41) {
          const off=uleb(q);
          q=off.p;

          const offset=off.v;

          if(w[q]===0x0b) q++;

          const len=uleb(q);
          q=len.p;

          const dataStart=q;
          const dataEnd=q+len.v;

          console.log("  OFFSET :",hex(offset));
          console.log("  SIZE   :",len.v);

          const segEnd=dataEnd;

          if(TARGET>=offset && TARGET<offset+len.v) {
            dataFound=true;

            console.log("  *** TARGET IS INSIDE THIS SEGMENT ***");

            const a=Math.max(offset,TARGET-32);
            const b=Math.min(offset+len.v,TARGET+64);

            console.log("  MEMORY RANGE:",hex(a),"..",hex(b));

            for(let addr=a;addr<b;addr+=16) {
              const row:number[]=[];

              for(let j=0;j<16 && addr+j<b;j++) {
                row.push(w[dataStart+(addr+j-offset)]);
              }

              console.log(
                "  "+hex(addr)+" : "+
                row.map(x=>x.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join(" ")
              );
            }
          }

          q=segEnd;
        }
      }
    }

    break;
  }

  p=end;
}

if(!dataFound) {
  console.log("TARGET NOT PRESENT DIRECTLY IN ACTIVE DATA SEGMENTS.");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectSelectorDispatchFamily.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGETS = [4788, 4789, 4790, 4791];

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function valTypeName(v: number): string {
    switch (v) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        case 0x7b: return "v128";
        case 0x70: return "funcref";
        case 0x6f: return "externref";
        default: return `0x${v.toString(16)}`;
    }
}

type FuncType = {
    params: number[];
    results: number[];
};

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SECTION LOCATOR
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const sections: {
    id: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    sections.push({
        id,
        start: p,
        end: p + size
    });

    p += size;
}

function getSection(id: number) {
    return sections.find(s => s.id === id);
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TYPE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const types: FuncType[] = [];

const typeSec = getSection(1);

if (!typeSec) {
    throw new Error("TYPE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

let t = typeSec.start;

const typeCountInfo = readULEB(t);
const typeCount = typeCountInfo.value;
t = typeCountInfo.next;

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount; i++) {

    const form = buf[t++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected type form at ${hex(t - 1)} = ${hex(form)}`
        );
    }

    const pc = readULEB(t);
    t = pc.next;

    const params: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
        params.push(buf[t++]);
    }

    const rc = readULEB(t);
    t = rc.next;

    const results: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
        results.push(buf[t++]);
    }

    types.push({
        params,
        results
    });
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ordinal -> type index
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

const funcSec = getSection(3);

if (!funcSec) {
    throw new Error("FUNCTION SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

let f = funcSec.start;

const funcCountInfo = readULEB(f);
const funcCount = funcCountInfo.value;
f = funcCountInfo.next;

for (let i = 0; i < funcCount; i++) {
    const x = readULEB(f);
    functionTypes.push(x.value);
    f = x.next;
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TABLE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const tableSec = getSection(4);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR DISPATCH FAMILY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TYPE COUNT       = ${types.length}`);
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS= ${funcCount}`);
console.log(`TARGET TABLE     = ${TARGETS.join(", ")}`);
console.log("============================================================");

if (tableSec) {

    let z = tableSec.start;

    const tableCountInfo = readULEB(z);
    const tableCount = tableCountInfo.value;
    z = tableCountInfo.next;

    console.log(`TABLE COUNT      = ${tableCount}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < tableCount; i++) {

        const elemType = buf[z++];

        const limFlags = readULEB(z);
        z = limFlags.next;

        const min = readULEB(z);
        z = min.next;

        let max: number | null = null;

        if (limFlags.value & 1) {
            const mx = readULEB(z);
            max = mx.value;
            z = mx.next;
        }

        console.log("");
        console.log(`TABLE #${i}`);
        console.log(`  ELEM TYPE = ${valTypeName(elemType)}`);
        console.log(`  MIN       = ${min.value}`);
        console.log(`  MAX       = ${max ?? "none"}`);
    }

} else {
    console.log("TABLE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ELEMENT SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const tableEntries = new Map<number, number>();

const elemSec = getSection(9);

if (!elemSec) {
    console.log("ELEMENT SECTION NOT FOUND");
} else {

    let e = elemSec.start;

    const segCountInfo = readULEB(e);
    const segCount = segCountInfo.value;
    e = segCountInfo.next;

    console.log("");
    console.log(`ELEMENT SEGMENT COUNT = ${segCount}`);

    for (let seg = 0; seg < segCount; seg++) {

        const flagsInfo = readULEB(e);
        const flags = flagsInfo.value;
        e = flagsInfo.next;

        //
        // We only decode the common active-table forms:
        //
        // flags 0 = active table 0, funcidx vector
        // flags 2 = active explicit table, funcidx vector
        //
        if (flags === 0 || flags === 2) {

            let tableIndex = 0;

            if (flags === 2) {
                const ti = readULEB(e);
                tableIndex = ti.value;
                e = ti.next;
            }

            //
            // offset expression
            //
            const opcode = buf[e++];

            if (opcode === 0x41) {
                const off = readSLEB(e);
                e = off.next;

                const end = buf[e++];

                if (end !== 0x0b) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Bad element offset expression at ${hex(e - 1)}`
                    );
                }

                const vecInfo = readULEB(e);
                const vecCount = vecInfo.value;
                e = vecInfo.next;

                const base = off.value >>> 0;

                for (let j = 0; j < vecCount; j++) {

                    const fi = readULEB(e);
                    const funcIndex = fi.value;
                    e = fi.next;

                    tableEntries.set(
                        base + j,
                        funcIndex
                    );
                }

                continue;
            }

            //
            // Unsupported offset expression:
            // skip conservatively is unsafe, so report and stop.
            //
            console.log(
                `SEGMENT ${seg}: unsupported offset opcode ${hex(opcode)}`
            );

            break;

        } else if (flags === 1) {

            //
            // passive element segment with elemkind + expressions
            //
            const elemKind = buf[e++];

            const vecInfo = readULEB(e);
            const count = vecInfo.value;
            e = vecInfo.next;

            //
            // Skip references conservatively.
            //
            for (let j = 0; j < count; j++) {
                //
                // ref.func is usually 0xd2 followed by function index.
                //
                const op = buf[e++];

                if (op === 0xd2) {
                    const fi = readULEB(e);
                    e = fi.next;
                } else {
                    //
                    // Not decoding passive expression form further.
                    //
                    console.log(
                        `SEGMENT ${seg}: unsupported passive expr ${hex(op)}`
                    );
                    break;
                }
            }

        } else {

            console.log(
                `SEGMENT ${seg}: flags=${flags} not decoded`
            );

            break;
        }
    }
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TARGET TABLE ENTRIES
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET TABLE ENTRIES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const tableIndex of TARGETS) {

    const globalFunc = tableEntries.get(tableIndex);

    if (globalFunc === undefined) {
        console.log(
            `TABLE[${tableIndex}] = NOT RESOLVED`
        );
        continue;
    }

    const typeIndex = globalFunc < 83
        ? null
        : functionTypes[globalFunc - 83];

    console.log("");
    console.log(`TABLE[${tableIndex}]`);
    console.log(`  GLOBAL FUNC = ${globalFunc}`);

    if (typeIndex === null) {
        console.log(`  TYPE        = IMPORTED`);
        continue;
    }

    const ft = types[typeIndex];

    console.log(`  TYPE INDEX  = ${typeIndex}`);
    console.log(
        `  PARAMS      = [${ft.params.map(valTypeName).join(", ")}]`
    );
    console.log(
        `  RESULTS     = [${ft.results.map(valTypeName).join(", ")}]`
    );
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SAME-TYPE TABLE MEMBERS
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SAME-TYPE TABLE MEMBERS");
console.log("============================================================");

const targetTypeIndices = new Set<number>();

for (const tableIndex of TARGETS) {

    const globalFunc = tableEntries.get(tableIndex);

    if (
        globalFunc !== undefined &&
        globalFunc >= 83
    ) {
        const typeIndex =
            functionTypes[globalFunc - 83];

        targetTypeIndices.add(typeIndex);
    }
}

for (const typeIndex of targetTypeIndices) {

    const ft = types[typeIndex];

    console.log("");
    console.log(`TYPE ${typeIndex}`);
    console.log(
        `  PARAMS  = [${ft.params.map(valTypeName).join(", ")}]`
    );
    console.log(
        `  RESULTS = [${ft.results.map(valTypeName).join(", ")}]`
    );

    let shown = 0;

    for (const [tableIndex, globalFunc] of tableEntries) {

        if (globalFunc < 83)
            continue;

        const ti =
            functionTypes[globalFunc - 83];

        if (ti !== typeIndex)
            continue;

        console.log(
            `  TABLE[${tableIndex}] -> FUNC ${globalFunc}`
        );

        shown++;

        if (shown >= 80) {
            console.log("  ... output limited to 80 entries");
            break;
        }
    }

    console.log(`  TOTAL SHOWN = ${shown}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\inspectSelectorMapFields.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const segments = [
  { id: 1165, file: 0x004c58b0, runtime: 0x001a7900, size: 110 },
  { id: 1166, file: 0x004c5926, runtime: 0x001a7980, size: 110 },
  { id: 1167, file: 0x004c599c, runtime: 0x001a7a00, size: 110 },
];

function u32(p) {
  return (
    b[p] |
    (b[p + 1] << 8) |
    (b[p + 2] << 16) |
    (b[p + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("SELECTOR MAP FIELD STRUCTURE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

for (const s of segments) {
  console.log(`\nSEGMENT ${s.id}`);
  console.log(`FILE    : 0x${s.file.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`RUNTIME : 0x${s.runtime.toString(16)}`);

  for (let off = 0; off + 8 <= s.size; off += 8) {
    const key = u32(s.file + off);
    const sel = u32(s.file + off + 4);

    console.log(
      `  +0x${off.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")} ` +
      `KEY=${key.toString().padStart(3)} ` +
      `SELECTOR=${sel.toString().padStart(4)} ` +
      `RUNTIME=0x${(s.runtime + off).toString(16)}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("\n" + "=".repeat(72));
console.log("TARGET SELECTORS");
console.log("5220 -> FUNC 1968");
console.log("5232 -> FUNC 1968");
console.log("5235 -> FUNC 1968");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x004db180;
const END   = 0x004db210;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSPECT SELECTOR WINDOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`RANGE : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let p = START; p < END; p += 16) {
  const row = wasm.subarray(p, Math.min(p + 16, END));

  const bytes = [...row]
    .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");

  const ascii = [...row]
    .map(b => b >= 0x20 && b <= 0x7e ? String.fromCharCode(b) : ".")
    .join("");

  console.log(
    `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.padEnd(47)}  ${ascii}`
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\inspectSharedHelpers.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file = 'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function readUleb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte;

    do {
        if (pos >= wasm.length) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF');
        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);
        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('Invalid ULEB');
    } while (byte & 0x80);

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function dump(start, end) {
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const stop = Math.min(p + 16, end);
        const bytes = [...wasm.subarray(p, stop)]
            .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
            .join(' ');
        console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${bytes}`);
    }
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];
    const size = readUleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error('Code section not found');

const count = readUleb(codeStart);
pos = count.next;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = readUleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    bodies.set(83 + ordinal, {
        start,
        end,
        size: size.value
    });

    pos = end;
}

const targets = [83, 394, 615, 700, 1022];

console.log('SHARED HELPER INSPECTION');
console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(`FILE SIZE = ${wasm.length}`);
console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES = ${count.value}`);

for (const func of targets) {
    const body = bodies.get(func);

    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC ${func}`);

    if (!body) {
        console.log('BODY NOT FOUND');
        continue;
    }

    console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE = ${body.size}`);
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    if (body.size <= 240) {
        dump(body.start, body.end);
    } else {
        console.log('FIRST 240 BYTES ONLY');
        dump(body.start, body.start + 240);
    }
}

console.log('\nDONE - READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION');

FILE: scripts\inspectStonPackagesV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

const NODE_MODULES = path.resolve(
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\node_modules'
);

const KEYWORDS = [
    'ston',
    'pool',
    'liquidity',
    'lp',
    'router',
    'swap',
    'callback',
    'provide',
    'transfer',
    'notification',
];

const MAX_RESULTS = 250;

let results = 0;

function score(name: string): number {
    const lower = name.toLowerCase();

    let s = 0;

    for (const keyword of KEYWORDS) {
        if (lower.includes(keyword)) {
            s++;
        }
    }

    return s;
}

function walk(dir: string) {
    let entries: fs.Dirent[];

    try {
        entries = fs.readdirSync(
            dir,
            { withFileTypes: true }
        );
    } catch {
        return;
    }

    for (const entry of entries) {
        if (results >= MAX_RESULTS) {
            return;
        }

        const full =
            path.join(dir, entry.name);

        if (entry.isDirectory()) {
            walk(full);
            continue;
        }

        if (!entry.isFile()) {
            continue;
        }

        const relative =
            path.relative(
                NODE_MODULES,
                full
            );

        const s = score(relative);

        if (s >= 2) {
            console.log(
                `[${s}] ${relative}`
            );

            results++;
        }
    }
}

export async function run() {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STON.FI PACKAGE / FILE STRUCTURE INSPECTOR'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Searching node_modules for STON.fi-related files...'
    );
    console.log('');

    walk(NODE_MODULES);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        `RESULTS: ${results}`
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Next step: identify the actual SDK package/file containing'
    );
    console.log(
        'Pool / LP / Router message builders or parsers.'
    );
}

import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

function inspectFunction(name: string, fn: unknown) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(name);
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    if (typeof fn !== 'function') {
        console.log('NOT FOUND');
        return;
    }

    console.log(
        Function.prototype.toString.call(fn)
    );

    console.log('');
}

export async function run() {

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('       STON.FI V2.2 BODY BUILDER INSPECTOR');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const routerProto =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.prototype as any;

    inspectFunction(
        'createProvideLiquidityBody',
        routerProto.createProvideLiquidityBody
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('                    END');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const router = DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(
        Address.parse(
            'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
        )
    );

    console.log('===== STON.FI ROUTER v2.1 =====');
    console.log('Address:', router.address.toString());

    const routerProvider = provider.provider(router.address);

    try {
        const version = await router.getRouterVersion(routerProvider);
        console.log('Router version:', version);
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('getRouterVersion ERROR:', e);
    }

    try {
        const data = await router.getRouterData(routerProvider);
        console.log('Router data:', data);
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('getRouterData ERROR:', e);
    }
}

FILE: scripts\inspectStonV22Exports.ts

import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'fs';
import { join } from 'path';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log(`
============================================================
 STON.FI v2.2 SDK EXPORT / SCHEMA INSPECTOR
 READ ONLY - LOCAL node_modules ONLY
============================================================
`);

    const root = join(
        process.cwd(),
        'node_modules',
        '@ston-fi',
        'sdk',
        'dist',
        'contracts',
        'dex',
        'v2_2'
    );

    const files = [
        'LpAccount/LpAccountV2_2.js',
        'LpAccount/LpAccountV2_2.cjs',

        'pool/BasePoolV2_2.js',
        'pool/BasePoolV2_2.cjs',
        'pool/CPIPoolV2_2.js',
        'pool/CPIPoolV2_2.cjs',

        'router/BaseRouterV2_2.js',
        'router/BaseRouterV2_2.cjs',
        'router/CPIRouterV2_2.js',
        'router/CPIRouterV2_2.cjs',
    ];

    const needles = [
        '0x50c6a654',
        '50c6a654',
        '0x178d4519',
        '178d4519',
        'addLiquidity',
        'ADD_LIQUIDITY',
        'provideLp',
        'provide_lp',
        'transfer',
        'mint',
        'burn',
        'callback',
        'excess',
        'refund',
        'minLpOut',
        'bothPositive',
    ];

    for (const relative of files) {
        const file = join(root, relative);

        if (!existsSync(file)) {
            console.log(`\n[MISSING] ${relative}`);
            continue;
        }

        console.log(`\n============================================================`);
        console.log(`FILE: ${relative}`);
        console.log(`============================================================`);

        const text = readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
        const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);

        let hits = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
            const line = lines[i];

            if (
                needles.some((needle) =>
                    line.toLowerCase().includes(needle.toLowerCase())
                )
            ) {
                hits++;

                console.log(`\n--- MATCH line ${i + 1} ---`);

                const start = Math.max(0, i - 4);
                const end = Math.min(lines.length, i + 7);

                for (let j = start; j < end; j++) {
                    console.log(
                        `${String(j + 1).padStart(6, ' ')} | ${lines[j]}`
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        console.log(`\nMatches in file: ${hits}`);
    }

    console.log(`
============================================================
 INSPECTION COMPLETE
============================================================

Focus:
  1. 0x50c6a654  -> ADD_LIQUIDITY
  2. 0x178d4519  -> LP-related transfer/callback path
  3. addLiquidity / provideLp
  4. LP mint / transfer / callback fields

No transaction was created or sent.
`);
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectStonV22SourceMaps.ts
====================================================================================================
import { existsSync, readFileSync, readdirSync } from 'fs';
import { join } from 'path';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

function inspectFile(file: string, label: string) {
    if (!existsSync(file)) {
        console.log(`\n[MISSING] ${label}`);
        return;
    }

    console.log(`\n============================================================`);
    console.log(`FILE: ${label}`);
    console.log(`============================================================`);

    const text = readFileSync(file, 'utf8');

    const needles = [
        '50c6a654',
        '0x50c6a654',
        '178d4519',
        '0x178d4519',
        'add_liquidity',
        'addLiquidity',
        'provideLp',
        'provide_lp',
        'ADD_LIQUIDITY',
        'LpAccountV2_2',
        'CPIPoolV2_2',
        'BasePoolV2_2',
        'CPIRouterV2_2',
        'minLpOut',
        'bothPositive',
        'lpOut',
        'lp_wallet',
        'jetton_wallet',
    ];

    let total = 0;

    for (const needle of needles) {
        let pos = 0;
        let count = 0;

        while (true) {
            const found = text
                .toLowerCase()
                .indexOf(needle.toLowerCase(), pos);

            if (found === -1) break;

            count++;
            total++;

            const from = Math.max(0, found - 250);
            const to = Math.min(text.length, found + needle.length + 500);

            console.log(`\n--- MATCH: "${needle}" #${count} ---`);
            console.log(text.slice(from, to));

            pos = found + needle.length;

            // جلوگیری از خروجی بیش از حد
            if (count >= 8) break;
        }

        if (count > 0) {
            console.log(`\nOccurrences shown for "${needle}": ${count}`);
        }
    }

    console.log(`\nTotal matches shown: ${total}`);
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log(`
============================================================
 STON.FI v2.2 SOURCE MAP / CHUNK INSPECTOR
 READ ONLY - LOCAL node_modules ONLY
============================================================
`);

    const sdkRoot = join(
        process.cwd(),
        'node_modules',
        '@ston-fi',
        'sdk',
        'dist'
    );

    const v22Root = join(
        sdkRoot,
        'contracts',
        'dex',
        'v2_2'
    );

    console.log(`SDK root : ${sdkRoot}`);
    console.log(`v2.2 root: ${v22Root}`);

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. v2.2 source maps
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const v22Dirs = [
        join(v22Root, 'LpAccount'),
        join(v22Root, 'pool'),
        join(v22Root, 'router'),
    ];

    for (const dir of v22Dirs) {
        if (!existsSync(dir)) continue;

        for (const name of readdirSync(dir)) {
            if (!name.endsWith('.map')) continue;

            inspectFile(
                join(dir, name),
                `v2_2/${dir.split('v2_2\\')[1]}\\${name}`
            );
        }
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. SDK chunks
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (existsSync(sdkRoot)) {
        for (const name of readdirSync(sdkRoot)) {
            if (
                name.startsWith('chunk-') &&
                (name.endsWith('.js') ||
                    name.endsWith('.cjs') ||
                    name.endsWith('.js.map') ||
                    name.endsWith('.cjs.map'))
            ) {
                inspectFile(
                    join(sdkRoot, name),
                    `dist/${name}`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. helpers / utility files
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const extraFiles = [
        join(sdkRoot, 'helpers.js'),
        join(sdkRoot, 'helpers.cjs'),
        join(
            sdkRoot,
            'utils',
            'createJettonTransferMessage.js'
        ),
        join(
            sdkRoot,
            'utils',
            'createJettonTransferMessage.cjs'
        ),
    ];

    for (const file of extraFiles) {
        inspectFile(file, file.replace(sdkRoot, 'dist'));
    }

    console.log(`
============================================================
 INSPECTION COMPLETE
============================================================

IMPORTANT TARGETS:

  0x50c6a654  = Pool ADD_LIQUIDITY
  0x178d4519  = LP-related message
  7.219004800 ROOSTER
              = 7219004800 nano ROOSTER

No transaction was created or sent.
`);
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectTable12B4.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x12b4;

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= -1 << shift;
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8): string {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function sectionEnd(start: number): number {
  const s = readUleb(start);
  return s.next + s.value;
}

console.log("INSPECT TABLE[0x12B4]");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET TABLE INDEX = ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;

let tableSection: {
  start: number;
  end: number;
} | null = null;

let elementSection: {
  start: number;
  end: number;
} | null = null;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];

  const size = readUleb(p);
  p = size.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + size.value;

  if (id === 4) {
    tableSection = { start, end };
  }

  if (id === 9) {
    elementSection = { start, end };
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!tableSection) {
  throw new Error("TABLE section not found");
}

if (!elementSection) {
  throw new Error("ELEMENT section not found");
}

console.log(
  `TABLE SECTION = ${hex(tableSection.start)} .. ${hex(tableSection.end)}`
);

console.log(
  `ELEMENT SECTION = ${hex(elementSection.start)} .. ${hex(elementSection.end)}`
);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TABLE SECTION");
console.log("============================================================");

let t = tableSection.start;

const tableCount = readUleb(t);
t = tableCount.next;

console.log(`TABLE COUNT = ${tableCount.value}`);

for (let i = 0; i < tableCount.value; i++) {
  const elemType = wasm[t++];

  const flags = readUleb(t);
  t = flags.next;

  const min = readUleb(t);
  t = min.next;

  let max: number | null = null;

  if (flags.value & 1) {
    const x = readUleb(t);
    t = x.next;
    max = x.value;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log(`TABLE #${i}`);
  console.log(`  ELEM TYPE = ${hex(elemType, 2)}`);
  console.log(`  FLAGS     = ${flags.value}`);
  console.log(`  MIN       = ${min.value}`);
  console.log(`  MAX       = ${max === null ? "none" : max}`);

  if (i === 0) {
    console.log(
      `  TARGET INDEX ${hex(TARGET)} IS ${
        TARGET < min.value ? "OUTSIDE MINIMUM" : "WITHIN MINIMUM"
      }`
    );

    if (max !== null) {
      console.log(
        `  TARGET INDEX ${hex(TARGET)} IS ${
          TARGET < max ? "BELOW MAXIMUM" : "AT/ABOVE MAXIMUM"
        }`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ELEMENT SECTION");
console.log("============================================================");

let e = elementSection.start;

const elemCount = readUleb(e);
e = elemCount.next;

console.log(`ELEMENT SEGMENT COUNT = ${elemCount.value}`);

let found = false;

for (let seg = 0; seg < elemCount.value; seg++) {
  const flags = readUleb(e);
  e = flags.next;

  /*
   * We decode the common active/passive function-index forms.
   *
   * flags:
   *   0 = active, table 0, i32 offset, vec(funcidx)
   *   1 = passive, elemkind + vec(funcidx)
   *   2 = active, explicit table, i32 offset, elemkind + vec(funcidx)
   *   3 = declarative, elemkind + vec(funcidx)
   *   4 = active, table 0, expr offset, vec(expr)
   *   5 = passive, ref.null/ref.func expressions
   *   6 = active, explicit table, expr offset
   *   7 = declarative expressions
   */

  console.log("");
  console.log(`ELEMENT SEGMENT #${seg} FLAGS=${flags.value}`);

  let mode = "";

  if (flags.value === 0) {
    mode = "active-func";
  } else if (flags.value === 1) {
    mode = "passive-func";
  } else if (flags.value === 2) {
    mode = "active-table-func";
  } else if (flags.value === 3) {
    mode = "declarative-func";
  } else if (flags.value === 4) {
    mode = "active-expr";
  } else if (flags.value === 5) {
    mode = "passive-expr";
  } else if (flags.value === 6) {
    mode = "active-table-expr";
  } else if (flags.value === 7) {
    mode = "declarative-expr";
  } else {
    throw new Error(`Unsupported element flags ${flags.value}`);
  }

  console.log(`  MODE = ${mode}`);

  let tableIndex = 0;
  let offsetValue: number | null = null;

  if (flags.value === 0 || flags.value === 2) {
    if (flags.value === 2) {
      const ti = readUleb(e);
      e = ti.next;
      tableIndex = ti.value;
    }

    const op = wasm[e++];

    if (op !== 0x41) {
      throw new Error(
        `Segment ${seg}: expected i32.const offset, got ${hex(op, 2)}`
      );
    }

    const off = readSleb(e);
    e = off.next;

    offsetValue = off.value >>> 0;

    const end = wasm[e++];

    if (end !== 0x0b) {
      throw new Error(`Segment ${seg}: malformed offset expr`);
    }
  }

  if (flags.value === 4 || flags.value === 6) {
    if (flags.value === 6) {
      const ti = readUleb(e);
      e = ti.next;
      tableIndex = ti.value;
    }

    const op = wasm[e++];

    if (op !== 0x41) {
      throw new Error(
        `Segment ${seg}: expected i32.const expr offset, got ${hex(op, 2)}`
      );
    }

    const off = readSleb(e);
    e = off.next;

    offsetValue = off.value >>> 0;

    const end = wasm[e++];

    if (end !== 0x0b) {
      throw new Error(`Segment ${seg}: malformed expr offset`);
    }
  }

  if (flags.value === 1 || flags.value === 3 || flags.value === 5 || flags.value === 7) {
    // passive/declarative: no active table offset
  }

  if (flags.value === 0 || flags.value === 2) {
    const kindOrCount = readUleb(e);
    e = kindOrCount.next;

    const count = kindOrCount.value;

    console.log(`  TABLE INDEX = ${tableIndex}`);
    console.log(`  OFFSET      = ${hex(offsetValue ?? 0)}`);
    console.log(`  ELEMENTS    = ${count}`);

    const targetStart =
      tableIndex === 0 &&
      offsetValue !== null &&
      TARGET >= offsetValue &&
      TARGET < offsetValue + count
        ? TARGET - offsetValue
        : -1;

    for (let j = 0; j < count; j++) {
      const f = readUleb(e);
      e = f.next;

      if (targetStart === j) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(">>> TARGET TABLE ENTRY FOUND <<<");
        console.log(`  TABLE INDEX = ${TARGET}`);
        console.log(`  SEGMENT     = ${seg}`);
        console.log(`  SEG OFFSET  = ${hex(offsetValue!)}`);
        console.log(`  ELEMENT POS = ${j}`);
        console.log(`  TARGET FUNC = ${f.value}`);
        console.log(`  FUNC INDEX  = ${f.value}`);
        found = true;
      }
    }

    continue;
  }

  if (flags.value === 1 || flags.value === 3) {
    const kind = wasm[e++];
    const count = readUleb(e);
    e = count.next;

    console.log(`  ELEM KIND = ${hex(kind, 2)}`);
    console.log(`  ELEMENTS  = ${count.value}`);

    for (let j = 0; j < count.value; j++) {
      const f = readUleb(e);
      e = f.next;

      if (
        flags.value === 1 &&
        TARGET === j
      ) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(">>> TARGET PASSIVE ELEMENT FOUND <<<");
        console.log(`  ELEMENT POS = ${j}`);
        console.log(`  TARGET FUNC = ${f.value}`);
        found = true;
      }
    }

    continue;
  }

  if (flags.value === 4 || flags.value === 6 || flags.value === 5 || flags.value === 7) {
    const count = readUleb(e);
    e = count.next;

    console.log(`  ELEMENT EXPRESSIONS = ${count.value}`);

    for (let j = 0; j < count.value; j++) {
      const op = wasm[e++];

      let funcIndex: number | null = null;

      if (op === 0xd2) {
        const f = readUleb(e);
        e = f.next;
        funcIndex = f.value;
      } else if (op === 0xd0) {
        const heapType = readSleb(e);
        e = heapType.next;
      } else {
        // Skip conservatively to end of expression.
        while (e < wasm.length && wasm[e++] !== 0x0b) {}
      }

      const end = wasm[e++];

      if (
        (flags.value === 4 || flags.value === 6) &&
        offsetValue !== null &&
        tableIndex === 0 &&
        TARGET === offsetValue + j &&
        funcIndex !== null
      ) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(">>> TARGET TABLE ENTRY FOUND <<<");
        console.log(`  TABLE INDEX = ${TARGET}`);
        console.log(`  SEGMENT     = ${seg}`);
        console.log(`  SEG OFFSET  = ${hex(offsetValue)}`);
        console.log(`  ELEMENT POS = ${j}`);
        console.log(`  TARGET FUNC = ${funcIndex}`);
        found = true;
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`TARGET TABLE INDEX = ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`FOUND = ${found}`);

if (!found) {
  console.log("");
  console.log(
    "TARGET ENTRY NOT RESOLVED BY STATIC ELEMENT SEGMENT SCAN"
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

FILE: scripts\inspectTable12B4_12B7.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [0x12b4, 0x12b5, 0x12b6, 0x12b7];

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= -1 << shift;
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8): string {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(idWanted: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === idWanted) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${idWanted} not found`);
}

console.log("INSPECT TABLE[0x12B4..0x12B7]");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  "TARGETS = " + TARGETS.map(x => hex(x)).join(", ")
);

console.log("");

const elementSec = findSection(9);

let p = elementSec.start;

const segmentCount = readUleb(p);
p = segmentCount.next;

console.log(`ELEMENT SEGMENT COUNT = ${segmentCount.value}`);

const resolved = new Map<number, number>();

for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount.value; seg++) {
  const flags = readUleb(p);
  p = flags.next;

  // This WASM has one active function element segment.
  if (flags.value !== 0) {
    throw new Error(
      `Unsupported element segment flags=${flags.value} at segment ${seg}`
    );
  }

  const op = wasm[p++];

  if (op !== 0x41) {
    throw new Error(
      `Segment ${seg}: expected i32.const, got ${hex(op, 2)}`
    );
  }

  const off = readSleb(p);
  p = off.next;

  const offset = off.value >>> 0;

  const end = wasm[p++];

  if (end !== 0x0b) {
    throw new Error(`Segment ${seg}: malformed offset expression`);
  }

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  console.log("");
  console.log(`SEGMENT #${seg}`);
  console.log(`OFFSET = ${hex(offset)}`);
  console.log(`COUNT  = ${count.value}`);

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const f = readUleb(p);
p = f.next;

const func = f.value;
const tableIndex = offset + i;

if (TARGETS.includes(tableIndex)) {
  resolved.set(tableIndex, func);

  console.log(
    `${hex(tableIndex)} -> FUNC ${func}`
  );
}
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL RESOLUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  const func = resolved.get(target);

  if (func === undefined) {
    console.log(`${hex(target)} -> NOT FOUND`);
  } else {
    console.log(`${hex(target)} -> FUNC ${func}`);
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("KNOWN CROSS-CHECK");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("Expected previous finding:");
console.log("TABLE[0x12B6] = FUNC 91");

const f12b6 = resolved.get(0x12b6);

console.log(
  `Observed TABLE[0x12B6] = ${
    f12b6 === undefined ? "NOT FOUND" : `FUNC ${f12b6}`
  }`
);

console.log("");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectTable4788Neighborhood.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 4788;
const BEFORE = 12;
const AFTER = 12;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const sections: {
    id: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {

    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    sections.push({
        id,
        start: p,
        end: p + size
    });

    p += size;
}

function section(id: number) {
    return sections.find(x => x.id === id);
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Type section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const types: {
    params: number[];
    results: number[];
}[] = [];

const typeSec = section(1);

if (!typeSec)
    throw new Error("TYPE SECTION NOT FOUND");

let t = typeSec.start;

const tc = readULEB(t);
t = tc.next;

for (let i = 0; i < tc.value; i++) {

    const form = buf[t++];

    if (form !== 0x60)
        throw new Error("Unexpected type form");

    const pc = readULEB(t);
    t = pc.next;

    const params: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
        params.push(buf[t++]);

    const rc = readULEB(t);
    t = rc.next;

    const results: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
        results.push(buf[t++]);

    types.push({ params, results });
}

function vt(v: number) {
    switch (v) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        case 0x7b: return "v128";
        default: return "0x" + v.toString(16);
    }
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const funcSec = section(3);

if (!funcSec)
    throw new Error("FUNCTION SECTION NOT FOUND");

let f = funcSec.start;

const fc = readULEB(f);
f = fc.next;

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {

    const x = readULEB(f);

    functionTypes.push(x.value);

    f = x.next;
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Element section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const elemSec = section(9);

if (!elemSec)
    throw new Error("ELEMENT SECTION NOT FOUND");

let e = elemSec.start;

const ec = readULEB(e);
e = ec.next;

const table = new Map<number, number>();

for (let seg = 0; seg < ec.value; seg++) {

    const flagsInfo = readULEB(e);
    const flags = flagsInfo.value;
    e = flagsInfo.next;

    if (flags !== 0 && flags !== 2) {
        console.log(
            `Unsupported element segment flags=${flags}`
        );
        break;
    }

    if (flags === 2) {
        const ti = readULEB(e);
        e = ti.next;
    }

    const opcode = buf[e++];

    if (opcode !== 0x41) {
        console.log(
            `Unsupported offset opcode ${hex(opcode)}`
        );
        break;
    }

    const off = readULEB(e);
    const base = off.value;
    e = off.next;

    const end = buf[e++];

    if (end !== 0x0b)
        throw new Error("Bad offset expression");

    const n = readULEB(e);
    const count = n.value;
    e = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

        const fi = readULEB(e);
        const funcIndex = fi.value;
        e = fi.next;

        table.set(base + i, funcIndex);
    }
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Output neighborhood
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TABLE 4788 NEIGHBORHOOD");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const start = TARGET - BEFORE;
const end = TARGET + AFTER;

for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) {

    const func = table.get(i);

    if (func === undefined) {
        console.log(
            `TABLE[${i}] = UNDEFINED`
        );
        continue;
    }

    if (func < 83) {
        console.log(
            `TABLE[${i}] = FUNC ${func} [IMPORTED]`
        );
        continue;
    }

    const typeIndex =
        functionTypes[func - 83];

    const ft = types[typeIndex];

    console.log(
        `TABLE[${i}] = FUNC ${func} | TYPE ${typeIndex} | ` +
        `(${ft.params.map(vt).join(",")}) -> (${ft.results.map(vt).join(",")})`
    );
}

console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function findSection(id: number) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sectionId = wasm[p++];
        const s = readULEB(p);
        p = s.next;

        if (sectionId === id) {
            return { start: p, end: p + s.value };
        }

        p += s.value;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

const sec = findSection(9); // Element Section
let p = sec.start;

const segCount = readULEB(p);
p = segCount.next;

console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`ELEMENT SEGMENTS = ${segCount.value}`);
console.log("");

let found = false;

for (let seg = 0; seg < segCount.value; seg++) {
    const flags = readULEB(p);
    p = flags.next;

    console.log(`SEGMENT ${seg} FLAGS=${flags.value}`);

    if (flags.value === 0) {
        // active, table 0, offset expression
        const op = wasm[p++];

        if (op !== 0x41) {
            console.log(`  Unexpected offset opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`);
            process.exit(1);
        }

        const off = readULEB(p);
        p = off.next;

        const end = wasm[p++];

        const count = readULEB(p);
        p = count.next;

        const elems: number[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
            const f = readULEB(p);
            p = f.next;
            elems.push(f.value);
        }

        const base = off.value;

        const targets = [4787, 4788, 4789, 4790, 4791, 4792, 4793];

        for (const target of targets) {
            const rel = target - base;

            if (rel >= 0 && rel < elems.length) {
                const func = elems[rel];

                console.log(
                    `  TABLE[${target}] = FUNC ${func} ` +
                    `(segment=${seg}, relative=${rel})`
                );

                found = true;
            }
        }
    } else if (flags.value === 2) {
        // active, explicit table index
        const tableIndex = readULEB(p);
        p = tableIndex.next;

        const op = wasm[p++];

        if (op !== 0x41) {
            console.log(`  Unexpected offset opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`);
            process.exit(1);
        }

        const off = readULEB(p);
        p = off.next;

        p++; // end

        const count = readULEB(p);
        p = count.next;

        const elems: number[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
            const f = readULEB(p);
            p = f.next;
            elems.push(f.value);
        }

        const base = off.value;

        const targets = [4787, 4788, 4789, 4790, 4791, 4792, 4793];

        for (const target of targets) {
            const rel = target - base;

            if (rel >= 0 && rel < elems.length) {
                const func = elems[rel];

                console.log(
                    `  TABLE[${target}] = FUNC ${func} ` +
                    `(segment=${seg}, table=${tableIndex.value}, relative=${rel})`
                );

                found = true;
            }
        }
    } else {
        console.log("  Unsupported element segment format; skipping.");
    }
}

function u32() {
    let r = 0;
    let s = 0;

    while (true) {

        const b = buf[p++];

        r |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            return r >>> 0;

        s += 7;
    }
}

function hex(x, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEBAt(pos) {

    let r = 0;
    let s = 0;
    let q = pos;

    while (true) {

        const b = buf[q++];

        r |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            return {
                value: r >>> 0,
                next: q
            };

        s += 7;
    }
}

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(p) {
  return buf[p];
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(pos++);

    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = u8(pos++);

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      break;
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function hex(n, w = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function skipInitExpr(pos) {
  const op = u8(pos++);

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const v = readSLEB(pos);
    pos = v.next;

    if (u8(pos++) !== 0x0b) {
      throw new Error("Invalid i32.const init expr");
    }

    return pos;
  }

  throw new Error(
    `Unsupported init expr opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" ELEMENT TABLE — VERIFIED FORMAT");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const sectionId = u8(p++);

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const sectionStart = p;
  const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId !== 9) {
    p = sectionEnd;
    continue;
  }

  console.log("SECTION 9");
  console.log("PAYLOAD START :", hex(sectionStart));
  console.log("PAYLOAD SIZE  :", sectionSize);
  console.log();

  const segInfo = readULEB(p);
  const segmentCount = segInfo.value;
  p = segInfo.next;

  console.log("SEGMENT COUNT :", segmentCount);
  console.log();

  for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount; seg++) {

    const flagsInfo = readULEB(p);
    const flags = flagsInfo.value;
    p = flagsInfo.next;

    console.log(`SEGMENT ${seg}`);
    console.log("FLAGS :", flags);

    let tableIndex = 0;
    let base = 0;

    /*
      flags = 0:

      active segment
      table index = 0
      offset expression
      vector of function indices

      IMPORTANT:
      There is NO element-type byte here.
    */

    if (flags === 0) {

      const exprStart = p;

      const op = u8(p++);

      if (op !== 0x41) {
        throw new Error(
          `Expected i32.const at ${hex(exprStart)}`
        );
      }

      const baseInfo = readSLEB(p);
      base = baseInfo.value;
      p = baseInfo.next;

      const endOp = u8(p++);

      if (endOp !== 0x0b) {
        throw new Error(
          `Expected end opcode at ${hex(p - 1)}`
        );
      }

      tableIndex = 0;

    } else {

      throw new Error(
        `Unsupported element segment flags=${flags}`
      );
    }

    console.log("TABLE :", tableIndex);
    console.log("BASE  :", base);

    const countInfo = readULEB(p);
    const vectorCount = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    console.log("VECTOR COUNT :", vectorCount);
    console.log();

    if (vectorCount < 2055) {
      throw new Error(
        `Vector too small: ${vectorCount}`
      );
    }

    console.log("TARGET WINDOW");
    console.log("--------------------------------------------");

    const targets = new Map();

    for (let i = 0; i < vectorCount; i++) {

      const f = readULEB(p);
      const func = f.value;
      p = f.next;

      const slot = base + i;

      if (slot >= 2045 && slot <= 2059) {

        targets.set(slot, func);

        console.log(
          `table[${slot}] = FUNC ${func} ` +
          `(relative=${i})`
        );
      }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("VERIFICATION");
    console.log("--------------------------------------------");

    const expected = {
      2049: 8954,
      2050: 8953,
      2051: 117,
      2052: 100,
      2053: 8952,
      2054: 8951,
      2055: 8950
    };

    for (const [slot, func] of Object.entries(expected)) {

      const actual = targets.get(Number(slot));

      console.log(
        `table[${slot}] = ${actual} ` +
        `(expected ${func}) ` +
        (actual === func ? "✓" : "✗")
      );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("RELATIVE OFFSETS");
    console.log("--------------------------------------------");

    for (const slot of [2049, 2050, 2051, 2052, 2053, 2054, 2055]) {

      console.log(
        `table[${slot}] → relative ${slot - base}`
      );
    }

    console.log();
  }

  break;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectTextTables.mjs

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);
const TARGET_START = 0x183e60;
const TARGET_END = 0x1840b0;

function hex(n, width = 8) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(width, '0');
}

function uleb(s) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (s.p >= wasm.length) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF in ULEB');
        const b = wasm[s.p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) return value >>> 0;
        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('ULEB too large');
    }
}

function sleb(s) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    do {
        if (s.p >= wasm.length) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF in SLEB');
        b = wasm[s.p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;
    } while (b & 0x80);

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) value |= (~0 << shift);
    return value | 0;
}

function sections() {
    const result = [];
    const s = { p: 8 };

    while (s.p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[s.p++];
        const size = uleb(s);
        const start = s.p;
        const end = start + size;

        if (end > wasm.length) throw new Error('Invalid section size');

        result.push({ id, start, end });
        s.p = end;
    }

    return result;
}

function initExpr(s) {
    const op = wasm[s.p++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const value = sleb(s);
        if (wasm[s.p++] !== 0x0b) throw new Error('Invalid i32 init expr');
        return { kind: 'i32', value: value >>> 0 };
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {
        const index = uleb(s);
        if (wasm[s.p++] !== 0x0b) throw new Error('Invalid global init expr');
        return { kind: 'global', index };
    }

    throw new Error(`Unsupported init expr opcode ${hex(op, 2)}`);
}

function parseData(sec) {
    const s = { p: sec.start };
    const count = uleb(s);
    const segments = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const flags = uleb(s);
        let active = false;
        let memoryIndex = 0;
        let expr = null;

        if (flags === 0) {
            active = true;
            expr = initExpr(s);
        } else if (flags === 1) {
            active = false;
        } else if (flags === 2) {
            active = true;
            memoryIndex = uleb(s);
            expr = initExpr(s);
        } else {
            throw new Error(`Unsupported data flags ${flags}`);
        }

        const length = uleb(s);
        const fileOffset = s.p;
        const fileEnd = fileOffset + length;

        if (fileEnd > sec.end) throw new Error('Segment exceeds Data section');

        segments.push({
            index: i,
            active,
            memoryIndex,
            expr,
            start: active && memoryIndex === 0 && expr?.kind === 'i32'
                ? expr.value
                : null,
            length,
            fileOffset,
            fileEnd,
        });

        s.p = fileEnd;
    }

    return segments;
}

const dataSection = sections().find(sec => sec.id === 11);
if (!dataSection) throw new Error('Data section not found');

const segments = parseData(dataSection);
const memory = Buffer.alloc(TARGET_END - TARGET_START, 0);
const covered = Buffer.alloc(TARGET_END - TARGET_START, 0);

for (const seg of segments) {
    if (!seg.active || seg.start === null) continue;

    const segEnd = seg.start + seg.length;
    const overlapStart = Math.max(seg.start, TARGET_START);
    const overlapEnd = Math.min(segEnd, TARGET_END);

    if (overlapStart >= overlapEnd) continue;

    const src = seg.fileOffset + overlapStart - seg.start;
    const dst = overlapStart - TARGET_START;
    const length = overlapEnd - overlapStart;

    wasm.copy(memory, dst, src, src + length);
    covered.fill(1, dst, dst + length);
}

console.log('RECONSTRUCTED INITIAL DATA MEMORY');
console.log('READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION');
console.log(`FILE SIZE: ${wasm.length}`);
console.log(`RANGE: ${hex(TARGET_START)} .. ${hex(TARGET_END)}`);
console.log('NOTE: Uncovered bytes are displayed as zero-initialized memory.');
console.log('They are not proof of their later runtime values.');

for (let address = TARGET_START; address < TARGET_END; address += 16) {
    const offset = address - TARGET_START;
    const bytes = memory.subarray(offset, Math.min(offset + 16, memory.length));
    const marks = covered.subarray(offset, Math.min(offset + 16, covered.length));

    const byteText = [...bytes]
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
        .join(' ');

    const coverage = [...marks]
        .map(b => b ? '#' : '.')
        .join('');

    const ascii = [...bytes]
        .map(b => b >= 0x20 && b <= 0x7e ? String.fromCharCode(b) : '.')
        .join('');

    console.log(`${hex(address)}  ${byteText.padEnd(47)}  ${ascii}  ${coverage}`);
}

console.log('\n32-BIT WORDS AT ALIGNED ADDRESSES');
for (let address = Math.ceil(TARGET_START / 4) * 4;
     address + 4 <= TARGET_END;
     address += 4) {
    const offset = address - TARGET_START;
    const value = memory.readUInt32LE(offset);
    const marks = [...covered.subarray(offset, offset + 4)]
        .map(b => b ? '#' : '.')
        .join('');

    console.log(`${hex(address)}  ${hex(value)}  ${value}  coverage=${marks}`);
}

console.log('\nDATA SEGMENTS NEAR TARGET');
for (const seg of segments) {
    if (seg.start === null) continue;

    const end = seg.start + seg.length;
    if (end <= TARGET_START - 0x100 || seg.start >= TARGET_END + 0x100) continue;

    console.log(
        `SEG ${seg.index}  mem=${hex(seg.start)}..${hex(end)} ` +
        `file=${hex(seg.fileOffset)}..${hex(seg.fileEnd)} ` +
        `length=${seg.length}`
    );
}

console.log('\nDONE - READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION');

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' TON CLIENT API INSPECTION');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY MODE');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const api = provider.api();

    console.log('API TYPE:');
    console.log(api.constructor.name);
    console.log('');

    if (!(api instanceof TonClient)) {
        console.log('Unexpected API type.');
        console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
        return;
    }

    /*
     * TonClient contains a protected HttpApi instance.
     * We access it only for inspection.
     */
    const internalApi = (api as any).api;

    console.log('INTERNAL API TYPE:');
    console.log(
        internalApi?.constructor?.name ?? 'undefined'
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('AVAILABLE METHODS:');

    if (internalApi) {
        const methods = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(
            Object.getPrototypeOf(internalApi)
        );

        for (const method of methods) {
            if (
                method.toLowerCase().includes('fee') ||
                method.toLowerCase().includes('estimate')
            ) {
                console.log(`- ${method}`);
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log('END');
    console.log('==============================================');
}
