import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import {
    TonConnect,
    CHAIN,
} from '@tonconnect/sdk';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const STORAGE_PATH = path.resolve(
    process.cwd(),
    'temp',
    'testnet',
    'tonconnect.json'
);

const MANIFEST_URL =
    'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ton-org/blueprint/main/tonconnect/manifest.json';

type RuntimeFeature = {
    name?: string;
    maxMessages?: number;
    [key: string]: unknown;
};

type RuntimeDevice = {
    platform?: string;
    appVersion?: string;
    features?: RuntimeFeature[];
};

type RuntimeWallet = {
    name?: string;
    appName?: string;
    device?: RuntimeDevice;
    account?: {
        address?: string;
        chain?: string;
    };
};

function section(title: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(60));
    console.log(title);
    console.log('='.repeat(60));
}

function createStorage() {
    return {
        async setItem(key: string, value: string): Promise<void> {
            const dir = path.dirname(STORAGE_PATH);

            if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
                fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
            }

            let data: Record<string, string> = {};

            if (fs.existsSync(STORAGE_PATH)) {
                try {
                    data = JSON.parse(
                        fs.readFileSync(STORAGE_PATH, 'utf8')
                    );
                } catch {
                    data = {};
                }
            }

            data[key] = value;

            fs.writeFileSync(
                STORAGE_PATH,
                JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)
            );
        },

        async getItem(key: string): Promise<string | null> {
            if (!fs.existsSync(STORAGE_PATH)) {
                return null;
            }

            try {
                const data: Record<string, string> = JSON.parse(
                    fs.readFileSync(STORAGE_PATH, 'utf8')
                );

                return data[key] ?? null;
            } catch {
                return null;
            }
        },

        async removeItem(key: string): Promise<void> {
            if (!fs.existsSync(STORAGE_PATH)) {
                return;
            }

            try {
                const data: Record<string, string> = JSON.parse(
                    fs.readFileSync(STORAGE_PATH, 'utf8')
                );

                delete data[key];

                fs.writeFileSync(
                    STORAGE_PATH,
                    JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)
                );
            } catch {
                // Intentionally ignored.
                // This script is diagnostic/read-only.
            }
        },
    };
}

function normalizeAddress(value: string): string {
    return Address.parse(value).toRawString();
}

function sameAddress(
    connected: string,
    expected: Address
): boolean {
    return normalizeAddress(connected) === expected.toRawString();
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('TONCONNECT WALLET CAPABILITY INSPECTOR V22');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('MODE: READ ONLY');
    console.log('NETWORK: TESTNET');
    console.log('SEND: NO');
    console.log('APPROVAL: NO');
    console.log('BROADCAST: NO');
    console.log('STATE CHANGE: NO');

    section('1. TONCONNECT STORAGE');

    console.log(`Storage: ${STORAGE_PATH}`);

    if (!fs.existsSync(STORAGE_PATH)) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: TonConnect storage file was not found.'
        );
    }

    console.log('Storage file: PRESENT');

    section('2. RESTORE TONCONNECT CONNECTION');

    const connector = new TonConnect({
        storage: createStorage(),
        manifestUrl: MANIFEST_URL,
    });

    await connector.restoreConnection();

    if (!connector.wallet) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: No restored TonConnect wallet was found.'
        );
    }

    /*
     * TonConnect SDK 2.2.0 exposes some wallet/feature properties
     * differently in TypeScript definitions than at runtime.
     *
     * We intentionally create a local runtime view.
     * No send method is accessed or called.
     */
    const wallet = connector.wallet as unknown as RuntimeWallet;

    console.log(
        `Wallet name: ${wallet.name ?? '(not reported)'}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Wallet appName: ${wallet.appName ?? '(not reported)'}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Wallet device platform: ${
            wallet.device?.platform ?? '(not reported)'
        }`
    );

    console.log(
        `Wallet device appVersion: ${
            wallet.device?.appVersion ?? '(not reported)'
        }`
    );

    const connectedAddress = wallet.account?.address;

    if (!connectedAddress) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Connected wallet did not provide an account address.'
        );
    }

    console.log(`Connected wallet: ${connectedAddress}`);

    console.log(
        `Expected wallet: ${USER.toString({
            bounceable: true,
            testOnly: true,
        })}`
    );

    if (!sameAddress(connectedAddress, USER)) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Connected wallet does not match expected USER.'
        );
    }

    console.log('Wallet identity: PASS');

    section('3. NETWORK');

    console.log(
        `Account chain: ${
            wallet.account?.chain ?? '(not reported)'
        }`
    );

    console.log(
        `Expected TESTNET chain: ${CHAIN.TESTNET}`
    );

    if (wallet.account?.chain !== CHAIN.TESTNET) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Connected wallet is not reporting TESTNET.'
        );
    }

    console.log('Network TESTNET: PASS');

    section('4. DEVICE CAPABILITIES');

    const features = wallet.device?.features;

    if (!Array.isArray(features)) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Wallet did not expose device.features.'
        );
    }

    console.log(`Feature count: ${features.length}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < features.length; i++) {
        const feature = features[i];

        console.log('');
        console.log(`FEATURE ${i + 1}`);

        console.log(
            `  name: ${feature.name ?? '(unknown)'}`
        );

        if (feature.name === 'SendTransaction') {
            console.log(
                `  maxMessages: ${
                    feature.maxMessages ?? '(not reported)'
                }`
            );
        }
    }

    section('5. SENDTRANSACTION CAPABILITY');

    const sendFeature = features.find(
        (feature) =>
            feature.name === 'SendTransaction'
    );

    if (!sendFeature) {
        console.log('SendTransaction feature: NOT FOUND');

        console.log('');
        console.log('STATUS: STOP');

        console.log(
            'Reason: Wallet did not advertise SendTransaction capability.'
        );

        section('6. ABSOLUTE SAFETY');

        console.log(
            'connector.sendTransaction(): NOT CALLED'
        );
        console.log('sender.send(): NOT CALLED');
        console.log('wallet.send(): NOT CALLED');
        console.log(
            'Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED'
        );
        console.log('Transaction broadcast: NO');
        console.log('Wallet state change: NO');
        console.log('Pool state change: NO');

        return;
    }

    console.log('SendTransaction feature: FOUND');

    const maxMessages = sendFeature.maxMessages;

    if (
        typeof maxMessages !== 'number' ||
        !Number.isFinite(maxMessages)
    ) {
        console.log(
            'maxMessages: NOT VALID / NOT REPORTED'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('STATUS: STOP');

        section('6. ABSOLUTE SAFETY');

        console.log(
            'connector.sendTransaction(): NOT CALLED'
        );
        console.log('sender.send(): NOT CALLED');
        console.log('wallet.send(): NOT CALLED');
        console.log(
            'Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED'
        );
        console.log('Transaction broadcast: NO');
        console.log('Wallet state change: NO');
        console.log('Pool state change: NO');

        return;
    }

    console.log(`maxMessages: ${maxMessages}`);

    if (maxMessages < 2) {
        console.log(
            'Two-message capability: FAIL'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'STOP: Wallet advertises fewer than 2 messages.'
        );

        section('6. ABSOLUTE SAFETY');

        console.log(
            'connector.sendTransaction(): NOT CALLED'
        );
        console.log('sender.send(): NOT CALLED');
        console.log('wallet.send(): NOT CALLED');
        console.log(
            'Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED'
        );
        console.log('Transaction broadcast: NO');
        console.log('Wallet state change: NO');
        console.log('Pool state change: NO');

        return;
    }

    console.log(
        'Two-message capability: PASS'
    );

    section('6. MULTI-MESSAGE CAPABILITY CHECK');

    console.log('Requested message count: 2');
    console.log(`Wallet maxMessages: ${maxMessages}`);

    if (2 <= maxMessages) {
        console.log(
            'Requested 2 <= wallet maximum: PASS'
        );
    } else {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Requested two messages exceed wallet capability.'
        );
    }

    section('7. RAW SENDTRANSACTION FEATURE');

    console.log(
        JSON.stringify(
            sendFeature,
            null,
            2
        )
    );

    section('8. FINAL RESULT');

    console.log('Wallet identity: PASS');
    console.log('TESTNET: PASS');
    console.log('SendTransaction: PASS');
    console.log(`maxMessages: ${maxMessages}`);
    console.log('Two-message capability: PASS');

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'STATUS: WALLET CAPABILITY CHECK PASSED'
    );

    section('9. ABSOLUTE SAFETY');

    console.log(
        'connector.sendTransaction(): NOT CALLED'
    );
    console.log('sender.send(): NOT CALLED');
    console.log('wallet.send(): NOT CALLED');
    console.log(
        'Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED'
    );
    console.log('Transaction broadcast: NO');
    console.log('Wallet state change: NO');
    console.log('Pool state change: NO');

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'IMPORTANT: This script only inspected the connected wallet.'
    );
}

FILE: scripts\inspectType1.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_TYPE = 1;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(p) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function valtype(x) {

  const names = {
    0x7f: "i32",
    0x7e: "i64",
    0x7d: "f32",
    0x7c: "f64",
    0x7b: "v128",
    0x70: "funcref",
    0x6f: "externref"
  };

  return names[x] || `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
}

function findTypeSection() {

  let p = 8;

  while (p < bytes.length) {

    const id = bytes[p++];

    const len = uleb(p);
    p = len.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + len.value;

    if (id === 1)
      return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("Type section not found");
}

function inspect() {

  const sec = findTypeSection();

  let p = sec.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("TYPE INSPECTOR");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`WASM SIZE : ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`);
  console.log(`TARGET    : TYPE ${TARGET_TYPE}`);
  console.log(`TYPE COUNT: ${count.value}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  if (TARGET_TYPE >= count.value) {
    throw new Error(
      `Type ${TARGET_TYPE} does not exist`
    );
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const form = bytes[p++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
      throw new Error(
        `Unexpected type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at type ${i}`
      );
    }

    const params = uleb(p);
    p = params.next;

    const paramTypes = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < params.value; j++) {
      paramTypes.push(
        valtype(bytes[p++])
      );
    }

    const results = uleb(p);
    p = results.next;

    const resultTypes = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < results.value; j++) {
      resultTypes.push(
        valtype(bytes[p++])
      );
    }

    if (i === TARGET_TYPE) {

      console.log(`TYPE INDEX : ${i}`);
      console.log(
        `PARAM COUNT: ${params.value}`
      );
      console.log(
        `PARAMS     : (${paramTypes.join(", ")})`
      );
      console.log(
        `RESULT CNT : ${results.value}`
      );
      console.log(
        `RESULTS    : (${resultTypes.join(", ")})`
      );

      console.log("");
      console.log("SIGNATURE");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      console.log(
        `(${paramTypes.join(", ")}) -> (${resultTypes.join(", ")})`
      );

      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
      console.log("END");
      console.log("============================================================");

      return;
    }
  }

  throw new Error(
    `Type ${TARGET_TYPE} not found`
  );
}

inspect();

FILE: scripts\inspectType2.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_TYPE = 2;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM TYPE 2 EXACT SIGNATURE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

function u32leb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value,
        next: p,
    };
}

function typeName(t: number) {
    switch (t) {
        case 0x7f:
            return "i32";
        case 0x7e:
            return "i64";
        case 0x7d:
            return "f32";
        case 0x7c:
            return "f64";
        case 0x7b:
            return "v128";
        case 0x70:
            return "funcref";
        case 0x6f:
            return "externref";
        case 0x40:
            return "void";
        default:
            return `0x${t.toString(16)}`;
    }
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

let p = 8;

let typeStart = -1;
let typeEnd = -1;

while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p];

    const sizeInfo = u32leb(p + 1);

    const payloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sizeInfo.value;

    if (id === 1) {
        typeStart = payloadStart;
        typeEnd = payloadEnd;
        break;
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

if (typeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("TYPE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log(
    `TYPE SECTION = ${hex(typeStart)} .. ${hex(typeEnd)}`
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let q = typeStart;

const countInfo = u32leb(q);
q = countInfo.next;

const count = countInfo.value;

console.log(`TYPE COUNT = ${count}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const form = buf[q++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at TYPE ${i}`
        );
    }

    const paramInfo = u32leb(q);
    q = paramInfo.next;

    const params: string[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < paramInfo.value; j++) {
        params.push(typeName(buf[q++]));
    }

    const resultInfo = u32leb(q);
    q = resultInfo.next;

    const results: string[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < resultInfo.value; j++) {
        results.push(typeName(buf[q++]));
    }

    if (i === TARGET_TYPE) {
        console.log(`TYPE INDEX = ${i}`);
        console.log(`PARAM COUNT = ${params.length}`);
        console.log(
            `PARAMS      = [ ${params.join(", ")} ]`
        );
        console.log(`RESULT COUNT = ${results.length}`);
        console.log(
            `RESULTS     = [ ${results.join(", ")} ]`
        );
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        console.log("KNOWN USERS OF TYPE 2:");
        console.log("  FUNC23  = imported \"a\".\"x\"");
        console.log("  FUNC265 = defined function");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        break;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectType4CallIndirect.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const TARGET_TYPE = 4;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TYPE-4 CALL_INDIRECT CONTEXT INSPECTOR");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = CODE_START; i < CODE_END - 2; i++) {

  if (b[i] !== 0x11)
    continue;

  try {

    const typeR = uleb(i + 1);
    const tableR = uleb(typeR.p);

    if (
      typeR.v !== TARGET_TYPE ||
      tableR.v !== 0
    )
      continue;

    hits++;

    console.log(
      `HIT #${hits} @ ${hex(i)}`
    );

    const from = Math.max(CODE_START, i - 80);
    const to = Math.min(CODE_END, tableR.p);

    console.log(
      Array.from(b.slice(from, to))
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );

    console.log("");
  }
  catch {
    // ignore malformed byte patterns
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectType4Func2055.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET_TYPE = 4;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let typeStart = null;
let typeEnd = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const z = uleb(p);
  const size = z.value;
  p = z.next;

  if (id === 1) {
    typeStart = p;
    typeEnd = p + size;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

if (typeStart === null) {
  console.log("TYPE SECTION NOT FOUND");
  process.exit(1);
}

p = typeStart;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 2055 TYPE RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TYPE INDEX : ${TARGET_TYPE}`);
console.log(`TYPE COUNT : ${count.value}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

  const form = b[p++];

  const pc = uleb(p);
  p = pc.next;

  const params = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
    params.push(b[p++]);

  const rc = uleb(p);
  p = rc.next;

  const results = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
    results.push(b[p++]);

  if (i === TARGET_TYPE) {

    const names = {
      0x7f: "i32",
      0x7e: "i64",
      0x7d: "f32",
      0x7c: "f64",
      0x70: "funcref",
      0x6f: "externref"
    };

    console.log(`FORM       : 0x${form.toString(16)}`);

    console.log(
      `PARAM COUNT: ${params.length}`
    );

    params.forEach((x, n) =>
      console.log(
        `PARAM${n}     : ${names[x] ?? `0x${x.toString(16)}`}`
      )
    );

    console.log(
      `RESULT COUNT: ${results.length}`
    );

    results.forEach((x, n) =>
      console.log(
        `RESULT${n}    : ${names[x] ?? `0x${x.toString(16)}`}`
      )
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("FUNC 2055 LOCALS 0..3:");
    console.log("  local0 = parameter 0");
    console.log("  local1 = parameter 1");
    console.log("  local2 = parameter 2");
    console.log("  local3 = parameter 3");

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("DONE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    process.exit(0);
  }
}

console.log("TYPE 4 NOT FOUND");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectType7Wrappers.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const targets = [3901, 6521, 8952];

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readU32LE(pos) {
  return (
    buf[pos] |
    (buf[pos + 1] << 8) |
    (buf[pos + 2] << 16) |
    (buf[pos + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readString(pos) {
  const r = readULEB(pos);
  const bytes = buf.subarray(r.next, r.next + r.value);
  return {
    value: Buffer.from(bytes).toString("utf8"),
    next: r.next + r.value,
  };
}

function typeName(v) {
  switch (v) {
    case 0x7f: return "i32";
    case 0x7e: return "i64";
    case 0x7d: return "f32";
    case 0x7c: return "f64";
    case 0x70: return "funcref";
    case 0x6f: return "externref";
    default: return "0x" + v.toString(16);
  }
}

let pos = 8;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let typeSection = null;
let functionSection = null;
let codeSection = null;

while (pos < buf.length) {
  const id = buf[pos++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
  const size = sizeInfo.value;
  pos = sizeInfo.next;

  const start = pos;
  const end = start + size;

  if (id === 1) typeSection = { start, end };
  if (id === 3) functionSection = { start, end };
  if (id === 10) codeSection = { start, end };

  pos = end;
}

if (!typeSection || !functionSection || !codeSection) {
  throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found.");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TYPE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const types = [];

{
  let p = typeSection.start;

  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  const count = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const form = buf[p++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
      throw new Error(
        `Unexpected function type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at ${p - 1}`
      );
    }

    const paramInfo = readULEB(p);
    const paramCount = paramInfo.value;
    p = paramInfo.next;

    const params = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
      params.push(buf[p++]);
    }

    const resultInfo = readULEB(p);
    const resultCount = resultInfo.value;
    p = resultInfo.next;

    const results = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
      results.push(buf[p++]);
    }

    types.push({ params, results });
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const definedTypes = [];

{
  let p = functionSection.start;

  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  const count = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const t = readULEB(p);
    definedTypes.push(t.value);
    p = t.next;
  }
}

// Imported functions = 83 for this module.
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let codeBodies = [];

{
  let p = codeSection.start;

  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  const count = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < count; bodyIndex++) {
    const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    const bodyStart = bodySizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    codeBodies.push({
      bodyIndex,
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// HELPERS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function functionType(funcIndex) {
  if (funcIndex < IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS) {
    return null;
  }

  const bodyIndex = funcIndex - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS;

  if (bodyIndex < 0 || bodyIndex >= definedTypes.length) {
    return null;
  }

  const typeIndex = definedTypes[bodyIndex];

  return {
    typeIndex,
    type: types[typeIndex],
  };
}

function readLocals(body) {
  let p = body.start;

  const localCountInfo = readULEB(p);
  const localDeclCount = localCountInfo.value;
  p = localCountInfo.next;

  const locals = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
    const countInfo = readULEB(p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const valueType = buf[p++];

    locals.push({
      count,
      valueType,
    });
  }

  return {
    localDeclCount,
    locals,
    instructionStart: p,
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// OUTPUT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSPECT TYPE 7 WRAPPERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :", IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS);
console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS :", codeBodies.length);
console.log("TOTAL FUNCTIONS :", IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + codeBodies.length);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const funcIndex of targets) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("FUNC", funcIndex);
  console.log("============================================================");

  const ft = functionType(funcIndex);

  if (!ft) {
    console.log("TYPE INFORMATION NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  const type = ft.type;

  console.log("TYPE INDEX :", ft.typeIndex);

  console.log(
    "SIGNATURE : (" +
      type.params.map(typeName).join(", ") +
      ") -> (" +
      type.results.map(typeName).join(", ") +
      ")"
  );

  console.log("PARAM COUNT :", type.params.length);
  console.log("RESULT COUNT :", type.results.length);

  console.log("");
  console.log("PARAMETERS:");

  type.params.forEach((p, i) => {
    console.log(
      `  param[${i}] = ${typeName(p)}`
    );
  });

  const bodyIndex = funcIndex - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS;
  const body = codeBodies[bodyIndex];

  console.log("");
  console.log("BODY INDEX :", bodyIndex);
  console.log(
    "BODY : 0x" +
      body.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
      " .. 0x" +
      (body.end - 1).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
  );

  const locals = readLocals(body);

  console.log("");
  console.log("LOCAL DECLARATIONS :", locals.localDeclCount);

  let localIndex = type.params.length;

  for (const decl of locals.locals) {
    for (let i = 0; i < decl.count; i++) {
      console.log(
        `  local[${localIndex}] = ${typeName(decl.valueType)}`
      );
      localIndex++;
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("TOTAL LOCALS :", localIndex);

  console.log("");
  console.log("INSTRUCTION START : 0x" +
    locals.instructionStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"));

  console.log("");
  console.log("KEY PARAMETER OBSERVATION:");

  if (type.params.length > 0) {
    console.log(
      "  local0 corresponds to param[0] =", typeName(type.params[0])
    );
  }

  if (type.params.length > 1) {
    console.log(
      "  local1 corresponds to param[1] =", typeName(type.params[1])
    );
  }

  if (type.params.length > 2) {
    console.log(
      "  local2 corresponds to param[2] =", typeName(type.params[2])
    );
  }

  if (type.params.length > 3) {
    console.log(
      "  local3 corresponds to param[3] =", typeName(type.params[3])
    );
  }

  if (type.params.length > 4) {
    console.log(
      "  local4 corresponds to param[4] =", typeName(type.params[4])
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectUnknownOpcode.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from "node:fs";

const file = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0, shift = 0, b;
  do {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;
  } while (b & 0x80);
  return { value, next: pos };
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[pos++];
  const size = uleb(pos);
  const payload = size.next;
  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = payload;
    break;
  }
  pos = payload + size.value;
}

if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error("Code section not found");

pos = codeStart;
const count = uleb(pos);
pos = count.next;

const wanted = new Set([9479, 9480]);

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
  const size = uleb(pos);
  const bodyStart = size.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size.value;
  const func = 83 + ordinal;

  if (wanted.has(func)) {
    console.log(`\nFUNC ${func}`);
    console.log(`BODY 0x${bodyStart.toString(16)} .. 0x${bodyEnd.toString(16)}`);
    for (let at = bodyStart; at < bodyEnd; at += 16) {
      const end = Math.min(at + 16, bodyEnd);
      const hex = [...wasm.subarray(at, end)]
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
      console.log(`0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${hex}`);
    }
  }

  pos = bodyEnd;
}

FILE: scripts\inspectUserPtonHistoryV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCFe1J8epgixmnGvSWBsB6O7gEABl1BiY8NJkL2pjqv1I6S'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const TARGET_FROM = 99490000000000n;
const TARGET_TO   = 99493000000000n;

function getOpcode(cell: Cell | null | undefined): string {
    if (!cell) return 'NONE';

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
        return 'NO_32BIT_OPCODE';
    }

    return (
        '0x' +
        s.loadUint(32).toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );
}

function printMessage(label: string, message: any) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(label);

    console.log('MESSAGE TYPE:');
    console.log(message.info.type);

    if (message.info.type === 'internal') {
        console.log('SOURCE:');
        console.log(message.info.src.toString());

        console.log('DESTINATION:');
        console.log(message.info.dest.toString());

        console.log('VALUE:');
        console.log(message.info.value.coins.toString());
    } else {
        console.log('SOURCE:');
        console.log(
            message.info.src
                ? message.info.src.toString()
                : 'NONE'
        );

        console.log('DESTINATION:');
        console.log(
            message.info.dest
                ? message.info.dest.toString()
                : 'NONE'
        );
    }

    console.log('OPCODE:');
    console.log(getOpcode(message.body));

    console.log('BODY BOC:');
    console.log(
        message.body
            .toBoc()
            .toString('base64')
    );
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 USER pTON HISTORY INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON WALLET:');
    console.log(PTON_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET LT RANGE:');
    console.log(`${TARGET_FROM} -> ${TARGET_TO}`);

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Connected to TON Center Testnet.');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching pTON wallet history...');

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        PTON_WALLET,
        {
            limit: 20,
            to_lt: TARGET_TO.toString(),
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(`TRANSACTIONS RECEIVED: ${txs.length}`);

    let found = 0;

    for (const tx of txs) {
        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        if (lt < TARGET_FROM || lt > TARGET_TO) {
            continue;
        }

        found++;

        console.log('');
        console.log('==============================================');
        console.log(`MATCH #${found}`);
        console.log('==============================================');

        console.log('');
        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(tx.lt.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('TIME:');
        console.log(
            new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString()
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('TRANSACTION HASH:');
        console.log(tx.hash().toString('base64'));

        printMessage(
            '--- IN MESSAGE ---',
            tx.inMessage
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `OUT MESSAGE COUNT: ${tx.outMessagesCount}`
        );

        let index = 0;

        for (const message of tx.outMessages.values()) {
            index++;

            printMessage(
                `--- OUT MESSAGE #${index} ---`,
                message
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(`MATCHES IN TARGET RANGE: ${found}`);
    console.log('==============================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
}

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const EXPECTED_PTON_ROUTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

async function getJettonWalletAddress(
    provider: NetworkProvider,
    jettonMaster: Address,
    owner: Address
): Promise<Address> {
    const contractProvider = provider.provider(jettonMaster);

    const result = await contractProvider.get('get_wallet_address', [
        {
            type: 'slice',
            cell: beginCell()
                .storeAddress(owner)
                .endCell(),
        },
    ]);

    return result.stack.readAddress();
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ROUTER JETTON WALLET INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('ROUTER:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('USER:');
    console.log(USER.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('ROOSTER MASTER:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON MASTER:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Calculating ROOSTER wallet owned by USER...');

    const userRoosterWallet = await getJettonWalletAddress(
        provider,
        ROOSTER_MASTER,
        USER
    );

    console.log('Calculating pTON wallet owned by ROUTER...');

    const routerPtonWallet = await getJettonWalletAddress(
        provider,
        PTON_MASTER,
        ROUTER
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('RESULTS');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER wallet owned by USER:');
    console.log(userRoosterWallet.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Expected known ROOSTER wallet:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER MATCH:',
        userRoosterWallet.equals(EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET)
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON wallet owned by ROUTER:');
    console.log(routerPtonWallet.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Expected known pTON wallet:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_PTON_ROUTER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'pTON MATCH:',
        routerPtonWallet.equals(EXPECTED_PTON_ROUTER_WALLET)
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

import { Address, beginCell } from "@ton/core";
import { DEX } from "@ston-fi/sdk";

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
  "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx";

const PTON_MASTER =
  "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S";

const USER =
  "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix";

const ROUTER =
  "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3";

const EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET =
  "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3";

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

export async function run(provider: any) {
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 SDK PARAMETER INSPECTION");
  console.log(" READ ONLY");
  console.log("==============================================");

  const user = Address.parse(USER);
  const roosterMaster = Address.parse(ROOSTER_MASTER);
  const ptonMaster = Address.parse(PTON_MASTER);
  const router = Address.parse(ROUTER);

  console.log("\n===== SDK OBJECT =====");

  const routerContract = DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(router);

  console.log("Router class created: PASS");
  console.log("Router address:");
  console.log(routerContract.address.toString());

  console.log("\n===== DIRECT ROOSTER WALLET =====");

  const roosterResult =
    await provider.provider(roosterMaster).get(
      "get_wallet_address",
      [
        {
          type: "slice",
          cell: beginCell()
            .storeAddress(user)
            .endCell(),
        },
      ]
    );

  const userRoosterWallet =
    roosterResult.stack.readAddress();

  console.log(userRoosterWallet.toString());

  console.log("\n===== DIRECT pTON WALLET =====");

  const ptonResult =
    await provider.provider(ptonMaster).get(
      "get_wallet_address",
      [
        {
          type: "slice",
          cell: beginCell()
            .storeAddress(router)
            .endCell(),
        },
      ]
    );

  const routerPtonWallet =
    ptonResult.stack.readAddress();

  console.log(routerPtonWallet.toString());

  console.log(
    "Known pTON wallet:",
    EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET
  );

  console.log(
    "pTON address match:",
    routerPtonWallet.toString() ===
      EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log("\n===== SDK METHOD CALL =====");

  console.log("sendTokenAddress:");
  console.log(roosterMaster.toString());

  console.log("otherTokenAddress:");
  console.log(ptonMaster.toString());

  console.log("userWalletAddress:");
  console.log(user.toString());

  console.log("sendAmount:");
  console.log(ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString());

  console.log("minLpOut:");
  console.log(MIN_LP_OUT.toString());

  try {
    const params =
      await routerContract.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
        provider.provider(router),
        {
          userWalletAddress: user,
          sendTokenAddress: roosterMaster,
          otherTokenAddress: ptonMaster,
          sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
          minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
          gasAmount: 300000000n,
          forwardGasAmount: 240000000n,
          transferExcessAddress: user,
        }
      );

    console.log("\n===== SDK RESULT =====");

    console.log("TX to:");
    console.log(params.to.toString());

    console.log("TX value:");
    console.log(params.value.toString());

    console.log(
      "Expected TX to:",
      userRoosterWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
      "TX destination:",
      params.to.toString() ===
        userRoosterWallet.toString()
        ? "PASS"
        : "FAIL"
    );

    console.log(
      "Gas:",
      BigInt(params.value.toString()) ===
        300000000n
        ? "PASS"
        : "FAIL"
    );

    console.log("\nSTATUS: SDK METHOD PASS");
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.");

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.log("\n===== SDK METHOD ERROR =====");

    console.log(
      error?.message ?? String(error)
    );

    console.log("\n===== IMPORTANT =====");

    console.log(
      "Direct Jetton wallet derivation: PASS"
    );

    console.log(
      "SDK getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams: FAIL"
    );

    console.log(
      "This confirms the failure is inside the SDK method path."
    );

    console.log(
      "NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT."
    );
  }
}

import { Address, TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI V2.2 WALLET TRANSACTION INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('NETWORK: TESTNET');
    console.log('WALLET:', USER.toString());
    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet-v4.tonhubapi.com',
    });

    const last = await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log('LATEST BLOCK:');
    console.log('SEQNO:', last.last.seqno);
    console.log('');

    const account = await client.getAccount(
        last.last.seqno,
        USER
    );

    console.log('ACCOUNT STATE:', account.account.state.type);
    console.log('');

    if (!account.account.last) {
        console.log('NO TRANSACTION HISTORY FOUND.');
        console.log('');
        return;
    }

    const lastLt = BigInt(account.account.last.lt);
    const lastHash = Buffer.from(
        account.account.last.hash,
        'base64'
    );

    console.log('LATEST ACCOUNT TRANSACTION:');
    console.log('LT:', lastLt.toString());
    console.log('HASH:', lastHash.toString('hex'));
    console.log('');

    const transactions =
        await client.getAccountTransactions(
            USER,
            lastLt,
            lastHash
        );

    console.log('TRANSACTIONS RETURNED:', transactions.length);
    console.log('');

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' RECENT TRANSACTIONS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    for (let i = 0; i < transactions.length; i++) {
        const item = transactions[i];
        const tx = item.tx;

        console.log('');
        console.log(`TRANSACTION #${i + 1}`);
        console.log('LT:', tx.lt.toString());
        console.log('HASH:', tx.hash().toString('hex'));
        console.log('TIME:', tx.now);
        console.log('BLOCK SEQNO:', item.block.seqno);
        console.log('BLOCK WORKCHAIN:', item.block.workchain);
        console.log('BLOCK SHARD:', item.block.shard);
        console.log('TOTAL FEES:', tx.totalFees.coins.toString());
        console.log('OUT MESSAGES:', tx.outMessagesCount);

        console.log('IN MESSAGE:', tx.inMessage ? 'YES' : 'NO');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('');

    void provider;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectWasmImports.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from 'node:fs';

const FILE = 'emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const TARGETS = new Set([1, 8, 14]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);
let pos = 8;
let globalFunctionIndex = 0;

function readU32() {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= wasm.length || shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid ULEB128 at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
        }

        const byte = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            return value >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readName() {
    const length = readU32();

    if (pos + length > wasm.length) {
        throw new Error('Import name exceeds file bounds');
    }

    const value = wasm.toString('utf8', pos, pos + length);
    pos += length;
    return value;
}

function skipLimits() {
    const flags = readU32();
    readU32(); // minimum

    if (flags & 1) {
        readU32(); // maximum
    }

    if (flags & ~7) {
        throw new Error(`Unknown limits flags: ${flags}`);
    }
}

function skipImport(kind) {
    switch (kind) {
        case 0:
            return readU32(); // function type index

        case 1:
            pos++; // reference type
            skipLimits();
            return null;

        case 2:
            skipLimits();
            return null;

        case 3:
            pos++; // value type
            pos++; // mutability
            return null;

        case 4:
            pos++; // tag attribute
            return readU32(); // tag type index

        default:
            throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
    }
}

function readSectionSize() {
    return readU32();
}

try {
    if (
        wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
        wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
        wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
        wasm[3] !== 0x6d
    ) {
        throw new Error('Invalid WASM magic');
    }

    while (pos < wasm.length) {
        const sectionId = wasm[pos++];
        const sectionSize = readSectionSize();
        const sectionEnd = pos + sectionSize;

        if (sectionEnd > wasm.length) {
            throw new Error('Section exceeds file bounds');
        }

        if (sectionId !== 2) {
            pos = sectionEnd;
            continue;
        }

        const importCount = readU32();

        console.log('WASM IMPORT INSPECTOR');
        console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(`Import entries: ${importCount}`);

        for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {
            const moduleName = readName();
            const fieldName = readName();
            const kind = wasm[pos++];

            const typeIndex = skipImport(kind);

            if (kind === 0) {
                if (TARGETS.has(globalFunctionIndex)) {
                    console.log(
                        `FUNCTION ${globalFunctionIndex}: ` +
                        `${moduleName}.${fieldName} ` +
                        `(type index ${typeIndex})`
                    );
                }

                globalFunctionIndex++;
            }
        }

        if (pos !== sectionEnd) {
            throw new Error(
                `Import section cursor mismatch: ` +
                `0x${pos.toString(16)} != 0x${sectionEnd.toString(16)}`
            );
        }

        console.log(`Imported functions: ${globalFunctionIndex}`);
        break;
    }

    if (globalFunctionIndex === 0) {
        throw new Error('Import section not found');
    }
} catch (error) {
    console.error('ERROR:', error.message);
    process.exitCode = 1;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectWasmImports.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from 'fs';

const WASM = './emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

let p = 8;

function readULEB(): number {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return value >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readString(): string {
    const len = readULEB();

    const s = b.subarray(
        p,
        p + len
    ).toString('utf8');

    p += len;

    return s;
}

console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' WASM IMPORT TABLE');
console.log('============================================================');

while (p < b.length) {

    const sectionId = b[p++];

    const sectionSize = readULEB();

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId !== 2) {
        p = sectionEnd;
        continue;
    }

    const count = readULEB();

    console.log(`Imports: ${count}`);
    console.log('');

    let funcIndex = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

        const moduleName = readString();
        const fieldName = readString();

        const kind = b[p++];

        let extra = '';

        if (kind === 0) {

            const typeIndex = readULEB();

            extra =
                `type=${typeIndex}`;

            console.log(
                `${funcIndex.toString().padStart(3, ' ')}  ` +
                `FUNC  ` +
                `${moduleName}.${fieldName}  ` +
                extra
            );

            funcIndex++;
        }

        else if (kind === 1) {

            const elementType = b[p++];

            const flags = readULEB();
            const min = readULEB();

            let max = '';

            if (flags & 1) {
                max = String(readULEB());
            }

            extra =
                `table elem=0x${elementType.toString(16)} ` +
                `min=${min}` +
                (max ? ` max=${max}` : '');

            console.log(
                `---  TABLE ${moduleName}.${fieldName} ${extra}`
            );
        }

        else if (kind === 2) {

            const flags = readULEB();
            const min = readULEB();

            let max = '';

            if (flags & 1) {
                max = String(readULEB());
            }

            extra =
                `memory min=${min}` +
                (max ? ` max=${max}` : '');

            console.log(
                `---  MEMORY ${moduleName}.${fieldName} ${extra}`
            );
        }

        else if (kind === 3) {

            const valueType = b[p++];
            const mutable = b[p++];

            console.log(
                `---  GLOBAL ${moduleName}.${fieldName} ` +
                `type=0x${valueType.toString(16)} ` +
                `mutable=${mutable}`
            );
        }
    }

    break;
}

console.log('');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log('DONE');
console.log('============================================================');

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectWasmKa.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from 'fs';

const WASM = './emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
let p = 8; // skip WASM magic + version

function readU32(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const byte = b[p++];
        result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readBytes(n: number): Buffer {
    const out = b.subarray(p, p + n);
    p += n;
    return out;
}

function readName(): string {
    const len = readU32();
    return readBytes(len).toString('utf8');
}

function valType(byte: number): string {
    switch (byte) {
        case 0x7f: return 'i32';
        case 0x7e: return 'i64';
        case 0x7d: return 'f32';
        case 0x7c: return 'f64';
        case 0x70: return 'funcref';
        case 0x6f: return 'externref';
        default: return `0x${byte.toString(16)}`;
    }
}

const types: { params: string[]; results: string[] }[] = [];
const importedFuncTypeIndices: number[] = [];
const definedFuncTypeIndices: number[] = [];
const exports: { name: string; kind: number; index: number }[] = [];

while (p < b.length) {
    const sectionId = b[p++];
    const sectionSize = readU32();

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

    switch (sectionId) {
        case 1: {
            // Type section
            const count = readU32();

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const form = b[p++];

                if (form !== 0x60) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Unsupported type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at type ${i}`
                    );
                }

                const paramCount = readU32();
                const params: string[] = [];

                for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
                    params.push(valType(b[p++]));
                }

                const resultCount = readU32();
                const results: string[] = [];

                for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
                    results.push(valType(b[p++]));
                }

                types.push({ params, results });
            }

            break;
        }

        case 2: {
            // Import section
            const count = readU32();

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                readName(); // module
                readName(); // name

                const kind = b[p++];

                if (kind === 0) {
                    const typeIndex = readU32();
                    importedFuncTypeIndices.push(typeIndex);
                } else if (kind === 1) {
                    p++; // table element type
                    const flags = readU32();
                    if (flags & 1) readU32();
                    readU32();
                } else if (kind === 2) {
                    const flags = readU32();
                    readU32();
                    if (flags & 1) readU32();
                } else if (kind === 3) {
                    p += 2;
                }
            }

            break;
        }

        case 3: {
            // Function section
            const count = readU32();

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                definedFuncTypeIndices.push(readU32());
            }

            break;
        }

        case 7: {
            // Export section
            const count = readU32();

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const name = readName();
                const kind = b[p++];
                const index = readU32();

                exports.push({ name, kind, index });
            }

            break;
        }
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
}

const ka = exports.find(x => x.name === 'Ka');

if (!ka) {
    throw new Error('Export Ka not found');
}

console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' WASM EXPORT ABI INSPECTOR');
console.log('============================================================');

console.log(`WASM: ${WASM}`);
console.log(`Size: ${b.length} bytes`);

console.log('');
console.log('=== Ka EXPORT ===');
console.log(`Name          : ${ka.name}`);
console.log(`Kind          : ${ka.kind} (0=function)`);
console.log(`Function index: ${ka.index}`);

const importedCount = importedFuncTypeIndices.length;

let typeIndex: number | undefined;

if (ka.index < importedCount) {
    typeIndex = importedFuncTypeIndices[ka.index];
    console.log(`Function kind : IMPORTED`);
} else {
    const definedIndex = ka.index - importedCount;
    typeIndex = definedFuncTypeIndices[definedIndex];

    console.log(`Function kind : DEFINED`);
    console.log(`Defined index : ${definedIndex}`);
}

console.log(`Type index    : ${typeIndex}`);

if (typeIndex === undefined || !types[typeIndex]) {
    throw new Error(`Type ${typeIndex} not found`);
}

const type = types[typeIndex];

console.log('');
console.log('=== Ka SIGNATURE ===');
console.log(`Parameters (${type.params.length}):`);

type.params.forEach((x, i) => {
    console.log(`  arg${i}: ${x}`);
});

console.log(`Results (${type.results.length}):`);

type.results.forEach((x, i) => {
    console.log(`  result${i}: ${x}`);
});

console.log('');
console.log('=== SUMMARY ===');
console.log(
    `Ka(${type.params.join(', ')}) -> (${type.results.join(', ')})`
);

FILE: scripts\inspectWasmKaBody.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from 'fs';

const WASM = './emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const TARGET_DEFINED_INDEX = 5456;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
let p = 8;

function readU32(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const byte = b[p++];

        result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readBytes(n: number): Buffer {
    const out = b.subarray(p, p + n);
    p += n;
    return out;
}

function hex(data: Buffer): string {
    return Array.from(data)
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
        .join(' ');
}

let codeSectionFound = false;

while (p < b.length) {
    const sectionId = b[p++];
    const sectionSize = readU32();

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeSectionFound = true;

        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' WASM CODE SECTION INSPECTOR');
        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log(`Code section offset : ${sectionStart}`);
        console.log(`Code section size   : ${sectionSize}`);

        const functionCount = readU32();

        console.log(`Defined functions   : ${functionCount}`);
        console.log(`Target defined idx  : ${TARGET_DEFINED_INDEX}`);

        if (TARGET_DEFINED_INDEX >= functionCount) {
            throw new Error(
                `Target ${TARGET_DEFINED_INDEX} >= function count ${functionCount}`
            );
        }

        for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {
            const bodySize = readU32();
            const bodyStart = p;

            if (i === TARGET_DEFINED_INDEX) {
                const body = readBytes(bodySize);

                console.log('');
                console.log('=== TARGET FUNCTION ===');
                console.log(`Defined index : ${i}`);
                console.log(`Body offset   : ${bodyStart}`);
                console.log(`Body size     : ${bodySize} bytes`);

                console.log('');
                console.log('=== FIRST 256 BYTES ===');

                const first = body.subarray(
                    0,
                    Math.min(256, body.length)
                );

                console.log(hex(first));

                console.log('');
                console.log('=== LAST 32 BYTES ===');

                const last = body.subarray(
                    Math.max(0, body.length - 32)
                );

                console.log(hex(last));

                console.log('');
                console.log('=== DONE ===');

                process.exit(0);
            }

            p += bodySize;
        }

        throw new Error('Target function body was not found');
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
}

if (!codeSectionFound) {
    throw new Error('WASM Code section not found');
}

FILE: scripts\inspectWasmKaTargets.ts

import fs from 'fs';

const WASM = './emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const TARGETS = [
    0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 12, 18, 54, 65, 82,
    83, 95, 106, 114, 170, 618, 1075, 1481, 1910
];

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

let p = 8;

function readU32(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const byte = b[p++];

        result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readName(): string {
    const len = readU32();
    const s = b.subarray(p, p + len).toString('utf8');
    p += len;
    return s;
}

function typeName(v: number): string {
    switch (v) {
        case 0x7f: return 'i32';
        case 0x7e: return 'i64';
        case 0x7d: return 'f32';
        case 0x7c: return 'f64';
        case 0x70: return 'funcref';
        case 0x6f: return 'externref';
        default: return `0x${v.toString(16)}`;
    }
}

const types: {
    params: string[];
    results: string[];
}[] = [];

const importedFuncTypes: number[] = [];
const definedFuncTypes: number[] = [];

while (p < b.length) {
    const sectionId = b[p++];
    const sectionSize = readU32();

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 1) {
        const count = readU32();

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const form = b[p++];

            if (form !== 0x60) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unsupported type form: 0x${form.toString(16)}`
                );
            }

            const paramCount = readU32();
            const params: string[] = [];

            for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
                params.push(typeName(b[p++]));
            }

            const resultCount = readU32();
            const results: string[] = [];

            for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
                results.push(typeName(b[p++]));
            }

            types.push({ params, results });
        }
    }

    if (sectionId === 2) {
        const count = readU32();

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            readName();
            readName();

            const kind = b[p++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                importedFuncTypes.push(readU32());
            } else if (kind === 1) {
                p++;
                const flags = readU32();
                readU32();
                if (flags & 1) readU32();
            } else if (kind === 2) {
                const flags = readU32();
                readU32();
                if (flags & 1) readU32();
            } else if (kind === 3) {
                p += 2;
            }
        }
    }

    if (sectionId === 3) {
        const count = readU32();

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            definedFuncTypes.push(readU32());
        }
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
}

console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' WASM Ka TARGET SIGNATURES');
console.log('============================================================');

console.log(`Imported functions : ${importedFuncTypes.length}`);
console.log(`Defined functions  : ${definedFuncTypes.length}`);

console.log('');

for (const target of TARGETS) {

    let typeIndex: number;
    let kind: string;

    if (target < importedFuncTypes.length) {
        typeIndex = importedFuncTypes[target];
        kind = `IMPORT #${target}`;
    } else {
        const definedIndex =
            target - importedFuncTypes.length;

        if (definedIndex >= definedFuncTypes.length) {
            console.log(
                `target=${target} INVALID`
            );
            continue;
        }

        typeIndex = definedFuncTypes[definedIndex];
        kind = `DEFINED #${definedIndex}`;
    }

    const type = types[typeIndex];

    if (!type) {
        console.log(
            `target=${target} ${kind} TYPE=${typeIndex} NOT_FOUND`
        );
        continue;
    }

    console.log(
        `target=${target.toString().padStart(4, ' ')}  ` +
        `${kind.padEnd(14, ' ')}  ` +
        `type=${typeIndex.toString().padStart(3, ' ')}  ` +
        `(${type.params.join(', ')}) -> (${type.results.join(', ')})`
    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectWasmOffset.ts
====================================================================================================

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const WASM_PATH = path.resolve("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 0x36cc7;
const RADIUS = 96;

function readU32(buf: Buffer, state: { p: number }): number {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (state.p < buf.length && shift < 35) {
        const b = buf[state.p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return value >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error(`Invalid ULEB at ${hex(state.p)}`);
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {
    const sections = new Map<number, { start: number; end: number }>();
    const state = { p: 8 };

    while (state.p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[state.p++];
        const size = readU32(buf, state);
        const start = state.p;
        const end = start + size;

        if (end > buf.length) {
            throw new Error(`Section ${id} exceeds file bounds`);
        }

        sections.set(id, { start, end });
        state.p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function readName(buf: Buffer, state: { p: number }): string {
    const len = readU32(buf, state);
    const end = state.p + len;

    if (end > buf.length) {
        throw new Error("Invalid import name");
    }

    const result = buf.toString("utf8", state.p, end);
    state.p = end;
    return result;
}

function countImportedFunctions(
    buf: Buffer,
    sec: { start: number; end: number }
): number {
    const state = { p: sec.start };
    const count = readU32(buf, state);
    let functions = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        readName(buf, state);
        readName(buf, state);

        const kind = buf[state.p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            readU32(buf, state);
            functions++;
        } else if (kind === 1) {
            state.p++; // reference type
            const flags = readU32(buf, state);
            readU32(buf, state);

            if (flags & 1) readU32(buf, state);
        } else if (kind === 2) {
            const flags = readU32(buf, state);
            readU32(buf, state);

            if (flags & 1) readU32(buf, state);
        } else if (kind === 3) {
            state.p += 2;
        } else if (kind === 4) {
            readU32(buf, state);
            readU32(buf, state);
        } else {
            throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
        }

        if (state.p > sec.end) {
            throw new Error("Import section overrun");
        }
    }

    return functions;
}

function main() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(WASM_PATH)) {
        throw new Error(`WASM file not found: ${WASM_PATH}`);
    }

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

    if (buf.toString("hex", 0, 4) !== "0061736d") {
        throw new Error("Invalid WASM magic");
    }

    console.log("WASM RAW OFFSET INSPECTOR");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FILE SIZE = ${buf.length}`);
    console.log(`TARGET    = ${hex(TARGET)}`);

    const sections = parseSections(buf);
    const importSec = sections.get(2);
    const codeSec = sections.get(10);

    if (!importSec || !codeSec) {
        throw new Error("Import or code section not found");
    }

    const importedFunctions = countImportedFunctions(buf, importSec);

    console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${importedFunctions}`);
    console.log(`CODE SECTION       = ${hex(codeSec.start)}..${hex(codeSec.end)}`);

    const state = { p: codeSec.start };
    const functionCount = readU32(buf, state);

    console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS  = ${functionCount}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let containing: {
        ordinal: number;
        globalFunc: number;
        sizeField: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
    } | null = null;

    let nearestBefore: {
        ordinal: number;
        globalFunc: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
    } | null = null;

    let nearestAfter: {
        ordinal: number;
        globalFunc: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
    } | null = null;

    for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < functionCount; ordinal++) {
        const sizeField = state.p;
        const size = readU32(buf, state);
        const bodyStart = state.p;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size;

        if (bodyEnd > codeSec.end) {
            throw new Error(
                `Body ${ordinal} exceeds code section at ${hex(bodyStart)}`
            );
        }

        const item = {
            ordinal,
            globalFunc: importedFunctions + ordinal,
            sizeField,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd
        };

        if (TARGET >= bodyStart && TARGET < bodyEnd) {
            containing = item;
        }

        if (bodyEnd <= TARGET) {
            if (!nearestBefore || bodyEnd > nearestBefore.bodyEnd) {
                nearestBefore = item;
            }
        }

        if (bodyStart > TARGET) {
            if (!nearestAfter || bodyStart < nearestAfter.bodyStart) {
                nearestAfter = item;
            }
        }

        state.p = bodyEnd;
    }

    if (state.p !== codeSec.end) {
        console.log(
            `WARNING: code parser ended at ${hex(state.p)}, expected ${hex(codeSec.end)}`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("TARGET FUNCTION MAPPING");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    if (containing) {
        console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC = ${containing.globalFunc}`);
        console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${containing.ordinal}`);
        console.log(`SIZE FIELD = ${hex(containing.sizeField)}`);
        console.log(`BODY START = ${hex(containing.bodyStart)}`);
        console.log(`BODY END   = ${hex(containing.bodyEnd)}`);
        console.log(`BODY SIZE  = ${containing.bodyEnd - containing.bodyStart}`);
    } else {
        console.log("TARGET is not inside any parsed function body.");
    }

    if (nearestBefore) {
        console.log("");
        console.log("NEAREST PREVIOUS FUNCTION");
        console.log(`FUNC ${nearestBefore.globalFunc}`);
        console.log(`${hex(nearestBefore.bodyStart)}..${hex(nearestBefore.bodyEnd)}`);
    }

    if (nearestAfter) {
        console.log("");
        console.log("NEAREST NEXT FUNCTION");
        console.log(`FUNC ${nearestAfter.globalFunc}`);
        console.log(`${hex(nearestAfter.bodyStart)}..${hex(nearestAfter.bodyEnd)}`);
    }

    const from = Math.max(0, TARGET - RADIUS);
    const to = Math.min(buf.length, TARGET + RADIUS + 1);

    console.log("");
    console.log("RAW BYTES AROUND TARGET");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let offset = from; offset < to; offset += 16) {
        const end = Math.min(offset + 16, to);
        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let p = offset; p < end; p++) {
            bytes.push(buf[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
        }

        console.log(`${hex(offset)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
    }

    if (containing) {
        console.log("");
        console.log("RAW FUNCTION BODY");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        const start = containing.bodyStart;
        const end = containing.bodyEnd;
        const windowStart = Math.max(start, TARGET - RADIUS);
        const windowEnd = Math.min(end, TARGET + RADIUS + 1);

        console.log(`WINDOW = ${hex(windowStart)}..${hex(windowEnd)}`);

        for (let offset = windowStart; offset < windowEnd; offset += 16) {
            const lineEnd = Math.min(offset + 16, windowEnd);
            const bytes: string[] = [];

            for (let p = offset; p < lineEnd; p++) {
                bytes.push(buf[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
            }

            console.log(`${hex(offset)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("END - NO WASM EXECUTION");
}

main();

FILE: scripts\inspectWasmTable.ts
