import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(p: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];
        v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

        if (!(x & 0x80))
            return { value: v, next: p };

        s += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM TABLE / ELEMENT INSPECTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;
let section = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const r = uleb(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    console.log(
        "SECTION " + id +
        " @ " + hex(start) +
        " SIZE=" + size
    );

    if (id === 4) {
        console.log("");
        console.log("**************** TABLE SECTION ****************");

        const q = uleb(p);
        const count = q.value;
        p = q.next;

        console.log("TABLE COUNT = " + count);

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const elemType = b[p++];

            const flags = b[p++];

            const min = uleb(p);
            p = min.next;

            let max: number | null = null;

            if (flags & 0x01) {
                const m = uleb(p);
                max = m.value;
                p = m.next;
            }

            console.log("");
            console.log("TABLE #" + i);
            console.log("  ELEM TYPE = 0x" + elemType.toString(16));
            console.log("  FLAGS     = 0x" + flags.toString(16));
            console.log("  MIN       = " + min.value);
            console.log("  MAX       = " + (max === null ? "none" : max));
        }
    }

    if (id === 9) {
        console.log("");
        console.log("**************** ELEMENT SECTION ****************");

        const q = uleb(p);
        const count = q.value;
        p = q.next;

        console.log("ELEMENT SEGMENT COUNT = " + count);

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            console.log("");
            console.log("ELEMENT SEGMENT #" + i);

            const flags = uleb(p);
            p = flags.next;

            console.log("  FLAGS = " + hex(flags.value));

            // Decode common active-table forms.
            if (flags.value === 0) {
                console.log("  MODE = active table 0");

                console.log("  OFFSET EXPR:");

                const opcode = b[p++];
                console.log(
                    "    opcode = " + hex(opcode)
                );

                if (opcode === 0x41) {
                    const off = uleb(p);
                    p = off.next;

                    console.log(
                        "    i32.const = " + hex(off.value)
                    );
                }

                const endOpcode = b[p++];
                console.log(
                    "    end = " + hex(endOpcode)
                );

                const n = uleb(p);
                const funcCount = n.value;
                p = n.next;

                console.log(
                    "  FUNCTION COUNT = " + funcCount
                );

                const show = Math.min(funcCount, 20);

                for (let j = 0; j < funcCount; j++) {
                    const f = uleb(p);
                    p = f.next;

                    if (j < show) {
                        console.log(
                            "    [" + j + "] = FUNC " +
                            f.value
                        );
                    }
                }

                if (funcCount > show)
                    console.log(
                        "    ... " +
                        (funcCount - show) +
                        " more"
                    );
            }
            else {
                console.log(
                    "  Non-zero element flags; raw parsing deferred."
                );
            }
        }
    }

    p = end;
    section++;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\instantiateFunction87ExactV1.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

function hex8(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 87 EXACT SSA -> FUNCTION 122 v2");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" RAW SSA INSTANTIATION");
console.log(" NO APPROXIMATE FORMULAS");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log();

type Expr =
  | { k: "sym"; v: string }
  | { k: "const"; v: bigint }
  | { k: "bin"; op: string; a: Expr; b: Expr };

function sym(v: string): Expr {
  return { k: "sym", v };
}

function cnst(v: bigint): Expr {
  return { k: "const", v };
}

function bin(op: string, a: Expr, b: Expr): Expr {
  return { k: "bin", op, a, b };
}

function render(x: Expr): string {
  if (x.k === "sym") return x.v;
  if (x.k === "const") return `i64(${x.v})`;

  return `(${render(x.a)} ${x.op} ${render(x.b)})`;
}

/*
 * These expressions are NOT manually derived.
 * They are the exact SSA expressions produced by
 * decodeFunction87ExactSSA.ts.
 */

const MASK = cnst(4294967295n);
const SH32 = cnst(32n);

/*
 * Function 87 initial parameters:
 *
 * local0 = destination
 * local1 = A
 * local2 = 0
 * local3 = B
 * local4 = 0
 *
 * local5 is an SSA temporary.
 */

function instantiate(
  destination: string,
  A: Expr,
  B: Expr
) {
  const L0_0 = sym(destination);
  const L1_0 = A;
  const L2_0 = cnst(0n);
  const L3_0 = B;
  const L4_0 = cnst(0n);

  /*
   * Exact SSA:
   *
   * L2_1 = L3_0 >>u 32
   * L4_1 = L1_0 >>u 32
   * L3_1 = L3_0 & 0xffffffff
   * L1_1 = L1_0 & 0xffffffff
   * L5_1 = L3_1 * L1_1
   */

  const L2_1 =
    bin(
      "i64.shr_u",
      L3_0,
      SH32
    );

  const L4_1 =
    bin(
      "i64.shr_u",
      L1_0,
      SH32
    );

  const L3_1 =
    bin(
      "i64.and",
      L3_0,
      MASK
    );

  const L1_1 =
    bin(
      "i64.and",
      L1_0,
      MASK
    );

  const L5_1 =
    bin(
      "i64.mul",
      L3_1,
      L1_1
    );

  /*
   * L3_2 =
   *
   * (L5_1 >>u 32)
   * +
   * (L3_1 * L4_1)
   */

  const L3_2 =
    bin(
      "i64.add",
      bin(
        "i64.shr_u",
        L5_1,
        SH32
      ),
      bin(
        "i64.mul",
        L3_1,
        L4_1
      )
    );

  /*
   * L1_2 =
   *
   * (L1_1 * L2_1)
   * +
   * ((L3_2 & 0xffffffff))
   */

  const L1_2 =
    bin(
      "i64.add",
      bin(
        "i64.mul",
        L1_1,
        L2_1
      ),
      bin(
        "i64.and",
        L3_2,
        MASK
      )
    );

  /*
   * STORE #1
   *
   * destination + 8
   *
   * Exact stack expression:
   *
   * (((L1_0 * L4_0)
   *   + (L2_0 * L3_0))
   *   + (L2_1 * L4_1))
   *   + (L3_2 >> 32)
   *   + (L1_2 >> 32)
   */

  const storeHigh =
    bin(
      "i64.add",
      bin(
        "i64.add",
        bin(
          "i64.add",
          bin(
            "i64.mul",
            L1_0,
            L4_0
          ),
          bin(
            "i64.mul",
            L2_0,
            L3_0
          )
        ),
        bin(
          "i64.mul",
          L2_1,
          L4_1
        )
      ),
      bin(
        "i64.add",
        bin(
          "i64.shr_u",
          L3_2,
          SH32
        ),
        bin(
          "i64.shr_u",
          L1_2,
          SH32
        )
      )
    );

  /*
   * STORE #2
   *
   * destination + 0
   *
   * Exact expression:
   *
   * (L5_1 & 0xffffffff)
   * |
   * ((L1_2 & 0xffffffff) << 32)
   */

  const storeLow =
    bin(
      "i64.or",
      bin(
        "i64.and",
        L5_1,
        MASK
      ),
      bin(
        "i64.shl",
        bin(
          "i64.and",
          L1_2,
          MASK
        ),
        SH32
      )
    );

  return {
    destination: L0_0,
    low: storeLow,
    high: storeHigh,
  };
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\listS1Readers.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (pos < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, end: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function findBodies() {
  let p = 8;
  const bodies = [];

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;
    const end = p + r.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      const c = uleb(p);
      let q = c.end;

      for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
        const z = uleb(q);
        q = z.end;

        bodies.push({
          start: q,
          end: q + z.value
        });

        q += z.value;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = findBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ALL S1 READERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET :", hex(TARGET));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let total = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < bodies.length; i++) {

  const func = IMPORTS + i;
  const body = bodies[i];

  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end - 4; p++) {

    if (
      b[p] === 0x41 &&
      b[p+1] === 0xcc &&
      b[p+2] === 0xa6 &&
      b[p+3] === 0xe5 &&
      b[p+4] === 0x00
    ) {

      console.log(
        `FUNC ${func}  CONST @ ${hex(p)}`
      );

      total++;
    }
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TOTAL S1 READERS :", total);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import { Address, TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';

const RPC_ENDPOINT = 'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com';

    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100_000_000_000n;
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249_908n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 150_109_265n;
const TOTAL_TX_VALUE = 610_249_908n;

function fmtTon(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;
    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0');

    return `${whole}.${frac}`;
}

function fmtRooster(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;
    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return frac ? `${whole}.${frac}` : `${whole}`;
}

export async function run() {
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 LIVE POOL PREFLIGHT');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');

    console.log('Router:', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('Pool:', POOL.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER master:', ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('pTON master:', PTON_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('User:', USER.toString());

    console.log('\n===== INPUTS =====');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER:',
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString(),
        '=',
        fmtRooster(ROOSTER_AMOUNT),
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON:',
        PTON_AMOUNT.toString(),
        '=',
        fmtTon(PTON_AMOUNT),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected TOTAL:',
        TOTAL_TX_VALUE.toString(),
        '=',
        fmtTon(TOTAL_TX_VALUE),
        'TON'
    );

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: RPC_ENDPOINT,
    });

    console.log('\n===== CURRENT BLOCK =====');

    const lastBlock = await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log(
        'Workchain:',
        lastBlock.last.workchain
    );

    console.log(
        'Shard:',
        lastBlock.last.shard
    );

    console.log(
        'Seqno:',
        lastBlock.last.seqno
    );

    console.log('\n===== POOL get_pool_data =====');

    let poolResult;

    try {
        poolResult = await client.runMethod(
            lastBlock.last.seqno,

            'get_pool_data'
        );

        console.log(
            'Exit code:',
            poolResult.exitCode
        );

        console.log(

        );

        console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
        console.log('================================================');

        return;

        console.log(

            poolResult.result[i]
        );

        poolResult.resultRaw ?? '(null)'
    );

    console.log('\n===== READER =====');

        poolResult.reader.constructor.name
    );

    console.log(' LIVE POOL READ COMPLETE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('================================================');
}



====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\locate4275DataSegment.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 0x004af36b;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("LOCATE RAW 4275 DATA SEGMENT");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGET    :", "0x" + TARGET.toString(16));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let start = pos;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: result >>> 0,
        next: pos,
        bytes: pos - start
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    byte = buf[pos++];

    result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
      }

      return {
        value: result | 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }
  }

  throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
}

function readString(pos) {
  const r = readULEB(pos);
  const len = r.value;
  const start = r.next;
  const end = start + len;

  return {
    value: buf.subarray(start, end).toString("utf8"),
    next: end
  };
}

function sectionName(id) {
  return {
    0: "CUSTOM",
    1: "TYPE",
    2: "IMPORT",
    3: "FUNCTION",
    4: "TABLE",
    5: "MEMORY",
    6: "GLOBAL",
    7: "EXPORT",
    8: "START",
    9: "ELEMENT",
    10: "CODE",
    11: "DATA",
    12: "DATACOUNT"
  }[id] || ("SECTION_" + id);
}

let p = 8;
let dataSectionFound = false;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const sectionStart = p;

  const id = buf[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const payloadStart = p;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

  console.log(
    "SECTION",
    id,
    sectionName(id),
    "START",
    "0x" + sectionStart.toString(16),
    "PAYLOAD",
    "0x" + payloadStart.toString(16),
    "..",
    "0x" + payloadEnd.toString(16)
  );

  if (id === 11) {

    dataSectionFound = true;

    let q = payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(q);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    q = countInfo.next;

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("DATA SEGMENT COUNT :", count);

    for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {

      const segStart = q;

      const flagInfo = readULEB(q);
      const flags = flagInfo.value;
      q = flagInfo.next;

      let memoryIndex = 0;
      let offset = null;

      if (flags === 0) {

        // active segment, memory 0
        if (buf[q] !== 0x41) {
          throw new Error(
            `Unexpected offset opcode at 0x${q.toString(16)}`
          );
        }

        q++;

        const off = readSLEB(q);
        offset = off.value;
        q = off.next;

        if (buf[q++] !== 0x0b) {
          throw new Error("Missing end opcode");
        }

      } else if (flags === 2) {

        const memInfo = readULEB(q);
        memoryIndex = memInfo.value;
        q = memInfo.next;

        if (buf[q] !== 0x41) {
          throw new Error(
            `Unexpected offset opcode at 0x${q.toString(16)}`
          );
        }

        q++;

        const off = readSLEB(q);
        offset = off.value;
        q = off.next;

        if (buf[q++] !== 0x0b) {
          throw new Error("Missing end opcode");
        }

      } else if (flags === 1 || flags === 3) {

        // passive / declarative segment
        const lenInfo = readULEB(q);
        const len = lenInfo.value;
        q = lenInfo.next;

        q += len;

        continue;

      } else {

        console.log(
          "UNKNOWN DATA FLAGS:",
          flags,
          "at",
          "0x" + segStart.toString(16)
        );

        break;
      }

      const lenInfo = readULEB(q);
      const dataLen = lenInfo.value;
      q = lenInfo.next;

      const dataStart = q;
      const dataEnd = dataStart + dataLen;

      const targetInSegment =
        offset !== null &&
        TARGET >= offset &&
        TARGET < offset + dataLen;

      if (targetInSegment) {

        const relative = TARGET - offset;

        console.log("");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("TARGET DATA SEGMENT FOUND");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("SEGMENT       :", seg);
        console.log("SEGMENT START :", "0x" + segStart.toString(16));
        console.log("FLAGS         :", flags);
        console.log("MEMORY INDEX  :", memoryIndex);
        console.log("MEMORY OFFSET :", "0x" + offset.toString(16));
        console.log("DATA LENGTH   :", dataLen);
        console.log("DATA START    :", "0x" + dataStart.toString(16));
        console.log("DATA END      :", "0x" + dataEnd.toString(16));
        console.log("TARGET        :", "0x" + TARGET.toString(16));
        console.log("RELATIVE      :", "0x" + relative.toString(16));
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        const from = Math.max(dataStart, TARGET - 32);
        const to = Math.min(dataEnd, TARGET + 64);

        console.log(
          "HEX AROUND TARGET:"
        );

        console.log(
          buf.subarray(from, to)
            .toString("hex")
            .match(/.{1,2}/g)
            .join(" ")
        );

        console.log("============================================================");
      }

      q = dataEnd;
    }
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

if (!dataSectionFound) {
  console.log("NO DATA SECTION FOUND");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\locate4275FileOffset.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

// IMPORTANT:
// This is FILE OFFSET, not linear-memory address.
const TARGET_FILE_OFFSET = 0x004af36b;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("LOCATE FILE OFFSET 0x4AF36B INSIDE DATA SEGMENTS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log(
  "TARGET FILE OFFSET :",
  "0x" + TARGET_FILE_OFFSET.toString(16)
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function readSLEB32(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    b = buf[pos++];

    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result -= Math.pow(2, shift);
      }

      return { value: result, next: pos };
    }
  }

  throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
}

function readOffsetExpr(pos) {
  const opcode = buf[pos++];

  if (opcode !== 0x41) {
    throw new Error(
      "Expected i32.const (0x41), got 0x" +
      opcode.toString(16) +
      " at 0x" +
      (pos - 1).toString(16)
    );
  }

  const v = readSLEB32(pos);
  pos = v.next;

  if (buf[pos++] !== 0x0b) {
    throw new Error("Expected end opcode 0x0B");
  }

  return {
    value: v.value,
    next: pos
  };
}

function sectionName(id) {
  const names = {
    0: "CUSTOM",
    1: "TYPE",
    2: "IMPORT",
    3: "FUNCTION",
    4: "TABLE",
    5: "MEMORY",
    6: "GLOBAL",
    7: "EXPORT",
    8: "START",
    9: "ELEMENT",
    10: "CODE",
    11: "DATA",
    12: "DATACOUNT"
  };

  return names[id] || ("SECTION_" + id);
}

let p = 8;
let dataFound = false;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const sectionFileStart = p;

  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const payloadStart = p;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId !== 11) {
    p = payloadEnd;
    continue;
  }

  dataFound = true;

  console.log("DATA SECTION");
  console.log(
    "FILE RANGE :",
    "0x" + payloadStart.toString(16),
    "..",
    "0x" + payloadEnd.toString(16)
  );

  let q = payloadStart;

  const countInfo = readULEB(q);
  const segmentCount = countInfo.value;
  q = countInfo.next;

  console.log("SEGMENT COUNT :", segmentCount);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount; seg++) {

    const segmentStart = q;

    const flagsInfo = readULEB(q);
    const flags = flagsInfo.value;
    q = flagsInfo.next;

    let memoryIndex = null;
    let memoryOffset = null;

    /*
      flags:
        0 = active, memory 0, offset expr
        1 = passive
        2 = active, explicit memory index + offset expr
    */

    if (flags === 0) {

      const off = readOffsetExpr(q);
      memoryOffset = off.value;
      q = off.next;

    } else if (flags === 1) {

      // Passive segment: no memory offset expression.

    } else if (flags === 2) {

      const mem = readULEB(q);
      memoryIndex = mem.value;
      q = mem.next;

      const off = readOffsetExpr(q);
      memoryOffset = off.value;
      q = off.next;

    } else {

      console.log(
        "UNKNOWN FLAGS",
        flags,
        "SEGMENT",
        seg,
        "AT FILE OFFSET",
        "0x" + segmentStart.toString(16)
      );

      break;
    }

    const lenInfo = readULEB(q);
    const dataLength = lenInfo.value;
    q = lenInfo.next;

    const dataStart = q;
    const dataEnd = dataStart + dataLength;

    const contains =
      TARGET_FILE_OFFSET >= dataStart &&
      TARGET_FILE_OFFSET < dataEnd;

    if (contains) {

      const relativeFileOffset =
        TARGET_FILE_OFFSET - dataStart;

      console.log("");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log("TARGET FOUND INSIDE DATA SEGMENT");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log("SEGMENT              :", seg);
      console.log(
        "SEGMENT HEADER FILE  :",
        "0x" + segmentStart.toString(16)
      );
      console.log("FLAGS                :", flags);
      console.log("MEMORY INDEX         :", memoryIndex);
      console.log(
        "MEMORY OFFSET        :",
        memoryOffset === null
          ? "PASSIVE"
          : "0x" + memoryOffset.toString(16)
      );
      console.log(
        "DATA FILE START      :",
        "0x" + dataStart.toString(16)
      );
      console.log(
        "DATA FILE END        :",
        "0x" + dataEnd.toString(16)
      );
      console.log("DATA LENGTH          :", dataLength);
      console.log(
        "TARGET FILE OFFSET   :",
        "0x" + TARGET_FILE_OFFSET.toString(16)
      );
      console.log(
        "RELATIVE IN SEGMENT  :",
        "0x" + relativeFileOffset.toString(16)
      );

      if (memoryOffset !== null) {
        console.log(
          "TARGET LINEAR MEMORY :",
          "0x" +
          (memoryOffset + relativeFileOffset).toString(16)
        );
      } else {
        console.log(
          "TARGET LINEAR MEMORY :",
          "N/A (PASSIVE SEGMENT)"
        );
      }

      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      const from = Math.max(dataStart, TARGET_FILE_OFFSET - 64);
      const to = Math.min(dataEnd, TARGET_FILE_OFFSET + 128);

      console.log(
        "FILE BYTES AROUND TARGET:"
      );

      for (let x = from; x < to; x += 16) {

        const rowEnd = Math.min(to, x + 16);

        const hex = [];

        for (let y = x; y < rowEnd; y++) {
          hex.push(
            buf[y]
              .toString(16)
              .padStart(2, "0")
          );
        }

        console.log(
          "0x" +
          x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
          " : " +
          hex.join(" ")
        );
      }

      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log("");

      /*
        Decode surrounding 32-bit little-endian values.
      */
      const rel = relativeFileOffset;

      const wordStart =
        dataStart + Math.floor(rel / 4) * 4;

      console.log("32-BIT VALUES AROUND TARGET:");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      for (
        let x = Math.max(dataStart, wordStart - 32);
        x + 4 <= Math.min(dataEnd, wordStart + 64);
        x += 4
      ) {

        const value =
          buf[x] |
          (buf[x + 1] << 8) |
          (buf[x + 2] << 16) |
          (buf[x + 3] << 24);

        console.log(
          "FILE 0x" +
          x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
          "  VALUE = " +
          (value >>> 0) +
          "  HEX = 0x" +
          (value >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
          (x === TARGET_FILE_OFFSET ? "  <-- TARGET" : "")
        );
      }

      console.log("============================================================");
    }

    q = dataEnd;
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

if (!dataFound) {
  console.log("DATA SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const decoderPath = path.resolve("scripts/disassembleAddressUsers.mjs");
const source = fs.readFileSync(decoderPath, "utf8");

const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const markerIndex = source.indexOf(marker);

if (markerIndex < 0) {
    throw new Error("Could not locate decoder bootstrap marker.");
}

const tempPath = path.join(
    os.tmpdir(),
    `rooster_locate_target_${process.pid}.mjs`
);

fs.writeFileSync(
    tempPath,
    source.slice(0, markerIndex) +
        "\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction };\n",
    "utf8"
);

let decoder;
try {
    decoder = await import(pathToFileURL(tempPath).href);
} finally {
    try {
        fs.unlinkSync(tempPath);
    } catch {}
}

const { bodies, decodeFunction } = decoder;
if (!(bodies instanceof Map) || typeof decodeFunction !== "function") {
    throw new Error("Could not initialize decoder exports.");
}

const TARGET = 0x256aac;
const CONTEXT = 8;
const output = [];
let decoded = 0;
let errors = 0;
let matches = 0;

function log(s = "") {
    output.push(String(s));
}

function txt(ins) {
    return ins?.text ?? "";
}

function addr(ins) {
    return Number.isFinite(ins?.start)
        ? `0x${ins.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        : "??????????";
}

for (const [func] of bodies) {
    let result;

    try {
        result = decodeFunction(func);
        decoded++;
    } catch (e) {
        errors++;
        continue;
    }

    const instructions = Array.isArray(result)
        ? result
        : result?.instructions;

    if (!Array.isArray(instructions)) {
        errors++;
        continue;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const s = txt(instructions[i]);
        const m = s.match(/\bi32\.const\s+(-?\d+)(?:\s+\(0x[0-9a-f]+\))?/i);

        if (!m || (Number(m[1]) >>> 0) !== TARGET) continue;

        matches++;
        log("\n" + "=".repeat(72));
        log(`MATCH ${matches} | FUNC ${func} | INSTRUCTION #${i}`);
        log(`TARGET ADDRESS: 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);

        const from = Math.max(0, i - CONTEXT);
        const to = Math.min(instructions.length - 1, i + CONTEXT);

        for (let j = from; j <= to; j++) {
            const ins = instructions[j];
            log(`${j === i ? " >>> " : "     "}#${j} ${addr(ins)} ${txt(ins)}`);
        }
    }
}

log("\n" + "=".repeat(72));
log("SUMMARY");
log(`Functions: ${bodies.size}`);
log(`Decoded: ${decoded}`);
log(`Errors: ${errors}`);
log(`Exact target constants: ${matches}`);

const outputPath = path.resolve("scripts/locate7006TargetConstants_output.txt");
fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, output.join("\n") + "\n", "utf8");

console.log(output.slice(0, 100).join("\n"));
console.log(`\nFull report: ${outputPath}`);

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGETS = [
    0x00124700,
    0x00124714,
];

const EXPECTED_SIZE = 5093309;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" LOCATE CALLER FUNCTION v49");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

if (wasm.length !== EXPECTED_SIZE) {
    throw new Error(
        `Unexpected WASM size ${wasm.length}`
    );
}

function ulebAt(pos: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        if (p >= wasm.length) {
            throw new Error("ULEB outside WASM");
        }

        const b = wasm[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too large");
        }
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function sectionName(id: number): string {
    switch (id) {
        case 0: return "custom";
        case 1: return "type";
        case 2: return "import";
        case 3: return "function";
        case 4: return "table";
        case 5: return "memory";
        case 6: return "global";
        case 7: return "export";
        case 8: return "start";
        case 9: return "element";
        case 10: return "code";
        case 11: return "data";
        case 12: return "data-count";
        default: return `section-${id}`;
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("Searching WASM sections...");

let p = 8;

let codeSectionStart = -1;
let codeSectionEnd = -1;
let functionBodies = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const sectionStart = p;

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = ulebAt(p);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

    if (payloadEnd > wasm.length) {
        throw new Error(
            `Invalid section at ${hex(sectionStart)}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        `${sectionName(id).padEnd(12)} ` +
        `${hex(sectionStart)} .. ${hex(payloadEnd)} ` +
        `size=${sectionSize}`
    );

    if (id === 10) {
        codeSectionStart = payloadStart;
        codeSectionEnd = payloadEnd;

        const countInfo = ulebAt(p);

        functionBodies = countInfo.value;

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `CODE FUNCTION COUNT : ${functionBodies}`
        );
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

if (codeSectionStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" SCANNING FUNCTION BODIES");
console.log("============================================================");

let q = codeSectionStart;

const countInfo = ulebAt(q);
const count = countInfo.value;
q = countInfo.next;

let found = 0;

for (let functionIndex = 0; functionIndex < count; functionIndex++) {

    const bodySizeInfo = ulebAt(q);

    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    const bodySizeFieldEnd = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = bodySizeFieldEnd;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    if (bodyEnd > codeSectionEnd) {
        throw new Error(
            `Function ${functionIndex} exceeds code section`
        );
    }

    for (const target of TARGETS) {

        if (target >= bodyStart && target < bodyEnd) {

            found++;

            console.log("");
            console.log("--------------------------------------------");
            console.log(`TARGET : ${hex(target)}`);
            console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${functionIndex}`);
            console.log(`BODY START     : ${hex(bodyStart)}`);
            console.log(`BODY END       : ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
            console.log(`BODY SIZE      : ${bodySize}`);
            console.log("--------------------------------------------");

            const localDecl = ulebAt(bodyStart);

            console.log(
                `Local declaration starts @ ${hex(bodyStart)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Local group count : ${localDecl.value}`
            );

            const previewStart =
                Math.max(bodyStart, target - 32);

            const previewEnd =
                Math.min(bodyEnd, target + 48);

            console.log("");
            console.log("RAW CONTEXT:");

            for (let x = previewStart; x < previewEnd; x += 16) {

                const end = Math.min(previewEnd, x + 16);

                const bytes: string[] = [];

                for (let y = x; y < end; y++) {
                    bytes.push(
                        wasm[y]
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(2, "0")
                    );
                }

                console.log(
                    `${hex(x)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    `Targets located : ${found}/${TARGETS.length}`
);

if (found !== TARGETS.length) {
    console.log(
        "WARNING: not all targets were located inside code bodies."
    );
} else {
    console.log(
        "STATUS: BOTH CALLS ARE INSIDE WASM CODE FUNCTION BODIES"
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("No bytes modified.");

FILE: scripts\locateDecoderSourceV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOT =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\node_modules\\@tact-lang\\opcode\\dist';

const PATTERNS = [
    'decodeInstruction',
    'Bad operand',
    'parseInstructions',
    'PUSHCONT',
    'operand c',
];

function walk(dir: string): string[] {
    const result: string[] = [];

    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
        return result;
    }

    for (const name of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
        const full = path.join(dir, name);
        const stat = fs.statSync(full);

        if (stat.isDirectory()) {
            result.push(...walk(full));
        } else if (name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.js')) {
            result.push(full);
        }
    }

    return result;
}

function inspectFile(file: string) {
    const text = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
    const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);

    let found = false;

    for (const pattern of PATTERNS) {
        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
            if (!lines[i].toLowerCase().includes(pattern.toLowerCase())) {
                continue;
            }

            if (!found) {
                console.log();
                console.log('============================================================');
                console.log(`FILE: ${file}`);
                console.log('============================================================');
                found = true;
            }

            console.log();
            console.log(`PATTERN: ${pattern}`);

            const start = Math.max(0, i - 6);
            const end = Math.min(lines.length, i + 16);

            console.log(`--- lines ${start + 1}-${end} ---`);

            for (let j = start; j < end; j++) {
                console.log(
                    `${String(j + 1).padStart(5, ' ')} | ${lines[j]}`
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 DECODER SOURCE LOCATOR');
    console.log(' LOCAL NODE_MODULES ONLY');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');

    console.log();
    console.log(`Search root: ${ROOT}`);
    console.log();

    const files = walk(ROOT);

    console.log(`JavaScript files found: ${files.length}`);
    console.log();

    for (const file of files) {
        inspectFile(file);
    }

    console.log();
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' SEARCH COMPLETE');
    console.log('========================================================================');
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SECTION LOCATOR");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;
let index = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const idPos = p;
  const id = b[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  const size = s.value;
  const payload = s.next;
  const end = payload + size;

  let name = "UNKNOWN";

  if (id === 0) name = "CUSTOM";
  if (id === 1) name = "TYPE";
  if (id === 2) name = "IMPORT";
  if (id === 3) name = "FUNCTION";
  if (id === 4) name = "TABLE";
  if (id === 5) name = "MEMORY";
  if (id === 6) name = "GLOBAL";
  if (id === 7) name = "EXPORT";
  if (id === 8) name = "START";
  if (id === 9) name = "ELEMENT";
  if (id === 10) name = "CODE";
  if (id === 11) name = "DATA";
  if (id === 12) name = "DATACOUNT";

  if (id === 4 || id === 9) {
    console.log(
      `SECTION ${name}` +
      ` | id=${id}` +
      ` | header=0x${idPos.toString(16)}` +
      ` | payload=0x${payload.toString(16)}` +
      ` | size=${size}` +
      ` | end=0x${end.toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log(
      "RAW FIRST 32 BYTES:",
      [...b.subarray(payload, Math.min(payload + 32, end))]
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  }

  p = end;
  index++;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\map413CandidatesToCallers.ts
====================================================================================================
import { readFileSync } from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const CANDIDATE_START = 4057;
const CANDIDATE_END = 4188;

function readU32LE(buf: Buffer, off: number): number {
  return (
    (buf[off] |
      (buf[off + 1] << 8) |
      (buf[off + 2] << 16) |
      (buf[off + 3] << 24)) >>>
    0
  );
}

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, off: number): {
  value: number;
  next: number;
} {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = off;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error(`ULEB too large at 0x${off.toString(16)}`);
    }
  }

  throw new Error("Unexpected EOF in ULEB");
}

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

type FuncInfo = {
  index: number;
  body: number;
  bodySize: number;
  codeStart: number;
  codeEnd: number;
};

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {
  let p = 8;

  let functionPayload = -1;
  let functionSize = 0;

  let codePayload = -1;
  let codeSize = 0;

  let importedFunctions = 0;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(buf, p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const payload = p;

    if (id === 2) {
      let q = payload;

      const c = readULEB(buf, q);
      const importCount = c.value;
      q = c.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {
        const moduleLen = readULEB(buf, q);
        q = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

        const nameLen = readULEB(buf, q);
        q = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

        const kind = buf[q++];

        if (kind === 0) {
          importedFunctions++;
          const typeIndex = readULEB(buf, q);
          q = typeIndex.next;
        } else if (kind === 1) {
          q += 4;
        } else if (kind === 2) {
          q += 4;
        } else if (kind === 3) {
          q += 2;
        }
      }
    }

    if (id === 3) {
      functionPayload = payload;
      functionSize = size;
    }

    if (id === 10) {
      codePayload = payload;
      codeSize = size;
    }

    p = payload + size;
  }

  return {
    functionPayload,
    functionSize,
    codePayload,
    codeSize,
    importedFunctions,
  };
}

function parseFunctionTypes(
  buf: Buffer,
  payload: number
): number[] {
  let p = payload;

  const count = readULEB(buf, p);
  p = count.next;

  const types: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const t = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = t.next;
    types.push(t.value);
  }

  return types;
}

function parseCodeBodies(
  buf: Buffer,
  payload: number,
  importedFunctions: number
): FuncInfo[] {
  let p = payload;

  const count = readULEB(buf, p);
  p = count.next;

  const funcs: FuncInfo[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const body = p;

    const s = readULEB(buf, p);
    const bodySize = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const codeStart = p;
    const codeEnd = p + bodySize - 1;

    funcs.push({
      index: importedFunctions + i,
      body,
      bodySize,
      codeStart,
      codeEnd,
    });

    p += bodySize;
  }

  return funcs;
}

export async function run() {
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(" SEGMENT 413 CANDIDATES → CALLER FUNCTIONS");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log();

  const wasm = readFileSync(WASM);

  console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

  if (
    wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
    wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
    wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
    wasm[3] !== 0x6d
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid WASM magic");
  }

  const sec = parseSections(wasm);

  console.log(`Imported functions : ${sec.importedFunctions}`);
  console.log();

  const functionTypes = parseFunctionTypes(
    wasm,
    sec.functionPayload
  );

  const funcs = parseCodeBodies(
    wasm,
    sec.codePayload,
    sec.importedFunctions
  );

  const byIndex = new Map<number, FuncInfo>();

  for (const f of funcs) {
    byIndex.set(f.index, f);
  }

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(" CANDIDATE FUNCTIONS");
  console.log("============================================================");

  for (
    let i = CANDIDATE_START;
    i <= CANDIDATE_END;
    i++
  ) {
    const f = byIndex.get(i);

    if (!f) {
      console.log(`MISSING ${i}`);
      continue;
    }

    const type =
      functionTypes[i - sec.importedFunctions] ?? -1;

    console.log(
      `${i.toString().padStart(5)} ` +
      `type=${type.toString().padStart(4)} ` +
      `code=${hex(f.codeStart)}..${hex(f.codeEnd)} ` +
      `size=${f.bodySize.toString().padStart(6)}`
    );
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(" SCANNING CALL INSTRUCTIONS");
  console.log("============================================================");

  type Caller = {
    caller: number;
    callOffset: number;
    target: number;
  };

  const callers = new Map<number, Caller[]>();

  let currentFunc = 0;

  for (const f of funcs) {
    currentFunc = f.index;

    let p = f.codeStart;

    while (p <= f.codeEnd) {
      const op = wasm[p];

      /*
       * CALL
       *
       * 0x10
       * followed by function index ULEB128
       */
      if (op === 0x10) {
        try {
          const imm = readULEB(wasm, p + 1);
          const target = imm.value;

          if (
            target >= CANDIDATE_START &&
            target <= CANDIDATE_END
          ) {
            const arr = callers.get(target) ?? [];

            arr.push({
              caller: currentFunc,
              callOffset: p,
              target,
            });

            callers.set(target, arr);
          }

          p = imm.next;
          continue;
        } catch {
          break;
        }
      }

      /*
       * CALL_INDIRECT
       *
       * 0x11
       *
       * The immediate here is a type index followed by
       * a table index / reserved byte depending on WASM encoding.
       *
       * We only record the opcode location here.
       */
      if (op === 0x11) {
        p++;
        continue;
      }

      /*
       * We intentionally do NOT attempt full WASM instruction
       * decoding here.
       *
       * This first pass searches raw code for opcode 0x10.
       */
      p++;
    }
  }

  console.log();

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(" CALLER RESULTS");
  console.log("============================================================");

  let withCallers = 0;
  let withoutCallers = 0;
  let totalCalls = 0;

  for (
    let target = CANDIDATE_START;
    target <= CANDIDATE_END;
    target++
  ) {
    const arr = callers.get(target) ?? [];

    if (arr.length === 0) {
      withoutCallers++;

      console.log(
        `${target.toString().padStart(5)}  CALLERS = 0`
      );

      continue;
    }

    withCallers++;
    totalCalls += arr.length;

    console.log(
      `${target.toString().padStart(5)}  CALLERS = ${arr.length}`
    );

    for (const c of arr) {
      const callerInfo = byIndex.get(c.caller);

      console.log(
        `       caller=${c.caller} ` +
        `CALL@${hex(c.callOffset)} ` +
        `callerCode=${
          callerInfo
            ? `${hex(callerInfo.codeStart)}..${hex(callerInfo.codeEnd)}`
            : "?"
        }`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(" SUMMARY");
  console.log("============================================================");

  console.log(`Candidates           : ${CANDIDATE_END - CANDIDATE_START + 1}`);
  console.log(`With direct CALL     : ${withCallers}`);
  console.log(`Without direct CALL  : ${withoutCallers}`);
  console.log(`Total direct CALLs   : ${totalCalls}`);

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(" END");
  console.log("============================================================");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\map413CandidatesToFunctions.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_START = 4057;
const TARGET_END = 4188;

function readU32LE(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    return buf.readUInt32LE(p);
}

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB128 too large");
        }
    }
}

function sectionName(id: number): string {
    switch (id) {
        case 0: return "custom";
        case 1: return "type";
        case 2: return "import";
        case 3: return "function";
        case 4: return "table";
        case 5: return "memory";
        case 6: return "global";
        case 7: return "export";
        case 8: return "start";
        case 9: return "element";
        case 10: return "code";
        case 11: return "data";
        default: return `section-${id}`;
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

type FuncInfo = {
    index: number;
    typeIndex: number;
    bodyOffset: number;
    bodySize: number;
    localsStart: number;
    codeStart: number;
    codeEnd: number;
};

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEGMENT 413 → WASM FUNCTION INDEX MAPPING");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    if (!fs.existsSync(WASM)) {
        throw new Error(`WASM not found:\n${WASM}`);
    }

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
    console.log(`Candidate : ${TARGET_START} .. ${TARGET_END}`);
    console.log();

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // WASM header
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    if (
        buf[0] !== 0x00 ||
        buf[1] !== 0x61 ||
        buf[2] !== 0x73 ||
        buf[3] !== 0x6d
    ) {
        throw new Error("Invalid WASM magic");
    }

    console.log("WASM magic : OK");
    console.log(
        `WASM version: ${buf.readUInt32LE(4) === 1 ? "1" : hex(buf.readUInt32LE(4))}`
    );
    console.log();

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Parse sections
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    let p = 8;

    let functionSection: {
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
        count: number;
        typeIndices: number[];
    } | null = null;

    let codeSection: {
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
        count: number;
        bodyOffsets: number[];
        bodySizes: number[];
    } | null = null;

    console.log("WASM SECTIONS");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const sectionId = buf[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        const payloadSize = sizeInfo.value;
        const payloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
        const payloadEnd = payloadStart + payloadSize;

        console.log(
            `id=${sectionId.toString().padStart(2)} ` +
            `${sectionName(sectionId).padEnd(8)} ` +
            `payload=${hex(payloadStart)} size=${payloadSize}`
        );

        if (payloadEnd > buf.length) {
            throw new Error(
                `Section exceeds file: id=${sectionId} end=${hex(payloadEnd)}`
            );
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Function section
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        if (sectionId === 3) {
            let q = payloadStart;

            const countInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
            const count = countInfo.value;
            q = countInfo.next;

            const typeIndices: number[] = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const x = readULEB(buf, q);
                typeIndices.push(x.value);
                q = x.next;
            }

            functionSection = {
                payloadStart,
                payloadEnd,
                count,
                typeIndices,
            };
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Code section
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        if (sectionId === 10) {
            let q = payloadStart;

            const countInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
            const count = countInfo.value;
            q = countInfo.next;

            const bodyOffsets: number[] = [];
            const bodySizes: number[] = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const bodyStart = q;

                const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
                const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
                q = bodySizeInfo.next;

                const bodyOffset = q;

                bodyOffsets.push(bodyOffset);
                bodySizes.push(bodySize);

                q += bodySize;

                if (q > payloadEnd) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Code body exceeds section: function=${i}`
                    );
                }

                // Keep bodyStart referenced conceptually for diagnostics.
                void bodyStart;
            }

            codeSection = {
                payloadStart,
                payloadEnd,
                count,
                bodyOffsets,
                bodySizes,
            };
        }

        p = payloadEnd;
    }

    console.log();

    if (!functionSection) {
        throw new Error("Function section not found");
    }

    if (!codeSection) {
        throw new Error("Code section not found");
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Imported functions
    //
    // Function section indices are NOT necessarily zero-based
    // relative to the function section itself.
    //
    // We need the number of imported functions before assigning
    // actual function indexes.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    // For this first pass, inspect the import section directly.
    let importedFunctions = 0;

    p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const sectionId = buf[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        const payloadSize = sizeInfo.value;
        const payloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
        const payloadEnd = payloadStart + payloadSize;

        if (sectionId === 2) {
            let q = payloadStart;

            const countInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
            const count = countInfo.value;
            q = countInfo.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                // module name
                const modLen = readULEB(buf, q);
                q = modLen.next + modLen.value;

                // field/name
                const fieldLen = readULEB(buf, q);
                q = fieldLen.next + fieldLen.value;

                const kind = buf[q++];

                switch (kind) {
                    case 0: {
                        // function import
                        const typeInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
                        q = typeInfo.next;
                        importedFunctions++;
                        break;
                    }

                    case 1: {
                        // table
                        q = skipTableType(buf, q);
                        break;
                    }

                    case 2: {
                        // memory
                        q = skipLimits(buf, q);
                        break;
                    }

                    case 3: {
                        // global
                        q = skipGlobalType(buf, q);
                        break;
                    }

                    case 4: {
                        // tag
                        q = skipTagType(buf, q);
                        break;
                    }

                    default:
                        throw new Error(
                            `Unknown import kind ${kind} at ${hex(q - 1)}`
                        );
                }
            }
        }

        p = payloadEnd;
    }

    console.log("FUNCTION / CODE COUNTS");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`Imported functions : ${importedFunctions}`);
    console.log(`Defined functions  : ${functionSection.count}`);
    console.log(`Code bodies        : ${codeSection.count}`);
    console.log(
        `Total function indexes expected : ${
            importedFunctions + functionSection.count
        }`
    );
    console.log();

    if (functionSection.count !== codeSection.count) {
        console.log("WARNING:");
        console.log("Function section count != Code section count");
        console.log();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Build defined function map
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const funcs = new Map<number, FuncInfo>();

    for (let i = 0; i < functionSection.count; i++) {
        const index = importedFunctions + i;

        const bodyOffset = codeSection.bodyOffsets[i];
        const bodySize = codeSection.bodySizes[i];

        // Decode local declaration vector to find actual instruction
        // start inside the function body.
        let q = bodyOffset;

        const localCountInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
        const localCount = localCountInfo.value;
        q = localCountInfo.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localCount; j++) {
            const n = readULEB(buf, q);
            q = n.next;

            // valtype byte
            q += 1;
        }

        const localsStart = bodyOffset;
        const codeStart = q;
        const codeEnd = bodyOffset + bodySize;

        funcs.set(index, {
            index,
            typeIndex: functionSection.typeIndices[i],
            bodyOffset,
            bodySize,
            localsStart,
            codeStart,
            codeEnd,
        });
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Candidate range
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" CANDIDATE FUNCTION MAPPING");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    for (let index = TARGET_START; index <= TARGET_END; index++) {
        const f = funcs.get(index);

        if (!f) {
            console.log(`${index} : NOT A DEFINED FUNCTION`);
            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            `${index.toString().padStart(5)} ` +
            `type=${f.typeIndex.toString().padStart(5)} ` +
            `body=${hex(f.bodyOffset)} ` +
            `size=${f.bodySize.toString().padStart(6)} ` +
            `code=${hex(f.codeStart)}..${hex(f.codeEnd)}`
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Boundary / continuity test
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" CONTINUITY TEST");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    let valid = 0;
    let invalid = 0;

    for (let index = TARGET_START; index <= TARGET_END; index++) {
        const f = funcs.get(index);

        if (!f) {
            invalid++;
            continue;
        }

        valid++;
    }

    console.log(`Valid candidate functions : ${valid}`);
    console.log(`Invalid / missing         : ${invalid}`);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Neighborhood
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION NEIGHBORHOOD");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    const neighborhoodStart = Math.max(0, TARGET_START - 5);
    const neighborhoodEnd = Math.min(
        importedFunctions + functionSection.count - 1,
        TARGET_END + 5
    );

    for (
        let index = neighborhoodStart;
        index <= neighborhoodEnd;
        index++
    ) {
        const f = funcs.get(index);

        if (!f) {
            console.log(`${index} : IMPORT / UNDEFINED`);
            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            `${index.toString().padStart(5)} ` +
            `type=${f.typeIndex.toString().padStart(5)} ` +
            `body=${hex(f.bodyOffset)} ` +
            `size=${f.bodySize.toString().padStart(6)} ` +
            `code=${hex(f.codeStart)}..${hex(f.codeEnd)}`
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" END");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

function skipLimits(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    const flags = buf[p++];

    const minInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = minInfo.next;

    if (flags & 0x01) {
        const maxInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = maxInfo.next;
    }

    return p;
}

function skipTableType(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    // element reference type
    p += 1;

    return skipLimits(buf, p);
}

function skipGlobalType(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    // valtype + mutability
    p += 2;
    return p;
}

function skipTagType(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    // attribute + type index
    p += 1;

    const typeInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    return typeInfo.next;
}

FILE: scripts\mapAddressesToCodeBodies.js

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_ADDRESSES = [
    0x003222c0,
    0x003222ff,
    0x003223fa,
    0x003224ab,
    0x0032256d,
    0x0032261e,
    0x00322ad1,
    0x00322b22,
    0x00322ca6,
    0x00322dae,
    0x00322eb6,
    0x00322ff9,
    0x00324183
];

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function readSection(pos) {
    const id = bytes[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);

    const payloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sizeInfo.value;

    return {
        id,
        size: sizeInfo.value,
        payloadStart,
        payloadEnd
    };
}

function findCodeSection() {
    let pos = 8;

    while (pos < bytes.length) {
        const section = readSection(pos);

        if (section.id === 10) {
            return section;
        }

        pos = section.payloadEnd;
    }

    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function hex(n) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const code = findCodeSection();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("MAP ADDRESSES TO CODE BODY RANGES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

console.log("WASM SIZE :", bytes.length);
console.log("CODE SECTION START :", hex(code.payloadStart));
console.log("CODE SECTION END   :", hex(code.payloadEnd));
console.log("");

const countInfo = readULEB(code.payloadStart);

const bodyCount = countInfo.value;

console.log("CODE BODY COUNT :", bodyCount);
console.log("");

let pos = countInfo.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);

    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;

    const bodyPayloadStart = sizeInfo.next;

    const bodyPayloadEnd = bodyPayloadStart + bodySize;

    /*
     * Function body begins at the body size payload.
     * The first bytes are local declarations.
     *
     * For address mapping we care about the complete body byte range.
     */

    bodies.push({
        bodyIndex,
        bodyStart: bodyPayloadStart,
        bodyEnd: bodyPayloadEnd,
        bodySize
    });

    pos = bodyPayloadEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET ADDRESS MAPPING");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const address of TARGET_ADDRESSES) {

    const matches = bodies.filter(
        b => address >= b.bodyStart && address < b.bodyEnd
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("ADDRESS :", hex(address));

    if (matches.length === 0) {
        console.log("RESULT  : NOT FOUND IN ANY CODE BODY");
        continue;
    }

    for (const b of matches) {

        console.log("BODY INDEX :", b.bodyIndex);
        console.log("BODY START :", hex(b.bodyStart));
        console.log("BODY END   :", hex(b.bodyEnd));
        console.log("BODY SIZE  :", b.bodySize);

        /*
         * Important:
         * Code section body index is a defined-function index,
         * but imported functions precede defined functions in the
         * global function index space.
         *
         * We therefore also calculate the likely global function
         * index using the known import count.
         */

        const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

        const globalFunctionIndex =
            IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + b.bodyIndex;

        console.log(
            "GLOBAL FUNC INDEX :",
            globalFunctionIndex
        );

        console.log(
            "OFFSET INSIDE BODY :",
            address - b.bodyStart,
            hex(address - b.bodyStart)
        );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("BODY RANGE CHECK");
console.log("============================================================");

/*
 * Print neighboring bodies around each target.
 * This is useful for catching off-by-one or boundary issues.
 */

for (const address of TARGET_ADDRESSES) {

    const index = bodies.findIndex(
        b => address >= b.bodyStart && address < b.bodyEnd
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("TARGET", hex(address));

    if (index === -1) {
        console.log("NO BODY");
        continue;
    }

    const from = Math.max(0, index - 2);
    const to = Math.min(bodies.length - 1, index + 2);

    for (let i = from; i <= to; i++) {

        const b = bodies[i];

        const marker =
            i === index ? ">>" : "  ";

        console.log(
            marker,
            `BODY=${b.bodyIndex}`,
            `FUNC=${IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + b.bodyIndex}`,
            `${hex(b.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(b.bodyEnd)}`,
            `SIZE=${b.bodySize}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET ADDRESSES :", TARGET_ADDRESSES.length);
console.log("CODE BODIES      :", bodies.length);
console.log("READ ONLY        : YES");
console.log("NETWORK          : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS     : NO");

FILE: scripts\mapCall96PatternFunctions.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function findSection(sectionId) {
  let p = 8; // WASM magic + version

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    if (id === sectionId) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("MAP CALL 96 PATTERN TO FUNCTIONS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const code = findSection(10);

if (!code) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

console.log(
  "CODE SECTION : 0x" +
  code.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
  " .. 0x" +
  code.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
);

let p = code.start;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const functionCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS :", functionCount);
console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS: 83");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const pattern = [
  0x41, 0x18,
  0x10, 0x60,
  0x22, 0x00
];

const hits = [];

for (let codeIndex = 0; codeIndex < functionCount; codeIndex++) {

  const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
  p = bodySizeInfo.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  for (let x = bodyStart; x <= bodyEnd - pattern.length; x++) {

    let ok = true;

    for (let j = 0; j < pattern.length; j++) {
      if (buf[x + j] !== pattern[j]) {
        ok = false;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (!ok) continue;

    const globalFunction = codeIndex + 83;

    hits.push({
      globalFunction,
      codeIndex,
      offset: x,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd
    });
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("TOTAL PATTERN HITS :", hits.length);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let current = -1;
let functionHits = 0;

for (const h of hits) {

  if (h.globalFunction !== current) {

    current = h.globalFunction;
    functionHits++;

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "FUNCTION " + h.globalFunction +
      "  CODE_INDEX " + h.codeIndex
    );

    console.log(
      "BODY : 0x" +
      h.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
      " .. 0x" +
      h.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
    );
  }

  console.log(
    "  HIT 0x" +
    h.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

const counts = new Map();

for (const h of hits) {
  counts.set(
    h.globalFunction,
    (counts.get(h.globalFunction) || 0) + 1
  );
}

console.log("PATTERN HITS       :", hits.length);
console.log("FUNCTIONS INVOLVED :", counts.size);
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("");
console.log("TOP FUNCTIONS:");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const sorted = [...counts.entries()]
  .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
  .slice(0, 30);

for (const [func, count] of sorted) {
  console.log(
    "FUNCTION " +
    String(func).padStart(5) +
    " : " +
    count +
    " hit(s)"
  );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\mapCallersReal87.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 87;
const IMPORTS = 83;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: pos };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function readVec(pos: number) {
  const r = uleb(pos);
  return { count: r.value, pos: r.next };
}

// Find code section
let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const sz = uleb(p);
  p = sz.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    codeSize = sz.value;
    break;

  p += sz.value;

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount.value; bodyIndex++) {

  const bodySize = uleb(q);
  q = bodySize.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize.value;

  for (let i = bodyStart; i < bodyEnd; i++) {

    if (wasm[i] !== 0x10)
      continue;

    const r = uleb(i + 1);

    if (r.value === TARGET) {

      const realFunc = IMPORTS + bodyIndex;

      if (!callers.has(realFunc))
        callers.set(realFunc, []);

      callers.get(realFunc)!.push(i);
    }
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [0x11aaa1, 0x129f71];

function readULEB(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let n = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos + n++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v, len: n };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSection(pos) {
  const id = b[pos++];
  const sz = readULEB(pos);
  return {
    id,
    start: pos + sz.len,
    size: sz.value
  };
}

let p = 8;
