let codeSize = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const s = readSection(p);

  if (s.id === 10) {
    codeStart = s.start;
    codeSize = s.size;
    break;
  }

  p = s.start + s.size;
}

);

q += count.len;

        `BODY 0x${bodyStart.toString(16)} .. 0x${bodyEnd.toString(16)}`
      );
    }
  }

}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x1295b;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 FRAME MAP v5");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log();

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("ULEB");
    }

    throw new Error("EOF");
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value -= 2 ** shift;
            }

            return { value, next: p };
        }

        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("SLEB");
    }

    throw new Error("EOF");
}

function matchLocalGet2(p: number): boolean {
    return buf[p] === 0x20 && buf[p + 1] === 0x02;
}

function decodeConst(p: number) {
    if (buf[p] !== 0x41) return null;

    try {
        return sleb(p + 1);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("FRAME SLOT ACCESS MAP");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const interesting = new Set([
    40,
    48,
    56,
    64,
    72,
    112,
    120,
    136,
    168,
    176,
    184,
    240,
    248,
    264,
    280,
    288,
    296,
    352,
    360,
    376,
    400,
    408,
    424,
    432,
    440,
    448,
    456,
    496,
    512
]);

let refs = 0;

for (let p = START; p < END - 4; p++) {

    if (!matchLocalGet2(p)) continue;

    const c = decodeConst(p + 2);

    if (!c) continue;

    if (!interesting.has(c.value)) continue;

    refs++;

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `REF #${refs} @ ${hex(p)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `  L2 + ${c.value} (${hex(c.value)})`
    );

    const a = Math.max(START, p - 8);
    const z = Math.min(END, c.next + 12);

    let line = "";

    for (let q = a; q < z; q++) {
        line += buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
    }

    console.log(`  bytes: ${line.trim()}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TARGET SLOT BYTE PATTERNS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const targets = [
    0x1a8,
    0x1b0,
    0x1b8,
    0x1c0,
    0x1f0
];

for (const off of targets) {

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `TARGET ${hex(off)} (${off})`
    );

    let found = 0;

    for (let p = START; p < END - 4; p++) {

        if (!matchLocalGet2(p)) continue;

        const c = decodeConst(p + 2);

        if (!c) continue;

        if (c.value !== off) continue;

        found++;

        console.log(
            `  ${hex(p)} -> L2+${off}`
        );
    }

    if (found === 0) {
        console.log("  NONE");
    }
}

console.log("CALL 87 DESTINATION MAP");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let calls87 = 0;

for (let p = START; p < END - 2; p++) {

    if (buf[p] !== 0x10) continue;

    let c;

    try {
        c = uleb(p + 1);
    } catch {
        continue;
    }

    if (c.value !== 87) continue;

    calls87++;

    // Search backwards up to 16 bytes for:
    // local.get 2 + i32.const

    let dest = "UNKNOWN";

    const a = Math.max(START, p - 20);

    for (let q = p - 2; q >= a; q--) {

        if (!matchLocalGet2(q)) continue;

        const k = decodeConst(q + 2);

        if (!k) continue;

        dest = `L2+${k.value} (${hex(k.value)})`;
        break;
    }

    console.log(
        `CALL 87 #${calls87} @ ${hex(p)} -> ${dest}`
    );
}

console.log();

console.log("END");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCTION_INDEX = 87;

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(
  buf: Buffer,
  pos: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB128 too large");
    }
  }
}

function readName(
  buf: Buffer,
  pos: number
): { value: string; next: number } {
  const r = readULEB(buf, pos);
  const end = r.next + r.value;

  return {
    value: buf.subarray(r.next, end).toString("utf8"),
    next: end,
  };
}

function findSection(
  wasm: Buffer,
  wantedId: number
): { start: number; end: number } | null {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sizeInfo.value;

    if (id === wantedId) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function skipLimits(buf: Buffer, pos: number): number {
  const flags = buf[pos++];

  const min = readULEB(buf, pos);
  pos = min.next;

  if (flags & 0x01) {
    const max = readULEB(buf, pos);
    pos = max.next;
  }

  return pos;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" MAP FUNCTION INDEX 87 v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

const functionSection = findSection(wasm, 3);
const codeSection = findSection(wasm, 10);

if (!importSection) {
  console.log("No import section found.");
} else {
  console.log("Import section");
  console.log("  start:", hex(importSection.start));
  console.log("  end  :", hex(importSection.end));
  console.log();

  let p = importSection.start;

  const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
  const importCount = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  console.log("Import count:", importCount);
  console.log();

  let importedFunctions = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {
    const moduleInfo = readName(wasm, p);
    p = moduleInfo.next;

    const fieldInfo = readName(wasm, p);
    p = fieldInfo.next;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0x00) {
      const typeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = typeInfo.next;

      console.log(
        `import #${i} FUNCTION index=${importedFunctions} ` +
        `module="${moduleInfo.value}" ` +
        `name="${fieldInfo.value}" ` +
        `type=${typeInfo.value}`
      );

      importedFunctions++;
    } else if (kind === 0x01) {
      p += 1;
      p = skipLimits(wasm, p);
    } else if (kind === 0x02) {
      p = skipLimits(wasm, p);
    } else if (kind === 0x03) {
      p += 2;
    } else {
      throw new Error(
        `Unknown import kind ${kind} at ${hex(p - 1)}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("Imported function count:", importedFunctions);
  console.log();

  if (TARGET_FUNCTION_INDEX < importedFunctions) {
    console.log(
      `Function index ${TARGET_FUNCTION_INDEX} is IMPORTED.`
    );
    console.log(
      "Therefore it has NO code body in the Code section."
    );
  } else {
    const bodyOrdinal =
      TARGET_FUNCTION_INDEX - importedFunctions;

    console.log(
      `Function index ${TARGET_FUNCTION_INDEX} maps to ` +
      `defined-function body ordinal ${bodyOrdinal}.`
    );
  }
}

  throw new Error("Function section not found");
}

if (!codeSection) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

  const r = readULEB(wasm, fp);
  typeIndices.push(r.value);
  fp = r.next;
}

FILE: scripts\mapFunction96.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(p, end) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (p < end) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function readString(p, end) {
  const n = uleb(p, end);
  if (!n) return null;

  const e = n.next + n.value;
  if (e > end) return null;

  return {
    text: buf.subarray(n.next, e).toString("utf8"),
    next: e
  };
}

let p = 8;

let importedFunctions = 0;
let definedFunctions = 0;

let codeSection = null;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const size = uleb(p, buf.length);
  if (!size) break;

  p = size.next;

  const sectionStart = p;
  const sectionEnd = p + size.value;

  if (sectionId === 2) {
    // Import section
    const count = uleb(p, sectionEnd);

    if (!count) {
      p = sectionEnd;
      continue;
    }

    p = count.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
      const mod = readString(p, sectionEnd);
      if (!mod) break;

      p = mod.next;

      const name = readString(p, sectionEnd);
      if (!name) break;

      p = name.next;

      const kind = buf[p++];

      if (kind === 0) {
        const typeIndex = uleb(p, sectionEnd);
        if (!typeIndex) break;

        p = typeIndex.next;
        importedFunctions++;
      } else if (kind === 1) {
        // table
        p++; // elem type

        const flags = uleb(p, sectionEnd);
        if (!flags) break;

        p = flags.next;

        const min = uleb(p, sectionEnd);
        if (!min) break;

        p = min.next;

        if (flags.value & 1) {
          const max = uleb(p, sectionEnd);
          if (!max) break;
          p = max.next;
        }
      } else if (kind === 2) {
        // memory
        const flags = uleb(p, sectionEnd);
        if (!flags) break;

        p = flags.next;

        const min = uleb(p, sectionEnd);
        if (!min) break;

        p = min.next;

        if (flags.value & 1) {
          const max = uleb(p, sectionEnd);
          if (!max) break;

          p = max.next;
        }
      } else if (kind === 3) {
        // global
        p += 2;
      } else {
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  if (sectionId === 3) {
    // Function section
    const count = uleb(p, sectionEnd);

    if (count) {
      definedFunctions = count.value;
    }
  }

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codeSection = {
      start: sectionStart,
      end: sectionEnd
    };
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM FUNCTION INDEX MAP");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE          :", buf.length.toLocaleString());
console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :", importedFunctions);
console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS  :", definedFunctions);
console.log(
  "TOTAL FUNCTIONS    :",
  importedFunctions + definedFunctions
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const target = 96;

if (target < importedFunctions) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("FUNCTION 96 IS IMPORTED.");
  console.log("IMPORT INDEX :", target);
} else {
  const codeIndex = target - importedFunctions;

  console.log("");
  console.log("FUNCTION 96 IS DEFINED.");
  console.log("GLOBAL INDEX :", target);
  console.log("CODE INDEX   :", codeIndex);

  if (codeSection) {
    let q = codeSection.start;

    const count = uleb(q, codeSection.end);
    q = count.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
      const body = uleb(q, codeSection.end);
      if (!body) break;

      q = body.next;

      const bodyStart = q;
      const bodyEnd = q + body.value;

      if (i === codeIndex) {
        console.log("");
        console.log("FUNCTION 96 BODY");
        console.log(
          "START : 0x" +
            bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
        );
        console.log(
          "END   : 0x" +
            bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
        );
        console.log("SIZE  :", body.value, "bytes");
        break;
      }

      q = bodyEnd;
    }
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

FILE: scripts\mapFunctionIndices.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function leb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function typeName(x: number) {
  return x === 0x7f ? "i32" :
         x === 0x7e ? "i64" :
         x === 0x7d ? "f32" :
         x === 0x7c ? "f64" :
         `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
}

let p = 8;
let importFuncCount = 0;
let types: any[] = [];
let definedTypeIndices: number[] = [];
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const r = leb(p);
  p = r.next;
  const end = p + r.value;

  if (id === 1) {
    const c = leb(p);
    p = c.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
      p++; // form

      const pc = leb(p);
      p = pc.next;
      const params = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) params.push(wasm[p++]);

      const rc = leb(p);
      p = rc.next;
      const results = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) results.push(wasm[p++]);

      types.push({ params, results });
    }
  }

  if (id === 2) {
    const c = leb(p);
    p = c.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
      const ml = leb(p); p = ml.next + ml.value;
      const nl = leb(p); p = nl.next + nl.value;

      const kind = wasm[p++];

      if (kind === 0) {
        const tr = leb(p);
        p = tr.next;
        importFuncCount++;
      } else if (kind === 1) {
        p++; // elemtype
        p++; // limits flags
        const a = leb(p); p = a.next;
        if (wasm[p - 1] === 1) {
          const b = leb(p); p = b.next;
        }
      } else if (kind === 2) {
        p++;
        const flags = wasm[p++];
        const a = leb(p); p = a.next;
        if (flags & 1) {
          const b = leb(p); p = b.next;
        }
      } else if (kind === 3) {
        p += 2;
      }
    }
  }

  if (id === 3) {
    const c = leb(p);
    p = c.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
      const t = leb(p);
      p = t.next;
      definedTypeIndices.push(t.value);
    }
  }

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { v, p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let importStart = -1;
let typeStart = -1;
let functionStart = -1;
let codeStart = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  p = z.p;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + z.v;

  if (id === 1) typeStart = start;
  if (id === 2) importStart = start;
  if (id === 3) functionStart = start;
  if (id === 10) codeStart = start;

  p = end;
}

// Count imported functions
p = importStart;
const importCountInfo = uleb(p);
p = importCountInfo.p;

let importedFunctions = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < importCountInfo.v; i++) {
  const ml = uleb(p);
  p = ml.p + ml.v;

  const nl = uleb(p);
  p = nl.p + nl.v;

  const kind = b[p++];

  if (kind === 0) {
    importedFunctions++;
    const t = uleb(p);
    p = t.p;
  } else if (kind === 1) {
    const x = uleb(p); p = x.p;
    const y = uleb(p); p = y.p;
    const z = uleb(p); p = z.p;
  } else if (kind === 2) {
    const x = uleb(p); p = x.p;
    const y = uleb(p); p = y.p;
  } else if (kind === 3) {
    p += 2;
  }
}

const globalIndex = 2827;
const bodyOrdinal = globalIndex - importedFunctions;

// Parse type section
const types: any[] = [];

p = typeStart;

const tc = uleb(p);
p = tc.p;

for (let i = 0; i < tc.v; i++) {
  if (b[p++] !== 0x60)
    throw new Error("bad functype");

  const np = uleb(p);
  p = np.p;

  const params: number[] = [];
  for (let j = 0; j < np.v; j++)
    params.push(b[p++]);

  const nr = uleb(p);
  p = nr.p;

  const results: number[] = [];
  for (let j = 0; j < nr.v; j++)
    results.push(b[p++]);

  types.push({ params, results });
}

// Get type index of body ordinal
p = functionStart;

const fc = uleb(p);
p = fc.p;

let typeIndex = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < fc.v; i++) {
  const x = uleb(p);
  p = x.p;

  if (i === bodyOrdinal)
    typeIndex = x.v;
}

// Locate code body
p = codeStart;

const cc = uleb(p);
p = cc.p;

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < cc.v; i++) {
  const sizePos = p;
  const x = uleb(p);
  p = x.p;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + x.v;

  if (i === bodyOrdinal) {
    bodyStart = start;
    bodyEnd = end;
  }

  p = end;
}

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readU32(buf, p) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readName(buf, p) {
    const n = readU32(buf, p);
    p = n.next;

    return {
        next: p + n.value
    };
}

function skipLimits(buf, p) {
    const flags = buf[p++];

    const min = readU32(buf, p);
    p = min.next;

    if (flags & 1) {
        const max = readU32(buf, p);
        p = max.next;
    }

    return p;
}

function getSections(buf) {
    const sections = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {

        const id = buf[p++];

        const size = readU32(buf, p);
        p = size.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = start + size.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start,
            end,
            size: size.value
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseTypes(buf, section) {

    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const types = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {

        const form = buf[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            types.push(null);
            continue;
        }

        const pc = readU32(buf, p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
            params.push(buf[p++]);
        }

        const rc = readU32(buf, p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
            results.push(buf[p++]);
        }

        types.push({
            params,
            results
        });
    }

    return types;
}

function parseImports(buf, section) {

    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const importedTypes = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {

        const module = readName(buf, p);
        p = module.next;

        const name = readName(buf, p);
        p = name.next;

        const kind = buf[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {

            const t = readU32(buf, p);
            p = t.next;

            importedTypes.push(t.value);

        } else if (kind === 1) {

            p += 1;
            p = skipLimits(buf, p);

        } else if (kind === 2) {

            p = skipLimits(buf, p);

        } else if (kind === 3) {

            p += 2;

        } else {

            throw new Error(
                `Unknown import kind ${kind}`
            );
        }
    }

    return importedTypes;
}

function parseFunctions(buf, section) {

    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const definedTypes = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {

        const t = readU32(buf, p);
        p = t.next;

        definedTypes.push(t.value);
    }

    return definedTypes;
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("MAP REAL TYPE 4 TARGETS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log();
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);

const sections = getSections(wasm);

const typeSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 1);
const importSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 2);
const functionSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const elementSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 9);

if (!typeSection ||
    !functionSection ||
    !elementSection) {

    throw new Error("Required sections missing");
}

const types = parseTypes(wasm, typeSection);

const importedTypes =
    importSection
        ? parseImports(wasm, importSection)
        : [];

const definedTypes =
    parseFunctions(wasm, functionSection);

const functionTypes =
    importedTypes.concat(definedTypes);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse REAL element section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = elementSection.start;

const segInfo = readU32(wasm, p);
p = segInfo.next;

if (segInfo.value !== 1) {
    throw new Error(
        `Unexpected segment count ${segInfo.value}`
    );
}

const flagsInfo = readU32(wasm, p);
p = flagsInfo.next;

if (flagsInfo.value !== 0) {
    throw new Error(
        `Unexpected element flags ${flagsInfo.value}`
    );
}

if (wasm[p++] !== 0x41) {
    throw new Error("Expected i32.const");
}

// offset
let offset = 0;
let shift = 0;

while (true) {

    const b = wasm[p++];

    offset |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
        break;
    }

    shift += 7;
}

if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b) {
    throw new Error("Invalid offset expression");
}

const countInfo = readU32(wasm, p);
p = countInfo.next;

const count = countInfo.value;

if (count !== 8992) {
    throw new Error(
        `Expected 8992 entries, got ${count}`
    );
}

const table = new Array(offset + count);

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    const f = readU32(wasm, p);
    p = f.next;

    table[offset + i] = f.value;
}

const type4 = [];

for (let i = 0; i < table.length; i++) {

    const functionIndex = table[i];

    if (functionIndex === undefined) {
        continue;
    }

    const typeIndex = functionTypes[functionIndex];

    if (typeIndex === 4) {

        type4.push({
            tableIndex: i,

}

        `TYPE=4`
    );
}

console.log();

console.log("TYPE DISTRIBUTION IN TABLE");
console.log("============================================================");

const distribution = new Map();

for (let i = 0; i < table.length; i++) {

    const f = table[i];

    if (f === undefined) continue;

    const t = functionTypes[f];

    distribution.set(
        t,
        (distribution.get(t) || 0) + 1
    );
}

for (
    const [type, n]

FILE: scripts\mapSegment413ToTable.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_MIN = 4057;
const TARGET_MAX = 4188;

function readULEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
        }
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {
    const sections: {
        id: number;
        start: number;
        end: number;
        size: number;
    }[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start,
            end,
            size: s.value
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function readU32LE(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): number {
    return (
        buf[p] |
        (buf[p + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[p + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

export async function run() {

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEGMENT 413 → ELEMENT TABLE MAPPING");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    const sections = parseSections(buf);

    const element = sections.find(
        x => x.id === 9
    );

    if (!element) {
        throw new Error("Element section not found");
    }

    /*
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     * ELEMENT SECTION
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     */

    let p = element.start;

    const seg = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = seg.next;

    const flags = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = flags.next;

    if (flags.value !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected flags=${flags.value}`
        );
    }

    const offsetOpcode = buf[p++];

    if (offsetOpcode !== 0x41) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected offset opcode ${hex(offsetOpcode)}`
        );
    }

    const off = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = off.next;

    const endOpcode = buf[p++];

    if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected end opcode ${hex(endOpcode)}`
        );
    }

    const tableOffset = off.value;

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const count = countInfo.value;

    const functionToTable =
        new Map<number, number>();

    const tableToFunction =
        new Map<number, number>();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

        /*
         * IMPORTANT:
         *
         * readULEB() returns:
         * { value, next }
         *
         * We must use fnInfo.value.
         */

        const fnInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = fnInfo.next;

        const fn = fnInfo.value;

        const tableIndex =
            tableOffset + i;

        tableToFunction.set(
            tableIndex,
            fn
        );

        if (!functionToTable.has(fn)) {
            functionToTable.set(
                fn,
                tableIndex
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("Element segments :", seg.value);
    console.log("Table offset     :", tableOffset);
    console.log("Function count   :", count);

    console.log(
        "Element cursor   :",
        hex(p)
    );

    console.log(
        "Expected cursor  :",
        hex(element.end)
    );

    if (p !== element.end) {
        console.log(
            "WARNING remaining:",
            element.end - p
        );
    }

    /*
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     * SEGMENT 413 FUNCTION → TABLE
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEGMENT 413 FUNCTION → TABLE INDEX");
    console.log("============================================================");

    let found = 0;

    for (
        let fn = TARGET_MIN;
        fn <= TARGET_MAX;
        fn++
    ) {

        const tableIndex =
            functionToTable.get(fn);

        if (tableIndex === undefined) {

            console.log(
                `function ${fn} -> NOT IN ELEMENT TABLE`
            );

        } else {

            console.log(
                `function ${fn} -> table[${hex(tableIndex)}]`
            );

            found++;
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "Functions in element table :",
        found,
        "/ 132"
    );

    /*
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     * DUPLICATES
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" DUPLICATE FUNCTION ENTRIES");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const occurrences =
        new Map<number, number[]>();

    for (const [
        tableIndex,
        fn
    ] of tableToFunction.entries()) {

        if (
            fn >= TARGET_MIN &&
            fn <= TARGET_MAX
        ) {

            if (!occurrences.has(fn)) {
                occurrences.set(fn, []);
            }

            occurrences
                .get(fn)!
                .push(tableIndex);
        }
    }

    let duplicateCount = 0;

    for (const [
        fn,
        indices
    ] of occurrences.entries()) {

        if (indices.length > 1) {

            duplicateCount++;

            console.log(
                `function ${fn} appears at:`
            );

            console.log(
                "  " +
                indices
                    .map(hex)
                    .join(", ")
            );
        }
    }

    if (duplicateCount === 0) {
        console.log("No duplicate target functions.");
    }

    /*
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     * SEGMENT 413 RAW DATA
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const SEG_START = 0x0018ab98;
    const SEG_END   = 0x0018f511;

    const DATA_START = 0x004a9de0;

    const segmentBytes =
        SEG_END - SEG_START + 1;

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEGMENT 413 EMBEDDED FUNCTION INDEX SCAN");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(
        "Runtime range :",
        `${hex(SEG_START)} .. ${hex(SEG_END)}`
    );

    console.log(
        "Data offset   :",
        hex(DATA_START)
    );

    console.log(
        "Bytes scanned :",
        segmentBytes
    );

    let embedded = 0;

    for (
        let file = DATA_START;
        file + 4 <= DATA_START + segmentBytes;
        file += 4
    ) {

        const value =
            readU32LE(buf, file);

        if (
            value >= TARGET_MIN &&
            value <= TARGET_MAX
        ) {

            const runtime =
                SEG_START +
                (file - DATA_START);

            const tableIndex =
                functionToTable.get(value);

            console.log("");

            console.log(
                `runtime ${hex(runtime)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  function    = ${value}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  table index = ${
                    tableIndex === undefined
                        ? "NOT PRESENT"
                        : hex(tableIndex)
                }`
            );

            embedded++;
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "Embedded target function values :",
        embedded
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" END");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\mapSegment669ConsumersV22.ts

import fs from "fs";

// ============================================================
// SEGMENT 669 CONSUMER → FUNCTION MAP v23
// READ ONLY - OFFLINE
// ============================================================

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
    0x00007c43,
    0x0000eed8,
    0x0000ece4,
    0x0002cc30,
    0x0002cc6b,
];

function readULEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    off: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 6 && off < buf.length; i++) {
        const b = buf[off++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: off };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: -1, next: off };
}

function hex(buf: Buffer, start: number, end: number): string {
    return Array.from(buf.subarray(start, end))
        .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {
    if (
        buf.length < 8 ||
        buf[0] !== 0x00 ||
        buf[1] !== 0x61 ||
        buf[2] !== 0x73 ||
        buf[3] !== 0x6d
    ) {
        throw new Error("Not a valid WASM binary");
    }

    let off = 8;

    const sections: {
        id: number;
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
        size: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (off < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[off++];

        const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, off);
        if (sizeInfo.value < 0) break;

        off = sizeInfo.next;

        const payloadStart = off;
        const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sizeInfo.value;

        if (payloadEnd > buf.length) {
            throw new Error(
                `Invalid section: id=${id} start=0x${payloadStart.toString(
                    16
                )} size=${sizeInfo.value}`
            );
        }

        sections.push({
            id,
            payloadStart,
            payloadEnd,
            size: sizeInfo.value,
        });

        off = payloadEnd;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseCodeSection(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
    }
) {
    let off = section.payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, off);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    off = countInfo.next;

    const bodies: {
        bodyIndex: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
        bodySize: number;
        codeStart: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, off);
        const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
        off = sizeInfo.next;

        const bodyStart = off;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

        if (bodyEnd > section.payloadEnd) {
            throw new Error(
                `Invalid code body ${i}: ` +
                    `start=0x${bodyStart.toString(16)} ` +
                    `size=${bodySize}`
            );
        }

        let p = bodyStart;

        // locals vector
        const localsInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        const localCount = localsInfo.value;
        p = localsInfo.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localCount; j++) {
            const n = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = n.next;

            // value type
            p += 1;
        }

        bodies.push({
            bodyIndex: i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            bodySize,
            codeStart: p,
        });

        off = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function findContainingBody(
    bodies: ReturnType<typeof parseCodeSection>,
    target: number
) {
    return bodies.find(
        (b) => target >= b.bodyStart && target < b.bodyEnd
    );
}

function main() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEGMENT 669 CONSUMER → FUNCTION MAP v23");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    if (!fs.existsSync(WASM_PATH)) {
        throw new Error(`WASM not found:\n${WASM_PATH}`);
    }

    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

    console.log(`WASM path : ${WASM_PATH}`);
    console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

    if (wasm.length !== 5_093_309) {
        console.log(
            `WARNING: expected 5,093,309 bytes, got ${wasm.length}`
        );
    }

    const sections = parseSections(wasm);

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" WASM SECTIONS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const s of sections) {
        console.log(
            `id=${s.id} ` +
                `payload=0x${s.payloadStart
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, "0")} ` +
                `end=0x${s.payloadEnd
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, "0")} ` +
                `size=${s.size}`
        );
    }

    const codeSection = sections.find((s) => s.id === 10);

    if (!codeSection) {
        throw new Error("Code section (id=10) not found");
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" CODE SECTION");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const bodies = parseCodeSection(wasm, codeSection);

    console.log(`function bodies : ${bodies.length}`);

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEGMENT 669 CONSUMER TARGETS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const target of TARGETS) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `TARGET @ 0x${target
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        const body = findContainingBody(bodies, target);

        if (!body) {
            console.log("  NO CONTAINING FUNCTION BODY");
            continue;
        }

        console.log(`  body index : ${body.bodyIndex}`);

        console.log(
            `  body start : 0x${body.bodyStart
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  body end   : 0x${body.bodyEnd
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  code start : 0x${body.codeStart
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        console.log(`  body size  : ${body.bodySize}`);

        const before = 64;
        const after = 128;

        const from = Math.max(body.codeStart, target - before);
        const to = Math.min(body.bodyEnd, target + after);

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `  context: 0x${from
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${to
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        console.log("  " + hex(wasm, from, to));

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `  target offset inside body: ${target - body.bodyStart}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  target offset inside code : ${target - body.codeStart}`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" IMPORTANT SEGMENT 669 TABLE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const tableFileOffset = 0x004b3f11;
    const tableRuntime = 0x00195348;

    console.log(
        `runtime address : 0x${tableRuntime
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
        `file offset     : 0x${tableFileOffset
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log("payload:");

    console.log(
        hex(
            wasm,
            tableFileOffset,
            Math.min(tableFileOffset + 29, wasm.length)
        )
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" DONE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no TON");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no wallet");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no transaction");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no signing");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TYPE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;

let typeStart = 0;
let typeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = u32(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id === 1) {
    typeStart = start;
    typeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Read TYPE signatures
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q = typeStart;

const typeCount = u32(q);
q = typeCount.next;

const types = [];

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount.value; i++) {

  const form = wasm[q++];

  const params = u32(q);
  q = params.next;

  const paramTypes = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < params.value; j++)
    paramTypes.push(wasm[q++]);

  const results = u32(q);
  q = results.next;

  const resultTypes = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < results.value; j++)
    resultTypes.push(wasm[q++]);

  types.push({
    index: i,
    form,
    params: paramTypes,
    results: resultTypes
  });
}

function sig(t) {

  const names = {
    0x7f: "i32",
    0x7e: "i64",
    0x7d: "f32",
    0x7c: "f64"
  };

  return (
    "(" +
    t.params.map(x => names[x] || hx(x)).join(",") +
    ") -> (" +
    t.results.map(x => names[x] || hx(x)).join(",") +
    ")"
  );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = 8;

let funcStart = 0;
let funcEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = u32(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id === 3) {
    funcStart = start;
    funcEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

q = funcStart;

const definedCount = u32(q);
q = definedCount.next;

const functionTypes = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount.value; i++) {

  const r = u32(q);
  q = r.next;

  functionTypes.push(r.value);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ELEMENT SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = 8;

let elemStart = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = u32(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id === 9) {
    elemStart = start;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

q = elemStart;

const segmentCount = u32(q);
q = segmentCount.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TYPE-2 TABLE MAP");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`TYPE COUNT       : ${types.length}`);
console.log(`ELEMENT SEGMENTS : ${segmentCount.value}`);

let tableIndex = 1;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse element segments
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount.value; seg++) {

  const flags = u32(q);
  q = flags.next;

  if (flags.value !== 0) {
    console.log(`SEGMENT ${seg}: unsupported flags=${flags.value}`);
    break;
  }

  // offset expression: i32.const <offset>, end
  if (wasm[q++] !== 0x41) {
    console.log("Unexpected element offset expression");
    break;
  }

  const off = u32(q);
  q = off.next;

  if (wasm[q++] !== 0x0b) {
    console.log("Unexpected element offset end");
    break;
  }

  const count = u32(q);
  q = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const funcIndex = u32(q);
    q = funcIndex.next;

    const f = funcIndex.value;

    let typeIndex = null;

    if (f >= 83 && f < 83 + functionTypes.length) {
      typeIndex = functionTypes[f - 83];
    }

    if (typeIndex === 2) {

      const typeSig = types[typeIndex];

      console.log(
        `TABLE[${tableIndex}] -> FUNC ${f}  TYPE 2  ${sig(typeSig)}`
      );
    }

    tableIndex++;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\mapType4TableTargets.js

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_TYPE = 4;

function readU32(buf, p) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readS32(buf, p) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1 << shift);
    }

    return { value: result | 0, next: p };
}

function readName(buf, p) {
    const n = readU32(buf, p);
    p = n.next;
    return {
        name: buf.subarray(p, p + n.value).toString("utf8"),
        next: p + n.value
    };
}

function skipLimits(buf, p) {
    const flags = buf[p++];

    const min = readU32(buf, p);
    p = min.next;

    if (flags & 1) {
        const max = readU32(buf, p);
        p = max.next;
    }

    return p;
}

function getSections(buf) {
    const sections = [];
    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readU32(buf, p);
        const size = sizeInfo.value;
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = start + size;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start,
            end,
            size
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseTypeSection(buf, section) {
    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const types = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const form = buf[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            types.push({
                index: i,
                params: [],
                results: [],
                form
            });
            continue;
        }

        const pc = readU32(buf, p);
        const paramCount = pc.value;
        p = pc.next;

        const params = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
            params.push(buf[p++]);
        }

        const rc = readU32(buf, p);
        const resultCount = rc.value;
        p = rc.next;

        const results = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
            results.push(buf[p++]);
        }

        types.push({
            index: i,
            params,
            results
        });
    }

    return types;
}

function parseImportFunctionTypes(buf, section) {
    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const importedTypes = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const mod = readName(buf, p);
        p = mod.next;

        const name = readName(buf, p);
        p = name.next;

        const kind = buf[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const t = readU32(buf, p);
            p = t.next;

            importedTypes.push(t.value);
        } else if (kind === 1) {
            p += 1;
            p = skipLimits(buf, p);
        } else if (kind === 2) {
            p = skipLimits(buf, p);
        } else if (kind === 3) {
            p += 2;
        } else {
            throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind} at 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);
        }
    }

    return importedTypes;
}

function parseFunctionSection(buf, section) {
    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const definedTypes = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const t = readU32(buf, p);
        p = t.next;
        definedTypes.push(t.value);
    }

    return definedTypes;
}

function parseElementSection(buf, section) {
    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const elements = [];

    for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {
        const flagsInfo = readU32(buf, p);
        const flags = flagsInfo.value;
        p = flagsInfo.next;

        let tableIndex = 0;

        if (flags === 0) {
            // active table 0
            // offset expression: i32.const <value>, end
            const opcode = buf[p++];

            if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unexpected element offset opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
                );
            }

            const offset = readS32(buf, p);
            p = offset.next;

            const end = buf[p++];

            if (end !== 0x0b) {
                throw new Error("Invalid element offset expression");
            }

            tableIndex = offset.value;
        } else if (flags === 2) {
            const ti = readU32(buf, p);
            tableIndex = ti.value;
            p = ti.next;

            const opcode = buf[p++];

            if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unexpected element offset opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
                );
            }

            const offset = readS32(buf, p);
            p = offset.next;

            const end = buf[p++];

            if (end !== 0x0b) {
                throw new Error("Invalid element offset expression");
            }

            tableIndex = offset.value;
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported element flags ${flags} in segment ${seg}`
            );
        }

        // element type
        const elemType = buf[p++];

        const n = readU32(buf, p);
        const countFunctions = n.value;
        p = n.next;

        const funcs = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < countFunctions; i++) {
            const f = readU32(buf, p);
            p = f.next;

            funcs.push({
                tableIndex: tableIndex + i,
                functionIndex: f.value,
                rawPosition: p - 1
            });
        }

        elements.push({
            seg,
            flags,
            elemType,
            offset: tableIndex,
            funcs
        });
    }

    return elements;
}

function hex(n, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("MAP TYPE 4 TABLE TARGETS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET TYPE:", TARGET_TYPE);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const sections = getSections(wasm);

const typeSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 1);
const importSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 2);
const functionSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);
const elementSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 9);

if (!typeSection) throw new Error("Type section not found");
if (!functionSection) throw new Error("Function section not found");
if (!elementSection) throw new Error("Element section not found");

const types = parseTypeSection(wasm, typeSection);

let importedTypes = [];

if (importSection) {
    importedTypes = parseImportFunctionTypes(wasm, importSection);
}

const definedTypes = parseFunctionSection(wasm, functionSection);

const functionTypes = importedTypes.concat(definedTypes);

const elements = parseElementSection(wasm, elementSection);

const table = new Map();

for (const segment of elements) {
    for (const e of segment.funcs) {
        table.set(e.tableIndex, e.functionIndex);
    }
}

console.log("TYPE COUNT :", types.length);
console.log("FUNCTION COUNT :", functionTypes.length);
console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS :", definedTypes.length);
console.log("TABLE ENTRIES :", table.size);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const type4Targets = [];

for (const [tableIndex, functionIndex] of table.entries()) {
    const typeIndex = functionTypes[functionIndex];

    if (typeIndex === TARGET_TYPE) {
        type4Targets.push({
            tableIndex,
            functionIndex,
            typeIndex
        });
    }
}

type4Targets.sort((a, b) => a.tableIndex - b.tableIndex);

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TYPE 4 TARGETS");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log("COUNT :", type4Targets.length);
console.log();

for (const x of type4Targets) {
    console.log(
        `TABLE[${String(x.tableIndex).padStart(5)}] -> ` +
        `FUNC ${String(x.functionIndex).padStart(5)} ` +
        `TYPE=${x.typeIndex}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET TYPE 4 SIGNATURE");
console.log("============================================================");

const sig = types[TARGET_TYPE];

if (sig) {
    console.log(
        "PARAMS :",
        sig.params.map(x => hex(x, 2)).join(", ")
    );

    console.log(
        "RESULTS:",
        sig.results.map(x => hex(x, 2)).join(", ")
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NEIGHBORHOOD SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

const windows = [579, 1209, 2053];

for (const center of windows) {
    console.log();
    console.log(`AROUND TABLE[${center}]`);

    const nearby = [];

    for (let i = center - 10; i <= center + 10; i++) {
        const f = table.get(i);

        if (f === undefined) continue;

        const t = functionTypes[f];

        nearby.push({
            tableIndex: i,
            functionIndex: f,
            typeIndex: t
        });
    }

    for (const x of nearby) {
        console.log(
            `TABLE[${String(x.tableIndex).padStart(5)}] -> ` +
            `FUNC ${String(x.functionIndex).padStart(5)} ` +
            `TYPE=${String(x.typeIndex).padStart(3)}` +
            (x.typeIndex === TARGET_TYPE ? "  <<< TYPE 4" : "")
        );
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TYPE 4 TABLE TARGET COUNT :", type4Targets.length);
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

import { Address, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { RoosterCoin } from '../wrappers/RoosterCoin';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    if (provider.network() !== 'testnet') {
        throw new Error('STOP: This script is only for TON Testnet.');
    }

    const adminAddress = provider.sender().address;

    if (!adminAddress) {
        throw new Error('STOP: Connected wallet address is not available.');
    }

    const recipient = adminAddress;

    const roosterCoinAddress = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

    const roosterCoin = provider.open(
        RoosterCoin.createFromAddress(roosterCoinAddress)
    );

    console.log('Network:', provider.network());
    console.log('Admin wallet:', adminAddress.toString());
    console.log('Recipient:', recipient.toString());
    console.log('Rooster Coin:', roosterCoinAddress.toString());
    console.log('Mint amount: 1,000,000,000 ROOSTER');
    console.log('TON amount: 0.1 TON');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Sending Mint transaction...');
    console.log('IMPORTANT: Approve ONLY if your wallet shows TESTNET.');

await roosterCoin.sendMint(
    provider.sender(),
    recipient,
    toNano('0.1')
);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Mint transaction sent.');
}

FILE: scripts\parseRealElementTable.js

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readU32(buf, p) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function getSections(buf) {
    const sections = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {

        const id = buf[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readU32(buf, p);
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start,
            end,
            size: sizeInfo.value
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("PARSE REAL ELEMENT TABLE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log();
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log();

const sections = getSections(wasm);

const element = sections.find(x => x.id === 9);

if (!element) {
    throw new Error("Element section not found");
}

console.log(
    "ELEMENT SECTION :",
    "0x" + element.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
    "..",
    "0x" + (element.end - 1).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
);

console.log("SIZE :", element.size);

let p = element.start;

const segmentCountInfo = readU32(wasm, p);
p = segmentCountInfo.next;

const segmentCount = segmentCountInfo.value;

console.log("SEGMENT COUNT :", segmentCount);

if (segmentCount !== 1) {
    throw new Error("Unexpected segment count");
}

const flagsInfo = readU32(wasm, p);
p = flagsInfo.next;

const flags = flagsInfo.value;

console.log("FLAGS :", flags);

if (flags !== 0) {
    throw new Error("Expected flags = 0");
}

// offset expression
const offsetOpcode = wasm[p++];

if (offsetOpcode !== 0x41) {
    throw new Error(
        `Expected i32.const, got 0x${offsetOpcode.toString(16)}`
    );
}

let offset = 0;
let shift = 0;

while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    offset |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
        break;
    }

    shift += 7;
}

const offsetEnd = wasm[p++];

if (offsetEnd !== 0x0b) {
    throw new Error("Invalid offset expression");
}

console.log("TABLE OFFSET :", offset);

const countInfo = readU32(wasm, p);
p = countInfo.next;

const functionCount = countInfo.value;

console.log("FUNCTION COUNT :", functionCount);
console.log("EXPECTED COUNT : 8992");

if (functionCount !== 8992) {
    throw new Error(
        `Unexpected function count ${functionCount}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("TABLE START RAW POSITION :",
    "0x" + p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
);

const table = new Array(functionCount + offset);

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {

    const rawPosition = p;

    const f = readU32(wasm, p);
    p = f.next;

    const tableIndex = offset + i;

    table[tableIndex] = {
        functionIndex: f.value,
        rawPosition
    };
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("VALIDATION");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log();

console.log("TABLE LENGTH :", table.length);
console.log("MAX INDEX    :", table.length - 1);

const targets = [
    { func: 3901, expectedTable: 1209 },
    { func: 6521, expectedTable: 579 },
    { func: 8952, expectedTable: 2053 }
];

console.log();

for (const target of targets) {

    let found = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < table.length; i++) {

        const e = table[i];

        if (e && e.functionIndex === target.func) {
            found.push({
                tableIndex: i,
                rawPosition: e.rawPosition
            });
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `FUNC ${target.func} EXPECTED TABLE[${target.expectedTable}]`
    );

    console.log(
        "FOUND COUNT :",
        found.length
    );

    for (const x of found) {

        console.log(
            `  TABLE[${x.tableIndex}] ` +
            `RAW=0x${x.rawPosition.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}` +
            (
                x.tableIndex === target.expectedTable
                    ? "  <<< EXPECTED"
                    : ""
            )
        );
    }

    console.log();
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET TABLE ENTRIES");
console.log("============================================================");

const selected = [579, 1209, 2053];

for (const center of selected) {

    console.log();
    console.log(`AROUND TABLE[${center}]`);

    for (
        let i = center - 5;
        i <= center + 5;
        i++
    ) {

        if (!table[i]) continue;

        console.log(
            `TABLE[${String(i).padStart(5)}] -> ` +
            `FUNC ${String(table[i].functionIndex).padStart(5)} ` +
            `RAW=0x${table[i].rawPosition.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RAW CONSUMPTION");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    "END POSITION :",
    "0x" + p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
);

console.log(
    "ELEMENT END  :",
    "0x" + element.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
);

console.log(
    "BYTES MATCH  :",
    p === element.end ? "YES" : "NO"
);

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("FUNCTION COUNT VERIFIED :", functionCount === 8992);
console.log("RAW END VERIFIED        :", p === element.end);
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
    );

    const roosterMaster = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const ptonMaster = Address.parse(
        'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
    );

    const knownPool = Address.parse(
        'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
    );

    console.log('===== V2.2 POOL IDENTITY TEST =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('ROOSTER Master:');
    console.log(roosterMaster.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON Master:');
    console.log(ptonMaster.toString());
    console.log('');

    const router = DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress);

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 1. Router calculated pool
    // ------------------------------------------------

    let calculatedPool: Address;

    try {

        calculatedPool = await router.getPoolAddress(
            provider.provider(routerAddress),
            {
                token0: roosterMaster,
                token1: ptonMaster
            }
        );

        console.log('Router getPoolAddress: SUCCESS');
        console.log(
            'Calculated Pool:',
            calculatedPool.toString()
        );

    } catch (error: any) {

        console.log('Router getPoolAddress: FAILED');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
        return;
    }

    console.log('');

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 2. Calculated pool state
    // ------------------------------------------------

    try {

        const state =
            await provider.provider(calculatedPool).getState();

        console.log('CALCULATED POOL STATE');
        console.log(state.state);
        console.log('Balance:', state.balance.toString());

    } catch (error: any) {

        console.log('Calculated pool state failed:');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
    }

    console.log('');

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 3. Known pool state
    // ------------------------------------------------

    try {

        const state =
            await provider.provider(knownPool).getState();

        console.log('KNOWN POOL STATE');
        console.log(state.state);
        console.log('Balance:', state.balance.toString());

    } catch (error: any) {

        console.log('Known pool state failed:');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
    }

    console.log('');

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 4. Known pool get_pool_data
    // ------------------------------------------------

    try {

        const result =
            await provider.provider(knownPool).get(
                'get_pool_data',
                []
            );

        console.log('KNOWN POOL get_pool_data: SUCCESS');
        console.log('');

        const stack = result.stack;

        console.log('Locked:', stack.readBigNumber().toString());

        const routerCell = stack.readCell();
        const totalLp = stack.readBigNumber();
        const reserve0 = stack.readBigNumber();
        const reserve1 = stack.readBigNumber();
        const token0Cell = stack.readCell();
        const token1Cell = stack.readCell();
        const lpFee = stack.readBigNumber();
        const protocolFee = stack.readBigNumber();

        console.log(
            'Router:',
            routerCell.beginParse().loadAddress()?.toString()
        );

        console.log('Total LP:', totalLp.toString());
        console.log('Reserve 0:', reserve0.toString());
        console.log('Reserve 1:', reserve1.toString());

        console.log(
            'Token 0 Wallet:',
            token0Cell.beginParse().loadAddress()?.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'Token 1 Wallet:',
            token1Cell.beginParse().loadAddress()?.toString()
        );

        console.log('LP Fee:', lpFee.toString());
        console.log('Protocol Fee:', protocolFee.toString());

    } catch (error: any) {

        console.log('KNOWN POOL get_pool_data: FAILED');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== TEST FINISHED =====');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
    );

    const roosterRouterWallet = Address.parse(
        'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
    );

    const ptonRouterWallet = Address.parse(
        'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
    );

    const knownPool = Address.parse(
        'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
    );

    console.log('===== V2.2 POOL IDENTITY FIXED TEST =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('ROOSTER Router Wallet:');
    console.log(roosterRouterWallet.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON Router Wallet:');
    console.log(ptonRouterWallet.toString());
    console.log('');

    const router = DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress);

    let calculatedPool: Address;

    try {

        calculatedPool = await router.getPoolAddress(
            provider.provider(routerAddress),
            {
                token0: roosterRouterWallet,
                token1: ptonRouterWallet
            }
        );

        console.log('getPoolAddress WITH ROUTER WALLETS: SUCCESS');
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Calculated Pool:',
            calculatedPool.toString()
        );

    } catch (error: any) {

        console.log('getPoolAddress FAILED');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
        return;
    }

    console.log('');

    console.log('Expected Known Pool:');
    console.log(knownPool.toString());

    console.log('');

    if (calculatedPool.equals(knownPool)) {
        console.log('POOL MATCH: YES');
    } else {
        console.log('POOL MATCH: NO');
    }

    console.log('');

    try {

        const state =
            await provider.provider(calculatedPool).getState();

        console.log('CALCULATED POOL STATE');
        console.log(state.state);
        console.log(
            'Balance:',
            state.balance.toString()
        );

    } catch (error: any) {

        console.log('Calculated pool state failed:');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
    }

    console.log('');

    /*
     * Now use the known active pool directly.
     */

    try {

        const pool =
            DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(knownPool);

        const poolData =
            await pool.getPoolData(
                provider.provider(knownPool)
            );

        console.log('===== ACTIVE POOL DATA =====');
        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'Reserve 0:',
            poolData.reserve0.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'Reserve 1:',
            poolData.reserve1.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'Token 0 Wallet:',
            poolData.token0WalletAddress.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'Token 1 Wallet:',
            poolData.token1WalletAddress.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'LP Fee:',
            poolData.lpFee.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'Protocol Fee:',
            poolData.protocolFee.toString()
        );

        console.log('');

        const token0Match =
            poolData.token0WalletAddress.equals(
                roosterRouterWallet
            );

        const token1Match =
            poolData.token1WalletAddress.equals(
                ptonRouterWallet
            );

        console.log(
            'TOKEN 0 MATCH:',
            token0Match
        );

        console.log(
            'TOKEN 1 MATCH:',
            token1Match
        );

        console.log('');

        if (token0Match && token1Match) {

            console.log(
                'POOL IDENTITY: CONFIRMED'
            );

        } else {

            console.log(
                'POOL IDENTITY: MISMATCH'
            );
        }

    } catch (error: any) {

        console.log('Active Pool SDK read failed:');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== TEST FINISHED =====');
}
