const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Minimal WASM helpers
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function u32(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function i32(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, b;
  while (true) {
    b = buf[pos++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << s);
  return { value: v | 0, next: pos };
}

function readString(pos) {
  const n = u32(pos);
  const start = n.next;
  return {
    value: buf.toString("utf8", start, start + n.value),
    next: start + n.value
  };
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse sections and function bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;

const sections = [];

while (p < buf.length) {
  const id = buf[p++];
  const sz = u32(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sz.value;

  sections.push({ id, start, end });
  p = end;
}

const typeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 1);
const funcSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!typeSec || !funcSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function section: type index for each defined function
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q = funcSec.start;
let fc = u32(q);
q = fc.next;

const definedTypeIndices = [];

for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
  const x = u32(q);
  q = x.next;
  definedTypeIndices.push(x.value);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code section: body offsets
// ------------------------------------------------------------

q = codeSec.start;
const cc = u32(q);
q = cc.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {
  const bs = u32(q);
  q = bs.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + bs.value;

  bodies.push({
    bodyIndex: i,
    start: bodyStart,
    end: bodyEnd,
    functionIndex: 83 + i,
    typeIndex: definedTypeIndices[i]
  });

  q = bodyEnd;
}

function bodyOf(fn) {
  return bodies.find(x => x.functionIndex === fn);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode only instructions needed for 6618.
// This decoder preserves offsets and resolves:
// local.get / local.set / local.tee
// i32.load
// call
// call_indirect
// constants
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function decodeBody(body) {
  let p = body.start;

  // body size already excludes size field
  const localDecl = u32(p);
  p = localDecl.next;

  // Skip local declarations.
  for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {
    const n = u32(p);
    p = n.next;
    p += 1; // valtype
  }

  const out = [];

  while (p < body.end) {
    const off = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    if (op === 0x0b) {
      out.push({ off, op, name: "end" });
      break;
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = u32(p);
      p = x.next;
      out.push({ off, op, name: "local.get", local: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = u32(p);
      p = x.next;
      out.push({ off, op, name: "local.set", local: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = u32(p);
      p = x.next;
      out.push({ off, op, name: "local.tee", local: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = i32(p);
      p = x.next;
      out.push({ off, op, name: "i32.const", value: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
      const align = u32(p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = u32(p);
      p = offset.next;

      out.push({
        off,
        op,
        name: "i32.load",
        align: align.value,
        offset: offset.value
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = u32(p);
      p = x.next;
      out.push({ off, op, name: "call", target: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const type = u32(p);
      p = type.next;

      const table = u32(p);
      p = table.next;

      out.push({
        off,
        op,
        name: "call_indirect",
        type: type.value,
        table: table.value
      });
      continue;
    }

    // Control-flow / arithmetic opcodes are retained but not decoded.
    out.push({
      off,
      op,
      name: "opcode",
      raw: op
    });

    // Known immediate-bearing opcodes that can occur around the target.
    if (
      op === 0x02 || // block
      op === 0x03 || // loop
      op === 0x04    // if
    ) {
      p += 1;
    } else if (
      op === 0x0c || // br
      op === 0x0d || // br_if
      op === 0x0e    // br_table
    ) {
      const x = u32(p);
      p = x.next;

      if (op === 0x0e) {
        for (let i = 0; i < x.value; i++) {
          const y = u32(p);
          p = y.next;
        }
        const y = u32(p);
        p = y.next;
      }
    }
  }

  return out;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find CALL 446 and CALL 248 in FUNC 6618
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const fn = 6618;
const body = bodyOf(fn);

if (!body) {
  throw new Error(`FUNC ${fn} body not found`);
}

const ins = decodeBody(body);

const calls = ins.filter(
  x =>
    (x.name === "call" && (x.target === 446 || x.target === 248))
);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("PROVE FUNC 6618 : 446 -> RESULT -> 248");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNC       : ${fn}`);
console.log(`BODY       : ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
console.log(`CALL SITES : ${calls.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const c of calls) {
  const idx = ins.indexOf(c);

  console.log(`\n>>> ${c.name.toUpperCase()} ${c.target} @ ${hex(c.off)}`);
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  const a = Math.max(0, idx - 8);
  const b = Math.min(ins.length, idx + 9);

  for (let i = a; i < b; i++) {
    const x = ins[i];

    let s = `${hex(x.off)} `;

    if (x.name === "local.get")
      s += `local.get ${x.local}`;
    else if (x.name === "local.set")
      s += `local.set ${x.local}`;
    else if (x.name === "local.tee")
      s += `local.tee ${x.local}`;
    else if (x.name === "i32.load")
      s += `i32.load offset=${x.offset}`;
    else if (x.name === "i32.const")
      s += `i32.const ${x.value}`;
    else if (x.name === "call")
      s += `CALL ${x.target}`;
    else if (x.name === "call_indirect")
      s += `CALL_INDIRECT type=${x.type}`;
    else
      s += `${x.name} 0x${x.op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

    if (i === idx)
      s += " <===";

    console.log(s);
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Explicit local1 flow around CALL 446
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("LOCAL FLOW CHECK");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
  const x = ins[i];

  if (x.name === "call" && x.target === 446) {
    console.log(`\nCALL 446 @ ${hex(x.off)}`);

    for (let j = i + 1; j < Math.min(ins.length, i + 8); j++) {
      const y = ins[j];

      if (y.name === "local.tee" && y.local === 1) {
        console.log(
          `  RESULT OF 446 -> local.tee 1 @ ${hex(y.off)}`
        );

        for (let k = j + 1; k < Math.min(ins.length, j + 8); k++) {
          const z = ins[k];

          if (z.name === "local.get" && z.local === 1) {
            console.log(
              `  local.get 1 @ ${hex(z.off)}`
            );
          }

          if (z.name === "call" && z.target === 248) {
            console.log(
              `  CALL 248 @ ${hex(z.off)}`
            );
            console.log(
              "  ==> STRONG EVIDENCE: result of CALL 446 is passed as first stack value to CALL 248"
            );
            break;
          }
        }
        break;
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

import { Address } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const ADD_ROOSTER = 100_000_000_000n;
const ADD_PTON = 249_908n;

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP CALCULATION PROOF');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    const pool = DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(POOL);
    const poolProvider = provider.provider(POOL);

    const data = await pool.getPoolData(poolProvider);

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:', POOL.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('RESERVE 0:', data.reserve0.toString());
    console.log('RESERVE 1:', data.reserve1.toString());
    console.log('TOTAL LP:', data.totalSupplyLP.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ADD ROOSTER:', ADD_ROOSTER.toString());
    console.log('ADD pTON   :', ADD_PTON.toString());

    const lpFromRooster =
        (ADD_ROOSTER * data.totalSupplyLP) / data.reserve0;

    const lpFromPton =
        (ADD_PTON * data.totalSupplyLP) / data.reserve1;

    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;

    const minLp95 =
        (expectedLp * 95n) / 100n;

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP FROM ROOSTER:');
    console.log(lpFromRooster.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP FROM pTON:');
    console.log(lpFromPton.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED LP = MIN(two sides):');
    console.log(expectedLp.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('95% MIN LP OUT:');
    console.log(minLp95.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'EXPECTED 158009753:',
        expectedLp === 158_009_753n ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log(
        'EXPECTED 150109265:',
        minLp95 === 150_109_265n ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
}

import {
    Address,
    beginCell,
    Cell,
    Contract,
    ContractProvider,
    Sender,
    SendMode,
    contractAddress,
    toNano,
} from '@ton/core';

import { Blockchain } from '@ton/sandbox';
import fs from 'fs';

const PTON_SOURCE =
    'pton_source/libs/src/wrappers/PTon.ts';

const DEPLOY_WALLET_OP = 0x6cc43573;

function extractCode(name: string): Cell {
    const source = fs.readFileSync(PTON_SOURCE, 'utf8');

    const marker = `export const ${name} = codeFromString("`;
    const start = source.indexOf(marker);

    if (start < 0) {
        throw new Error(`Cannot find ${name}`);
    }

    const valueStart = start + marker.length;
    const valueEnd = source.indexOf('"', valueStart);

    if (valueEnd < 0) {
        throw new Error(`Cannot parse ${name}`);
    }

    const hex = source.substring(valueStart, valueEnd);

    console.log(`${name}: ${hex.length} hex characters`);

    return Cell.fromBoc(Buffer.from(hex, 'hex'))[0];
}

const PTON_MINTER_CODE_V1 =
    extractCode('PTON_MINTER_CODE_v1');

const PTON_WALLET_CODE_V1 =
    extractCode('PTON_WALLET_CODE_v1');

class PtonMinterV1 implements Contract {

    readonly init: {
        code: Cell;
        data: Cell;
    };

    constructor(
        readonly address: Address,
        init: {
            code: Cell;
            data: Cell;
        }
    ) {
        this.init = init;
    }

    static create() {

        const data = beginCell()
            .storeRef(PTON_WALLET_CODE_V1)
            .endCell();

        const init = {
            code: PTON_MINTER_CODE_V1,
            data,
        };

        const address = contractAddress(0, init);

        return new PtonMinterV1(address, init);
    }

    async sendDeploy(
        provider: ContractProvider,
        via: Sender,
        value: bigint
    ) {
        await provider.internal(via, {
            value,
            sendMode: SendMode.PAY_GAS_SEPARATELY,
            body: beginCell().endCell(),
        });
    }

    async sendDeployWallet(
        provider: ContractProvider,
        via: Sender,
        ownerAddress: Address,
        excessesAddress: Address,
        value: bigint
    ) {

        const body = beginCell()
            .storeUint(DEPLOY_WALLET_OP, 32)
            .storeUint(0, 64)
            .storeAddress(ownerAddress)
            .storeAddress(excessesAddress)
            .endCell();

        console.log('');
        console.log('Deploy Wallet message:');
        console.log(
            `Opcode:          0x${DEPLOY_WALLET_OP.toString(16)}`
        );
        console.log(
            `Owner:           ${ownerAddress.toString()}`
        );
        console.log(
            `Excesses:        ${excessesAddress.toString()}`
        );
        console.log(
            `Value:            ${Number(value) / 1e9} TON`
        );
        console.log(
            `Body bits:        ${body.bits.length}`
        );
        console.log('');

        await provider.internal(via, {
            value,
            sendMode: SendMode.PAY_GAS_SEPARATELY,
            body,
        });
    }

    getWalletAddress(ownerAddress: Address): Address {

        const data = beginCell()
            .storeCoins(0)
            .storeAddress(ownerAddress)
            .storeAddress(this.address)
            .endCell();

        return contractAddress(0, {
            code: PTON_WALLET_CODE_V1,
            data,
        });
    }
}

export async function run() {

    console.log('');

FILE: scripts\rawCall96CodeSection.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(p, end) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (p < end && shift <= 35) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const s = uleb(p, buf.length);
  if (!s) throw new Error("Invalid section");

  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

let q = codeStart;

const count = uleb(q, codeEnd);
if (!count) throw new Error("Invalid code count");

q = count.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RAW CALL 96 INSIDE CODE SECTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE     :", buf.length.toLocaleString());
console.log("CODE SECTION  :",
  "0x" + codeStart.toString(16),
  "..",
  "0x" + codeEnd.toString(16)
);
console.log("DEFINED FUNCS :", count.value);
console.log("TARGET BYTES   : 10 60");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let total = 0;

for (let codeIndex = 0; codeIndex < count.value; codeIndex++) {
  const body = uleb(q, codeEnd);
  if (!body) throw new Error("Invalid body size");

  q = body.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + body.value;

  for (let x = bodyStart; x + 1 < bodyEnd; x++) {
    if (buf[x] === 0x10 && buf[x + 1] === 0x60) {
      total++;

      const globalFunctionIndex = codeIndex + 83;

      console.log("");
      console.log("HIT #" + total);
      console.log("CODE INDEX :", codeIndex);
      console.log("GLOBAL FUNC:", globalFunctionIndex);
      console.log(
        "OFFSET     : 0x" +
          x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
      );
      console.log(
        "BODY       : 0x" +
          bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
          " .. 0x" +
          bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
      );
    }
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("RAW 10 60 INSIDE CODE BODIES :", total);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const TARGET_LT = '99491281000001';

const TARGET_HASH =
    'iGqT9ftOmAjasbHcEntrKYL4acopbvdJrUnoiGmYE9c=';

const ENDPOINT =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' RAW EXACT MAIN TRANSACTION CHECK');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ADDRESS:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT);

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET HASH:');
    console.log(TARGET_HASH);

    const params = {
        address: USER.toString(),
        limit: 10,
        lt: TARGET_LT,
        hash: TARGET_HASH,
    };

    console.log('');
    console.log('RPC REQUEST:');
    console.log(JSON.stringify(params, null, 2));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Calling TON Center JSON-RPC...');

    const response = await fetch(ENDPOINT, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
            jsonrpc: '2.0',
            id: 1,
            method: 'getTransactions',
            params,
        }),
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('HTTP STATUS:');
    console.log(response.status);

    const text = await response.text();

    console.log('');
    console.log('RAW RESPONSE LENGTH:');
    console.log(text.length);

    let json: any;

    try {
        json = JSON.parse(text);
    } catch {
        console.log('');
        console.log('RAW RESPONSE:');
        console.log(text);

        console.log('');
        console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('RPC RESPONSE KEYS:');
    console.log(Object.keys(json));

    if (json.error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('RPC ERROR:');
        console.log(JSON.stringify(json.error, null, 2));

        console.log('');
        console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
        return;
    }

    const result = json.result;

    console.log('');
    console.log('RESULT TYPE:');
    console.log(Array.isArray(result) ? 'ARRAY' : typeof result);

    if (!Array.isArray(result)) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('RESULT:');
        console.log(JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));

        console.log('');
        console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('TRANSACTIONS RETURNED:');
    console.log(result.length);

    for (let i = 0; i < result.length; i++) {
        const tx = result[i];

        console.log('');
        console.log('==============================================');
        console.log(`RAW TRANSACTION #${i + 1}`);
        console.log('==============================================');

        console.log('');
        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(tx.transaction_id?.lt);

        console.log('');
        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(tx.transaction_id?.hash);

        console.log('');
        console.log('ACCOUNT:');
        console.log(tx.account);

        console.log('');
        console.log('UTIME:');
        console.log(tx.utime);

        console.log('');
        console.log('FULL RAW JSON:');
        console.log(JSON.stringify(tx, null, 2));
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log('TARGET MATCH CHECK');
    console.log('==============================================');

    const exact = result.find((tx: any) => {
        return (
            tx.transaction_id?.lt === TARGET_LT &&
            tx.transaction_id?.hash === TARGET_HASH
        );
    });

    if (exact) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('*** EXACT TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND ***');
    } else {
        console.log('');
        console.log('EXACT TARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND.');

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'This means TON Center returned a different transaction set.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGET = 5079;

// 5079 = 0x13d7
// ULEB128 = d7 27
const a = [0x10, 0xd7, 0x27];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RAW CALL 5079 SEARCH v42");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Target CALL : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`Pattern     : ${a.map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(" ")}`);
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i <= b.length - a.length; i++) {

  if (
    b[i] === a[0] &&
    b[i + 1] === a[1] &&
    b[i + 2] === a[2]
  ) {

    hits++;

    console.log(
      `HIT #${hits} @ 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
      "bytes :",
      Array.from(
        b.slice(Math.max(0, i - 8), Math.min(b.length, i + 11))
      )
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL RAW HITS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END V42");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\rawFunc119.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return [v >>> 0, p];

    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const [size, q] = uleb(p);
  p = q;

  if (id !== 10) {
    p += size;
    continue;
  }

  const [count, q2] = uleb(p);
  p = q2;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const [bodySize, q3] = uleb(p);
    p = q3;

    const func = 83 + i;

    if (func === 119) {
      const start = p;
      const end = p + bodySize;

      console.log("FUNC 119 RAW BODY");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log(
        `START : 0x${start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
      );
      console.log(
        `END   : 0x${end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
      );
      console.log(`SIZE  : ${bodySize}`);
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      const hex = [...b.subarray(start, end)]
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");

      console.log(hex);
      console.log("============================================================");
      process.exit(0);
    }

    p += bodySize;
  }

  process.exit(0);
}

console.log("FUNC 119 NOT FOUND");

FILE: scripts\rawGetLibraries.ts
====================================================================================================
import { ADNLClientTCP } from 'adnl';
import { TLWriteBuffer, TLReadBuffer } from 'ton-tl';
import { Functions, Codecs } from 'ton-lite-client/dist/schema';
import { randomBytes } from 'tweetnacl';

const SERVER = 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787';
const SERVER_KEY = 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=';

const LIBRARIES = [
    'fa0d2b89d20ae82e5f5544eff4d621224573519899c0c98f6a1ced78b5c2ddf3',
    '631cf98bbb3ee540dacc48256975d8e2c3080c6342e3ff4a9edaac6db73730041',
    '7c1f3aa01265453058887378a358cf37314856350400804c169b648c3fdfbe4c',
    '1b63154f10acd98345a65890997094e9856aa2be4f1e37060865519fa42032fd',
];

function toHex(value: any): string {
    if (Buffer.isBuffer(value)) {
        return value.toString('hex');
    }

    if (value instanceof Uint8Array) {
        return Buffer.from(value).toString('hex');
    }

    if (value?.data && Array.isArray(value.data)) {
        return Buffer.from(value.data).toString('hex');
    }

    return String(value);
}

async function main() {
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi ROUTER LIBRARY CHECK');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const client = new ADNLClientTCP(SERVER, SERVER_KEY);

    client.on('error', (err: any) => {
        console.error('ADNL ERROR:', err);
    });

    console.log('Connecting...');

    await client.connect();

    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
            reject(new Error('ADNL ready timeout'));
        }, 10000);

        client.once('ready', () => {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            resolve();
        });

        client.once('error', (err: any) => {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            reject(err);
        });
    });

    console.log('ADNL READY');
    console.log('');

    const request = {
        kind: 'liteServer.getLibraries' as const,
        libraryList: LIBRARIES.map((h) => Buffer.from(h, 'hex')),
    };

    const writer = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Functions.liteServer_getLibraries.encodeRequest(
        request,
        writer
    );

    const body = writer.build();

    const queryWriter = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Functions.liteServer_query.encodeRequest(
        {
            kind: 'liteServer.query',
            data: body,
        },
        queryWriter
    );

    const query = queryWriter.build();
    const queryId = Buffer.from(randomBytes(32));

    const adnlWriter = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Codecs.adnl_Message.encode(
        {
            kind: 'adnl.message.query',
            queryId,
            query,
        },
        adnlWriter
    );

    const packet = adnlWriter.build();

    const result = await new Promise<any>((resolve, reject) => {
        const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
            reject(new Error('getLibraries timeout'));
        }, 10000);

        const onData = (data: Buffer) => {
            try {
                const message = Codecs.adnl_Message.decode(
                    new TLReadBuffer(data)
                );

                if (message.kind !== 'adnl.message.answer') {
                    return;
                }

                if (!message.queryId.equals(queryId)) {
                    return;
                }

                clearTimeout(timeout);

                const answer = message.answer;

                if (answer.readInt32LE(0) === -1146494648) {
                    const error =
                        Codecs.liteServer_Error.decode(
                            new TLReadBuffer(answer)
                        );

                    reject(
                        new Error(
                            'LiteServer error: ' +
                            error.message
                        )
                    );

                    return;
                }

                const decoded =
                    Functions.liteServer_getLibraries.decodeResponse(
                        new TLReadBuffer(answer)
                    );

                resolve(decoded);

            } catch (err) {
                clearTimeout(timeout);
                reject(err);
            }
        };

        client.once('data', onData);
        client.write(packet);
    });

    console.log('===== LIBRARY RESULT =====');
    console.log('Returned entries:', result.result.length);
    console.log('');

    const returned = new Set<string>();

    for (const entry of result.result) {
        const hash = toHex(entry.hash);
        returned.add(hash);

        console.log(
            'RETURNED:',
            hash,
            '| data bytes:',
            entry.data.length
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== EXPECTED LIBRARIES =====');

    let allFound = true;

    for (let i = 0; i < LIBRARIES.length; i++) {
        const expected = LIBRARIES[i];
        const found = returned.has(expected);

        console.log(
            `Library ${i + 1}:`,
            expected,
            '->',
            found ? 'FOUND' : 'NOT FOUND'
        );

        if (!found) {
            allFound = false;
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');

    if (allFound) {
        console.log('RESULT: ALL 4 LIBRARIES FOUND');
    } else {
        console.log('RESULT: ONE OR MORE LIBRARIES NOT FOUND');
    }

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT.');

    client.end();
}

main().catch((err: any) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('TEST FAILED');
    console.error(err?.stack || err);
    process.exit(1);
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\rawGetMasterchainInfo.ts
====================================================================================================
import { ADNLClientTCP } from 'adnl';
import { TLWriteBuffer } from 'ton-tl';
import { Functions, Codecs } from 'ton-lite-client/dist/schema';
import { randomBytes } from 'tweetnacl';

const SERVER = 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787';
const SERVER_KEY = 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=';

console.log('========================================');
console.log(' RAW liteServer.getMasterchainInfo TEST');
console.log('========================================');
console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
console.log('');
console.log('Server:', SERVER);

async function main() {
    const client = new ADNLClientTCP(SERVER, SERVER_KEY);

    client.on('error', (err: any) => {
        console.error('ADNL ERROR:', err);
    });

    client.on('close', () => {
        console.log('ADNL CLOSED');
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== CONNECT =====');

   await client.connect();

console.log('===== ADNL CONNECTED =====');
console.log('Waiting for ADNL READY...');

await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
        reject(new Error('ADNL ready timeout'));
    }, 10000);

    client.once('ready', () => {
        clearTimeout(timeout);
        console.log('===== ADNL READY =====');
        resolve();
    });

    client.once('error', (err: any) => {
        clearTimeout(timeout);
        reject(err);
    });
});

    const queryId = randomBytes(32);

    // liteServer.getMasterchainInfo request
    const writer = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Functions.liteServer_getMasterchainInfo.encodeRequest(
        {},
        writer
    );

    const body = writer.build();

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== getMasterchainInfo ENCODED =====');
    console.log('Bytes:', body.length);
    console.log('Hex:', body.toString('hex'));

    // Wrap in liteServer.query
    const queryWriter = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Functions.liteServer_query.encodeRequest(
        {
            kind: 'liteServer.query',
            data: body,
        },
        queryWriter
    );

    const queryBody = queryWriter.build();

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== liteServer.query ENCODED =====');
    console.log('Bytes:', queryBody.length);
    console.log('Hex:', queryBody.toString('hex'));

    // Wrap in ADNL query
    const adnlWriter = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Codecs.adnl_Message.encode(
        {
            kind: 'adnl.message.query',
            queryId,
            query: queryBody,
        },
        adnlWriter
    );

    const packet = adnlWriter.build();

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ADNL PACKET =====');
    console.log('Query ID:', Buffer.from(queryId).toString('hex'));
    console.log('Packet bytes:', packet.length);

    let finished = false;

    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
        if (!finished) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('===== TIMEOUT =====');
            console.log('No LiteServer response received.');
            console.log('');
            console.log('ADNL connection itself was successful.');
            process.exit(1);
        }
    }, 10000);

    client.on('data', (data: Buffer) => {
        if (finished) return;

        try {
            console.log('');
            console.log('===== ADNL RESPONSE =====');
            console.log('Bytes:', data.length);
            console.log('Hex:', data.toString('hex'));

            const reader = new (require('ton-tl').TLReadBuffer)(data);

            const message = Codecs.adnl_Message.decode(reader);

            console.log('');
            console.log('===== DECODED ADNL MESSAGE =====');
            console.log('Kind:', message.kind);

            if (message.kind !== 'adnl.message.answer') {
                console.log('Unexpected ADNL message kind.');
                return;
            }

            console.log('');
            console.log('===== LITESERVER ANSWER =====');

            const answerReader = new (require('ton-tl').TLReadBuffer)(
                message.answer
            );

            const result =
                Functions.liteServer_getMasterchainInfo.decodeResponse(
                    answerReader
                );

            console.log(JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));

            finished = true;
            clearTimeout(timeout);

            console.log('');
            console.log('===== SUCCESS =====');
            console.log('Current masterchain information received.');
            console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT.');

            process.exit(0);

        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error('');
            console.error('===== DECODE ERROR =====');
            console.error(err?.stack || err);

            finished = true;
            clearTimeout(timeout);

            process.exit(1);
        }
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== SEND QUERY =====');

    client.write(packet);

    console.log('Query sent.');
    console.log('Waiting for LiteServer response...');
}

main().catch((err) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('===== FATAL ERROR =====');
    console.error(err?.stack || err);
    process.exit(1);
});

import { ADNLClientTCP } from 'adnl';
import { TLWriteBuffer, TLReadBuffer } from 'ton-tl';
import { Functions, Codecs } from 'ton-lite-client/dist/schema';
import { randomBytes } from 'tweetnacl';

const SERVER = 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787';
const SERVER_KEY = 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=';

const MISSING = [
    'fa0d2b89d20ae82e5f5544eff4d621224573519899c0c98f6a1ced78b5c2ddf3',
    '631cf98bbb3ee540dacc48256975d8e2c3080c6342e3ff4a9edaac6db73730041',
];

function toHex(value: any): string {
    if (Buffer.isBuffer(value)) return value.toString('hex');
    if (value instanceof Uint8Array) {
        return Buffer.from(value).toString('hex');
    }
    if (value?.data && Array.isArray(value.data)) {
        return Buffer.from(value.data).toString('hex');
    }
    return String(value);
}

async function main() {
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' MISSING STON.fi LIBRARIES CHECK');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const client = new ADNLClientTCP(SERVER, SERVER_KEY);

    client.on('error', (err: any) => {
        console.error('ADNL ERROR:', err);
    });

    console.log('Connecting...');
    await client.connect();

    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
            reject(new Error('ADNL ready timeout'));
        }, 10000);

        client.once('ready', () => {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            resolve();
        });

        client.once('error', (err: any) => {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            reject(err);
        });
    });

    console.log('ADNL READY');
    console.log('');

    const request = {
        kind: 'liteServer.getLibraries' as const,
        libraryList: MISSING.map((h) => Buffer.from(h, 'hex')),
    };

    const writer = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Functions.liteServer_getLibraries.encodeRequest(
        request,
        writer
    );

    const body = writer.build();

    const queryWriter = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Functions.liteServer_query.encodeRequest(
        {
            kind: 'liteServer.query',
            data: body,
        },
        queryWriter
    );

    const query = queryWriter.build();
    const queryId = Buffer.from(randomBytes(32));

    const adnlWriter = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Codecs.adnl_Message.encode(
        {
            kind: 'adnl.message.query',
            queryId,
            query,
        },
        adnlWriter
    );

    const packet = adnlWriter.build();

    console.log('Requested libraries:');
    for (const hash of MISSING) {
        console.log(hash);
    }

    const result = await new Promise<any>((resolve, reject) => {
        const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
            reject(new Error('getLibraries timeout'));
        }, 10000);

        const onData = (data: Buffer) => {
            try {
                const message = Codecs.adnl_Message.decode(
                    new TLReadBuffer(data)
                );

                if (message.kind !== 'adnl.message.answer') return;
                if (!message.queryId.equals(queryId)) return;

                clearTimeout(timeout);

                const answer = message.answer;

                if (answer.readInt32LE(0) === -1146494648) {
                    const error =
                        Codecs.liteServer_Error.decode(
                            new TLReadBuffer(answer)
                        );

                    reject(
                        new Error(
                            'LiteServer error: ' +
                            error.message
                        )
                    );
                    return;
                }

                const decoded =
                    Functions.liteServer_getLibraries.decodeResponse(
                        new TLReadBuffer(answer)
                    );

                resolve(decoded);

            } catch (err) {
                clearTimeout(timeout);
                reject(err);
            }
        };

        client.once('data', onData);
        client.write(packet);
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== RESULT =====');
    console.log('Returned entries:', result.result.length);
    console.log('');

    const returned = new Set<string>();

    for (const entry of result.result) {
        const hash = toHex(entry.hash);
        returned.add(hash);

        console.log('FOUND:', hash);
        console.log('Data bytes:', entry.data.length);
        console.log('');
    }

    console.log('===== FINAL =====');

    for (const hash of MISSING) {
        console.log(
            hash,
            '->',
            returned.has(hash) ? 'FOUND' : 'NOT FOUND'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT.');

    client.end();
}

main().catch((err: any) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('TEST FAILED');
    console.error(err?.stack || err);
    process.exit(1);
});

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';

export async function run() {
    const endpoint =
        'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/runGetMethod';

    const router = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

   const roosterWallet = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const ptonWallet = Address.parse(
    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
);

    function addressSlice(address: Address) {
        return {
            bytes: beginCell()
                .storeAddress(address)
                .endCell()
                .toBoc()
                .toString('base64'),
        };
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== RAW TESTNET POOL GETTER =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Router:', router.toString());
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER Router Wallet:',
        roosterWallet.toString()
    );
    console.log(
        'pTON Router Wallet:',
        ptonWallet.toString()
    );

    const body = {
        address: router.toString(),
        method: 'get_pool_address',
        stack: [
            ['slice', addressSlice(roosterWallet)],
            ['slice', addressSlice(ptonWallet)],
        ],
    };

    console.log('');
    console.log('Calling get_pool_address...');
    console.log('');

    try {
        const response = await fetch(endpoint, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(body),
        });

        const text = await response.text();

        console.log('HTTP:', response.status);
        console.log('');
        console.log('RAW RESPONSE:');
        console.log(text);
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('REQUEST ERROR:');
        console.log(String(error));
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('================================');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';

        const result = await provider.provider(router).get(
            'get_router_version',
            []
        );

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\rawRunSmcMethod.ts
====================================================================================================
import { ADNLClientTCP } from 'adnl';
import { TLWriteBuffer, TLReadBuffer } from 'ton-tl';
import { Functions, Codecs } from 'ton-lite-client/dist/schema';
import { crc16 } from 'ton-lite-client/dist/utils/crc16';
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { randomBytes } from 'tweetnacl';

const SERVER = 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787';
const SERVER_KEY = 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=';

const ROUTER =
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval';

const METHOD = 'get_router_data';

console.log('========================================');
console.log(' RAW CURRENT-BLOCK get_router_data TEST');
console.log('========================================');
console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
console.log('');
console.log('Server:', SERVER);
console.log('Router:', ROUTER);
console.log('Method:', METHOD);

async function main() {
    const client = new ADNLClientTCP(SERVER, SERVER_KEY);

    client.on('error', (err: any) => {
        console.error('ADNL ERROR:', err);
    });

    client.on('close', () => {
        console.log('ADNL CLOSED');
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== CONNECT =====');

    await client.connect();

    console.log('===== ADNL CONNECTED =====');
    console.log('Waiting for ADNL READY...');

    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
            reject(new Error('ADNL ready timeout'));
        }, 10000);

        client.once('ready', () => {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            console.log('===== ADNL READY =====');
            resolve();
        });

        client.once('error', (err: any) => {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            reject(err);
        });
    });

    // ============================================================
    // STEP 1: GET CURRENT MASTERCHAIN INFO
    // ============================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' STEP 1: getMasterchainInfo');
    console.log('========================================');

    const mcWriter = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Functions.liteServer_getMasterchainInfo.encodeRequest(
        {},
        mcWriter
    );

    const mcBody = mcWriter.build();

    const mcQueryWriter = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Functions.liteServer_query.encodeRequest(
        {
            kind: 'liteServer.query',
            data: mcBody,
        },
        mcQueryWriter
    );

    const mcQuery = mcQueryWriter.build();

    const mcQueryId = Buffer.from(randomBytes(32));

    const mcAdnlWriter = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Codecs.adnl_Message.encode(
        {
            kind: 'adnl.message.query',
            queryId: mcQueryId,
            query: mcQuery,
        },
        mcAdnlWriter
    );

    const mcPacket = mcAdnlWriter.build();

    const masterchain = await new Promise<any>((resolve, reject) => {
        const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
            reject(new Error('getMasterchainInfo timeout'));
        }, 10000);

        const onData = (data: Buffer) => {
            try {
                const message = Codecs.adnl_Message.decode(
                    new TLReadBuffer(data)
                );

                if (message.kind !== 'adnl.message.answer') {
                    return;
                }

                if (!message.queryId.equals(mcQueryId)) {
                    return;
                }

                clearTimeout(timeout);

                const result =
                    Functions.liteServer_getMasterchainInfo.decodeResponse(
                        new TLReadBuffer(message.answer)
                    );

                resolve(result);
            } catch (err) {
                clearTimeout(timeout);
                reject(err);
            }
        };

        client.once('data', onData);
        client.write(mcPacket);
    });

    const block = masterchain.last;

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== CURRENT MASTERCHAIN BLOCK =====');
    console.log('Workchain:', block.workchain);
    console.log('Shard:', block.shard.toString());
    console.log('Seqno:', block.seqno);
    console.log(
        'Root hash:',
        Buffer.from(block.rootHash).toString('hex')
    );
    console.log(
        'File hash:',
        Buffer.from(block.fileHash).toString('hex')
    );

    // ============================================================
    // STEP 2: RUN get_router_data ON CURRENT BLOCK
    // ============================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' STEP 2: runSmcMethod');
    console.log('========================================');

    const routerAddress = Address.parse(ROUTER);

    const methodId =
        (((crc16(METHOD) & 0xffff) | 0x10000)) >>> 0;

    console.log('Method ID:', methodId);

    const request = {
        mode: 4,
        id: {
            kind: 'tonNode.blockIdExt',
            workchain: block.workchain,
            shard: block.shard,
            seqno: block.seqno,
            rootHash: Buffer.from(block.rootHash),
            fileHash: Buffer.from(block.fileHash),
        },
        account: {
            kind: 'liteServer.accountId',
            workchain: routerAddress.workChain,
            id: routerAddress.hash,
        },
        methodId,
        params: Buffer.alloc(0),
    };

    const smcWriter = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Functions.liteServer_runSmcMethod.encodeRequest(
        request,
        smcWriter
    );

    const smcBody = smcWriter.build();

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== runSmcMethod ENCODED =====');
    console.log('Bytes:', smcBody.length);
    console.log('Hex:', smcBody.toString('hex'));

    const lsWriter = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Functions.liteServer_query.encodeRequest(
        {
            kind: 'liteServer.query',
            data: smcBody,
        },
        lsWriter
    );

    const lsBody = lsWriter.build();

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== liteServer.query ENCODED =====');
    console.log('Bytes:', lsBody.length);
    console.log('Hex:', lsBody.toString('hex'));

    const queryId = Buffer.from(randomBytes(32));

    const adnlWriter = new TLWriteBuffer();

    Codecs.adnl_Message.encode(
        {
            kind: 'adnl.message.query',
            queryId,
            query: lsBody,
        },
        adnlWriter
    );

    const packet = adnlWriter.build();

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ADNL PACKET =====');
    console.log(
        'Query ID:',
        queryId.toString('hex')
    );
    console.log('Packet bytes:', packet.length);

    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
            reject(new Error('runSmcMethod timeout'));
        }, 10000);

        const onData = (data: Buffer) => {
            try {
                console.log('');
                console.log('===== ADNL RESPONSE =====');
                console.log('Bytes:', data.length);
                console.log('Hex:', data.toString('hex'));

                const message = Codecs.adnl_Message.decode(
                    new TLReadBuffer(data)
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('===== DECODED ADNL MESSAGE =====');
                console.log('Kind:', message.kind);

                if (message.kind !== 'adnl.message.answer') {
                    return;
                }

                if (!message.queryId.equals(queryId)) {
                    return;
                }

                clearTimeout(timeout);

                const answer = message.answer;

                console.log('');
                console.log('===== LITESERVER ANSWER =====');
                console.log(
                    'Answer bytes:',
                    answer.length
                );

                // liteServer.error constructor
                if (answer.readInt32LE(0) === -1146494648) {
                    const error =
                        Codecs.liteServer_Error.decode(
                            new TLReadBuffer(answer)
                        );

                    console.error('');
                    console.error('===== LITESERVER ERROR =====');
                    console.error(error.message);

                    reject(
                        new Error(
                            'LiteServer error: ' +
                            error.message
                        )
                    );

                    return;
                }

                const result =
                    Functions.liteServer_runSmcMethod.decodeResponse(
                        new TLReadBuffer(answer)
                    );

                console.log('');
                console.log('===== runSmcMethod RESULT =====');
                console.log(JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));

                resolve();
            } catch (err: any) {
                clearTimeout(timeout);
                reject(err);
            }
        };

        client.once('data', onData);

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== SEND QUERY =====');
        client.write(packet);
        console.log('Query sent.');
        console.log('Waiting for LiteServer response...');
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' FINAL RESULT');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('Current block:', block.seqno);
    console.log('Router:', ROUTER);
    console.log('Getter:', METHOD);
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT.');

    client.end();
}

main().catch((err: any) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('========================================');
    console.error(' TEST FAILED');
    console.error('========================================');
    console.error(err?.stack || err);
    process.exit(1);
});

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const candidates = [
    {
        no: 5,
        router: 'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3',
        pool: 'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf',
    },
    {
        no: 6,
        router: 'EQDAPye7HAPAAl4WXpz5jOCdhf2H9h9QkkzRQ-6K5usiuQeC',
        pool: 'EQBKYklRf-T_jRhwDqJg-_vDYolCOMK6s8fD9yJN2yMM3aH6',

        router: 'EQBQ_UBQvR9ryUjKDwijtoiyyga2Wl-yJm6Y8gl0k-HDh_5x',

                `token0Wallet: ${data.token0WalletAddress.toString()}`
            );
            console.log(
                `token1Wallet: ${data.token1WalletAddress.toString()}`
            );
            console.log(`lpFee: ${data.lpFee.toString()}`);
            console.log(`protocolFee: ${data.protocolFee.toString()}`);

            console.log('\nExpected masters:');
            console.log(`ROOSTER: ${ROOSTER}`);
            console.log(`pTON:    ${PTON}`);

            console.log('\nNOTE:');
            console.log(
                'token0Wallet/token1Wallet are Router/Pool Jetton Wallets,'
            );
            console.log(
                'so they are NOT directly equal to the master addresses.'
            );
        } catch (e) {
            console.log(
                'POOL DATA ERROR:',
                e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('\n===== DONE =====');
}

const LP_ACCOUNT =
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e';

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000012n;

const API =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3/transactions';

async function main() {

    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 LP ACCOUNT - TONCENTER V3');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO WALLET');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    console.log('\nLP ACCOUNT:');
    console.log(LP_ACCOUNT);

    console.log('\nTARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    const url = new URL(API);

    url.searchParams.set('account', LP_ACCOUNT);
    url.searchParams.set('limit', '20');
    url.searchParams.set('offset', '0');
    url.searchParams.set('sort', 'desc');

    console.log('\nREQUEST:');
    console.log(url.toString());

    console.log('\nFetching...');

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        const body = await response.text();

        throw new Error(
            `HTTP ${response.status}\n${body}`
        );
    }

    const data = await response.json();

    const transactions = data.transactions ?? [];

    console.log('\nTRANSACTIONS RETURNED:');
    console.log(transactions.length);

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' TRANSACTION TIMELINE');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    for (const tx of transactions) {

        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(70));

        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(tx.lt);

        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(tx.hash);

        console.log('NOW:');
        console.log(tx.now);

        console.log('END STATUS:');
        console.log(tx.end_status);

        console.log('TOTAL FEES:');
        console.log(tx.total_fees);

        if (lt === TARGET_LT) {
            console.log('\n*** TARGET TRANSACTION ***');
        }

        if (lt > TARGET_LT) {
            console.log('\n*** NEWER THAN TARGET ***');
        }
    }

    const target = transactions.find(
        (tx: any) => BigInt(tx.lt) === TARGET_LT
    );

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' TARGET SEARCH');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    if (target) {

        console.log('\nTARGET FOUND: YES');

        console.log('\nTARGET TRANSACTION JSON:');

        console.log(
            JSON.stringify(target, null, 2)
        );

    } else {

        console.log('\nTARGET FOUND: NO');

        console.log(
            '\nThe first 20 transactions did not contain the target LT.'
        );
    }

    const newer = transactions.filter(
        (tx: any) => BigInt(tx.lt) > TARGET_LT
    );

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' TRANSACTIONS AFTER TARGET');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    console.log('\nCOUNT:');
    console.log(newer.length);

    for (const tx of newer) {

        console.log('\nLT:', tx.lt);
        console.log('HASH:', tx.hash);
        console.log('NOW:', tx.now);
        console.log('STATUS:', tx.end_status);
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' COMPLETE');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log('READ ONLY');
    console.log('NO WALLET USED');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
}

main().catch((error) => {

    console.error('\nERROR:');

    console.error(error);

    process.exit(1);
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\readMemory19534CInit.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readI32LE(pos) {
    return (
        wasm[pos] |
        (wasm[pos + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[pos + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[pos + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function hx(v, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("READ INITIAL MEMORY AT 0x19534C");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`TARGET ADDRESS : ${hx(TARGET)}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// DATA SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let found = false;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = uleb(pos);
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const sectionStart = pos;
    const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sizeInfo.value;

    if (sectionId === 11) {

        console.log("DATA SECTION FOUND");
        console.log(`SECTION : ${hx(sectionStart)} .. ${hx(sectionEnd)}`);
        console.log();

        let p = sectionStart;

        const countInfo = uleb(p);
        const count = countInfo.value;
        p = countInfo.next;

        console.log(`DATA SEGMENTS : ${count}`);
        console.log("-".repeat(72));

        for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {

            const flagsInfo = uleb(p);
            const flags = flagsInfo.value;
            p = flagsInfo.next;

            let memoryIndex = 0;
            let offsetValue = null;

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // Active segment
            // ------------------------------------------------
            if (flags === 0) {

                // offset expression
                const opcode = wasm[p++];

                if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                    console.log(
                        `SEGMENT ${seg}: unsupported offset opcode ${hx(opcode, 2)}`
                    );

                    process.exit(1);
                }

                const off = uleb(p);
                offsetValue = off.value;
                p = off.next;

                // end expression
                if (wasm[p] !== 0x0b) {
                    console.log(
                        `SEGMENT ${seg}: invalid offset expression`
                    );
                    process.exit(1);
                }

                p++;

            } else if (flags === 2) {

                const mem = uleb(p);
                memoryIndex = mem.value;
                p = mem.next;

                const opcode = wasm[p++];

                if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                    console.log(
                        `SEGMENT ${seg}: unsupported offset opcode`
                    );
                    process.exit(1);
                }

                const off = uleb(p);
                offsetValue = off.value;
                p = off.next;

                if (wasm[p] !== 0x0b) {
                    console.log(
                        `SEGMENT ${seg}: invalid offset expression`
                    );
                    process.exit(1);
                }

                p++;

            } else if (flags === 1) {

                // Passive segment: no fixed memory address.
                const lenInfo = uleb(p);
                const len = lenInfo.value;
                p = lenInfo.next + len;

                console.log(
                    `SEGMENT ${seg}: PASSIVE size=${len}`
                );

                continue;

            } else {

                console.log(
                    `SEGMENT ${seg}: unsupported flags=${flags}`
                );

                process.exit(1);
            }

            const sizeInfo2 = uleb(p);
            const dataSize = sizeInfo2.value;
            p = sizeInfo2.next;

            const dataStart = p;
            const dataEnd = p + dataSize;

            const segStart = offsetValue;
            const segEnd = offsetValue + dataSize;

            console.log(
                `SEGMENT ${seg}  memory=${memoryIndex} ` +
                `offset=${hx(offsetValue)} size=${dataSize} ` +
                `range=${hx(segStart)}..${hx(segEnd)}`
            );

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // Does this segment contain 0x19534C?
            // ------------------------------------------------
            if (
                memoryIndex === 0 &&
                TARGET >= segStart &&
                TARGET + 4 <= segEnd
            ) {

                found = true;

                const localOffset = TARGET - segStart;
                const wasmPos = dataStart + localOffset;

                const value = readI32LE(wasmPos);

                console.log();
                console.log("!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!");
                console.log("TARGET FOUND IN INITIAL DATA");
                console.log("!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!");
                console.log(`TARGET ADDRESS : ${hx(TARGET)}`);
                console.log(`SEGMENT        : ${seg}`);
                console.log(`SEGMENT OFFSET : ${hx(segStart)}`);
                console.log(`LOCAL OFFSET   : ${hx(localOffset)}`);
                console.log(`WASM DATA POS  : ${hx(wasmPos)}`);
                console.log(`RAW BYTES      : ${
                    [...wasm.slice(wasmPos, wasmPos + 4)]
                        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                        .join(" ")
                }`);
                console.log(`I32 VALUE      : ${value}`);
                console.log(`I32 HEX        : ${hx(value)}`);
                console.log();
                console.log("THIS IS THE VALUE USED BY");
                console.log("CALL_INDIRECT IN FUNC 122.");
                console.log();
            }

            p = dataEnd;
        }
    }

    pos = sectionEnd;
}

console.log();

if (!found) {
    console.log("TARGET NOT FOUND IN INITIAL ACTIVE DATA SEGMENTS.");
    console.log("The value may be initialized dynamically.");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\readRouterJettonWallets.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { JettonMaster } from '@ton/ton';

const ROUTER =
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3';

const TOKEN0_WALLET =
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq';

const TOKEN1_WALLET =
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3';

async function getMasterFromWallet(
    provider: NetworkProvider,
    walletAddress: string,
): Promise<Address> {
    const result = await provider.provider(
        Address.parse(walletAddress)
    ).get('get_wallet_data', []);

    // get_wallet_data:
    // 0 = balance
    // 1 = owner_address
    // 2 = jetton_master_address
    // 3 = wallet_code

    result.stack.readBigNumber();
    result.stack.readCell();

    const masterCell = result.stack.readCell();

    return masterCell.beginParse().loadAddress();
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('===== ROUTER JETTON WALLETS =====');

    const router = Address.parse(ROUTER);

    console.log('\nReading TOKEN0 master from Pool Wallet...');
    const token0Master =
        await getMasterFromWallet(provider, TOKEN0_WALLET);

    console.log(
        `TOKEN0 master: ${token0Master.toString()}`
    );

    console.log('\nReading TOKEN1 master from Pool Wallet...');
    const token1Master =
        await getMasterFromWallet(provider, TOKEN1_WALLET);

    console.log(
        `TOKEN1 master: ${token1Master.toString()}`
    );

    const token0JettonMaster = provider.open(
        JettonMaster.create(token0Master)
    );

    const token1JettonMaster = provider.open(
        JettonMaster.create(token1Master)
    );

    console.log('\nGetting Router TOKEN0 wallet...');

    const token0RouterWallet =
        await token0JettonMaster.getWalletAddress(router);

    console.log(
        `TOKEN0 Router wallet: ${token0RouterWallet.toString()}`
    );

    console.log('\nGetting Router TOKEN1 wallet...');

    const token1RouterWallet =
        await token1JettonMaster.getWalletAddress(router);

    console.log(
        `TOKEN1 Router wallet: ${token1RouterWallet.toString()}`
    );

    console.log('\n===== RESULT =====');
    console.log(`Router: ${ROUTER}`);

    console.log(
        `TOKEN0 master: ${token0Master.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `TOKEN0 router wallet: ${token0RouterWallet.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `TOKEN1 master: ${token1Master.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `TOKEN1 router wallet: ${token1RouterWallet.toString()}`
    );

    console.log('\n===== DONE =====');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';
import { writeFileSync } from 'fs';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const EXPECTED_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

// Current pool-ratio deposit
const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100_000_000_000n; // 100 ROOSTER
const PTON_AMOUNT = 187_446n;             // current proportional pTON

// Current calculated LP:
// ROOSTER side = 136826497
// pTON side    = 136825923
// limiting side = 136825923
// 95% = 129984626
const MIN_LP_OUT = 129_984_626n;

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 CURRENT-RATIO BOC BUILDER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log(' NO WALLET SIGNATURE');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SUBMISSION');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    const router = DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);
    const routerProvider = provider.provider(ROUTER);

    const pool = await router.getPoolAddressByJettonMinters(
        routerProvider,
        {
            token0: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            token1: PTON_MASTER,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('SDK POOL:', pool.toString());
    console.log('EXPECTED:', EXPECTED_POOL.toString());
    console.log(
        'POOL MATCH:',
        pool.equals(EXPECTED_POOL) ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'
    );

    if (!pool.equals(EXPECTED_POOL)) {
        throw new Error('POOL ADDRESS MISMATCH');
    }

    const proxyTon = new DEX.v2_2.pTON(PTON_MASTER);

    const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const deadline = now + 600;

    console.log('');
    console.log('CURRENT UNIX:', now);
    console.log('NEW DEADLINE:', deadline);
    console.log('VALID FOR: 600 seconds');

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * ROOSTER -> ROUTER
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: USER,

                sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
                otherTokenAddress: PTON_MASTER,

                sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
                minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,

                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                transferExcessAddress: USER,

                deadline,
            }
        );

    const roosterBody = roosterTx.body!.toBoc({
        idx: false,
    });

    writeFileSync(
        './rooster_v22_current_ratio.boc',
        roosterBody
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER:');
    console.log('TO:', roosterTx.to.toString());
    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        roosterTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );
    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        Number(roosterTx.value) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );
    console.log(
        'EXPECTED DESTINATION:',
        EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString()
    );
    console.log(
        'DESTINATION:',
        roosterTx.to.equals(EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET)
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'FAIL'
    );
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BOC SAVED: ./rooster_v22_current_ratio.boc'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * pTON -> ROUTER
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: USER,

                proxyTon,

                otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,

                sendAmount: PTON_AMOUNT,
                minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,

                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,

                deadline,
            }
        );

    const ptonBody = ptonTx.body!.toBoc({
        idx: false,
    });

    writeFileSync(
        './pton_v22_current_ratio.boc',
        ptonBody
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON:');
    console.log('TO:', ptonTx.to.toString());
    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        ptonTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );
    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        Number(ptonTx.value) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );
    console.log(
        'pTON BOC SAVED: ./pton_v22_current_ratio.boc'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * EXPECTED VALUE CHECKS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const expectedRoosterValue = 300_000_000n;

    const expectedPtonValue =
        PTON_AMOUNT +
        300_000_000n +
        10_000_000n;

    console.log('');
    console.log('VALUE CHECKS');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER EXPECTED:',
        expectedRoosterValue.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER ACTUAL  :',
        roosterTx.value.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX VALUE:',
        roosterTx.value === expectedRoosterValue
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON EXPECTED:',
        expectedPtonValue.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON ACTUAL  :',
        ptonTx.value.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON TX VALUE:',
        ptonTx.value === expectedPtonValue
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'FAIL'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * SUMMARY
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log('CURRENT-RATIO PARAMETERS');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER AMOUNT:',
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString()
    );
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER DISPLAY:',
        Number(ROOSTER_AMOUNT) / 1e9,
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON AMOUNT:',
        PTON_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON DISPLAY:',
        Number(PTON_AMOUNT) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT:',
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'DEADLINE:',
        deadline
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER BOC:');
    console.log('./rooster_v22_current_ratio.boc');

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON BOC:');
    console.log('./pton_v22_current_ratio.boc');

    console.log('');
    console.log('*** CURRENT-RATIO BOC CREATED ***');
    console.log('*** READ ONLY ***');
    console.log('*** NO TRANSACTION SENT ***');
    console.log('*** NO WALLET SIGNATURE ***');
    console.log('*** NO FUNDS MOVED ***');

    console.log('='.repeat(72));
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\rebuildExactPtonV22.ts
