import { Address, beginCell, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { readFileSync } from 'fs';

const ORIGINAL_BOC_FILE =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\pton_v22_generated.boc';

const TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x01f3835d;
const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Exact values decoded from the original BOC
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const QUERY_ID = 0n;
const TON_AMOUNT = 249908n;

const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROUTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const MIN_LP_OUT = 150109265n;

const BOTH_POSITIVE = true;

const DEX_CUSTOM_PAYLOAD_FORWARD_GAS = 0n;

// Decoded from original BOC
const DEADLINE = 1790763877n;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Helpers
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function hex(data: Buffer | Uint8Array): string {
    return Buffer.from(data).toString('hex');
}

function cellBoc(cell: Cell): string {
    return hex(cell.toBoc());
}

function cellHash(cell: Cell): string {
    return hex(cell.hash());
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Header
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log('='.repeat(75));
console.log(' STON.FI pTON v2.2 EXACT BOC REBUILDER');
console.log(' ORIGINAL vs REBUILT');
console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
console.log('='.repeat(75));

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Load original BOC
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const originalBoc = readFileSync(ORIGINAL_BOC_FILE);

const originalRoots = Cell.fromBoc(originalBoc);

if (originalRoots.length !== 1) {
    throw new Error(
        `Expected exactly 1 original root, got ${originalRoots.length}`
    );
}

const originalRoot = originalRoots[0];

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Build PROVIDE_LP params ref
//
// SDK:
//
// beginCell()
//   .storeCoins(minLpOut)
//   .storeAddress(receiverAddress)
//   .storeUint(bothPositive ? 1 : 0, 1)
//   .storeCoins(dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount)
//   .storeMaybeRef(dexCustomPayload)
//   .endCell()
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const paramsRef = beginCell()
    .storeCoins(MIN_LP_OUT)
    .storeAddress(USER_WALLET)
    .storeUint(BOTH_POSITIVE ? 1 : 0, 1)
    .storeCoins(DEX_CUSTOM_PAYLOAD_FORWARD_GAS)
    .storeBit(false)
    .endCell();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Build PROVIDE_LP
//
// SDK:
//
// storeUint(PROVIDE_LP, 32)
// storeAddress(routerWalletAddress)
// storeAddress(refundAddress)
// storeAddress(excessesAddress)
// storeUint(deadline, 64)
// storeRef(paramsRef)
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const provideLp = beginCell()
    .storeUint(PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE, 32)
    .storeAddress(ROUTER_WALLET)
    .storeAddress(USER_WALLET)
    .storeAddress(USER_WALLET)
    .storeUint(DEADLINE, 64)
    .storeRef(paramsRef)
    .endCell();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Build pTON TON_TRANSFER
//
// SDK:
//
// storeUint(TON_TRANSFER, 32)
// storeUint(queryId, 64)
// storeCoins(tonAmount)
// storeAddress(refundAddress)
// storeBit(true)
// storeRef(forwardPayload)
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const rebuiltRoot = beginCell()
    .storeUint(TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE, 32)
    .storeUint(QUERY_ID, 64)
    .storeCoins(TON_AMOUNT)
    .storeAddress(USER_WALLET)
    .storeBit(true)
    .storeRef(provideLp)
    .endCell();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Display structural information
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log('');
console.log('----- ORIGINAL ROOT -----');

console.log(
    'bits:',
    originalRoot.bits.length
);

console.log(
    'refs:',
    originalRoot.refs.length
);

console.log(
    'hash:',
    cellHash(originalRoot)
);

console.log('');
console.log('----- REBUILT ROOT -----');

console.log(
    'bits:',
    rebuiltRoot.bits.length
);

console.log(
    'refs:',
    rebuiltRoot.refs.length
);

console.log(
    'hash:',
    cellHash(rebuiltRoot)
);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Root cell comparison
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const originalRootBoc = cellBoc(originalRoot);
const rebuiltRootBoc = cellBoc(rebuiltRoot);

console.log('');
console.log('----- ROOT BOC COMPARISON -----');

console.log(
    'Original BOC:',
    originalRootBoc
);

console.log(
    'Rebuilt BOC:',
    rebuiltRootBoc
);

console.log(
    'ROOT BOC EXACT MATCH:',
    originalRootBoc === rebuiltRootBoc
);

console.log(
    'ROOT CELL HASH MATCH:',
    cellHash(originalRoot) === cellHash(rebuiltRoot)
);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Compare PROVIDE_LP child
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const originalProvideLp =
    originalRoot.refs[0];

if (!originalProvideLp) {
    throw new Error(
        'Original root has no PROVIDE_LP reference'
    );
}

console.log('');
console.log('----- PROVIDE_LP COMPARISON -----');

console.log(
    'Original bits:',
    originalProvideLp.bits.length
);

console.log(
    'Rebuilt bits:',
    provideLp.bits.length
);

console.log(
    'Original refs:',
    originalProvideLp.refs.length
);

console.log(
    'Rebuilt refs:',
    provideLp.refs.length
);

console.log(
    'Original hash:',
    cellHash(originalProvideLp)
);

console.log(
    'Rebuilt hash:',
    cellHash(provideLp)
);

console.log(
    'PROVIDE_LP HASH MATCH:',
    cellHash(originalProvideLp) === cellHash(provideLp)
);

console.log(
    'PROVIDE_LP BOC MATCH:',
    cellBoc(originalProvideLp) === cellBoc(provideLp)
);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Compare params ref
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const originalParams =
    originalProvideLp.refs[0];

if (!originalParams) {
    throw new Error(
        'Original PROVIDE_LP has no params reference'
    );
}

console.log('');
console.log('----- PARAMS REF COMPARISON -----');

console.log(
    'Original bits:',
    originalParams.bits.length
);

console.log(
    'Rebuilt bits:',
    paramsRef.bits.length
);

console.log(
    'Original refs:',
    originalParams.refs.length
);

console.log(
    'Rebuilt refs:',
    paramsRef.refs.length
);

console.log(
    'Original hash:',
    cellHash(originalParams)
);

console.log(
    'Rebuilt hash:',
    cellHash(paramsRef)
);

console.log(
    'PARAMS HASH MATCH:',
    cellHash(originalParams) === cellHash(paramsRef)
);

console.log(
    'PARAMS BOC MATCH:',
    cellBoc(originalParams) === cellBoc(paramsRef)
);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Full original BOC vs rebuilt BOC
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const rebuiltBoc = rebuiltRoot.toBoc();

console.log('');
console.log('----- FULL BOC COMPARISON -----');

console.log(
    'Original file size:',
    originalBoc.length,
    'bytes'
);

console.log(
    'Rebuilt BOC size:',
    rebuiltBoc.length,
    'bytes'
);

console.log(
    'FULL BOC HEX MATCH:',
    hex(originalBoc) === hex(rebuiltBoc)
);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// First differing byte
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const originalHex = hex(originalBoc);
const rebuiltHex = hex(rebuiltBoc);

if (originalHex === rebuiltHex) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('===========================================================');
    console.log(' EXACT MATCH');
    console.log('===========================================================');

    console.log(
        'Original BOC and rebuilt BOC are byte-for-byte identical.'
    );

} else {

    console.log('');
    console.log('===========================================================');
    console.log(' BOC DIFFERENCE DETECTED');
    console.log('===========================================================');

    const maxLength = Math.max(
        originalHex.length,
        rebuiltHex.length
    );

    let firstDifference = -1;

    for (let i = 0; i < maxLength; i++) {

        if (originalHex[i] !== rebuiltHex[i]) {
            firstDifference = i;
            break;
        }
    }

    console.log(
        'First differing HEX character:',
        firstDifference
    );

    if (firstDifference >= 0) {

        const byteOffset =
            Math.floor(firstDifference / 2);

        console.log(
            'Approximate byte offset:',
            byteOffset
        );

        console.log(
            'Original:',
            originalHex.slice(
                Math.max(0, firstDifference - 32),
                firstDifference + 64
            )
        );

        console.log(
            'Rebuilt:',
            rebuiltHex.slice(
                Math.max(0, firstDifference - 32),
                firstDifference + 64
            )
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Final
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log('');
console.log('='.repeat(75));
console.log(' REBUILD COMPLETE');
console.log(' READ ONLY');
console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
console.log('='.repeat(75));

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'

const USER = Address.parse(

    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'

function printHeader(title: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('======================================================================');
    console.log(` ${title}`);
    console.log('======================================================================');
}

function check(
    name: string,
    ok: boolean,
    actual?: string,
    expected?: string
) {
    console.log(
        `${name}:`,
        ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'
    );

    if (!ok) {
        if (actual !== undefined) {
            console.log('  ACTUAL  :', actual);
        }

        if (expected !== undefined) {
            console.log('  EXPECTED:', expected);
        }
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    printHeader(

    // ================================================================

        Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

        now + 600;

        now
    );

    console.log(

        deadline
    );

        'seconds'
    );

                    MIN_LP_OUT,

                receiverAddress:
                    USER,

                    USER,

                    USER,

                transferExcessAddress:
                    USER,

                deadline:
    deadline,
            }

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const targets = [
  [6464, 0x22838e],
  [6611, 0x22c948],
  [6611, 0x22c9ef],
  [6613, 0x22cb34],
  [6615, 0x22cc55],
  [6615, 0x22cc5e],
  [6618, 0x22d083],
  [7467, 0x25093d],
  [7579, 0x2562b8],
] as const;

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function decode(p: number) {
  const op = wasm[p];

  if (op === 0x20)
    return [`local.get ${wasm[p + 1]}`, p + 2];

  if (op === 0x21)
    return [`local.set ${wasm[p + 1]}`, p + 2];

  if (op === 0x22)
    return [`local.tee ${wasm[p + 1]}`, p + 2];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = uleb(p + 1);
    return [`i32.const ${x.value}`, x.next];
  }

  if (op === 0x28) {
    const x = uleb(p + 1);
    return [`i32.load offset=${x.value}`, x.next];
  }

  if (op === 0x36) {
    const x = uleb(p + 1);
    return [`i32.store offset=${x.value}`, x.next];
  }

  const simple: Record<number,string> = {
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",
    0x47: "i32.lt_u",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x10: "call"
  };

  if (simple[op])
    return [simple[op], p + 1];

  return [`opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`, p + 1];
}

// Find the exact code body containing an offset.
function findBody(off: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10 && off >= start && off < end)
      return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 113 ARG0 BACKWARD RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const [func, target] of targets) {

  const body = findBody(target);

  console.log(`\nFUNC ${func}  CALL113 @ ${hex(target)}`);
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  if (!body) {
    console.log("BODY NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  // Decode the preceding ~100 bytes.
  let p = Math.max(body.start, target - 100);

  while (p < target) {
    const at = p;
    const [txt, next] = decode(p);

    console.log(`${hex(at)}  ${txt}`);

    p = next;
  }

  console.log(`${hex(target)}  >>> CALL 113`);
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\resolve122Triple.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const targets = new Set([
  141606,
  144293,
  149912,
  149932,
]);

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function readConst(pos: number) {
  if (wasm[pos] !== 0x41) return null;
  return uleb(pos + 1);
}

function fmt(p: number) {
  return "0x" + p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opName(b: number) {
  const m: Record<number, string> = {
    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",
    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x79: "i32.rotl",
    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
    0x10: "call",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x04: "if",
    0x02: "block",
    0x0b: "end",
  };
  return m[b] ?? `OP 0x${b.toString(16)}`;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 — BACKWARD ARGUMENT RESOLVER");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let total = 0;

for (let p = 0; p < wasm.length - 1; p++) {
  const c1 = readConst(p);
  if (!c1 || !targets.has(c1.value)) continue;

  // Search shortly after CONST1 for CONST2 + CALL 122
  let c2Pos = -1;
  let c2Value = -1;
  let callPos = -1;

  for (let q = c1.next; q < Math.min(wasm.length, c1.next + 20); q++) {
    const c2 = readConst(q);
    if (!c2) continue;

    for (let r = c2.next; r < Math.min(wasm.length, c2.next + 10); r++) {
      if (wasm[r] !== 0x10) continue;

      const f = uleb(r + 1);
      if (f.value === 122) {
        c2Pos = q;
        c2Value = c2.value;
        callPos = r;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (callPos >= 0) break;
  }

  if (callPos < 0) continue;

  total++;

  console.log("");
  console.log(`CALL122 #${total} @ ${fmt(callPos)}`);
  console.log(`  CONST1 = ${c1.value} (${fmt(c1.value)})`);
  console.log(`  CONST2 = ${c2Value} (${fmt(c2Value)})`);
  console.log("  BACKWARD:");

  // Show previous 18 bytes, decoding simple producers
  const start = Math.max(0, p - 45);

  for (let x = start; x < p; ) {
    const b = wasm[x];
    let text = opName(b);
    let next = x + 1;

    if (b === 0x20 || b === 0x21 || b === 0x22) {
      const z = uleb(x + 1);
      text += ` ${z.value}`;
      next = z.next;
    } else if (b === 0x41 || b === 0x42) {
      const z = uleb(x + 1);
      text += ` ${z.value}`;
      next = z.next;
    } else if (b === 0x10) {
      const z = uleb(x + 1);
      text += ` FUNC_${z.value}`;
      next = z.next;
    }

    console.log(`    ${fmt(x)}  ${text}`);
    x = next;
  }

  if (total >= 20) break;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL MATCHES SHOWN : ${total}`);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\resolve5311DataSegment.ts

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_FILE = 0x4c605c;

let p = 8;

function u(): number {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x: number;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return v >>> 0;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RESOLVE DATA SEGMENT FOR FILE OFFSET 0x4c605c");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const size = u();
  const sectionStart = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (id !== 11) {
    p = end;
    continue;
  }

  const segmentCount = u();

  console.log("DATA SECTION START : 0x" + sectionStart.toString(16));
  console.log("SEGMENT COUNT       :", segmentCount);
  console.log("");

  for (let s = 0; s < segmentCount; s++) {
    const flags = u();

    let memoryOffset = 0;

    if (flags === 0) {
      if (b[p++] !== 0x41) {
        console.log("Unexpected offset expression");
        process.exit(1);
      }
      memoryOffset = u();

      if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) {
        console.log("Unexpected offset end");
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else if (flags === 2) {
      u(); // memory index
      if (b[p++] !== 0x41) {
        console.log("Unexpected offset expression");
        process.exit(1);
      }
      memoryOffset = u();
      if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) {
        console.log("Unexpected offset end");
        process.exit(1);
      }
    } else {
      console.log("UNSUPPORTED FLAGS:", flags);
      process.exit(1);
    }

    const len = u();
    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + len;

    if (
      TARGET_FILE >= payloadStart &&
      TARGET_FILE < payloadEnd
    ) {
      const delta = TARGET_FILE - payloadStart;
      const runtimeAddress = memoryOffset + delta;

      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log("TARGET SEGMENT FOUND");
      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log("SEGMENT       :", s);
      console.log("FLAGS         :", flags);
      console.log("MEMORY START  : 0x" + memoryOffset.toString(16));
      console.log("PAYLOAD START : 0x" + payloadStart.toString(16));
      console.log("PAYLOAD END   : 0x" + payloadEnd.toString(16));
      console.log("TARGET FILE   : 0x" + TARGET_FILE.toString(16));
      console.log("DELTA         : 0x" + delta.toString(16));
      console.log("RUNTIME ADDR  : 0x" + runtimeAddress.toString(16));

      console.log("");
      console.log("TARGET VALUE  : MEM[0x" +
        runtimeAddress.toString(16) + "] = 5311");

      console.log("");
      console.log("CHECK +56 RELATION:");
      console.log("TABLE BASE    : 0x" +
        (runtimeAddress - 56).toString(16));
      console.log("TABLE +56     : 0x" +
        runtimeAddress.toString(16));

      console.log("############################################################");
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
  }

  break;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolve656CallArgs.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const CALLS = [
  { func: 473,  addr: 0x00020087 },
  { func: 599,  addr: 0x000284fb },
  { func: 1249, addr: 0x00057842 },
  { func: 1601, addr: 0x00071954 },
  { func: 2161, addr: 0x0009b0b8 },
  { func: 2161, addr: 0x0009b0fe },
  { func: 3490, addr: 0x0010da53 },
  { func: 6665, addr: 0x0022fa9e },
  { func: 7020, addr: 0x0023d95e },
];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function section(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];
    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (sid === id) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

function getBodies() {
  const sec = section(10);
  let p = sec.start;

  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.next;

  const out: {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    code: number;
  }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const sz = uleb(p);
    p = sz.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + sz.value;

    const locals = uleb(p);
    p = locals.next;

    for (let j = 0; j < locals.value; j++) {
      const count = uleb(p);
      p = count.next;

      // value type
      p++;
    }

    out.push({
      start,
      end,
      code: p,
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return out;
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

type Expr = string;

function readI32Const(p: number) {
  const r = uleb(p);
  return {
    value: r.value | 0,
    next: r.next,
  };
}

function decodeBefore(
  start: number,
  target: number
) {
  const stack: Expr[] = [];
  const locals = new Map<number, Expr>();

  let p = start;

  function pop(): Expr {
    if (!stack.length) return "<?>";

    return stack.pop()!;
  }

  function push(x: Expr) {
    stack.push(x);
  }

  while (p < target) {
    const opPos = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    switch (op) {
      case 0x20: {
        // local.get
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;

        const local = r.value;

        push(
          locals.get(local) ?? `local[${local}]`
        );
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        // local.set
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;

        const local = r.value;
        const v = pop();

        locals.set(local, v);
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        // local.tee
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;

        const local = r.value;
        const v = pop();

        locals.set(local, v);
        push(v);
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const r = readI32Const(p);
        p = r.next;

        push(`i32.const(${r.value})`);
        break;
      }

      case 0x28: {
        // i32.load
        const align = uleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const off = uleb(p);
        p = off.next;

        const addr = pop();

        push(
          `i32.load(${addr}+${hex(off.value)})`
        );
        break;
      }

      case 0x36: {
        // i32.store
        const align = uleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const off = uleb(p);
        p = off.next;

        const value = pop();
        const addr = pop();

        locals.set(
          -1,
          `STORE[${addr}+${hex(off.value)}]=${value}`
        );
        break;
      }

      case 0x6a: {
        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(`(${a}+${b})`);
        break;
      }

      case 0x6b: {
        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(`(${a}-${b})`);
        break;
      }

      case 0x6c: {
        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(`(${a}*${b})`);
        break;
      }

      case 0x4a: {
        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(`(${a}!=${b})`);
        break;
      }

      case 0x45: {
        const a = pop();

        push(`(${a}==0)`);
        break;
      }

      case 0x47: {
        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(`(${a}==${b})`);
        break;
      }

      case 0x04: {
        // if
        const blockType = wasm[p++];

        console.log(
          `  ${hex(opPos)} IF type=${blockType.toString(16)}`
        );

        pop();
        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        console.log(`  ${hex(opPos)} ELSE`);
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        console.log(`  ${hex(opPos)} END`);
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;

        console.log(
          `  ${hex(opPos)} BR ${r.value}`
        );
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;

        console.log(
          `  ${hex(opPos)} BR_IF ${r.value}`
        );

        pop();
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        console.log(`  ${hex(opPos)} RETURN`);
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.next;

        const args = [...stack];

        stack.length = 0;

        push(
          `CALL[${r.value}](${args.join(", ")})`
        );

        console.log(
          `  ${hex(opPos)} CALL ${r.value}`
        );

        break;
      }

      case 0x1a: {
        pop();
        break;
      }

      case 0x1b: {
        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(`select(${a},${b})`);
        break;
      }

      case 0x36:
        p++;
        break;

      default: {
        /*
         * Unknown opcode.
         * Stop instead of corrupting the symbolic stack.
         */
        return {
          stack,
          locals,
          stoppedAt: opPos,
          opcode: op,
        };
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    stack,
    locals,
    stoppedAt: target,
    opcode: -1,
  };
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("RESOLVE ARGUMENTS OF DIRECT CALL 656");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const c of CALLS) {
  console.log("");

  console.log(`CALL @       = ${hex(c.addr)}`);

  const body = bodies.find(
    b => c.addr >= b.code && c.addr < b.end
  );

    `CODE        = ${hex(body.code)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`

  const windowStart = Math.max(body.code, c.addr - 80);

  const result = decodeBefore(
    windowStart,
    c.addr

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_BODIES = new Set([
    0x0001ddd6,
    0x0001dee3
]);

function uleb(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function findCodeSection() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        if (id === 10) {
            return { start, end };
        }

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

/*
 * Count imported functions.
 */
function countImportedFunctions() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        if (id === 2) {
            let x = start;

            const c = uleb(wasm, x);
            x = c.next;

            let count = 0;

            for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {

                const ml = uleb(wasm, x);
                x = ml.next + ml.value;

                const nl = uleb(wasm, x);
                x = nl.next + nl.value;

                const kind = wasm[x++];

                if (kind === 0) {
                    count++;

                    const t = uleb(wasm, x);
                    x = t.next;
                }
                else if (kind === 1) {
                    x++; // ref type
                    x = skipLimits(x);
                }
                else if (kind === 2) {
                    x = skipLimits(x);
                }
                else if (kind === 3) {
                    x += 2;
                }
                else if (kind === 4) {
                    x++;
                    const t = uleb(wasm, x);
                    x = t.next;
                }
            }

            return count;
        }

        p = end;
    }

    return 0;
}

function skipLimits(p: number) {
    const flags = wasm[p++];

    const min = uleb(wasm, p);
    p = min.next;

    if (flags & 1) {
        const max = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = max.next;
    }

    return p;
}

const importedFunctions = countImportedFunctions();
const code = findCodeSection();

console.log("RESOLVE TARGET CODE BODIES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS =", importedFunctions);
console.log("CODE SECTION       =", hex(code.start), "..", hex(code.end));
console.log("");

let p = code.start;

const fc = uleb(wasm, p);
const definedCount = fc.value;
p = fc.next;

let found = 0;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < definedCount; ordinal++) {

    const bs = uleb(wasm, p);
    p = bs.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bs.value;

    if (TARGET_BODIES.has(bodyStart)) {

        const globalFunc = importedFunctions + ordinal;

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log("TARGET BODY FOUND");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log("BODY START       =", hex(bodyStart));
        console.log("BODY END         =", hex(bodyEnd));
        console.log("BODY SIZE        =", bs.value);
        console.log("CODE ORDINAL     =", ordinal);
        console.log("GLOBAL FUNC IDX  =", globalFunc);
        console.log("");

        /*
         * Show first bytes of body.
         */
        const previewEnd = Math.min(bodyEnd, bodyStart + 48);

        console.log(
            Array.from(wasm.subarray(bodyStart, previewEnd))
                .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                .join(" ")
        );

        console.log("");

        found++;
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FOUND =", found);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolveDeepProducer4154.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 4154;
const TARGET_SLOT = 114;
const TARGET_TYPE = 0;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32(p: number): number {
    return (
        wasm[p] |
        (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

interface Ins {
    off: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
    memOffset?: number;
}

interface FuncBody {
    index: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    code: number[];
}

function parseSections(): {
    codePayload: number;
    codeSize: number;
    codeCount: number;
} {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];
        const s = readULEB(p);
        const size = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        const payload = p;

        if (id === 10) {
            const c = readULEB(p);
            return {
                codePayload: payload,
                codeSize: size,
                codeCount: c.value
            };
        }

        p += size;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function parseCodeBodies(): FuncBody[] {
    const sec = parseSections();

    let p = sec.codePayload;
    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next;

    const bodies: FuncBody[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const s = readULEB(p);
        const bodySize = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

        const localDecl = readULEB(p);
        p = localDecl.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localDecl.value; j++) {
            const n = readULEB(p);
            p = n.next;
            p++; // valtype
        }

        const codeStart = p;
        const code: number[] = [];

        while (p < bodyEnd)
            code.push(wasm[p++]);

        bodies.push({
            index: 83 + i,
            start: codeStart,
            end: bodyEnd,
            code
        });
    }

    return bodies;
}

function decodeAt(body: FuncBody, rel: number): Ins | null {
    const b = body.code;
    const p0 = rel;

    if (p0 >= b.length)
        return null;

    const op = b[p0];

    const one = (text: string): Ins => ({
        off: body.start + p0,
        op,
        text
    });

    // control
    if (op === 0x00) return one("unreachable");
    if (op === 0x01) return one("nop");
    if (op === 0x02) return one("block");
    if (op === 0x03) return one("loop");
    if (op === 0x04) return one("if");
    if (op === 0x05) return one("else");
    if (op === 0x0b) return one("end");

    if (op === 0x0c) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + p0 + 1);
        return { off: body.start + p0, op, text: `br ${x.value}`, imm: x.value };
    }

    if (op === 0x0d) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + p0 + 1);
        return { off: body.start + p0, op, text: `br_if ${x.value}`, imm: x.value };
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        let q = body.start + p0 + 1;
        const n = readULEB(q);
        q = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
            const x = readULEB(q);
            q = x.next;
        }

        const d = readULEB(q);

        return {
            off: body.start + p0,
            op,
            text: `br_table`,
            imm: d.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x0f) return one("return");

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + p0 + 1);
        return {
            off: body.start + p0,
            op,
            text: `call ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const t = readULEB(body.start + p0 + 1);
        const tb = readULEB(t.next);

        return {
            off: body.start + p0,
            op,
            text: `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`,
            imm: t.value
        };
    }

    // locals
    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + p0 + 1);
        return {
            off: body.start + p0,
            op,
            text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + p0 + 1);
        return {
            off: body.start + p0,
            op,
            text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + p0 + 1);
        return {
            off: body.start + p0,
            op,
            text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + p0 + 1);
        return {
            off: body.start + p0,
            op,
            text: `global.get ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x24) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + p0 + 1);
        return {
            off: body.start + p0,
            op,
            text: `global.set ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    // constants
    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readSLEB(body.start + p0 + 1);
        return {
            off: body.start + p0,
            op,
            text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = readSLEB(body.start + p0 + 1);
        return {
            off: body.start + p0,
            op,
            text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    // memory load family
    const loads: Record<number, string> = {
        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
        0x30: "i64.load8_s",
        0x31: "i64.load8_u",
        0x32: "i64.load16_s",
        0x33: "i64.load16_u",
        0x34: "i64.load32_s",
        0x35: "i64.load32_u"
    };

    if (loads[op]) {
        const a = readULEB(body.start + p0 + 1);
        const o = readULEB(a.next);

        return {
            off: body.start + p0,
            op,
            text: `${loads[op]} offset=${o.value}`,
            memOffset: o.value
        };
    }

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
        0x67: "i32.clz",
        0x68: "i32.ctz",
        0x69: "i32.popcnt",
        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",
        0x77: "i32.rotl",
        0x78: "i32.rotr"
    };

    if (names[op])
        return one(names[op]);

    return one(`op=0x${op.toString(16)}`);
}

function decodeBody(body: FuncBody): Ins[] {
    const out: Ins[] = [];

    let p = 0;

    while (p < body.code.length) {
        const ins = decodeAt(body, p);
        if (!ins)
            break;

        out.push(ins);

        const rel = ins.off - body.start;
        let next = rel + 1;

        const op = body.code[rel];

        if (
            op === 0x0c ||
            op === 0x0d ||
            op === 0x10 ||
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24 ||
            op === 0x41 ||
            op === 0x42
        ) {
            const x = readULEB(body.start + next);
            next = x.next - body.start;
        }
        else if (op === 0x11) {
            const a = readULEB(body.start + next);
            const b = readULEB(a.next);
            next = b.next - body.start;
        }
        else if (
            op >= 0x28 &&
            op <= 0x35
        ) {
            const a = readULEB(body.start + next);
            const b = readULEB(a.next);
            next = b.next - body.start;
        }
        else if (op === 0x0e) {
            const a = readULEB(body.start + next);
            let q = a.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < a.value; i++) {
                q = readULEB(q).next;
            }

            q = readULEB(q).next;
            next = q - body.start;
        }

        p = next;
    }

    return out;
}

function hex(x: number): string {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function showWindow(ins: Ins[], center: number, radius = 7) {
    const i = ins.findIndex(x => x.off === center);

    if (i < 0)
        return;

    const a = Math.max(0, i - radius);
    const b = Math.min(ins.length, i + radius + 1);

    for (let j = a; j < b; j++) {
        const x = ins[j];
        console.log(
            `    ${hex(x.off)} ${x.text}`
        );
    }
}

function findPrevLocalDefs(
    ins: Ins[],
    index: number,
    local: number
): number[] {
    const out: number[] = [];

    for (let i = index - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const x = ins[i];

        if (
            (x.text === `local.set ${local}`) ||
            (x.text === `local.tee ${local}`)
        ) {
            out.push(i);

            if (out.length >= 12)
                break;
        }
    }

    return out;
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" RESOLVE DEEP PRODUCER 4154");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");

    console.log("WASM size :", wasm.length);
    console.log("Target function :", TARGET_FUNC);
    console.log("Target table slot :", TARGET_SLOT);
    console.log("Target type :", TARGET_TYPE);
    console.log("");

    const bodies = parseCodeBodies();

    console.log("Code bodies :", bodies.length);
    console.log("");

    let indirectSites = 0;
    let targetTypeSites = 0;

    const interesting: {
        body: FuncBody;
        ins: Ins[];
        index: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (const body of bodies) {
        const ins = decodeBody(body);

        for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
            const x = ins[i];

            if (
                x.text ===
                `call_indirect type=${TARGET_TYPE} table=0`
            ) {
                indirectSites++;
                targetTypeSites++;

                interesting.push({
                    body,
                    ins,
                    index: i
                });
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("type-0/table-0 sites :", targetTypeSites);
    console.log("");

    let exact114 = 0;
    let constantOther = 0;
    let load = 0;
    let call = 0;
    let local = 0;
    let arithmetic = 0;
    let unknown = 0;

    let printed = 0;

    for (const site of interesting) {
        const { body, ins, index } = site;

        /*
         * The stack immediately before call_indirect contains:
         *
         *   ... function-index
         *
         * We inspect the instruction immediately before it and
         * recursively follow local definitions.
         */

        let i = index - 1;

        if (i < 0) {
            unknown++;
            continue;
        }

        const x = ins[i];

        if (x.text === `i32.const ${TARGET_SLOT}`) {
            exact114++;

            if (printed < 20) {
                console.log(
                    `EXACT CONST(114): func=${body.index} call=${hex(ins[index].off)}`
                );
                showWindow(ins, ins[index].off);
                console.log("");
                printed++;
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (
            x.text.startsWith("i32.const ")
        ) {
            constantOther++;

            if (printed < 20) {
                console.log(
                    `OTHER CONST: func=${body.index} value=${x.imm} call=${hex(ins[index].off)}`
                );
                showWindow(ins, ins[index].off);
                console.log("");
                printed++;
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (
            x.text.startsWith("i32.load") ||
            x.text.startsWith("i64.load")
        ) {
            load++;

            if (printed < 20) {
                console.log(
                    `ROOT LOAD: func=${body.index} offset=${x.memOffset} call=${hex(ins[index].off)}`
                );
                showWindow(ins, ins[index].off);
                console.log("");
                printed++;
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (x.text.startsWith("call ")) {
            call++;

            if (printed < 20) {
                console.log(
                    `ROOT CALL: func=${body.index} ${x.text} call=${hex(ins[index].off)}`
                );
                showWindow(ins, ins[index].off);
                console.log("");
                printed++;
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (x.text.startsWith("local.get")) {
            local++;

            const m = x.text.match(/local\.get (\d+)/);

            if (m && printed < 20) {
                const localNo = Number(m[1]);
                const defs = findPrevLocalDefs(ins, i, localNo);

                console.log(
                    `ROOT LOCAL: func=${body.index} local=${localNo} call=${hex(ins[index].off)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  definitions found : ${defs.length}`
                );

                for (const d of defs.slice(0, 4)) {
                    console.log(
                        `  DEF @ ${hex(ins[d].off)} ${ins[d].text}`
                    );
                }

                showWindow(ins, ins[index].off);
                console.log("");
                printed++;
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (
            x.text === "i32.add" ||
            x.text === "i32.sub" ||
            x.text === "i32.mul" ||
            x.text === "i32.and" ||
            x.text === "i32.or" ||
            x.text === "i32.xor" ||
            x.text === "i32.shl" ||
            x.text === "i32.shr_s" ||
            x.text === "i32.shr_u"
        ) {
            arithmetic++;

            if (printed < 20) {
                console.log(
                    `ROOT ARITH: func=${body.index} ${x.text} call=${hex(ins[index].off)}`
                );
                showWindow(ins, ins[index].off);
                console.log("");
                printed++;
            }

            continue;
        }

        unknown++;
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("SUMMARY");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");
    console.log("type-0/table-0 sites :", targetTypeSites);
    console.log("Exact CONST(114)     :", exact114);
    console.log("Other constants      :", constantOther);
    console.log("LOAD roots           :", load);
    console.log("CALL roots           :", call);
    console.log("LOCAL roots          :", local);
    console.log("Arithmetic roots     :", arithmetic);
    console.log("Unknown roots        :", unknown);
    console.log("");

    if (exact114 > 0) {
        console.log(
            "!!! DIRECT 114 PRODUCER FOUND !!!"
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            "No direct CONST(114) immediately before call_indirect."
        );
        console.log(
            "Next step: resolve LOAD/CALL/LOCAL roots to actual values."
        );
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolveDirectTypes113306.ts

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function readVec(pos: number) {
  const n = uleb(pos);
  const arr: number[] = [];
  pos = n.next;
  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(pos);
    arr.push(x.value);
    pos = x.next;
  }
  return { arr, next: pos };
}

// Parse sections and collect function type indices.
// Function section gives type index for defined funcs.
// Import section is needed because function numbering includes imports.

let p = 8;
let funcSection: number[] = [];
let importedFuncCount = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const sz = uleb(p);
  const start = sz.next;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (id === 2) {
    let q = start;
    const n = uleb(q); q = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      const ml = uleb(q); q = ml.next + ml.value;
      const nl = uleb(q); q = nl.next + nl.value;

      const kind = b[q++];

      if (kind === 0) {
        uleb(q); q = uleb(q).next; // overwritten below
      }

      // Reparse cleanly is easier from section start, so stop here.
      // This pass only counts imported functions by scanning kind.
    }
  }

  if (id === 3) {
    const r = readVec(start);
    funcSection = r.arr;
  }

  p = end;
}

// Reparse import section correctly.
p = 8;
while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const sz = uleb(p);
  const start = sz.next;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (id === 2) {
    let q = start;
    const n = uleb(q); q = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      const ml = uleb(q); q = ml.next + ml.value;
      const nl = uleb(q); q = nl.next + nl.value;

      const kind = b[q++];

      if (kind === 0) {
        uleb(q); q = uleb(q).next;
        importedFuncCount++;
      } else if (kind === 1) {
        q++; // elemtype
        const flags = uleb(q); q = flags.next;
        const limMin = uleb(q); q = limMin.next;
        if (flags.value & 1) {
          q = uleb(q).next;
        }
      } else if (kind === 2) {
        q = uleb(q).next;
      } else if (kind === 3) {
        q += 2;
      }
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

function funcType(funcNo: number) {
  const definedIndex = funcNo - importedFuncCount;
  if (definedIndex < 0 || definedIndex >= funcSection.length)
    return null;
  return funcSection[definedIndex];
}

// Parse type section.
let types: { params: number[]; results: number[] }[] = [];

p = 8;
while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const sz = uleb(p);
  const start = sz.next;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (id === 1) {
    let q = start;
    const n = uleb(q); q = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      const form = b[q++];
      if (form !== 0x60) throw new Error("bad functype");

      const np = uleb(q); q = np.next;
      const params = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < np.value; j++) {
        params.push(b[q++]);
      }

      const nr = uleb(q); q = nr.next;
      const results = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < nr.value; j++) {
        results.push(b[q++]);
      }

      types.push({ params, results });
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

for (const fn of [113, 306]) {
  const ti = funcType(fn);
  console.log(`FUNC ${fn}`);
  console.log(`IMPORT COUNT : ${importedFuncCount}`);
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX   : ${ti}`);

  if (ti !== null) {
    const t = types[ti];
    console.log(
      `SIGNATURE    : (${t.params.map(x => x === 0x7f ? "i32" : "0x" + x.toString(16)).join(", ")}) -> (${t.results.map(x => x === 0x7f ? "i32" : "0x" + x.toString(16)).join(", ")})`
    );
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
}

FILE: scripts\resolveDispatchFamily.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v+=(x&127)*2**s;
    if(!(x&128)) return [v>>>0,p];
    s+=7;
  }
}

// Find sections
let p=8;
let typeSec=null, funcSec=null, tableSec=null, elemSec=null, codeSec=null;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const [n,q]=u(p); p=q;
  const start=p, end=p+n;

  if(id===1) typeSec=[start,end];
  if(id===3) funcSec=[start,end];
  if(id===4) tableSec=[start,end];
  if(id===9) elemSec=[start,end];
  if(id===10) codeSec=[start,end];

  p=end;
}

// ---------------- TYPES ----------------
const types=[];

{
  let q=typeSec[0];
  const [count,q0]=u(q); q=q0;

  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const form=b[q++];
    const [pc,q1]=u(q); q=q1;

    const params=[];
    for(let j=0;j<pc;j++) params.push(b[q++]);

    const [rc,q2]=u(q); q=q2;

    const results=[];
    for(let j=0;j<rc;j++) results.push(b[q++]);

    types.push({params,results});
  }
}

// ---------------- FUNCTION TYPE ----------------
let funcTypes=[];

{
  let q=funcSec[0];
  const [count,q0]=u(q); q=q0;

  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const [t,q1]=u(q); q=q1;
    funcTypes.push(t);
  }
}

// ---------------- TABLE 0 ----------------
let table0=[];

{
  let q=elemSec[0];
  const [segments,q0]=u(q); q=q0;

  for(let s=0;s<segments;s++){

    const flags=b[q++];

    // flags 0 = active table0, offset expr follows
    if(flags===0){
      q++; // i32.const opcode

      const [,q1]=u(q); q=q1;

      q++; // end

      const [cnt,q2]=u(q); q=q2;

      for(let i=0;i<cnt;i++){
        const [f,q3]=u(q); q=q3;
        table0.push(f);
      }
    }
    else {
      // This script only needs active segment 0.
      // Stop parsing other forms conservatively.
      break;
    }
  }
}

// ---------------- RESULT ----------------
console.log("DISPATCH FAMILY 4788");
console.log("============================================");

for(let i=4778;i<=4800;i++){
  const f=table0[i];

  if(f===undefined){
    console.log(`TABLE[${i}] = <none>`);
    continue;
  }

  const ti=funcTypes[f];

  const sig=types[ti];

  console.log(
    `TABLE[${i}] = FUNC ${f} `+
    `TYPE ${ti} `+
    `(${sig.params.join(",")}) -> (${sig.results.join(",")})`
  );
}

console.log("============================================");
console.log("TARGET TABLE[4788] =",table0[4788]);
console.log("TARGET TYPE       =",funcTypes[table0[4788]]);

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos:number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;
  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return { value:v >>> 0, next:p };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCH TYPE RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;
let types:any[] = [];
let imported = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.next;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id === 1) {
    let q = p;
    const n = uleb(q); q = n.next;

    for (let i=0; i<n.value; i++) {
      const form = wasm[q++];
      const pc = uleb(q); q = pc.next;

      const params:number[] = [];
      for(let j=0;j<pc.value;j++) {
        params.push(wasm[q++]);
      }

      const rc = uleb(q); q = rc.next;
      const results:number[] = [];
      for(let j=0;j<rc.value;j++) {
        results.push(wasm[q++]);
      }

      types.push({i,form,params,results});
    }
  }

  if(id === 2) {
    let q = p;
    const n = uleb(q); q = n.next;

    for(let i=0;i<n.value;i++) {
      const ml=uleb(q); q=ml.next+ml.value;
      const nl=uleb(q); q=nl.next+nl.value;
      const kind=wasm[q++];

      if(kind===0) {
        q=uleb(q).next;
        imported++;
      } else if(kind===1) {
        q++;
        q=uleb(q).next;
        q=uleb(q).next;
      } else if(kind===2) {
        q=uleb(q).next;
        q=uleb(q).next;
      } else if(kind===3) {
        q+=2;
      }
    }
  }

  if(id === 10) {
    let q=p;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.next;

    const targets=[113,306];

    for(let i=0;i<n.value;i++) {
      const bs=uleb(q); q=bs.next;
      const st=q, en=q+bs.value;
      const fn=imported+i;

      if(targets.includes(fn)) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(`FUNC ${fn}`);
        console.log(`BODY 0x${st.toString(16)} .. 0x${en.toString(16)}`);

        for(let z=st;z<en;) {
          const pos=z;
          const op=wasm[z++];
          let imm="";

          if(
            op===0x20 || op===0x21 || op===0x22 ||
            op===0x10 || op===0x0c || op===0x0d ||
            op===0x41 || op===0x42
          ) {
            const x=uleb(z);
            imm=` ${x.value}`;
            z=x.next;
          }
          else if(op>=0x28 && op<=0x3e) {
            const a=uleb(z); z=a.next;
            const o=uleb(z); z=o.next;
            imm=` align=${a.value} offset=0x${o.value.toString(16)}`;
          }
          else if(op===0x11) {
            const t=uleb(z); z=t.next;
            const tab=uleb(z); z=tab.next;
            imm=` type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
          }

          console.log(
            `0x${pos.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
            `${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}${imm}`
          );
        }
      }

      q=en;
    }
    break;
  }

  p=end;
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
for(const i of [1,3]) {
  const t=types[i];
  if(t) {
    console.log(
      `TYPE ${i}: params=${t.params.map((x:number)=>"0x"+x.toString(16)).join(",")} ` +
      `results=${t.results.map((x:number)=>"0x"+x.toString(16)).join(",")}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\resolveFunc104Body.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 104;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p];
  const s = uleb(p + 1);
  const payload = s.next;
  const end = payload + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = payload;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }
  p = end;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

if (TARGET >= count.value)
  throw new Error("FUNC 104 خارج از محدوده است");

let target = null;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const sz = uleb(q);
  const start = sz.next;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (i === TARGET) {
    target = {
      start,
      end: end - 1,
      size: sz.value
    };
    break;
  }

  q = end;
}

console.log("FUNC 104 EXACT BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CODE SECTION : 0x${codeStart.toString(16)} .. 0x${codeEnd.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`FUNC         : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`BODY SIZE    : ${target.size}`);
console.log(
  `BODY         : 0x${target.start.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} .. ` +
  `0x${target.end.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("RAW BODY");

for (let p = target.start; p <= target.end; p += 16) {
  const e = Math.min(p + 15, target.end);
  const hex = [...b.slice(p, e + 1)]
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");

  console.log(
    `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ${hex}`
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolveFunc104Selector16.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x11793;
const END   = 0x11887;

function readULEB(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let n = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    n++;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function readSLEB(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let n = 0;
  let x;

  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
    n++;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: v | 0, len: n };
}

function hx(v) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 104 SELECTOR 16 RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`BODY : ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log("SELECTOR : 16");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

/*
 * Find br_table.
 *
 * FUNC 104 contains:
 *
 *   local.get 1
 *   br_table ...
 *
 * We decode the complete label vector and default label.
 */

let p = START;
let found = false;

while (p < END) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  if (op !== 0x0e)
    continue;

  found = true;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  if (!count) break;

  p += count.len;

  console.log(`br_table @ ${hx(at)}`);
  console.log(`LABEL COUNT : ${count.value}`);
  console.log("");

  const labels = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    if (!r) break;

    p += r.len;

    labels.push(r.value);

    console.log(
      `LABEL[${i}] = ${r.value}`
    );
  }

  const def = readULEB(p);

  if (def) {
    p += def.len;

    console.log(`DEFAULT    = ${def.value}`);
  }

  console.log("");

  if (16 < labels.length) {
    console.log(
      `SELECTOR 16 -> LABEL ${labels[16]}`
    );
  } else {
    console.log("SELECTOR 16 -> DEFAULT");
    console.log(`DEFAULT LABEL = ${def?.value}`);
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("-".repeat(72));

  /*
   * Print the complete body with offsets so the selected
   * branch can be located from its nesting/label structure.
   */

  p = START;

  while (p < END) {

    const q = p;
    const x = b[p++];

    let s =
      `${hx(q)}  ${x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

    if (x === 0x20) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      s += `  local.get ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (x === 0x21) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      s += `  local.set ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (x === 0x22) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      s += `  local.tee ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (x === 0x41) {
      const r = readSLEB(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      s += `  i32.const ${r.value} (${hx(r.value)})`;
    }

    else if (x === 0x28 || x === 0x36) {
      const align = b[p++];
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;

      s +=
        `  ${x === 0x28 ? "i32.load" : "i32.store"}` +
        ` align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (x === 0x10) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      s += `  call ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (x === 0x0e) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;

      p += r.len;

      const labs = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
        const z = readULEB(p);
        if (!z) break;
        p += z.len;
        labs.push(z.value);
      }

      const d = readULEB(p);
      if (d) p += d.len;

      s +=
        `  br_table count=${r.value} ` +
        `labels=[${labs.join(",")}] default=${d?.value}`;
    }

    else if (x === 0x0f) {
      s += "  return";
    }

    else if (x === 0x0b) {
      s += "  end";
    }

    else if (x === 0x0c || x === 0x0d) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      s += `${x === 0x0c ? "  br" : "  br_if"} ${r.value}`;
    }

    else {
      s += `  opcode=0x${x.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;
    }

    console.log(s);
  }

  break;
}

if (!found)
  console.log("br_table NOT FOUND");

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\resolveFunc106Type.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 106;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let importedFunctions = 0;
let targetType = null;
let functionSection = null;
let typeSection = null;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND SECTIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

while (p < b.length) {

  const id = b[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id === 2) {
    // IMPORT SECTION
    let q = start;

    const count = uleb(q);
    q = count.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

      // module name
      const ml = uleb(q);
      q = ml.next + ml.value;

      // import name
      const nl = uleb(q);
      q = nl.next + nl.value;

      const kind = b[q++];

      if (kind === 0) {
        // function
        const t = uleb(q);
        q = t.next;
        importedFunctions++;

      } else if (kind === 1) {
        // table
        q++;

        const flags = uleb(q);
        q = flags.next;

        q = uleb(q).next;

        if (flags.value & 1)
          q = uleb(q).next;

      } else if (kind === 2) {
        // memory
        const flags = uleb(q);
        q = flags.next;

        q = uleb(q).next;

        if (flags.value & 1)
          q = uleb(q).next;

      } else if (kind === 3) {
        // global
        q += 2;
      }
    }
  }

  if (id === 3) {
    functionSection = {
      start,
      end
    };
  }

  if (id === 1) {
    typeSection = {
      start,
      end
    };
  }

  p = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 106 TYPE RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTION COUNT : ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`TARGET FUNCTION         : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q = functionSection.start;

const functionCount = uleb(q);
q = functionCount.next;

const definedOrdinal =
  TARGET_FUNC - importedFunctions;

console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTION COUNT  : ${functionCount.value}`);
console.log(`TARGET CODE ORDINAL      : ${definedOrdinal}`);

if (
  definedOrdinal < 0 ||
  definedOrdinal >= functionCount.value
) {
  throw new Error(
    `FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} outside defined function range`
  );
}

for (let i = 0; i <= definedOrdinal; i++) {

  const t = uleb(q);
  q = t.next;

  if (i === definedOrdinal) {
    targetType = t.value;
  }
}

console.log(`TYPE INDEX               : ${targetType}`);
console.log("");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TYPE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

q = typeSection.start;

const typeCount = uleb(q);
q = typeCount.next;

function typeName(x) {

  if (x === 0x7f) return "i32";
  if (x === 0x7e) return "i64";
  if (x === 0x7d) return "f32";
  if (x === 0x7c) return "f64";
  if (x === 0x70) return "funcref";
  if (x === 0x6f) return "externref";

  return `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
}

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount.value; i++) {

  const form = b[q++];

  if (form !== 0x60)
    throw new Error(
      `Unexpected type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at type ${i}`
    );

  const pc = uleb(q);
  q = pc.next;

  const params = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
    params.push(b[q++]);

  const rc = uleb(q);
  q = rc.next;

  const results = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
    results.push(b[q++]);

  if (i === targetType) {

    console.log("TARGET TYPE FOUND");
    console.log("--------------------------------------------");

    console.log(`TYPE INDEX  : ${i}`);
    console.log(`PARAM COUNT : ${params.length}`);

    params.forEach((x, n) => {
      console.log(
        `  PARAM ${n} : ${typeName(x)}`
      );
    });

    console.log(`RESULT COUNT : ${results.length}`);

    results.forEach((x, n) => {
      console.log(
        `  RESULT ${n} : ${typeName(x)}`
      );
    });

    console.log("");

    console.log(
      `SIGNATURE : (${params.map(typeName).join(", ")}) -> (${results.map(typeName).join(", ")})`
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 122;
const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGET_SELECTOR = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: v, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hx(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let pos = 8;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const section = wasm[pos++];

    const s = uleb(pos);
    pos = s.next;

    const end = pos + s.value;

    if (section === 10) {

        let p = pos;

        const c = uleb(p);
        p = c.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < c.value; ordinal++) {

            const b = uleb(p);
            const size = b.value;
            p = b.next;

            const start = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + size;

            const func = IMPORTS + ordinal;

            if (func === TARGET) {

                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log("FUNC 122 SELECTOR RESOLUTION");
                console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log(`BODY : ${hx(start)} .. ${hx(bodyEnd)}`);
                console.log(`TARGET SELECTOR : ${hx(TARGET_SELECTOR)}`);
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                let q = start;

                while (q < bodyEnd) {

                    const op = wasm[q];

                    // i32.const
                    if (op === 0x41) {

                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        if (r.value === TARGET_SELECTOR) {

                            console.log(
                                `${hx(q)}  i32.const ${r.value} (${hx(r.value)})`
                            );

                            console.log(
                                `      EXACT MATCH: selector 0x19534C`
                            );
                        }

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.tee
                    if (op === 0x22) {

                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  local.tee ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.get
                    if (op === 0x20) {

                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  local.get ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.set
                    if (op === 0x21) {

                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  local.set ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // call_indirect
                    if (op === 0x11) {

                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log();
                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  CALL_INDIRECT`
                        );
                        console.log(
                            `      TYPE INDEX : ${r.value}`
                        );
                        console.log(
                            `      TABLE IDX  : ${wasm[r.next]}`
                        );

                        q = r.next + 1;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // direct call
                    if (op === 0x10) {

                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  CALL FUNC ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // return
                    if (op === 0x0f) {
                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  RETURN`
                        );
                        q++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    q++;
                }

                console.log();
                console.log("-".repeat(72));
                console.log("SEARCHING ALL RAW i32.const 0x19534C OCCURRENCES");
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                let hits = 0;

                for (let i = 0; i < wasm.length - 1; i++) {

                    if (wasm[i] !== 0x41) continue;

                    try {
                        const r = uleb(i + 1);

                        if (r.value === TARGET_SELECTOR) {

                            console.log(
                                `${hx(i)}  i32.const 0x19534C`
                            );

                            hits++;
                        }
                    } catch {}
                }

                console.log();
                console.log(`TOTAL RAW i32.const MATCHES : ${hits}`);
                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log("DONE");

                process.exit(0);
            }

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    pos = end;
}

console.log("FUNC 122 NOT FOUND");

FILE: scripts\resolveFunc1968Type.mjs
