import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 1968;

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

const typeNames = {
  0x7f: "i32",
  0x7e: "i64",
  0x7d: "f32",
  0x7c: "f64"
};

let p = 8;
let typeSection = null;
let funcSection = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  let size;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 1) typeSection = [start, end];
  if (id === 3) funcSection = [start, end];

  p = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 1968 TYPE RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

// Parse type section
const types = [];

{
  let q = typeSection[0];
  let n;
  [n, q] = u(q);

  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const form = b[q++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
      console.log("UNEXPECTED TYPE FORM", form);
      break;
    }

    let pc;
    [pc, q] = u(q);

    const params = [];
    for (let j = 0; j < pc; j++) {
      params.push(typeNames[b[q++]] ?? "unknown");
    }

    let rc;
    [rc, q] = u(q);

    const results = [];
    for (let j = 0; j < rc; j++) {
      results.push(typeNames[b[q++]] ?? "unknown");
    }

    types.push({ params, results });
  }
}

// Function section
let targetType = null;

{
  let q = funcSection[0];
  let n;
  [n, q] = u(q);

  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    let typeidx;
    [typeidx, q] = u(q);

    const func = i + 91;

    if (func === TARGET) {
      targetType = typeidx;
      break;
    }
  }
}

console.log("FUNC        :", TARGET);
console.log("TYPE INDEX  :", targetType);

if (targetType !== null) {
  console.log(
    "PARAMS      :",
    types[targetType].params.join(", ") || "()"
  );

  console.log(
    "RESULTS     :",
    types[targetType].results.join(", ") || "()"
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolveFunc1974StoreAddresses.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGETS = new Set([
  0x0019534c,
  0x00195350,
  0x00195354,
  0x00195358,
]);

const FUNC = 1974;
const BODY_START = 0x00093e16;
const BODY_END = 0x00093f30;

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("RESOLVE FUNC1974 STORE ADDRESSES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(60));
console.log(`FUNCTION = ${FUNC}`);
console.log(`BODY = ${hex(BODY_START)} .. ${hex(BODY_END)}`);
console.log("TARGETS:");
for (const x of TARGETS) console.log(`  ${hex(x)}`);
console.log();

console.log("IMPORTANT:");
console.log("We resolve the ADDRESS operand of i32.store.");
console.log("The preceding constant 0x19534c is NOT assumed to be");
console.log("the store destination.");
console.log();

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

type Instr = {
  addr: number;
  op: number;
  name: string;
  imm?: number;
};

const localNames: Record<number, string> = {};

function localName(i: number) {
  return `local${i}`;
}

function decode(start: number, end: number): Instr[] {
  const out: Instr[] = [];
  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x0b) {
      out.push({ addr, op, name: "end" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      out.push({
        addr,
        op,
        name: "local.get",
        imm: x.value,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      out.push({
        addr,
        op,
        name: "local.set",
        imm: x.value,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      out.push({
        addr,
        op,
        name: "local.tee",
        imm: x.value,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      out.push({
        addr,
        op,
        name: "i32.const",
        imm: x.value >>> 0,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const y = uleb(p);
      p = y.next;

      out.push({
        addr,
        op,
        name: `i32.load offset=${hex(y.value)}`,
        imm: y.value,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const y = uleb(p);
      p = y.next;

      out.push({
        addr,
        op,
        name: `i32.store offset=${hex(y.value)}`,
        imm: y.value,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
      out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.add" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6b) {
      out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.sub" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x47) {
      out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.ne" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x45) {
      out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.eqz" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        addr,
        op,
        name: "call",
        imm: x.value,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0d) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        addr,
        op,
        name: "br_if",
        imm: x.value,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x24) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        addr,
        op,
        name: "global.set",
        imm: x.value,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        addr,
        op,
        name: "global.get",
        imm: x.value,
      });
      continue;
    }

    // i32 load/store variants and common simple instructions
    // that are not needed for exact destination resolution.
    out.push({
      addr,
      op,
      name: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
    });
  }

  return out;
}

const ins = decode(BODY_START, BODY_END);

console.log(`INSTRUCTIONS = ${ins.length}`);
console.log();

function showWindow(index: number, radius = 14) {
  const a = Math.max(0, index - radius);
  const b = Math.min(ins.length, index + radius + 1);

  for (let i = a; i < b; i++) {
    const x = ins[i];

    let extra = "";

    if (
      x.name === "local.get" ||
      x.name === "local.set" ||
      x.name === "local.tee"
    ) {
      extra = ` ${localName(x.imm!)}`;
    } else if (x.name === "i32.const") {
      extra = ` ${hex(x.imm!)}`;
    }

    console.log(
      `${hex(x.addr)} ${x.name}${extra}`
    );
  }
}

const stores = ins.filter(x => x.name.startsWith("i32.store"));

console.log(`I32.STORE COUNT = ${stores.length}`);
console.log();

for (const s of stores) {
  const index = ins.indexOf(s);

  console.log("-".repeat(60));
  console.log(`STORE @ ${hex(s.addr)}`);
  console.log(`OFFSET = ${hex(s.imm ?? 0)}`);

  showWindow(index, 12);

  console.log();

  // Look immediately backward for the final local.get
  // that supplies the store address.
  let addrLocal: number | undefined;

  for (let i = index - 1; i >= Math.max(0, index - 8); i--) {
    if (ins[i].name === "local.get") {
      addrLocal = ins[i].imm;
      break;
    }

    if (
      ins[i].name === "local.set" ||
      ins[i].name === "local.tee"
    ) {
      break;
    }
  }

  if (addrLocal === undefined) {
    console.log("ADDRESS LOCAL = NOT RESOLVED");
    continue;
  }

  console.log(`ADDRESS LOCAL = ${localName(addrLocal)}`);

  // Find all recent definitions before the store.
  const defs: Instr[] = [];

  for (let i = index - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (
      (ins[i].name === "local.set" ||
        ins[i].name === "local.tee") &&
      ins[i].imm === addrLocal
    ) {
      defs.push(ins[i]);

      if (defs.length >= 5) break;
    }
  }

  if (defs.length === 0) {
    console.log("LOCAL DEFINITION = NONE FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  for (const d of defs) {
    console.log(
      `LOCAL DEFINITION @ ${hex(d.addr)} = ${d.name}`
    );

    const di = ins.indexOf(d);

    for (
      let j = Math.max(0, di - 8);
      j < di;
      j++
    ) {
      const x = ins[j];

      let extra = "";

      if (
        x.name === "local.get" ||
        x.name === "local.set" ||
        x.name === "local.tee"
      ) {
        extra = ` ${localName(x.imm!)}`;
      } else if (x.name === "i32.const") {
        extra = ` ${hex(x.imm!)}`;
      }

      console.log(
        `  ${hex(x.addr)} ${x.name}${extra}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log();
}

console.log("=".repeat(60));
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("EXACT TARGET STORE = NOT ASSUMED");
console.log("ADDRESS PROVENANCE REQUIRES DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolveFunc32.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 32;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readString(pos) {
  const x = uleb(pos);
  const start = x.next;
  const end = start + x.value;

  return {
    value: b.toString("utf8", start, end),
    next: end
  };
}

let p = 8;
let importedFunctions = 0;

let importFound = null;
let codeSection = null;

while (p < b.length) {

  const id = b[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.next;

  const sectionStart = p;
  const sectionEnd = p + s.value;

  // IMPORT SECTION
  if (id === 2) {

    let q = sectionStart;

    const count = uleb(q);
    q = count.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

      const mod = readString(q);
      q = mod.next;

      const name = readString(q);
      q = name.next;

      const kind = b[q++];

      if (kind === 0) {

        const type = uleb(q);
        q = type.next;

        if (importedFunctions === TARGET) {

          importFound = {
            index: importedFunctions,
            module: mod.value,
            name: name.value,
            type: type.value
          };
        }

        importedFunctions++;

      } else if (kind === 1) {

        q++;

        const flags = uleb(q);
        q = flags.next;

        const min = uleb(q);
        q = min.next;

        if (flags.value & 1) {
          const max = uleb(q);
          q = max.next;
        }

      } else if (kind === 2) {

        const flags = uleb(q);
        q = flags.next;

        const min = uleb(q);
        q = min.next;

        if (flags.value & 1) {
          const max = uleb(q);
          q = max.next;
        }

      } else if (kind === 3) {

        q += 2;
      }
    }
  }

  // CODE SECTION
  if (id === 10) {
    codeSection = {
      start: sectionStart,
      end: sectionEnd
    };
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 32 RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTION COUNT : ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`TARGET FUNCTION         : ${TARGET}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (importFound) {

  console.log("");
  console.log("TARGET IS IMPORTED");
  console.log("----------------------------------------");
  console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${importFound.index}`);
  console.log(`MODULE         : ${importFound.module}`);
  console.log(`IMPORT NAME    : ${importFound.name}`);
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX     : ${importFound.type}`);

} else {

  console.log("");
  console.log("TARGET IS NOT IMPORTED");
  console.log("----------------------------------------");
  console.log(
    `DEFINED FUNCTION INDEX RANGE : ${importedFunctions} .. ${importedFunctions + 99999}`
  );

  if (!codeSection) {
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
  }

  let q = codeSection.start;

  const count = uleb(q);
  q = count.next;

  console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTION COUNT : ${count.value}`);

  const ordinal = TARGET - importedFunctions;

  console.log(`EXPECTED CODE ORDINAL  : ${ordinal}`);

  if (ordinal >= 0 && ordinal < count.value) {

    for (let i = 0; i <= ordinal; i++) {

      const size = uleb(q);
      q = size.next;

      const bodyStart = q;
      const bodyEnd = q + size.value;

      if (i === ordinal) {

        console.log("");
        console.log("TARGET FOUND IN CODE");
        console.log("----------------------------------------");
        console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${TARGET}`);
        console.log(`CODE ORDINAL   : ${i}`);
        console.log(
          `BODY           : 0x${bodyStart.toString(16)} .. 0x${bodyEnd.toString(16)}`
        );
        console.log(`SIZE           : ${size.value}`);

        console.log("");
        console.log("RAW BODY:");

        for (let x = bodyStart; x < bodyEnd; x += 16) {

          const e = Math.min(x + 16, bodyEnd);

          const bytes = [];

          for (let y = x; y < e; y++) {
            bytes.push(
              b[y].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
          }

          console.log(
            `0x${x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
          );
        }

        break;
      }

      q = bodyEnd;
    }

  } else {
    console.log("ERROR: expected code ordinal is outside CODE section.");
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\resolveFunc3315Indirect.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 0x001078a9;
const FUNC = 3315;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// READ WASM
// ------------------------------------------------------------
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ULEB128
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function readULEB(buf, pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB128 overflow");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// LOCATE CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const s = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    pos = s.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// LOCATE FUNC 3315 BODY
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
const functionCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + size;

    if (i === FUNC) {
        bodyStart = start;
        bodyEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (bodyStart < 0) {
    throw new Error(`FUNC ${FUNC} not found`);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// VERIFY TARGET
// ------------------------------------------------------------
if (TARGET < bodyStart || TARGET >= bodyEnd) {
    throw new Error(
        `TARGET 0x${TARGET.toString(16)} is outside FUNC ${FUNC}`
    );
}

// call_indirect is:
// 0x11 <typeidx:ULEB> <tableidx:ULEB>
//
// At 0x1078A9:
//   11 03 00
//
// therefore:
//   opcode  = 0x11
//   typeidx = 3
//   tableidx = 0
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 3315 CALL_INDIRECT RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

console.log(
    `FUNC BODY : 0x${bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. ` +
    `0x${bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);

console.log(
    `TARGET    : 0x${TARGET.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);

console.log("-".repeat(72));

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// RAW BYTES AROUND TARGET
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("RAW BYTES AROUND TARGET");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

const rawStart = Math.max(bodyStart, TARGET - 24);
const rawEnd = Math.min(bodyEnd, TARGET + 24);

for (let addr = rawStart; addr < rawEnd; addr += 16) {
    const end = Math.min(addr + 16, rawEnd);

    const bytes = [];

    for (let i = addr; i < end; i++) {
        bytes.push(
            wasm[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        );
    }

    console.log(
        `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// DECODE call_indirect IMMEDIATES
// ------------------------------------------------------------
const opcode = wasm[TARGET];

if (opcode !== 0x11) {
    throw new Error(
        `Expected call_indirect 0x11 at target, found 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
    );
}

const typeInfo = readULEB(wasm, TARGET + 1);
const tableInfo = readULEB(wasm, typeInfo.next);

console.log();
console.log("CALL_INDIRECT");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

console.log(
    `opcode      : 0x${opcode.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
);

console.log(
    `type index  : ${typeInfo.value}`
);

console.log(
    `table index : ${tableInfo.value}`
);

console.log(
    `instruction end : 0x${tableInfo.next.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// PRINT STACK OPERANDS IMMEDIATELY BEFORE call_indirect
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log();
console.log("IMMEDIATE CALL CONTEXT");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

const contextStart = Math.max(bodyStart, TARGET - 32);

for (let addr = contextStart; addr < TARGET; addr++) {
    console.log(
        `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
        `0x${wasm[addr].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND OTHER call_indirect USING SAME TYPE INDEX + TABLE INDEX
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log();
console.log("OTHER call_indirect INSTANCES IN FUNC 3315");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

for (let addr = bodyStart; addr < bodyEnd; addr++) {

    if (wasm[addr] !== 0x11) {
        continue;
    }

    try {
        const t = readULEB(wasm, addr + 1);
        const tb = readULEB(wasm, t.next);

        console.log(
            `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
            `type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`
        );

    } catch {
        // Ignore malformed/non-instruction byte matches.
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// GLOBAL SEARCH FOR SAME TYPE/TABLE PAIR
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log();
console.log("GLOBAL SEARCH: SAME call_indirect TYPE/TABLE");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

let matches = 0;

for (let addr = 0; addr < wasm.length - 3; addr++) {

    if (wasm[addr] !== 0x11) {
        continue;
    }

    try {
        const t = readULEB(wasm, addr + 1);
        const tb = readULEB(wasm, t.next);

        if (
            t.value === typeInfo.value &&
            tb.value === tableInfo.value
        ) {
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                `type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`
            );

            matches++;
        }

    } catch {
        // Ignore invalid byte patterns.
    }
}

console.log();
console.log(`GLOBAL MATCHES : ${matches}`);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FINAL
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolveFunc7151Call.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 7151;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function hex(v, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readULEB(buf, pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 7151 DIRECT CALL RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

let importedFunctions = 0;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND IMPORT COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 2) {

        let p = start;

        const c = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const count = c.value;
        p = c.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

            let r = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = r.next + r.value;

            r = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = r.next + r.value;

            const kind = wasm[p++];

            if (kind === 0) {

                importedFunctions++;

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;

            } else if (kind === 1) {

                p++;

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;

                if (wasm[p - 1] & 1) {
                    r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                    p = r.next;
                }

            } else if (kind === 2) {

                const flags = wasm[p++];

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                    p = r.next;
                }

            } else if (kind === 3) {

                p += 2;

            } else if (kind === 4) {

                p++;

                r = readULEB(wasm, p);
                p = r.next;
            }
        }
    }

    pos = end;
}

console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTION COUNT : ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND FUNC 7151 BODY
// ------------------------------------------------------------
pos = 8;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {

        let p = start;

        const c = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const bodyCount = c.value;
        p = c.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount; ordinal++) {

            const size = readULEB(wasm, p);
            const bodySize = size.value;
            p = size.next;

            const bodyStart = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

            const functionIndex =
                importedFunctions + ordinal;

            if (functionIndex === TARGET_FUNC) {

                console.log("TARGET BODY");
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                console.log(
                    `FUNCTION INDEX : ${functionIndex}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `CODE ORDINAL   : ${ordinal}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `BODY           : ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `SIZE           : ${bodySize}`
                );

                console.log();
                console.log("RAW CALL SCAN");
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                for (let q = bodyStart; q < bodyEnd; q++) {

                    const opcode = wasm[q];

                    if (opcode === 0x10) {

                        const target = readULEB(
                            wasm,
                            q + 1
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  CALL FUNC ${target.value} ` +
                            `(ULEB bytes: ${target.next - (q + 1)})`
                        );
                    }
                }

                console.log();
                console.log("DECODED BODY");
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                let q = bodyStart;

                while (q < bodyEnd) {

                    const opcode = wasm[q];

                    if (opcode === 0x00) {
                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  unreachable`
                        );
                        q++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (opcode === 0x20) {

                        const local = readULEB(
                            wasm,
                            q + 1
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  local.get ${local.value}`
                        );

                        q = local.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (opcode === 0x10) {

                        const target = readULEB(
                            wasm,
                            q + 1
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  call ${target.value}`
                        );

                        q = target.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (opcode === 0x0b) {

                        console.log(
                            `${hex(q)}  end`
                        );

                        q++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(q)}  opcode=0x${opcode.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
                    );

                    q++;
                }

                console.log();
                console.log("=".repeat(72));
                console.log("DONE");
                console.log("=".repeat(72));

                process.exit(0);
            }

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    pos = end;
}

console.log(
    `FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} NOT FOUND`
);

FILE: scripts\resolveFunc725Body.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 725;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, x;
  while (true) {
    x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v -= 2 ** shift;
  return { value: v | 0, next: pos };
}

// Find Code Section
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p];
  const s = uleb(p + 1);
  const payload = s.next;
  const end = payload + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = payload;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

let target = null;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const sz = uleb(q);
  const start = sz.next;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (i === TARGET) {
    target = {
      start,
      end: end - 1,
      size: sz.value
    };
    break;
  }

  q = end;
}

if (!target) throw new Error("FUNC725 not found");

console.log("FUNC 725 EXACT RAW + BASIC DECODE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY SIZE : ${target.size}`);
console.log(
  `BODY      : 0x${target.start.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} .. ` +
  `0x${target.end.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("RAW BODY");

for (let x = target.start; x <= target.end; x += 16) {
  const e = Math.min(x + 15, target.end);
  console.log(
    `0x${x.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ` +
    [...b.slice(x, e + 1)]
      .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0"))
      .join(" ")
  );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("I32.CONST / CALL / CALL_INDIRECT / MEMORY OPS");

let x = target.start;

while (x <= target.end) {
  const at = x;
  const op = b[x++];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(x);
    console.log(
      `I32.CONST @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `VALUE=${r.value} HEX=0x${(r.value >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
    );
    x = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(x);
    console.log(
      `CALL @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} FUNC=${r.value}`
    );
    x = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const r1 = uleb(x);
    const r2 = uleb(r1.next);
    console.log(
      `CALL_INDIRECT @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `TYPE=${r1.value} TABLE=${r2.value}`
    );
    x = r2.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x38 || op === 0x39 ||
    op === 0x3a
  ) {
    const a = uleb(x);
    const o = uleb(a.next);

    const names = {
      0x28: "I32.LOAD",
      0x29: "I64.LOAD",
      0x36: "I32.STORE",
      0x37: "I64.STORE",
      0x38: "F32.STORE",
      0x39: "F64.STORE",
      0x3a: "I32.STORE8"
    };

    console.log(
      `${names[op] || "MEMORY"} @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
    );

    x = o.next;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

function skipSection(pos) {
  const id = b[pos++];
  const s = uleb(pos);
  return {
    id,
    start: pos,
    end: pos = s.next + s.value
  };
}

console.log("RESOLVE FUNC 83 BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;
let code = null;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p];
  const sz = uleb(p + 1);
  const payload = sz.next;
  const end = payload + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = payload;
    code = { start: payload, end };
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!code) throw new Error("Code section not found");

let q = code.start;
const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

console.log(`CODE SECTION : 0x${code.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${code.end.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCS : ${count.value}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (83 >= count.value) {
  throw new Error("FUNC 83 is outside defined-function range");
}

let target = null;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const bodySize = uleb(q);
  const bodyStart = bodySize.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

  if (i === 83) {
    target = {
      func: i,
      bodySize: bodySize.value,
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd - 1
    };
    break;
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

if (!target) throw new Error("FUNC 83 body not resolved");

console.log(`FUNC       : ${target.func}`);
console.log(`BODY SIZE  : ${target.bodySize}`);
console.log(
  `BODY       : 0x${target.start.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} .. ` +
  `0x${target.end.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("RAW BODY");

for (let p = target.start; p <= target.end; p += 16) {
  const e = Math.min(p + 15, target.end);
  const hex = [...b.slice(p, e + 1)]
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");

  console.log(
    `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ${hex}`
  );
}

    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

  let v = 0, shift = 0, x;
  while (true) {
    x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v -= 2 ** shift;

}

    console.log(
      `I32.CONST @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `VALUE=${r.value} ` +
      `HEX=0x${(r.value >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
    );
    p = r.next;
  }
  else if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    console.log(
      `CALL @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} FUNC=${r.value}`
    );
    p = r.next;
  }
  else if (op === 0x11) {
    const a = uleb(p);
    const t = uleb(a.next);
    console.log(
      `CALL_INDIRECT @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `TYPE=${a.value} TABLE=${t.value}`
    );
    p = t.next;
  }
  else if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    console.log(
      `LOCAL.GET @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} L${r.value}`
    );
    p = r.next;
  }
  else if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    console.log(
      `LOCAL.TEE @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} L${r.value}`
    );
    p = r.next;
  }
  else if (op === 0x21) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    console.log(
      `LOCAL.SET @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} L${r.value}`
    );
    p = r.next;
  }
  else if (op === 0x0f) {
    console.log(`RETURN @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`);
  }
  else {
    console.log(
      `OPCODE @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 91;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readVecTypes(pos) {
  const n = uleb(pos);
  pos = n.next;
  const types = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    types.push(b[pos++]);
  }
  return { types, next: pos };
}

const typeNames = {
  0x7f: "i32",
  0x7e: "i64",
  0x7d: "f32",
  0x7c: "f64"
};

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TYPE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = 8;
let typeSection = null;
let functionSection = null;
let codeSection = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p];
  const s = uleb(p + 1);
  const payload = s.next;
  const end = payload + s.value;

  if (id === 1) typeSection = { start: payload, end };
  if (id === 3) functionSection = { start: payload, end };
  if (id === 10) {
    codeSection = { start: payload, end };
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!typeSection || !functionSection || !codeSection)
  throw new Error("Required sections not found");

// Parse types
let t = typeSection.start;
const typeCount = uleb(t);
t = typeCount.next;

const types = [];

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount.value; i++) {
  const form = b[t++];

  if (form !== 0x60)
    throw new Error(`Unexpected type form at ${i}: 0x${form.toString(16)}`);

  const params = readVecTypes(t);
  t = params.next;

  const results = readVecTypes(t);
  t = results.next;

  types.push({
    params: params.types,
    results: results.types
  });
}

// Parse function section: defined function -> type index
let f = functionSection.start;
const funcCount = uleb(f);
f = funcCount.next;

const funcTypes = [];

for (let i = 0; i < funcCount.value; i++) {
  const r = uleb(f);
  funcTypes.push(r.value);
  f = r.next;
}

const typeIndex = funcTypes[TARGET];
const sig = types[typeIndex];

console.log("FUNC 91 EXACT SIGNATURE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`FUNC       : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX : ${typeIndex}`);

console.log(
  `PARAMS     : (${sig.params.map(x => typeNames[x] || `0x${x.toString(16)}`).join(",")})`
);

console.log(
  `RESULTS    : (${sig.results.map(x => typeNames[x] || `0x${x.toString(16)}`).join(",")})`
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("PARAMETER → LOCAL MAPPING");

sig.params.forEach((x, i) => {
  console.log(
    `ARG${i} → L${i} (${typeNames[x] || `0x${x.toString(16)}`})`
  );
});

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// Resolve exact body
let q = codeSection.start;
const codeCount = uleb(q);
q = codeCount.next;

let body = null;

for (let i = 0; i < codeCount.value; i++) {
  const sz = uleb(q);
  const start = sz.next;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (i === TARGET) {
    body = { start, end: end - 1, size: sz.value };
    break;
  }

  q = end;
}

if (!body) throw new Error("FUNC91 body not found");

console.log(
  `BODY       : 0x${body.start.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} .. ` +
  `0x${body.end.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
);

console.log(`BODY SIZE  : ${body.size}`);

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 91;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(data: Buffer, pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = data[pos++];

        if (b === undefined) {
            throw new Error("Unexpected EOF while reading ULEB");
        }

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too large");
        }
    }
}

function valType(v: number): string {
    switch (v) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        case 0x7b: return "v128";
        case 0x70: return "funcref";
        case 0x6f: return "externref";
        default: return `0x${v.toString(16)}`;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RESOLVE FUNC 91 TYPE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log();

let p = 8;

let importFuncCount = 0;
let functionSectionStart = -1;
let functionSectionEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    if (sectionId === 2) {
        let q = start;

        const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
        const count = countInfo.value;
        q = countInfo.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const moduleLen = readULEB(wasm, q);
            q = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

            const nameLen = readULEB(wasm, q);
            q = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

            const kind = wasm[q++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                const typeInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
                q = typeInfo.next;
                importFuncCount++;
            } else if (kind === 1) {
                q++;

                const flags = wasm[q++];

                const minInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
                q = minInfo.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    const maxInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
                    q = maxInfo.next;
                }
            } else if (kind === 2) {
                const flags = wasm[q++];

                const minInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
                q = minInfo.next;

                if (flags & 1) {
                    const maxInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
                    q = maxInfo.next;
                }
            } else if (kind === 3) {
                q += 2;
            }
        }
    }

    if (sectionId === 3) {
        functionSectionStart = start;
        functionSectionEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (functionSectionStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("FUNCTION section not found");
}

console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${importFuncCount}`);
console.log(
    `FUNCTION SECTION = 0x${functionSectionStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. ` +
    `0x${functionSectionEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);
console.log();

let q = functionSectionStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
const definedCount = countInfo.value;
q = countInfo.next;

console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${definedCount}`);
console.log();

const definedOrdinal = TARGET_FUNC - importFuncCount;

console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`DEFINED ORDINAL    = ${definedOrdinal}`);
console.log();

if (definedOrdinal < 0 || definedOrdinal >= definedCount) {
    throw new Error("Target function is not a defined function");
}

let targetType = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {
    const typeInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
    const typeIndex = typeInfo.value;
    q = typeInfo.next;

    if (i === definedOrdinal) {
        targetType = typeIndex;
        break;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX = ${targetType}`);
console.log(`TYPE HEX   = 0x${targetType.toString(16)}`);
console.log();

// Resolve the actual type signature.

let tp = 8;
let typeSectionStart = -1;
let typeSectionEnd = -1;

while (tp < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[tp++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, tp);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    tp = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = tp;
    const end = tp + size;

    if (sectionId === 1) {
        typeSectionStart = start;
        typeSectionEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    tp = end;
}

if (typeSectionStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("TYPE section not found");
}

const typeCount = typeCountInfo.value;

    const paramInfo = readULEB(wasm, t);
    const paramCount = paramInfo.value;
    t = paramInfo.next;

    const params: string[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
        params.push(valType(wasm[t++]));
    }

    const resultInfo = readULEB(wasm, t);
    const resultCount = resultInfo.value;
    t = resultInfo.next;

    const results: string[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
        results.push(valType(wasm[t++]));
    }

    if (i === targetType) {
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`TYPE ${i}`);
        console.log(`FORM    = 0x${form.toString(16)}`);
        console.log(`PARAMS  = [ ${params.join(", ")} ]`);
        console.log(`RESULTS = [ ${results.join(", ")} ]`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        break;
    }
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_FUNC = 1020;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" RESOLVE FUNCTION 1020 BODY v28");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log("");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: p };

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("Invalid ULEB");
}

function readName(pos: number) {
    const n = readULEB(pos);
    const start = n.next;
    const end = start + n.value;

    return {
        value: Buffer.from(wasm.subarray(start, end)).toString("utf8"),
        next: end
    };
}

function hex(x: number) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function raw(pos: number, n: number) {
    return Array.from(
        wasm.subarray(pos, Math.min(pos + n, wasm.length))
    )
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

function sectionName(id: number) {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0: "custom",
        1: "type",
        2: "import",
        3: "function",
        4: "table",
        5: "memory",
        6: "global",
        7: "export",
        8: "start",
        9: "element",
        10: "code",
        11: "data",
        12: "data_count",
        13: "tag"
    };

    return names[id] ?? `section_${id}`;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    "magic:",
    Array.from(wasm.subarray(0, 4))
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
);

console.log(
    "version:",
    Array.from(wasm.subarray(4, 8))
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
);

console.log("");

    start: number;
    payloadStart: number;
    payloadEnd: number;

        throw new Error("Invalid section boundary");

    p = payloadEnd;
}

            const type = readULEB(ip);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolveFunctionByByteOffset.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const FILE = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_OFFSET = Number(process.argv[2] ?? 1660683);
const b = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    if (pos >= b.length)
      throw new Error("Unexpected EOF while reading ULEB");

    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 49)
      throw new Error("ULEB too large");
  }
}

function findSection(id) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const sectionId = b[p++];
    const size = uleb(p);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (end > b.length)
      throw new Error("Section exceeds file size");

    if (sectionId === id)
      return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

const importSection = findSection(2);
const codeSection = findSection(10);

if (!importSection || !codeSection)
  throw new Error("Import or code section not found");

// Count imported functions.
let p = importSection.start;
const imports = uleb(p);
p = imports.next;

let importedFunctions = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < imports.value; i++) {
  let item = uleb(p);
  p = item.next + item.value;

  item = uleb(p);
  p = item.next + item.value;

  const kind = b[p++];

  if (kind === 0) {
    p = uleb(p).next;
    importedFunctions++;
  } else if (kind === 1) {
    p++; // element type
    const flags = uleb(p);
    p = flags.next;
    p = uleb(p).next;
    if (flags.value & 1)
      p = uleb(p).next;
  } else if (kind === 2) {
    const flags = uleb(p);
    p = flags.next;
    p = uleb(p).next;
    if (flags.value & 1)
      p = uleb(p).next;
  } else if (kind === 3) {
    p += 2;
  } else if (kind === 4) {
    // Exception tag import: attribute + type index.
    p = uleb(p + 1).next;
  } else {
    throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
  }
}

// Walk the code-section body boundaries.
p = codeSection.start;
const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

let found = null;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
  const size = uleb(p);
  const start = size.next;
  const end = start + size.value;

  if (TARGET_OFFSET >= start && TARGET_OFFSET < end) {
    found = {
      globalFunction: importedFunctions + ordinal,
      ordinal,
      start,
      end,
      size: size.value,
      offsetWithinBody: TARGET_OFFSET - start
    };
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

console.log("FUNCTION BY BYTE OFFSET");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM SIZE          : ${b.length}`);
console.log(`TARGET OFFSET      : ${TARGET_OFFSET}`);
console.log(`TARGET HEX         : 0x${TARGET_OFFSET.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS : ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS  : ${count.value}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (!found) {
  console.log("NO FUNCTION BODY CONTAINS THIS OFFSET.");
} else {
  console.log(`GLOBAL FUNCTION    : ${found.globalFunction}`);
  console.log(`CODE ORDINAL       : ${found.ordinal}`);
  console.log(`BODY START         : 0x${found.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
  console.log(`BODY END EXCLUSIVE : 0x${found.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
  console.log(`BODY SIZE          : ${found.size}`);
  console.log(`OFFSET IN BODY     : ${found.offsetWithinBody}`);
}

console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\resolveGlobalFunctionBody.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = Number(process.argv[2] ?? 656);

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = 8;

let importSection = null;
let codeSection = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p];

  const s = uleb(p + 1);
  const payload = s.next;
  const end = payload + s.value;

  if (id === 2)
    importSection = { start: payload, end };

  if (id === 10) {
    codeSection = { start: payload, end };
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!importSection)
  throw new Error("Import section not found");

if (!codeSection)
  throw new Error("Code section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Count imported FUNCTION entries
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let q = importSection.start;

const importCount = uleb(q);
q = importCount.next;

let importedFunctions = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < importCount.value; i++) {

  const moduleLen = uleb(q);
  q = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

  const nameLen = uleb(q);
  q = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

  const kind = b[q++];

  if (kind === 0x00) {
    // function import
    uleb(q);
    q = uleb(q).next;
    importedFunctions++;
  }

  else if (kind === 0x01) {
    // table
    const elemType = b[q++];
    const flags = uleb(q);
    q = flags.next;

    const min = uleb(q);
    q = min.next;

    if (flags.value & 1) {
      const max = uleb(q);
      q = max.next;
    }
  }

  else if (kind === 0x02) {
    // memory
    const flags = uleb(q);
    q = flags.next;

    const min = uleb(q);
    q = min.next;

    if (flags.value & 1) {
      const max = uleb(q);
      q = max.next;
    }
  }

  else if (kind === 0x03) {
    // global
    q += 2;
  }

  else {
    throw new Error(`Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)}`);
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
q = codeSection.start;

const definedCount = uleb(q);
q = definedCount.next;

const definedOrdinal = TARGET - importedFunctions;

console.log("GLOBAL FUNCTION BODY RESOLVER");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL FUNC : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS : ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS  : ${definedCount.value}`);

if (TARGET < importedFunctions) {
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("TARGET IS AN IMPORTED FUNCTION");
  console.log("No Code Section body exists.");
  console.log("============================================================");
  process.exit(0);
}

if (definedOrdinal >= definedCount.value) {
  throw new Error("Target exceeds defined function range");
}

console.log(`DEFINED ORDINAL    : ${definedOrdinal}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Resolve exact body
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let body = null;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < definedCount.value; ordinal++) {

  const sz = uleb(q);

  const start = sz.next;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (ordinal === definedOrdinal) {
    body = {
      ordinal,
      start,
      end: end - 1,
      size: sz.value
    };
    break;
  }

  q = end;
}

if (!body)
  throw new Error("Body not found");

console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC        : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`CODE ORDINAL       : ${body.ordinal}`);
console.log(`BODY SIZE          : ${body.size}`);

console.log(
  `BODY               : 0x${body.start.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} .. ` +
  `0x${body.end.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("RAW BODY");

for (let x = body.start; x <= body.end; x += 16) {

  const e = Math.min(x + 15, body.end);

  console.log(
    `0x${x.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ` +
    [...b.slice(x, e + 1)]
      .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0"))
      .join(" ")
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\resolveGMinus300BasesV26.ts

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const targets = [0x95a47, 0x95d4f];

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { v: v >>> 0, p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (-1 << s);
  return { v: v | 0, p };
}

function decode(start: number, end: number) {
  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {
    const at = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      const n: Record<number,string> = {
        0x20:"local.get",
        0x21:"local.set",
        0x22:"local.tee",
        0x23:"global.get",
        0x24:"global.set"
      };

      console.log(`${hex(at)} ${n[op]} ${r.v}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.const ${r.v}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      const r = sleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.const ${r.v}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} call ${r.v}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.p;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.p;

      const type = op >= 0x36 ? "STORE" : "LOAD";

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} ${type} op=0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
        `align=${a.v} offset=${o.v}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b ||
        op === 0x71 || op === 0x72 ||
        op === 0x73 || op === 0x74 ||
        op === 0x75 || op === 0x76 ||
        op === 0x7e || op === 0x7f ||
        op === 0x80 || op === 0x81 ||
        op === 0x82 || op === 0x83 ||
        op === 0x84 || op === 0x85 ||
        op === 0x86 || op === 0x87 ||
        op === 0x88) {

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0b || op === 0x05 || op === 0x1a) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} control/drop op=0x${op.toString(16)}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} ${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${r.v}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      if (b[p] === 0x40) {
        p++;
        console.log(`${hex(at)} block/loop/if 0x40`);
      } else {
        const r = sleb(p);
        p = r.p;
        console.log(`${hex(at)} blocktype ${r.v}`);
      }
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0xfc) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} 0xfc subop=${r.v}`);

      if (r.v === 8 || r.v === 10) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.p;
        const c = uleb(p);
        p = c.p;
      }
      continue;
    }

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
    );
  }
}

for (const target of targets) {
  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`TARGET ${hex(target)}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  decode(target - 180, target + 25);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END V26");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolveIndexValue4154.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 114;

function uleb(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
  throw Error("ULEB");
}

function sleb(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  while (p < b.length) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 127) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 128)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 64)) v |= -1 << s;
  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function readName(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  const n = uleb(b, p);
  return n.next + n.value;
}

function sections(b: Buffer): any {
  let p = 8;
  const r: any = {};

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const s = uleb(b, p);
    p = s.next;
    r[id] = { start: p, size: s.value };
    p += s.value;
  }

  return r;
}

function importedFuncs(b: Buffer, sec: any) {
  let p = sec.start;
  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  let count = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    p = readName(b, p);
    p = readName(b, p);

    const kind = b[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      p = uleb(b, p).next;
      count++;
    } else if (kind === 1 || kind === 2) {
      const f = uleb(b, p);
      p = f.next;
      p = uleb(b, p).next;
      if (f.value & 1) p = uleb(b, p).next;
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return count;
}

function bodies(b: Buffer, sec: any, imported: number) {
  let p = sec.start;
  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  const out: any[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const s = uleb(b, p);
    p = s.next;

    out.push({
      func: imported + i,
      start: p,
      end: p + s.value,
      bytes: b.subarray(p, p + s.value)
    });

    p += s.value;
  }

  return out;
}

type I = {
  off: number;
  op: number;
  imm: number[];
  text: string;
};

function decode(b: Buffer, base: number): I[] {
  const a: I[] = [];
  let p = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const off = base + p;
    const op = b[p++];
    const imm: number[] = [];
    let text = `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`;

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text =
        op === 0x20 ? `local.get ${r.value}` :
        op === 0x21 ? `local.set ${r.value}` :
        `local.tee ${r.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = uleb(b, p);
      p = t.next;
      const tab = uleb(b, p);
      p = tab.next;
      imm.push(t.value, tab.value);
      text = `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `call ${r.value}`;
    }
    else if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
      const al = uleb(b, p);
      p = al.next;
      const of = uleb(b, p);
      p = of.next;
      imm.push(al.value, of.value);
      text = `load offset=${of.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x23) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x24) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x6a) text = "i32.add";
    else if (op === 0x6b) text = "i32.sub";
    else if (op === 0x6c) text = "i32.mul";
    else if (op === 0x1a) text = "drop";
    else if (op === 0x0b) text = "end";
    else if (op === 0x05) text = "else";
    else if (op === 0x0f) text = "return";
    else if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      p++;
      text = op === 2 ? "block" : op === 3 ? "loop" : "if";
    }
    else if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text = op === 0x0c ? `br ${r.value}` : `br_if ${r.value}`;
    }

    a.push({ off, op, imm, text });
  }

  return a;
}

function show(a: I[], i: number) {
  for (
    let j = Math.max(0, i - 10);
    j <= Math.min(a.length - 1, i + 4);
    j++
  ) {
    console.log(
      `${j === i ? " >>>" : "    "} ` +
      `0x${a[j].off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ${a[j].text}`
    );
  }
}

export async function run() {
  const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
  const s = sections(wasm);

  const imp = importedFuncs(wasm, s[2]);
  const bs = bodies(wasm, s[10], imp);

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(" RESOLVE INDEX VALUE -> 114");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length}`);
  console.log();

  let sites = 0;
  let exact = 0;
  let derived = 0;
  let unresolved = 0;

  for (const body of bs) {
    let a: I[];

    try {
      a = decode(body.bytes, body.start);
    } catch {
      continue;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
      const c = a[i];

      if (
        c.op !== 0x11 ||
        c.imm[0] !== 0 ||
        c.imm[1] !== 0
      ) continue;

      sites++;

      /*
       * Direct constant immediately before call.
       */
      if (
        i > 0 &&
        a[i - 1].op === 0x41 &&
        a[i - 1].imm[0] === TARGET
      ) {
        exact++;
        console.log(
          `EXACT CONST(114): func=${body.func} ` +
          `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
        );
        show(a, i);
        continue;
      }

      /*
       * Look for:
       *   local.get
       *   load
       *   call_indirect
       *
       * or:
       *   load
       *   load
       *   call_indirect
       */
      const p1 = i > 0 ? a[i - 1] : undefined;
      const p2 = i > 1 ? a[i - 2] : undefined;

      if (
        p1 &&
        p1.op >= 0x28 &&
        p1.op <= 0x3e
      ) {
        derived++;

        /*
         * Strong structural pattern:
         * previous instruction is another load.
         */
        if (
          p2 &&
          p2.op >= 0x28 &&
          p2.op <= 0x3e
        ) {
          console.log(
            `CHAIN: func=${body.func} ` +
            `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)} ` +
            `outerLoad=0x${p1.off.toString(16)} ` +
            `offset=${p1.imm[1]} ` +
            `innerLoad=0x${p2.off.toString(16)} ` +
            `offset=${p2.imm[1]}`
          );
        }

        continue;
      }

      unresolved++;
    }
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("SUMMARY");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`Type-0/table-0 sites : ${sites}`);
  console.log(`Exact CONST(114)     : ${exact}`);
  console.log(`Memory-derived       : ${derived}`);
  console.log(`Unresolved           : ${unresolved}`);
  console.log();
  console.log(
    "Next target: identify which memory field actually contains 114."
  );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolveIndirectIndex.ts
