import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGETS = [91, 119, 656];

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value: v, next: p };
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v: number) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];

    const sz = uleb(p);
    p = sz.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sz.value;

    if (sid === id) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${id} not found`);
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * TYPE SECTION
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

const typeSec = readSection(1);

let p = typeSec.start;

const typeCount = uleb(p);
p = typeCount.next;

const types: {
  params: number[];
  results: number[];
}[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount.value; i++) {
  const form = wasm[p++];

  if (form !== 0x60) {
    throw new Error(
      `Unexpected function type form at ${hex(p - 1)}: ${hex(form)}`
    );
  }

  const pc = uleb(p);
  p = pc.next;

  const params: number[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
    params.push(wasm[p++]);
  }

  const rc = uleb(p);
  p = rc.next;

  const results: number[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
    results.push(wasm[p++]);
  }

  types.push({ params, results });
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * FUNCTION SECTION
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

const funcSec = readSection(3);

p = funcSec.start;

const funcCount = uleb(p);
p = funcCount.next;

const funcTypes: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < funcCount.value; i++) {
  const x = uleb(p);
  funcTypes.push(x.value);
  p = x.next;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * CODE SECTION
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

const codeSec = readSection(10);

p = codeSec.start;

const codeCount = uleb(p);
p = codeCount.next;

const bodies = new Map<number, {
  start: number;
  end: number;
  type: number;
}>();

for (let i = 0; i < codeCount.value; i++) {
  const sz = uleb(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  const globalFunc = IMPORTS + i;

  bodies.set(globalFunc, {
    start,
    end,
    type: funcTypes[i]
  });

  p = end;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * VALUE REPRESENTATION
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

type Value = {
  kind: string;
  detail?: string;
};

function val(kind: string, detail?: string): Value {
  return { kind, detail };
}

function show(v: Value | undefined) {
  if (!v) return "UNKNOWN";
  return v.detail ? `${v.kind}(${v.detail})` : v.kind;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * TARGET FUNCTION
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

function analyze(func: number) {
  const body = bodies.get(func);

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}: body not found`);
    return;
  }

  const type = types[body.type];

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC = ${func}`);
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX  = ${body.type}`);
  console.log(`PARAMS      = ${type.params.length}`);
  console.log(`RESULTS     = ${type.results.length}`);
  console.log(`BODY        = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end - 1)}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  /*
   * Decode local declarations.
   */
  let p = body.start;

  const localDecls = uleb(p);
  p = localDecls.next;

  const localTypes: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {
    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const vt = wasm[p++];

    for (let j = 0; j < count.value; j++) {
      localTypes.push(vt);
    }
  }

  const localCount = type.params.length + localTypes.length;

  console.log(`TOTAL LOCALS = ${localCount}`);

  /*
   * Symbolic locals.
   */
  const locals: Value[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < type.params.length; i++) {
    locals.push(val("PARAM", `local${i}`));
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < localTypes.length; i++) {
    locals.push(val("LOCAL", `local${type.params.length + i}`));
  }

  const stack: Value[] = [];

  function push(v: Value) {
    stack.push(v);
  }

  function pop(): Value {
    if (stack.length === 0) {
      return val("UNKNOWN", "STACK_UNDERFLOW");
    }

    return stack.pop()!;
  }

  function dumpStack() {
    if (stack.length === 0) {
      console.log("    STACK = []");
      return;
    }

    console.log(
      "    STACK = [" +
      stack.map(show).join(", ") +
      "]"
    );
  }

  /*
   * Track structured IF depth.
   */
  let ifDepth = 0;

  while (p < body.end) {
    const opPos = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    switch (op) {

      case 0x41: { // i32.const
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        push(val(
          "I32_CONST",
          `${x.value} (${hex(x.value)})`
        ));

        break;
      }

      case 0x20: { // local.get
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        const v = locals[x.value] ??
          val("UNKNOWN", `local${x.value}`);

        push(v);

        break;
      }

      case 0x21: { // local.set
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        const v = pop();

        locals[x.value] = v;

        break;
      }

      case 0x22: { // local.tee
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        const v = pop();

        locals[x.value] = v;
        push(v);

        break;
      }

      case 0x28: { // i32.load
        const align = uleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = uleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        const address = pop();

        push(val(
          "I32_LOAD",
          `${show(address)} + ${hex(offset.value)}`
        ));

        break;
      }

      case 0x47: { // i32.ne
        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(val(
          "I32_NE",
          `${show(a)} != ${show(b)}`
        ));

        break;
      }

      case 0x04: { // if
        const cond = pop();

        ifDepth++;

        console.log();
        console.log(`IF @ ${hex(opPos)}`);
        console.log(`    CONDITION = ${show(cond)}`);
        dumpStack();

        break;
      }

      case 0x05: { // else
        console.log(`ELSE @ ${hex(opPos)}`);
        dumpStack();
        break;
      }

      case 0x0b: { // end
        if (ifDepth > 0) {
          ifDepth--;
        }

        break;
      }

      case 0x0f: { // return
        console.log(`RETURN @ ${hex(opPos)}`);
        dumpStack();
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: { // call_indirect
        const typeIndex = uleb(p);
        p = typeIndex.next;

        const tableIndex = uleb(p);
        p = tableIndex.next;

        const targetType = types[typeIndex.value];

        console.log();
        console.log("************************************************************");
        console.log(`CALL_INDIRECT @ ${hex(opPos)}`);
        console.log(`TYPE         = ${typeIndex.value}`);
        console.log(`TABLE        = ${tableIndex.value}`);
        console.log(`PARAM COUNT  = ${targetType.params.length}`);
        console.log(`RESULT COUNT = ${targetType.results.length}`);

        const argc = targetType.params.length;

        const args: Value[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < argc; i++) {
          args.unshift(pop());
        }

        const index = pop();

        console.log("ARGS:");

        args.forEach((a, i) => {
          console.log(`    ARG${i} = ${show(a)}`);
        });

        console.log(`INDEX       = ${show(index)}`);

        if (index.kind === "I32_CONST") {
          console.log("INDEX SOURCE = CONSTANT");
        } else if (index.kind === "PARAM") {
          console.log("INDEX SOURCE = FUNCTION PARAMETER");
        } else if (index.kind === "LOCAL") {
          console.log("INDEX SOURCE = LOCAL");
        } else if (index.kind === "I32_LOAD") {
          console.log("INDEX SOURCE = MEMORY LOAD");
        } else if (index.kind === "I32_NE") {
          console.log("INDEX SOURCE = COMPARISON");
        } else if (index.kind === "UNKNOWN") {
          console.log("INDEX SOURCE = UNKNOWN / STACK UNDERFLOW");
        } else {
          console.log(`INDEX SOURCE = ${index.kind}`);
        }

        dumpStack();

        /*
         * Symbolically push result(s).
         */
        for (let i = 0; i < targetType.results.length; i++) {
          push(val(
            "CALL_RESULT",
            `call_indirect(type=${typeIndex.value})`
          ));
        }

        console.log("************************************************************");
        break;
      }

      default: {
        /*
         * For this focused trace we don't execute unknown opcodes.
         * This is intentional: unknown instructions can affect stack.
         */
        break;
      }
    }

    /*
     * Focus output around the relevant memory constant.
     */
    if (
      op === 0x41 &&
      wasm[opPos + 1] === 0xd4 &&
      wasm[opPos + 2] === 0xa6 &&
      wasm[opPos + 3] === 0xe5 &&
      wasm[opPos + 4] === 0x00
    ) {
      console.log();
      console.log(
        `TARGET MEMORY ADDRESS @ ${hex(opPos)}`
      );
      dumpStack();
    }
  }
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * MAIN
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log("RESOLVE INDIRECT INDEX");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log();
console.log(`TYPE COUNT = ${types.length}`);

for (const f of TARGETS) {
  analyze(f);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\resolveIndirectTargetSources.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;
const IMPORTS = 83;

const TARGETS = new Map<number, string>([
  [2053, "Function 8952"],
  [2101, "Function 8927"],
  [2612, "Function 8637"],
]);

const SEG412_START = 0x0018744c;
const SEG412_END   = 0x0018ab8d;

const SEG413_START = 0x0018ab98;
const SEG413_END   = 0x0018f512;

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value, next: pos };
}

/*
 * Exact verified function-body parser.
 */
function parseFunctions() {
  const countInfo = readULEB(CODE_START);
  const count = countInfo.value;

  let p = countInfo.next;

  const funcs: Array<{
    body: number;
    global: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
  }> = [];

  for (let body = 0; body < count; body++) {
    const s = readULEB(p);

    const start = s.next;
    const end = start + s.value;

    funcs.push({
      body,
      global: IMPORTS + body,
      start,
      end
    });

    p = end;
  }

  if (p !== CODE_END) {
    throw new Error(
      `Function parser mismatch ${hex(p)} != ${hex(CODE_END)}`
    );
  }

  return funcs;
}

/*
 * Return instruction size for the opcodes relevant to our
 * backward stack reconstruction. Unknown opcodes are treated
 * conservatively as one byte.
 */
function instrSize(pos: number): number {
  const op = buf[pos];

  if (
    op === 0x00 || op === 0x01 ||
    op === 0x05 ||
    op === 0x0b || op === 0x0f ||
    op === 0x1a || op === 0x1b
  ) return 1;

  if (
    op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 || op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    return readULEB(pos + 1).next - pos;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    return readULEB(pos + 1).next - pos;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
    const b = readULEB(a.next);
    return b.next - pos;
  }

  if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2a || op === 0x2b ||
      op === 0x2c || op === 0x2d ||
      op === 0x2e || op === 0x2f ||
      op === 0x30 || op === 0x31 ||
      op === 0x32 || op === 0x33 ||
      op === 0x34 || op === 0x35 ||
      op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x38 || op === 0x39 ||
      op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b ||
      op === 0x3c || op === 0x3d ||
      op === 0x3e) {

    const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
    const b = readULEB(a.next);
    return b.next - pos;
  }

  if (op === 0x41 || op === 0x42) {
    return readSLEB(pos + 1).next - pos;
  }

  if (op === 0x43) return 5;
  if (op === 0x44) return 9;

  if (
    op === 0x02 ||
    op === 0x03 ||
    op === 0x04
  ) {
    return readSLEB(pos + 1).next - pos;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    return readULEB(pos + 1).next - pos;
  }

  return 1;
}

/*
 * Decode a function into instruction positions/opcodes.
 */
function decodeFunction(fn: {
  start: number;
  end: number;
}) {
  let p = fn.start;

  const locals = readULEB(p);
  p = locals.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next + 1;
  }

  const result: Array<{
    pos: number;
    op: number;
    size: number;
  }> = [];

  while (p < fn.end) {
    const size = instrSize(p);

    if (size <= 0 || p + size > fn.end) break;

    result.push({
      pos: p,
      op: buf[p],
      size
    });

    p += size;
  }

  return result;
}

function opname(pos: number): string {
  const op = buf[pos];

  if (op === 0x20) {
    return `local.get ${readULEB(pos + 1).value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x21) {
    return `local.set ${readULEB(pos + 1).value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    return `local.tee ${readULEB(pos + 1).value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x23) {
    return `global.get ${readULEB(pos + 1).value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x24) {
    return `global.set ${readULEB(pos + 1).value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
    const b = readULEB(a.next);
    return `i32.load offset=${b.value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x2d) {
    const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
    const b = readULEB(a.next);
    return `i32.load8_u offset=${b.value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    return `i32.const ${readSLEB(pos + 1).value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    return `call ${readULEB(pos + 1).value}`;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
    const b = readULEB(a.next);
    return `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;
  }

  return `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

const functions = parseFunctions();

const TARGET_BODY_SET = new Set(
  [...TARGETS.keys()].map(x => x - IMPORTS)
);

/*
 * We need only type=4 call_indirect.
 */
interface Hit {
  fn: typeof functions[number];
  pos: number;
  instructions: ReturnType<typeof decodeFunction>;
  indexSource: string;
  sourcePos: number | null;
}

const hits: Hit[] = [];

for (const fn of functions) {
  let ins: ReturnType<typeof decodeFunction>;

  try {
    ins = decodeFunction(fn);
  } catch {
    continue;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
    const cur = ins[i];

    if (cur.op !== 0x11) continue;

    const a = readULEB(cur.pos + 1);
    const b = readULEB(a.next);

    if (a.value !== 4) continue;

    /*
     * Look backwards up to 12 instructions for the index
     * producer. We specifically classify:
     *
     * local.get X
     * i32.load + offset
     *
     * i32.const
     * i32.load + offset
     *
     * nested load patterns.
     */
    const before = ins.slice(Math.max(0, i - 12), i);

    let source = "UNKNOWN";
    let sourcePos: number | null = null;

    for (let j = before.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
      const x = before[j];

      if (x.op === 0x28) {
        const a2 = readULEB(x.pos + 1);
        const o2 = readULEB(a2.next).value;

        source = `i32.load(offset=${o2})`;
        sourcePos = x.pos;

        /*
         * Check preceding instruction for obvious base.
         */
        if (j > 0) {
          const prev = before[j - 1];

          if (prev.op === 0x20) {
            source +=
              ` <- local.get ${readULEB(prev.pos + 1).value}`;
          } else if (prev.op === 0x41) {
            source +=
              ` <- i32.const ${readSLEB(prev.pos + 1).value}`;
          }
        }

        break;
      }

      if (x.op === 0x41) {
        source =
          `i32.const ${readSLEB(x.pos + 1).value}`;
        sourcePos = x.pos;
        break;
      }

      if (x.op === 0x20) {
        source =
          `local.get ${readULEB(x.pos + 1).value}`;
        sourcePos = x.pos;
        break;
      }
    }

    hits.push({
      fn,
      pos: cur.pos,
      instructions: ins,
      indexSource: source,
      sourcePos
    });
  }
}

    );

  printed++;

  if (printed >= 80) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("OUTPUT LIMITED TO FIRST 80 LOAD-BASED CALLS");
    break;
  }
}

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LIBRARY_HASH_HEX =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TESTNET_CONFIG_URL =
    'https://ton-blockchain.github.io/testnet-global.config.json';

function intToIP(ip: number): string {
    const n = ip >>> 0;

    return [
        (n >>> 24) & 255,
        (n >>> 16) & 255,
        (n >>> 8) & 255,
        n & 255,
    ].join('.');
}

async function getTestnetConfig(): Promise<any> {
    const response = await fetch(TESTNET_CONFIG_URL);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Cannot download Testnet config: HTTP ${response.status}`
        );
    }

    return await response.json();
}

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

async function waitForEngine(
    engine: LiteSingleEngine,
    timeoutMs: number
): Promise<void> {
    const started = Date.now();

    while (Date.now() - started < timeoutMs) {
        if (engine.isReady()) {
            return;
        }

        if (engine.isClosed()) {
            await sleep(250);
            continue;
        }

        await sleep(250);
    }

    throw new Error(
        `LiteSingleEngine did not become ready within ${timeoutMs} ms`
    );
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ON-CHAIN LIBRARY RESOLVER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    console.log(`Library hash: ${LIBRARY_HASH_HEX}`);

    const config = await getTestnetConfig();

    if (!config.liteservers || config.liteservers.length === 0) {
        throw new Error('No Testnet LiteServers found');
    }

    console.log(
        `Testnet LiteServers available: ${config.liteservers.length}`
    );

    const libraryHash = Buffer.from(LIBRARY_HASH_HEX, 'hex');

    let lastError: unknown = null;

    for (let i = 0; i < config.liteservers.length; i++) {
        const server = config.liteservers[i];

        const ip = intToIP(server.ip);
        const port = server.port;
        const publicKey = Buffer.from(server.id.key, 'base64');

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `Trying LiteServer ${i + 1}/${config.liteservers.length}: ${ip}:${port}`
        );

        let engine: LiteSingleEngine | null = null;

        try {
            engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: `tcp://${ip}:${port}`,
                publicKey,
                reconnectTimeout: 3000,
            });

            console.log(
                `Initial state: closed=${engine.isClosed()} ready=${engine.isReady()}`
            );

            await waitForEngine(engine, 15000);

            console.log(
                `Engine ready: closed=${engine.isClosed()} ready=${engine.isReady()}`
            );

            const client = new LiteClient({
                engine,
            });

            const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo({
                timeout: 10000,
            });

            console.log(
                `Masterchain seqno: ${master.last.seqno}`
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('Calling getLibraries()...');

            const result = await client.getLibraries(
                [libraryHash],
                {
                    timeout: 15000,
                }
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('getLibraries() returned');
            console.log(`Entries: ${result.result.length}`);

            if (result.result.length === 0) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('RESULT: LIBRARY NOT RETURNED');

                engine.close();
                return;
            }

            for (const entry of result.result) {
                const hashHex = Buffer.from(entry.hash).toString('hex');

                console.log('');
                console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
                console.log(`Entry hash: ${hashHex}`);
                console.log(
                    `Requested : ${LIBRARY_HASH_HEX.toLowerCase()}`
                );

                const data = Buffer.from(entry.data);

                console.log(`Library data bytes: ${data.length}`);

                console.log('');
                console.log('Library data hex (first 64 bytes):');
                console.log(
                    data.subarray(0, 64).toString('hex')
                );

                let cells: Cell[];

                try {
                    cells = Cell.fromBoc(data);
                } catch (e) {
                    console.log('');
                    console.log('Cell.fromBoc() failed.');
                    console.log(String(e));

                    engine.close();
                    return;
                }

                console.log('');
                console.log(`BOC root cells: ${cells.length}`);

                if (cells.length === 0) {
                    engine.close();
                    return;
                }

                const root = cells[0];

                console.log(`Root bits   : ${root.bits.length}`);
                console.log(`Root refs   : ${root.refs.length}`);
                console.log(`Root exotic : ${root.isExotic}`);

                const actualHash = root.hash().toString('hex');

                console.log('');
                console.log(`Root cell hash: ${actualHash}`);
                console.log(
                    `Expected hash : ${LIBRARY_HASH_HEX.toLowerCase()}`
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    `HASH MATCH: ${
                        actualHash.toLowerCase() ===
                        LIBRARY_HASH_HEX.toLowerCase()
                    }`
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('Library BOC:');
                console.log(data.toString('base64'));

                console.log('');
                console.log('RESULT: ON-CHAIN LIBRARY RESOLVED');

                engine.close();
                return;
            }

            engine.close();
            return;
        } catch (e) {
            lastError = e;

            console.log(`LiteServer failed: ${String(e)}`);

            if (engine) {
                try {
                    engine.close();
                } catch {
                    // ignore
                }
            }
        }
    }

    throw new Error(
        `All Testnet LiteServers failed. Last error: ${String(lastError)}`
    );
}

import { LiteSingleEngine, LiteClient } from 'ton-lite-client';

const LIBRARY_HASH_HEX =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF2';

const LIBRARY_HASH = Buffer.from(
    LIBRARY_HASH_HEX,
    'hex'
);

function separator() {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
}

function hex(value: Buffer): string {
    return value.toString('hex').toUpperCase();
}

async function main() {
    separator();
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ON-CHAIN LIBRARY RESOLVER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - TON LITESERVER');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION');

    console.log();
    console.log('HASH BYTES:');

    /*
     * We first inspect the installed ton-lite-client package
     * through its public LiteClient API.
     *
     * The exact LiteServer endpoint configuration is supplied
     * through LiteSingleEngine.
     *
     * For now, use the public TON testnet lite-server discovery
     * configuration exposed by the package.
     */

    console.log();
    console.log('Initializing TON Lite Client...');

    const client = new LiteClient({

        batchSize: 1,
    });

    console.log();

    ]);

    separator();

    console.log();

    console.log();

        console.log();

        console.log(
            'The requested hash was not resolved by this LiteServer.'
        );
        console.log();
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
        return;
    }

    for (const entry of result.result) {
        console.log();
        separator();
        console.log(' LIBRARY ENTRY');
        separator();

        console.log();
        console.log('ENTRY KIND:');
        console.log(entry.kind);

        console.log();

            hex(entry.hash) === LIBRARY_HASH_HEX
        );

        console.log();
        console.log('DATA BYTES:');
        console.log(entry.data.length);

        console.log();
        console.log('DATA HEX PREVIEW:');

        const preview = entry.data.subarray(
            0,
            Math.min(entry.data.length, 64)
        );

        console.log(hex(preview));

        console.log();
        console.log('Parsing returned Library data as BOC...');

        try {
            const cells = Cell.fromBoc(entry.data);

            console.log();
            console.log('ROOT CELL COUNT:');
            console.log(cells.length);

            if (cells.length > 0) {
                const libraryCell = cells[0];

                console.log();
                console.log('LIBRARY CELL:');
                console.log('bits:', libraryCell.bits.length);
                console.log('refs:', libraryCell.refs.length);
                console.log('exotic:', libraryCell.isExotic);

                console.log();
                console.log('LIBRARY CELL HASH:');
                console.log(
                    libraryCell
                        .hash()
                        .toString('hex')
                        .toUpperCase()
                );

                console.log();
                console.log('LIBRARY CELL BOC:');
                console.log(
                    libraryCell
                        .toBoc()
                        .toString('base64')
                );
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.log();
            console.log(
                'Returned data was not parsed as a normal BOC.'
            );

            console.log();
            console.log(error);
        }
    }

    console.log();
    separator();
    console.log(' COMPLETE');
    separator();

    console.log();
    console.log('No transaction was sent.');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    await main();
}

async function main() {

        throw new Error(

        );
    }

    if (libraryHash.length !== 32) {
        throw new Error(

        );

        );

        );

            Buffer.from(entry.data).toString('hex').toUpperCase()
        );

        console.log(
            'data base64 =',
            Buffer.from(entry.data).toString('base64')
        );

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x012200;
const END   = 0x01295b;
const TARGET = 0x0126b4;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function sleb(pos: number, bits: number) {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (
                shift + 7n < BigInt(bits) &&
                (b & 0x40)
            ) {
                value |=
                    (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }

    throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    const b = uleb(a.next);

    return {
        offset: b.value,
        next: b.next
    };
}

interface Ins {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    kind: string;
    local?: number;
    value?: bigint | number;
    offset?: number;
    call?: number;
}

function decode(pos: number): Ins {
    const start = pos;
    const op = buf[pos++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.get",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.set",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.tee",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = sleb(pos, 32);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "i32.const",
            value: Number(x.value)
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = sleb(pos, 64);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "i64.const",
            value: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `call ${x.value}`,
            kind: "call",
            call: x.value
        };
    }

    if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37
    ) {
        const m = memarg(pos);

        let name = "memory";

        if (op === 0x28)
            name = "i32.load";

        if (op === 0x29)
            name = "i64.load";

        if (op === 0x36)
            name = "i32.store";

        if (op === 0x37)
            name = "i64.store";

        return {
            start,
            end: m.next,
            op,
            text: `${name} offset=${m.offset}`,
            kind: name,
            offset: m.offset
        };
    }

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",

        0xad: "i64.extend32_s",
        0xac: "i64.extend_i32_u"
    };

    return {
        start,
        end: pos,
        op,
        text:
            names[op] ??
            `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        kind: "other"
    };
}

function H(n: number) {
    return "0x" +
        n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const ins: Ins[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const x = decode(p);
    ins.push(x);
    p = x.end;
}

if (p !== END) {
    throw new Error(
        `decode ended ${H(p)} expected ${H(END)}`
    );
}

console.log(
    "============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " FUNCTION 122 LOCAL2 RESOLUTION FOR CALL #26 v2"
);
console.log(
    " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Decoded   : ${ins.length}`);

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * 1. All writes to local2
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " ALL WRITES TO LOCAL 2"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

const local2Writes: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
    const x = ins[i];

    if (
        (x.kind === "local.set" ||
         x.kind === "local.tee") &&
        x.local === 2
    ) {
        local2Writes.push(i);

        console.log(
            `${H(x.start)}  ${x.text}`
        );

        const from = Math.max(0, i - 5);
        const to = Math.min(ins.length - 1, i + 3);

        for (let j = from; j <= to; j++) {
            console.log(
                `  ${H(ins[j].start)}  ${ins[j].text}`
            );
        }

        console.log();
    }
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * 2. Last write before CALL #26
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log(
    "============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " LAST LOCAL2 WRITE BEFORE CALL #26"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

let lastLocal2 = -1;

for (const i of local2Writes) {
    if (ins[i].start < TARGET)
        lastLocal2 = i;
}

if (lastLocal2 < 0) {
    console.log(
        "NO local2 write found before CALL #26."
    );
} else {
    console.log(
        `Last local2 write @ ${H(ins[lastLocal2].start)}`
    );

    const from = Math.max(0, lastLocal2 - 12);
    const to = Math.min(
        ins.length - 1,
        lastLocal2 + 2
    );

    for (let i = from; i <= to; i++) {
        console.log(

}

 * ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log(

    " EXACT CALL #26 NEIGHBORHOOD"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

let targetIndex =
    ins.findIndex(x => x.start === TARGET);

if (targetIndex < 0)
    throw new Error("CALL #26 not found");

for (
    let i = targetIndex - 12;
    i <= targetIndex + 8;
    i++
) {
    if (i < 0 || i >= ins.length)
        continue;

    console.log(

    );
}

/*

 * ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"

console.log(
    " DESTINATION PATTERN"
);

const a = ins[targetIndex - 3];
const b = ins[targetIndex - 2];
const c = ins[targetIndex - 1];

console.log(

);

if (
    a.kind === "local.get" &&
    a.local === 2 &&
    b.kind === "i32.const" &&
    Number(b.value) === -64 &&
    c.text === "i32.sub"

    console.log(
        "\nPROVEN:"
    );

    console.log(

/*

    const x = ins[i];

    if (

    ) {
        console.log(

        const to = Math.min(

        console.log();
    }

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolveMemoryBase4154.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 114;

function uleb(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 128))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }

  throw Error("ULEB");
}

function sleb(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let x = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    x = b[p++];

    v |= (x & 127) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 128))
      break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 64))
    v |= -1 << s;

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function skipName(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  const n = uleb(b, p);
  return n.next + n.value;
}

function sections(b: Buffer): any {
  let p = 8;
  const r: any = {};

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(b, p);
    p = s.next;

    r[id] = {
      start: p,
      size: s.value
    };

    p += s.value;
  }

  return r;
}

function importedFuncs(b: Buffer, sec: any) {
  let p = sec.start;

  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  let count = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    p = skipName(b, p);
    p = skipName(b, p);

    const kind = b[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      p = uleb(b, p).next;
      count++;
    }
    else if (kind === 1 || kind === 2) {
      const f = uleb(b, p);
      p = f.next;

      p = uleb(b, p).next;

      if (f.value & 1)
        p = uleb(b, p).next;
    }
    else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return count;
}

function getBodies(
  b: Buffer,
  sec: any,
  imported: number
) {
  let p = sec.start;

  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  const out: any[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const s = uleb(b, p);
    p = s.next;

    out.push({
      func: imported + i,
      start: p,
      end: p + s.value,
      bytes: b.subarray(p, p + s.value)
    });

    p += s.value;
  }

  return out;
}

type I = {
  off: number;
  op: number;
  imm: number[];
  text: string;
};

function decode(b: Buffer, base: number): I[] {
  const a: I[] = [];

  let p = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const off = base + p;
    const op = b[p++];

    const imm: number[] = [];
    let text = `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`;

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22
    ) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text =
        op === 0x20
          ? `local.get ${r.value}`
          : op === 0x21
          ? `local.set ${r.value}`
          : `local.tee ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x23 ||
      op === 0x24
    ) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text =
        op === 0x23
          ? `global.get ${r.value}`
          : `global.set ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text = `call ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = uleb(b, p);
      p = t.next;

      const tab = uleb(b, p);
      p = tab.next;

      imm.push(t.value, tab.value);

      text =
        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
    }

    else if (
      op >= 0x28 &&
      op <= 0x3e
    ) {
      const al = uleb(b, p);
      p = al.next;

      const of = uleb(b, p);
      p = of.next;

      imm.push(al.value, of.value);

      text = `load offset=${of.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x6a)
      text = "i32.add";

    else if (op === 0x6b)
      text = "i32.sub";

    else if (op === 0x6c)
      text = "i32.mul";

    else if (op === 0x1a)
      text = "drop";

    else if (op === 0x0f)
      text = "return";

    else if (op === 0x0b)
      text = "end";

    else if (op === 0x05)
      text = "else";

    else if (
      op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04
    ) {
      if (p < b.length)
        p++;

      text =
        op === 0x02
          ? "block"
          : op === 0x03
          ? "loop"
          : "if";
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d
    ) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text =
        op === 0x0c
          ? `br ${r.value}`
          : `br_if ${r.value}`;
    }

    a.push({
      off,
      op,
      imm,
      text
    });
  }

  return a;
}

function show(
  a: I[],
  i: number
) {
  console.log();

  for (
    let j = Math.max(0, i - 12);
    j <= Math.min(a.length - 1, i + 3);
    j++
  ) {
    console.log(
      `${j === i ? " >>>" : "    "} ` +
      `0x${a[j].off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
      a[j].text
    );
  }
}

export async function run() {
  const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
  const sec = sections(wasm);

  const imported = importedFuncs(
    wasm,
    sec[2]
  );

  const bodies = getBodies(
    wasm,
    sec[10],
    imported
  );

  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );
  console.log(
    " RESOLVE MEMORY BASE -> INDIRECT INDEX 114"
  );
  console.log(
    " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
  );
  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );

  console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length}`);
  console.log(`Target    : ${TARGET}`);
  console.log();

  let sites = 0;
  let memorySites = 0;
  let simpleBases = 0;
  let constants = 0;
  let interesting = 0;

  for (const body of bodies) {
    let a: I[];

    try {
      a = decode(
        body.bytes,
        body.start
      );
    }
    catch {
      continue;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
      const c = a[i];

      if (
        c.op !== 0x11 ||
        c.imm[0] !== 0 ||
        c.imm[1] !== 0
      )
        continue;

      sites++;

      if (i < 1)
        continue;

      const outer = a[i - 1];

      if (
        outer.op < 0x28 ||
        outer.op > 0x3e
      )
        continue;

      memorySites++;

      const inner =
        i >= 2 ? a[i - 2] : undefined;

      const base =
        i >= 3 ? a[i - 3] : undefined;

      /*
       * Pattern:
       *
       * BASE
       *   load +inner
       *   load +outer
       *   call_indirect
       */

      if (
        inner &&
        inner.op >= 0x28 &&
        inner.op <= 0x3e
      ) {
        if (
          base &&
          base.op === 0x20
        ) {
          simpleBases++;

          if (interesting < 80) {
            console.log(
              `BASE LOCAL: func=${body.func} ` +
              `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)} ` +
              `local=${base.imm[0]} ` +
              `inner=+${inner.imm[1]} ` +
              `outer=+${outer.imm[1]}`
            );

            interesting++;
          }
        }
        else if (
          base &&
          base.op === 0x41
        ) {
          constants++;

          /*
           * Direct static-memory candidate.
           */
          const addr =
            base.imm[0] +
            inner.imm[1];

          if (
            addr >= 0 &&
            addr + 4 <= wasm.length
          ) {
            const value =
              wasm.readUInt32LE(addr);

            if (value === TARGET) {
              console.log(
                `FOUND STATIC 114: ` +
                `func=${body.func} ` +
                `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)} ` +
                `addr=0x${addr.toString(16)}`
              );

              show(a, i);
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  console.log();

  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );
  console.log("SUMMARY");
  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );

  console.log(
    `Indirect sites      : ${sites}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Memory-derived      : ${memorySites}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Local-base chains   : ${simpleBases}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Constant-base chains: ${constants}`
  );

  console.log();

  console.log(
    "NEXT: resolve LOCAL-base chains backward."
  );
}

import { Address, Cell, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TARGET = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

const EXPECTED_HASH =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TONCENTER_TESTNET =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

function separator() {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
}

function bufferToHex(buffer: Buffer): string {
    return buffer.toString('hex').toUpperCase();
}

async function main() {
    separator();
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ON-CHAIN LIBRARY RESOLVER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    separator();

    console.log();
    console.log('TARGET:');
    console.log(TARGET.toString());

    console.log();
    console.log('EXPECTED LIBRARY HASH:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_HASH);

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: TONCENTER_TESTNET,
    });

    console.log();
    console.log('Fetching account state...');

    const state = await client.getContractState(TARGET);

    console.log();
    console.log('STATE:', state.state);
    console.log('BALANCE:', state.balance.toString());

    if (state.state !== 'active') {
        console.log();
        console.log('Account is not active.');
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
        return;
    }

    if (!state.code) {
        console.log();
        console.log('NO CODE BOC RETURNED.');
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
        return;
    }

    const codeBoc = state.code;

    console.log();
    console.log('CODE BOC BYTES:');
    console.log(codeBoc.length);

    let cells: Cell[];

    try {
        cells = Cell.fromBoc(codeBoc);
    } catch (error) {
        console.log();
        console.log('FAILED TO PARSE CODE BOC.');
        console.log(error);
        console.log();
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
        return;
    }

    if (cells.length === 0) {
        console.log();
        console.log('NO ROOT CELL.');
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
        return;
    }

    const code = cells[0];

    console.log();
    separator();
    console.log(' ON-CHAIN CODE CELL');
    separator();

    console.log('bits:', code.bits.length);
    console.log('refs:', code.refs.length);
    console.log('exotic:', code.isExotic);

    console.log();
    console.log('CELL HASH:');
    console.log(
        code.hash().toString('hex').toUpperCase()
    );

    /*
     * IMPORTANT:
     *
     * The installed TON version uses:
     *
     *     beginParse(allowExotic)
     *
     * where the argument is boolean.
     */

    let slice;

    try {
        slice = code.beginParse(true);
    } catch (error) {
        console.log();
        console.log('FAILED TO PARSE CODE CELL.');
        console.log(error);
        console.log();
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
        return;
    }

    console.log();
    console.log('REMAINING BITS:');
    console.log(slice.remainingBits);

    console.log();
    console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
    console.log(slice.remainingRefs);

    if (slice.remainingBits < 8) {
        console.log();
        console.log('Not enough bits for exotic type.');
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
        return;
    }

    const exoticType = slice.loadUint(8);

    console.log();
    separator();
    console.log(' EXOTIC CELL');
    separator();

    console.log(
        'type:',
        `0x${exoticType.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')}`
    );

    console.log(
        'decimal type:',
        exoticType
    );

    /*
     * TON Library Reference:
     *
     * 0x02
     * followed by 256-bit library hash
     */

    if (exoticType !== 2) {
        console.log();
        console.log(
            'RESULT: NOT A LIBRARY REFERENCE'
        );

        console.log();
        console.log(
            'Expected exotic type: 0x02'
        );

        console.log();
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
        return;
    }

    if (slice.remainingBits < 256) {
        console.log();
        console.log(
            'ERROR: Library Reference has fewer than 256 hash bits.'
        );

        console.log();
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
        return;
    }

    const libraryHash = slice.loadBuffer(32);

    const actualHash = bufferToHex(libraryHash);

    console.log();
    separator();
    console.log(' LIBRARY REFERENCE');
    separator();

    console.log();
    console.log('LIBRARY HASH FROM ON-CHAIN CODE:');
    console.log(actualHash);

    console.log();
    console.log('EXPECTED HASH:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_HASH);

    console.log();
    console.log(
        'HASH MATCH:',
        actualHash === EXPECTED_HASH
    );

    console.log();
    console.log('HASH LENGTH:');
    console.log(actualHash.length);

    console.log();
    console.log('REMAINING BITS AFTER HASH:');
    console.log(slice.remainingBits);

    console.log();
    console.log('REMAINING REFS AFTER HASH:');
    console.log(slice.remainingRefs);

    console.log();

    if (actualHash === EXPECTED_HASH) {
        separator();
        console.log(' RESULT: VERIFIED ON-CHAIN');
        separator();

        console.log();
        console.log(
            'The target account contains the expected'
        );

        console.log(
            '256-bit Library Reference hash.'
        );

        console.log();
        console.log(
            'Next step: resolve the Library itself.'
        );
    } else {
        separator();
        console.log(' RESULT: HASH MISMATCH');
        separator();

        console.log();
        console.log(
            'The on-chain Library Reference hash differs'
        );

        console.log(
            'from the previously observed hash.'
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log('No transaction was sent.');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    await main();
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_FILES = [
  0x004c58c4,
  0x004c593a,
  0x004c59b0
];

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = 8;
let dataStart = 0;
let dataEnd = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  let size;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  if (id === 11) {
    dataStart = p;
    dataEnd = p + size;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

let q = dataStart;
let segmentCount;
[segmentCount, q] = u(q);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RESOLVE SELECTOR DATA");
console.log("5220 / 5232 / 5235");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DATA SEGMENTS :", segmentCount);

for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount; seg++) {
  const segStart = q;

  let flags;
  [flags, q] = u(q);

  let memStart;

  if (flags === 0) {
    if (b[q++] !== 0x41) continue;

    [memStart, q] = u(q);

    if (b[q++] !== 0x0b) continue;
  } else if (flags === 2) {
    [memStart, q] = u(q);
  } else {
    console.log("SKIP SEG", seg, "FLAGS", flags);
    break;
  }

  let size;
  [size, q] = u(q);

  const payloadStart = q;
  const payloadEnd = q + size;

  for (const fileTarget of TARGET_FILES) {
    if (
      fileTarget >= payloadStart &&
      fileTarget + 4 <= payloadEnd
    ) {
      const runtime =
        memStart + (fileTarget - payloadStart);

      const value =
        b[fileTarget] |
        (b[fileTarget + 1] << 8) |
        (b[fileTarget + 2] << 16) |
        (b[fileTarget + 3] << 24);

      console.log("");
      console.log("--------------------------------------------");
      console.log("SEGMENT       :", seg);
      console.log("FLAGS         :", flags);
      console.log("FILE PAYLOAD  :", hex(payloadStart));
      console.log("FILE TARGET    :", hex(fileTarget));
      console.log("RUNTIME       :", hex(runtime));
      console.log("VALUE         :", value >>> 0);
    }
  }

  q = payloadEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\resolveSelectorPointerFamily.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const targets = [];
for (let x = 0x1462; x <= 0x1489; x++) targets.push(x);

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = 8;
let dataStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  let size;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  if (id === 11) {
    dataStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

let q = dataStart;
let count;
[count, q] = u(q);

const hits = new Map();

for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {
  let flags;
  [flags, q] = u(q);

  if (flags !== 0) break;

  if (b[q++] !== 0x41) break;

  let memStart;
  [memStart, q] = u(q);

  if (b[q++] !== 0x0b) break;

  let size;
  [size, q] = u(q);

  const start = q;
  const end = q + size;

  for (let x = start; x + 4 <= end; x++) {
    const value =
      b[x] |
      (b[x + 1] << 8) |
      (b[x + 2] << 16) |
      (b[x + 3] << 24);

    const v = value >>> 0;

    if (targets.includes(v)) {
      const arr = hits.get(v) || [];
      arr.push({
        seg,
        file: x,
        runtime: memStart + x - start
      });
      hits.set(v, arr);
    }
  }

  q = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR POINTER FAMILY");
console.log("0x1462 .. 0x1489");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const v of targets) {
  const arr = hits.get(v) || [];

  console.log("");
  console.log(hx(v), "DEC", v, "HITS", arr.length);

  for (const h of arr) {
    console.log(
      "  SEG", h.seg,
      "FILE", hx(h.file),
      "RUNTIME", hx(h.runtime)
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolveStaticIndex4154.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 114;
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

interface Segment {
    id: number;
    runtime: number;
    dataOffset: number;
    size: number;
    endRuntime: number;
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function hex(x: number): string {
    return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readMemoryI32(
    memory: Map<number, number>,
    addr: number
): number | null {
    const b0 = memory.get(addr);
    const b1 = memory.get(addr + 1);
    const b2 = memory.get(addr + 2);
    const b3 = memory.get(addr + 3);

    if (
        b0 === undefined ||
        b1 === undefined ||
        b2 === undefined ||
        b3 === undefined
    )
        return null;

    return (
        b0 |
        (b1 << 8) |
        (b2 << 16) |
        (b3 << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function parseDataSegments(): {
    segments: Segment[];
    memory: Map<number, number>;
} {
    let p = 8;

    const memory = new Map<number, number>();
    const segments: Segment[] = [];

    let segmentId = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sectionId = wasm[p++];

        const s = readULEB(p);
        const sectionSize = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        const payloadStart = p;
        const payloadEnd = p + sectionSize;

        if (sectionId !== 11) {
            p = payloadEnd;
            continue;
        }

        const c = readULEB(p);
        const count = c.value;
        p = c.next;

        console.log("");
        console.log("Data section found");
        console.log("Segments :", count);
        console.log("");

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const flags = readULEB(p);
            p = flags.next;

            let runtimeAddress = 0;

            /*
             * flags 0:
             *   active, memory 0, offset expression
             *
             * flags 1:
             *   passive
             *
             * flags 2:
             *   active, explicit memory index
             */
            if (flags.value === 0) {
                const op = wasm[p++];

                if (op !== 0x41) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Unexpected data offset opcode 0x${op.toString(16)} at ${hex(p - 1)}`
                    );
                }

                const x = readSLEB(p);
                runtimeAddress = x.value >>> 0;
                p = x.next;

                const end = wasm[p++];

                if (end !== 0x0b) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Unexpected data offset end 0x${end.toString(16)}`
                    );
                }
            }
            else if (flags.value === 1) {
                // passive segment
            }
            else if (flags.value === 2) {
                const memidx = readULEB(p);
                p = memidx.next;

                const op = wasm[p++];

                if (op !== 0x41) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Unexpected active-memory offset opcode`
                    );
                }

                const x = readSLEB(p);
                runtimeAddress = x.value >>> 0;
                p = x.next;

                const end = wasm[p++];

                if (end !== 0x0b) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Unexpected active-memory offset end`
                    );
                }
            }
            else {
                console.log(
                    `WARNING: unsupported data flags ${flags.value} segment ${i}`
                );

                /*
                 * Best effort:
                 * skip conservatively is unsafe, so stop.
                 */
                throw new Error(
                    `Unsupported data segment flags=${flags.value}`
                );
            }

            const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
            const size = sizeInfo.value;
            p = sizeInfo.next;

            const dataOffset = p;

            if (flags.value !== 1) {
                for (let j = 0; j < size; j++) {
                    memory.set(
                        runtimeAddress + j,
                        wasm[dataOffset + j]
                    );
                }
            }

            segments.push({
                id: i,
                runtime: runtimeAddress,
                dataOffset,
                size,
                endRuntime: runtimeAddress + size
            });

            segmentId++;

            p += size;
        }

        break;
    }

    return {
        segments,
        memory
    };
}

function findSegment(
    segments: Segment[],
    addr: number
): Segment | undefined {
    return segments.find(
        s =>
            addr >= s.runtime &&
            addr + 3 < s.endRuntime
    );
}

function dumpNearby(
    memory: Map<number, number>,
    addr: number,
    radius = 16
) {
    const start = addr - radius;
    const end = addr + radius + 4;

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 4) {
        const v = readMemoryI32(memory, p);

        if (v === null)
            continue;

        console.log(
            `    ${hex(p)} : ${hex(v)} (${v})`
        );
    }
}

function checkCandidate(
    memory: Map<number, number>,
    segments: Segment[],
    base: number,
    loadOffset: number,
    label: string
) {
    const addr = (base + loadOffset) >>> 0;
    const value = readMemoryI32(memory, addr);
    const seg = findSegment(segments, addr);

    console.log("");
    console.log(`CANDIDATE: ${label}`);
    console.log(`  base       = ${hex(base)} (${base})`);
    console.log(`  loadOffset = ${loadOffset}`);
    console.log(`  address    = ${hex(addr)} (${addr})`);

    if (!seg) {
        console.log("  segment    = NOT INITIALIZED");
        console.log("  value      = UNKNOWN");
        return;
    }

    console.log(`  segment    = ${seg.id}`);
    console.log(
        `  segOffset  = ${hex(addr - seg.runtime)}`
    );

    if (value === null) {
        console.log("  value      = UNKNOWN");
        return;
    }

    console.log(
        `  value      = ${hex(value)} (${value})`
    );

    if (value === TARGET) {
        console.log(
            "  >>> MATCH: VALUE == TABLE SLOT 114 <<<"
        );
    }

    console.log("  nearby:");
    dumpNearby(memory, addr);
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" STATIC INDEX RESOLUTION FOR FUNCTION 4154");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");

    console.log("WASM size :", wasm.length);
    console.log("Target function :", 4154);
    console.log("Target table slot :", TARGET);
    console.log("Target slot hex :", hex(TARGET));
    console.log("");

    const { segments, memory } = parseDataSegments();

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" DATA MEMORY");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");

    console.log("Active segments :", segments.length);
    console.log("Initialized bytes :", memory.size);
    console.log("");

    /*
     * First: scan ALL initialized memory for exact i32 value 114.
     */
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" GLOBAL SCAN FOR i32 == 114");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");

    let exactCount = 0;
    const matches: number[] = [];

    for (const [addr, byte] of memory) {
        if (byte !== 0x72)
            continue;

        const v = readMemoryI32(memory, addr);

        if (v !== TARGET)
            continue;

        exactCount++;
        matches.push(addr);

        if (exactCount <= 100) {
            const seg = findSegment(segments, addr);

            console.log(
                `MATCH #${exactCount} @ ${hex(addr)}`
            );

            if (seg) {
                console.log(
                    `  segment=${seg.id} segOffset=${hex(addr - seg.runtime)}`
                );
            }

            console.log("  nearby:");
            dumpNearby(memory, addr, 8);
            console.log("");
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("Exact i32(114) matches :", exactCount);
    console.log("");

    /*
     * Candidate static addresses obtained from the previous
     * deep producer analysis.
     */
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" KNOWN STATIC CANDIDATES");
    console.log("============================================================");

    checkCandidate(
        memory,
        segments,
        2428436,
        0,
        "func 1643 static base"
    );

    checkCandidate(
        memory,
        segments,
        2428356,
        0,
        "func 1647 static base"
    );

    checkCandidate(
        memory,
        segments,
        2428420,
        0,
        "func 2247 static base"
    );

    checkCandidate(
        memory,
        segments,
        1837608,
        0,
        "func 3658 static base"
    );

    checkCandidate(
        memory,
        segments,
        2447980,
        0,
        "func 3850 static base"
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" INTERESTING MATCHES");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");

    let interesting = 0;

    for (const addr of matches) {
        const seg = findSegment(segments, addr);

        if (!seg)
            continue;

        /*
         * Print only matches located near the known static regions.
         */
        const nearKnown =
            Math.abs(addr - 2428436) < 512 ||
            Math.abs(addr - 2428356) < 512 ||
            Math.abs(addr - 2428420) < 512 ||
            Math.abs(addr - 1837608) < 512 ||
            Math.abs(addr - 2447980) < 512;

        if (!nearKnown)
            continue;

        interesting++;

        console.log(
            `MATCH NEAR CANDIDATE @ ${hex(addr)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  segment=${seg.id}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  segment offset=${hex(addr - seg.runtime)}`
        );

        dumpNearby(memory, addr, 16);

        console.log("");
    }

    console.log(
        "Interesting nearby matches :",
        interesting
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" END");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGETS = [4588, 4589];

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let element = null;

while (p < b.length) {

  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  const size = z.value;
  p = z.next;

  if (id === 9) {
    element = {
      start: p,
      end: p + size
    };
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

if (!element) {
  console.log("ELEMENT SECTION NOT FOUND");
  process.exit(1);
}

p = element.start;

const segCount = uleb(p);
p = segCount.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TABLE 0 RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`ELEMENT SEGMENTS : ${segCount.value}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let seg = 0; seg < segCount.value; seg++) {

  const flags = uleb(p);
  p = flags.next;

  console.log(`SEGMENT ${seg} FLAGS : ${flags.value}`);

  if (flags.value === 0) {

    const opcode = b[p++];

    if (opcode !== 0x41) {
      console.log("Unexpected offset expression");
      process.exit(1);
    }

    const off = uleb(p);
    p = off.next;

    if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) {
      console.log("Invalid offset expression");
      process.exit(1);
    }

    const tableIndex = 0;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    console.log(`TABLE            : ${tableIndex}`);
    console.log(`BASE             : ${off.value}`);
    console.log(`ELEMENT COUNT    : ${count.value}`);

    const values = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      values.push(x.value);
    }

    for (const target of TARGETS) {

      const idx = target - off.value;

      if (idx < 0 || idx >= values.length) {
        console.log(
          `table[${target}] : OUT OF RANGE`
        );
        continue;
      }

      const func = values[idx];

      console.log(
        `table[${target}] : FUNC ${func}`
      );
    }
  }

  else {
    console.log(
      `Unsupported element flags: ${flags.value}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\resolveTable4601.mjs
