import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_TABLE = 0;
const TARGET_INDEX = 4601;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// READ WASM
// ------------------------------------------------------------
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function hex(v, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ULEB128
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function readULEB(buf, pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB128 overflow");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SLEB128
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function readSLEB(buf, pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let byte;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        byte = buf[p++];

        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
                value |= (~0 << shift);
            }

            return {
                value: value | 0,
                next: p
            };
        }
    }

    throw new Error("SLEB128 overflow");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SECTION NAME
// ------------------------------------------------------------
const sectionNames = {
    0: "CUSTOM",
    1: "TYPE",
    2: "IMPORT",
    3: "FUNCTION",
    4: "TABLE",
    5: "MEMORY",
    6: "GLOBAL",
    7: "EXPORT",
    8: "START",
    9: "ELEMENT",
    10: "CODE",
    11: "DATA",
    12: "DATACOUNT",
    13: "TAG"
};

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// WASM HEADER
// ------------------------------------------------------------
if (
    wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
    wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
    wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
    wasm[3] !== 0x6d
) {
    throw new Error("Invalid WASM header");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("WASM TABLE[4601] RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

console.log(`TARGET TABLE : ${TARGET_TABLE}`);
console.log(`TARGET INDEX : ${TARGET_INDEX}`);

console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SCAN SECTIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;

const sections = [];

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionOffset = pos;

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

    sections.push({
        id: sectionId,
        name: sectionNames[sectionId] || `UNKNOWN_${sectionId}`,
        start: payloadStart,
        end: payloadEnd,
        size: sectionSize
    });

    pos = payloadEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// PRINT ELEMENT SECTIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------
const elementSections = sections.filter(
    s => s.id === 9
);

console.log("ELEMENT SECTIONS");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

if (elementSections.length === 0) {
    console.log("NO ELEMENT SECTION FOUND");
}

for (const s of elementSections) {

    console.log(
        `${s.name}  ` +
        `${hex(s.start)} .. ${hex(s.end)}  ` +
        `SIZE=${s.size}`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ELEMENT EXPRESSION DECODER
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function skipInitExpr(buf, pos) {

    while (pos < buf.length) {

        const opcode = buf[pos++];

        // i32.const
        if (opcode === 0x41) {
            const r = readSLEB(buf, pos);
            pos = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // i64.const
        if (opcode === 0x42) {
            const r = readSLEB(buf, pos);
            pos = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // f32.const
        if (opcode === 0x43) {
            pos += 4;
            continue;
        }

        // f64.const
        if (opcode === 0x44) {
            pos += 8;
            continue;
        }

        // global.get
        if (opcode === 0x23) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, pos);
            pos = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // ref.null
        if (opcode === 0xd0) {
            pos += 1;
            continue;
        }

        // ref.func
        if (opcode === 0xd2) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, pos);
            pos = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // end
        if (opcode === 0x0b) {
            return pos;
        }

        // Unknown opcode
        throw new Error(
            `Unknown init-expr opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)} at ${hex(pos - 1)}`
        );
    }

    throw new Error("Init expression did not terminate");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// PARSE ELEMENT SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let found = false;

for (const section of elementSections) {

    console.log();
    console.log("=".repeat(72));
    console.log(`PARSING ELEMENT SECTION @ ${hex(section.start)}`);
    console.log("=".repeat(72));

    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const segmentCount = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    console.log(`SEGMENT COUNT : ${segmentCount}`);

    for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount; seg++) {

        const segmentStart = p;

        const flagInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const flags = flagInfo.value;
        p = flagInfo.next;

        console.log();
        console.log(`SEGMENT #${seg}`);
        console.log(`FLAGS : ${flags}`);

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // MVP active element segment:
        //
        // flags 0:
        //   offset expr
        //   vec(funcidx)
        //
        // flags 1:
        //   elemkind
        //   vec(funcidx)
        //
        // flags 2:
        //   tableidx
        //   offset expr
        //   elemkind
        //   vec(funcidx)
        //
        // flags 3:
        //   elemkind
        //   vec(funcidx)
        //
        // newer reference forms:
        //   flags 4,5,6,7
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        let tableIndex = 0;
        let segmentBase = null;
        let elementKind = null;
        let functionIndices = [];

        if (flags === 0) {

            tableIndex = 0;

            const offsetStart = p;
            p = skipInitExpr(wasm, p);

            // vector of function indices
            const vecInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
            const count = vecInfo.value;
            p = vecInfo.next;

            segmentBase = "constant-expression";

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const f = readULEB(wasm, p);
                functionIndices.push(f.value);
                p = f.next;
            }

            const segEnd = p;

            if (
                TARGET_TABLE === tableIndex &&
                TARGET_INDEX >= 0 &&
                TARGET_INDEX < count
            ) {

                console.log();
                console.log("!!! POSSIBLE TARGET SEGMENT !!!");
                console.log(`SEGMENT START : ${hex(segmentStart)}`);
                console.log(`SEGMENT END   : ${hex(segEnd)}`);
                console.log(`TABLE         : ${tableIndex}`);
                console.log(`ELEMENT COUNT : ${count}`);

                // For flag 0 the exact base may require evaluating
                // the init expression. We handle simple i32.const.
                let q = offsetStart;

                if (wasm[q] === 0x41) {

                    const off = readSLEB(wasm, q + 1);

                    console.log(`BASE OFFSET   : ${off.value}`);

                    const absoluteIndex =
                        off.value + TARGET_INDEX;

                    if (
                        absoluteIndex >= 0 &&
                        absoluteIndex < functionIndices.length
                    ) {
                        console.log(
                            `table[${TARGET_INDEX}] = ` +
                            `FUNC ${functionIndices[absoluteIndex]}`
                        );
                        found = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (flags === 1) {

            elementKind = wasm[p++];

            const vecInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
            const count = vecInfo.value;
            p = vecInfo.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const f = readULEB(wasm, p);
                functionIndices.push(f.value);
                p = f.next;
            }

            console.log(
                `ELEMENT KIND : 0x${elementKind.toString(16)}`
            );

            console.log(`ELEMENT COUNT : ${count}`);

            continue;
        }

        if (flags === 2) {

            const tableInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
            tableIndex = tableInfo.value;
            p = tableInfo.next;

            const offsetStart = p;
            p = skipInitExpr(wasm, p);

            elementKind = wasm[p++];

            const vecInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
            const count = vecInfo.value;
            p = vecInfo.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const f = readULEB(wasm, p);
                functionIndices.push(f.value);
                p = f.next;
            }

            console.log(`TABLE         : ${tableIndex}`);
            console.log(`ELEMENT COUNT : ${count}`);

            if (TARGET_TABLE === tableIndex) {

                let q = offsetStart;

                if (wasm[q] === 0x41) {

                    const off = readSLEB(wasm, q + 1);

                    console.log(`BASE OFFSET   : ${off.value}`);

                    const relative =
                        TARGET_INDEX - off.value;

                    if (
                        relative >= 0 &&
                        relative < functionIndices.length
                    ) {

                        const func =
                            functionIndices[relative];

                        console.log();
                        console.log(
                            `FOUND: table[${TARGET_INDEX}] = FUNC ${func}`
                        );

                        console.log(
                            `SEGMENT ELEMENT POSITION : ${relative}`
                        );

                        found = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (flags === 3) {

            elementKind = wasm[p++];

            const vecInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
            const count = vecInfo.value;
            p = vecInfo.next;

            console.log(
                `ELEMENT KIND : 0x${elementKind.toString(16)}`
            );

            console.log(`ELEMENT COUNT : ${count}`);

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const f = readULEB(wasm, p);
                functionIndices.push(f.value);
                p = f.next;
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Flags 4-7: reference-type element segments
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (flags === 4) {

            const offsetStart = p;
            p = skipInitExpr(wasm, p);

            const vecInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
            const count = vecInfo.value;
            p = vecInfo.next;

            const refs = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const refOpcode = wasm[p++];

                if (refOpcode === 0xd2) {
                    const f = readULEB(wasm, p);
                    refs.push(f.value);
                    p = f.next;
                } else {
                    refs.push(null);
                }

                p = skipInitExpr(wasm, p - 1);
            }

            if (TARGET_TABLE === 0) {
                let q = offsetStart;

                if (wasm[q] === 0x41) {

                    const off = readSLEB(wasm, q + 1);

                    const relative =
                        TARGET_INDEX - off.value;

                    if (
                        relative >= 0 &&
                        relative < refs.length
                    ) {
                        console.log(
                            `FOUND REF TABLE[${TARGET_INDEX}] = ` +
                            `${refs[relative]}`
                        );
                        found = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            `UNHANDLED ELEMENT FLAGS: ${flags}`
        );

        break;
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FINAL STATUS
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));

if (!found) {
    console.log(
        `TARGET table[${TARGET_INDEX}] NOT RESOLVED`
    );
    console.log(
        "Further table/import/global analysis is required."
    );
} else {
    console.log(
        `TARGET table[${TARGET_INDEX}] RESOLVED`
    );
}

console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 4788;

function uleb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v, n = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("RESOLVE TABLE[4788]");

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const si = uleb(p);
    p = si.next;

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = p + si.value;

    if (sectionId !== 9) {
        p = sectionEnd;
        continue;
    }

    console.log(`ELEMENT SECTION : ${hex(sectionStart)} .. ${hex(sectionEnd)}`);
    console.log();

    const ci = uleb(p);
    const count = ci.value;
    p = ci.next;

    console.log(`SEGMENTS : ${count}`);
    console.log();

    for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {

        const fi = uleb(p);
        const flags = fi.value;
        p = fi.next;

        console.log(`SEGMENT ${seg} FLAGS=${flags}`);

        if (flags !== 0) {
            console.log(`Unsupported flags ${flags}`);
            process.exit(1);
        }

        const opcode = wasm[p++];

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            console.log(
                `Unsupported offset opcode ${hex(opcode, 2)}`
            );
            process.exit(1);
        }

        const oi = uleb(p);
        const base = oi.value;
        p = oi.next;

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b) {
            console.log("Invalid offset expression");
            process.exit(1);
        }

        const vi = uleb(p);
        const countFuncs = vi.value;
        p = vi.next;

        console.log(`BASE : ${base}`);
        console.log(`COUNT: ${countFuncs}`);

        const targetInSegment =
            TARGET >= base &&
            TARGET < base + countFuncs;

        let targetFunc = null;

        for (let i = 0; i < countFuncs; i++) {

            const fi2 = uleb(p);

            if (targetInSegment && i === TARGET - base) {
                targetFunc = fi2.value;
            }

            p = fi2.next;
        }

        if (targetFunc !== null) {

            console.log();
            console.log("!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!");
            console.log("TARGET FOUND");
            console.log("!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!");
            console.log();
            console.log(`TABLE INDEX : ${TARGET}`);
            console.log(`TABLE OFFSET: ${TARGET - base}`);
            console.log(`FUNC INDEX  : ${targetFunc}`);
            console.log(`FUNC HEX    : ${hex(targetFunc)}`);
            console.log();

            console.log("CALL CHAIN:");
            console.log("FUNC 122");
            console.log("   ↓");
            console.log("MEM[0x19534C]");
            console.log("   ↓");
            console.log("4788 / 0x12B4");
            console.log("   ↓");
            console.log("TABLE[4788]");
            console.log("   ↓");
            console.log(`FUNC ${targetFunc}`);
            console.log();

            process.exit(0);
        }
    }

    break;
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos:number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;
  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TABLE / ELEMENT RESOLUTION 4788..4791");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.next;

  const secStart = p;
  const secEnd = p + s.value;

  if (id === 4) {
    console.log(`TABLE SECTION : 0x${secStart.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE          : ${s.value}`);
    
    let q = p;
    const n = uleb(q);
    q = n.next;

    console.log(`TABLE COUNT   : ${n.value}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      console.log(`TABLE ${i} @ 0x${q.toString(16)}`);

      const elemType = wasm[q++];
      console.log(`  ELEM TYPE   : 0x${elemType.toString(16)}`);

      const flags = uleb(q);
      q = flags.next;
      console.log(`  FLAGS       : ${flags.value}`);

      const min = uleb(q);
      q = min.next;
      console.log(`  MIN         : ${min.value}`);

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = uleb(q);
        q = max.next;
        console.log(`  MAX         : ${max.value}`);
      }
    }
  }

  if (id === 9) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("ELEMENT SECTION");
    console.log(`SIZE : ${s.value}`);

    let q = p;
    const n = uleb(q);
    q = n.next;

    console.log(`SEGMENTS : ${n.value}`);

    for (let seg = 0; seg < n.value; seg++) {
      const segStart = q;

      const flags = uleb(q);
      q = flags.next;

      console.log("");
      console.log(`SEGMENT ${seg} @ 0x${segStart.toString(16)} FLAGS=${flags.value}`);

      // Active segment
      if ((flags.value & 3) === 0) {
        const op = wasm[q++];
        console.log(`  OFFSET OP  : 0x${op.toString(16)}`);

        const off = uleb(q);
        q = off.next;

        console.log(`  OFFSET      : ${off.value}`);

        const endOp = wasm[q++];
        console.log(`  END OFFSET  : 0x${endOp.toString(16)}`);
      } else if ((flags.value & 3) === 2) {
        const tableIdx = uleb(q);
        q = tableIdx.next;

        console.log(`  TABLE INDEX : ${tableIdx.value}`);

        const op = wasm[q++];
        const off = uleb(q);
        q = off.next;

        console.log(`  OFFSET      : ${off.value}`);

        q++; // end
      }

      const count = uleb(q);
      q = count.next;

      console.log(`  FUNCTIONS   : ${count.value}`);

      const first = q;

      for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const f = uleb(q);
        q = f.next;

        if (i >= 4780 && i <= 4800) {
          console.log(`    TABLE[${i}] = FUNC ${f.value}`);
        }
      }

      // For large segments, explicitly inspect entries if this segment
      // overlaps target indices.
      if (count.value > 4788) {
        console.log(`  RAW TARGET WINDOW:`);
        let r = first;
        for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(count.value, 4800); i++) {
          const f = uleb(r);
          r = f.next;

          if (i >= 4788 && i <= 4791) {
            console.log(`    [${i}] -> FUNC ${f.value}`);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  p = secEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\resolveTable4790.ts

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_TABLE_INDEX = 0x12B6;

function uleb(p: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

        if (!(x & 0x80))
            return { value: v, next: p };

        s += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = 8;

while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const r = uleb(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    const end = p + size;

    if (id === 9) {
        const c = uleb(p);
        const segmentCount = c.value;
        p = c.next;

        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("RESOLVE TABLE[0x12B6]");
        console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("TARGET TABLE INDEX = " + TARGET_TABLE_INDEX);
        console.log("DECIMAL            = " + TARGET_TABLE_INDEX);
        console.log("SEGMENTS            = " + segmentCount);
        console.log("");

        for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount; seg++) {

            const flags = uleb(p);
            p = flags.next;

            if (flags.value !== 0) {
                console.log("Non-zero flags: " + flags.value);
                break;
            }

            const offsetOpcode = b[p++];

            if (offsetOpcode !== 0x41) {
                console.log("Unexpected offset opcode");
                break;
            }

            const offset = uleb(p);
            p = offset.next;

            const offsetEnd = b[p++];

            const n = uleb(p);
            const count = n.value;
            p = n.next;

            console.log("ELEMENT SEGMENT #" + seg);
            console.log("OFFSET = " + hex(offset.value));
            console.log("COUNT  = " + count);
            console.log("");

            // table index = offset + element position
            const elementPos =
                TARGET_TABLE_INDEX - offset.value;

            console.log(
                "ELEMENT POSITION = " + elementPos
            );

            if (
                elementPos < 0 ||
                elementPos >= count
            ) {
                console.log("TARGET OUT OF RANGE");
                break;
            }

            let targetFunc = -1;

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const f = uleb(p);
                p = f.next;

                if (i === elementPos)
                    targetFunc = f.value;
            }

            console.log("");
            console.log("============================================================");
            console.log("RESULT");
            console.log("============================================================");
            console.log(
                "table[" + TARGET_TABLE_INDEX + "] = FUNC " +
                targetFunc
            );
            console.log(
                "TABLE INDEX = " + hex(TARGET_TABLE_INDEX)
            );
            console.log(
                "TARGET FUNC = " + hex(targetFunc)
            );
            console.log("============================================================");

            break;
        }

        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

FILE: scripts\resolveType2Table.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p: number): [number, number] {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);
  return [v, p];
}

function section(id: number): [number, number] {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const sid = b[p++];

    let size: number;
    [size, p] = u(p);

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    if (sid === id) return [start, end];

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("SECTION NOT FOUND " + id);
}

/* ============================================================
   SECTION 3 — FUNCTION TYPE INDICES
   ============================================================ */

let [p] = section(3);

let funcCount: number;
[funcCount, p] = u(p);

const typeOf: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < funcCount; i++) {
  let t: number;
  [t, p] = u(p);
  typeOf.push(t);
}

/* ============================================================
   SECTION 9 — ELEMENT
   ============================================================ */

let [es] = section(9);
p = es;

let segCount: number;
[segCount, p] = u(p);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TABLE 0 EXACT RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION COUNT :", funcCount);
console.log("ELEMENT SEGMENTS:", segCount);

for (let s = 0; s < segCount; s++) {

  let flags: number;
  [flags, p] = u(p);

  console.log("");
  console.log("SEGMENT", s, "FLAGS", flags);

  if (flags !== 0) {
    console.log("UNSUPPORTED FLAGS:", flags);
    break;
  }

  /* active table 0, offset expression */

  const op = b[p++];

  let offset: number;

  if (op === 0x41) {
    [offset, p] = u(p);
  } else {
    throw new Error(
      "Unexpected offset opcode 0x" + op.toString(16)
    );
  }

  if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) {
    throw new Error("Missing offset END");
  }

  let count: number;
  [count, p] = u(p);

  console.log("TABLE OFFSET :", offset);
  console.log("FUNCTIONS    :", count);

  const wantedStart = 4780;
  const wantedEnd = 4800;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    let f: number;
    [f, p] = u(p);

    const ti = offset + i;

    if (ti >= wantedStart && ti <= wantedEnd) {
      console.log(
        `TABLE[${ti}] = FUNC ${f} TYPE ${typeOf[f]}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\resolveType6.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];

  const r = readULEB(p);
  const size = r.value;
  p = r.next;

  if (id === 1) {
    const end = p + size;

    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next;

    console.log("TYPE 6 RESOLUTION");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("========================================================================");
    console.log(`TYPE COUNT : ${count.value}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
      const form = wasm[p++];

      const np = readULEB(p);
      p = np.next;

      const params = [];

      for (let j = 0; j < np.value; j++) {
        params.push(wasm[p++]);
      }

      const nr = readULEB(p);
      p = nr.next;

      const results = [];

      for (let j = 0; j < nr.value; j++) {
        results.push(wasm[p++]);
      }

      if (i === 6) {
        console.log(`TYPE[6] FORM : 0x${form.toString(16)}`);
        console.log(`PARAM COUNT  : ${params.length}`);
        console.log(
          "PARAMS       :",
          params.map(x => `0x${x.toString(16)}`).join(", ")
        );
        console.log(`RESULT COUNT : ${results.length}`);
        console.log(
          "RESULTS      :",
          results.map(x => `0x${x.toString(16)}`).join(", ")
        );
      }
    }

    console.log("========================================================================");
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

FILE: scripts\resolveTypeIndices.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let typeStart = 0;
let typeEnd = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p];
  const s = uleb(p + 1);
  const start = s.next;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (id === 1) {
    typeStart = start;
    typeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!typeStart)
  throw new Error("Type section not found");

let q = typeStart;
const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

console.log("TYPE INDEX RESOLVER");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TYPE COUNT : ${count.value}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

  const form = b[q++];

  if (form !== 0x60) {
    console.log(`TYPE[${i}] FORM=0x${form.toString(16)}`);
    continue;
  }

  const pc = uleb(q);
  q = pc.next;

  const params = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
    params.push(b[q++]);

  const rc = uleb(q);
  q = rc.next;

  const results = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
    results.push(b[q++]);

  if (i === 4 || i === 6) {
    const fmt = x =>
      x.map(v => `0x${v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`).join(",");

    console.log(`TYPE[${i}]`);
    console.log(`  PARAMS : ${fmt(params)}`);
    console.log(`  RESULTS: ${fmt(results)}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
const TARGETS = [1, 2, 3];

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let typeSec = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const z = uleb(p);
  const size = z.value;
  p = z.next;

  if (id === 1)
    typeSec = { start: p, end: p + size };

  p += size;
}

const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM TYPE RESOLUTION : TYPE 1 / 2 / 3");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TYPE COUNT : ${count.value}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

  const form = b[q++];

  if (form !== 0x60) {
    console.log(`TYPE ${i}: FORM 0x${form.toString(16)}`);
    continue;
  }

  const pc = uleb(q);
  q = pc.next;

  const params = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
    params.push(b[q++]);

  const rc = uleb(q);
  q = rc.next;

  const results = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
    results.push(b[q++]);

  if (TARGETS.includes(i)) {

    const name = x => {
      switch (x) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        case 0x70: return "funcref";
        default:
          return `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
      }
    };

    console.log(`TYPE ${i}`);
    console.log(`PARAM COUNT  : ${params.length}`);
    console.log(
      `PARAMS       : (${params.map(name).join(", ")})`
    );
    console.log(`RESULT COUNT : ${results.length}`);
    console.log(
      `RESULTS      : (${results.map(name).join(", ")})`
    );
    console.log(
      `SIGNATURE    : (${params.map(name).join(", ")}) -> (${results.map(name).join(", ")})`
    );
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  }
}

FILE: scripts\routerCodeHash.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { createHash } from 'crypto';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const router = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    const state = await provider.provider(router).getState();

    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER CODE HASH');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY ONLY');
    console.log('==========================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router:', router.toString());
    console.log('State:', state.state.type);

    if (state.state.type === 'active' && state.state.code) {
        const code = state.state.code;

        console.log('Code bytes:', code.length);
        console.log('Code SHA256:', createHash('sha256').update(code).digest('hex'));

        try {
            const cell = Cell.fromBoc(code)[0];

            console.log('Cell hash:', cell.hash().toString('hex'));
            console.log('Cell depth:', cell.depth());
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('Cell decode failed:', String(e));
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

FILE: scripts\routerOnchainRaw.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const address = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    console.log('===== ROUTER ONCHAIN DATA CHECK =====');
    console.log('Router:', address.toString());
    console.log('');

    const state = await provider.provider(address).getState();

    if (state.state.type !== 'active') {
        console.log('STATE:', state.state.type);
        return;
    }

    const code = state.state.code;
    const data = state.state.data;

    if (!code || !data) {
        console.log('CODE/DATA NOT AVAILABLE');
        return;
    }

    console.log('CODE BYTES:', code.length);
    console.log('DATA BYTES:', data.length);
    console.log('');

    // ===== CODE =====
    const codeCells = Cell.fromBoc(code);

    if (codeCells.length === 0) {
        console.log('CODE BOC COULD NOT BE PARSED');
        return;
    }

    const codeCell = codeCells[0];

    console.log('===== CODE =====');
    console.log('Bits:', codeCell.bits.length);
    console.log('Refs:', codeCell.refs.length);
    console.log('Exotic:', codeCell.isExotic);
    console.log('Code hash:', codeCell.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('');

    if (codeCell.isExotic) {
        const slice = codeCell.beginParse(true);
        const type = slice.loadUint(8);

        console.log('Exotic type:', type);

        if (type === 2) {
            const libraryHash = slice.loadBuffer(32);
            console.log(
                'Library hash:',
                libraryHash.toString('hex')
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');

    // ===== DATA =====
    console.log('===== RAW DATA =====');
    console.log('Data base64:');
    console.log(data.toString('base64'));
    console.log('');

    const dataCells = Cell.fromBoc(data);

    if (dataCells.length === 0) {
        console.log('DATA BOC COULD NOT BE PARSED');
        return;
    }

    const dataCell = dataCells[0];

    console.log('===== PARSED DATA =====');
    console.log('Bits:', dataCell.bits.length);
    console.log('Refs:', dataCell.refs.length);
    console.log('Exotic:', dataCell.isExotic);
    console.log('Data hash:', dataCell.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('');

    // ===== FIRST 32 BYTES =====
    const s = dataCell.beginParse();

    console.log('===== DATA PREFIX =====');
    console.log('Remaining bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('Remaining refs:', s.remainingRefs);

    const bytesToRead = Math.min(
        32,
        Math.floor(s.remainingBits / 8)
    );

    if (bytesToRead > 0) {
        const prefix = s.loadBuffer(bytesToRead);
        console.log(
            'First bytes:',
            prefix.toString('hex')
        );
    }
    // ===== DATA STRUCTURE INSPECTION =====
    console.log('');
    console.log('===== DATA STRUCTURE INSPECTION =====');

    const ds = dataCell.beginParse();

    console.log('Root bits:', ds.remainingBits);
    console.log('Root refs:', ds.remainingRefs);

    if (ds.remainingBits >= 32) {
        const first32 = ds.loadUint(32);
        console.log(
            'First uint32:',
            '0x' + first32.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );
    }

    console.log('After uint32 - bits:', ds.remainingBits);
    console.log('After uint32 - refs:', ds.remainingRefs);

    console.log('');

    for (let i = 0; i < dataCell.refs.length; i++) {
        const ref = dataCell.refs[i];

        console.log(`REF ${i}:`);
        console.log('  bits:', ref.bits.length);
        console.log('  refs:', ref.refs.length);
        console.log('  exotic:', ref.isExotic);
        console.log('  hash:', ref.hash().toString('hex'));

        const rs = ref.beginParse();

        const bytes = Math.min(
            32,
            Math.floor(rs.remainingBits / 8)
        );

        if (bytes > 0) {
            console.log(
                '  first bytes:',
                rs.loadBuffer(bytes).toString('hex')
            );
        }

        console.log('');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const values = [
  139014, 140310, 141606, 142202, 142783,
  144293, 145780, 146505, 147141, 148289,
  149912, 149932
];

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
}

for (const v of values) {
  console.log("");
  console.log(`VALUE ${v} = 0x${v.toString(16)}`);

  let hits = 0;

  for (let p = 0; p < wasm.length - 1; p++) {
    if (wasm[p] !== 0x41) continue;

    const x = uleb(p + 1);

    if (x.value !== v) continue;

    hits++;

    console.log(
      `  @ 0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    if (hits >= 12) break;
  }

  console.log(`  HITS_SHOWN : ${hits}`);
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\scan190000CodeReferences.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const RANGE_START = 0x18f000;
const RANGE_END   = 0x191000;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN CODE FOR REFERENCES NEAR DATA TABLE 4271..4279");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log(
  "ADDRESS RANGE :",
  "0x" + RANGE_START.toString(16),
  "..",
  "0x" + RANGE_END.toString(16)
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << (shift + 7));
      }

      return {
        value: result | 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Locate CODE section.
 */
let p = 8;

let codePayloadStart = null;
let codePayloadEnd = null;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  if (!sizeInfo) break;

  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const payloadStart = p;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codePayloadStart = payloadStart;
    codePayloadEnd = payloadEnd;
    break;
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

if (codePayloadStart === null) {
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

console.log(
  "CODE SECTION :",
  hex(codePayloadStart),
  "..",
  hex(codePayloadEnd)
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/*
 * Decode function bodies sufficiently to identify:
 *
 *   i32.const <value>
 *
 * and print values close to 0x190000.
 */
let q = codePayloadStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(q);
q = countInfo.next;

const functionCount = countInfo.value;

console.log("CODE FUNCTION COUNT :", functionCount);
console.log("");

let totalHits = 0;

for (let func = 0; func < functionCount; func++) {

  const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(q);
  q = bodySizeInfo.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySizeInfo.value;

  if (bodyEnd > codePayloadEnd) {
    console.log("BODY OUT OF RANGE:", func);
    break;
  }

  let x = bodyStart;

  /*
   * Local declarations.
   */
  const localGroupsInfo = readULEB(x);
  x = localGroupsInfo.next;

  const localGroups = localGroupsInfo.value;

  for (let g = 0; g < localGroups; g++) {

    const nInfo = readULEB(x);
    x = nInfo.next;

    // valtype
    x++;
  }

  while (x < bodyEnd) {

    const opPos = x;
    const opcode = buf[x++];

    /*
     * i32.const
     */
    if (opcode === 0x41) {

      const r = readSLEB(x);

      if (!r) break;

      const signed = r.value;
      const value = signed >>> 0;

      x = r.next;

      if (
        value >= RANGE_START &&
        value <= RANGE_END
      ) {

        totalHits++;

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(
          "FUNC :",
          func
        );
        console.log(
          "I32.CONST :",
          hex(value)
        );
        console.log(
          "CODE POS :",
          hex(opPos)
        );

        /*
         * Show surrounding bytes.
         */
        const from = Math.max(bodyStart, opPos - 16);
        const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, x + 32);

        console.log("CONTEXT:");

        for (let z = from; z < to; z += 16) {

          const rowEnd = Math.min(to, z + 16);

          const bytes = [];

          for (let k = z; k < rowEnd; k++) {
            bytes.push(
              buf[k]
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(2, "0")
            );
          }

          console.log(
            "  " +
            hex(z) +
            " : " +
            bytes.join(" ")
          );
        }
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Common immediates that need skipping.
     */

    // local.get / local.set / local.tee
    if (
      opcode === 0x20 ||
      opcode === 0x21 ||
      opcode === 0x22
    ) {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;
      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    // call
    if (opcode === 0x10) {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;
      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    // call_indirect
    if (opcode === 0x11) {
      const a = readULEB(x);
      if (!a) break;
      x = a.next;

      const b = readULEB(x);
      if (!b) break;
      x = b.next;

      continue;
    }

    // br / br_if / br_table
    if (opcode === 0x0c || opcode === 0x0d) {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;
      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (opcode === 0x0e) {

      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;

      x = r.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
        const t = readULEB(x);
        if (!t) break;
        x = t.next;
      }

      const def = readULEB(x);
      if (!def) break;

      x = def.next;
      continue;
    }

    // block / loop / if
    if (
      opcode === 0x02 ||
      opcode === 0x03 ||
      opcode === 0x04
    ) {
      x++;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * memory instructions with memarg:
     * alignment + offset
     */
    if (
      opcode === 0x28 || // i32.load
      opcode === 0x29 || // i64.load
      opcode === 0x2c || // i32.load8_s
      opcode === 0x2d || // i32.load8_u
      opcode === 0x2e || // i32.load16_s
      opcode === 0x2f || // i32.load16_u
      opcode === 0x30 || // i64.load8_s
      opcode === 0x31 || // i64.load8_u
      opcode === 0x32 || // i64.load16_s
      opcode === 0x33 || // i64.load16_u
      opcode === 0x34 || // i64.load32_s
      opcode === 0x35 || // i64.load32_u
      opcode === 0x36 || // i32.store
      opcode === 0x37 || // i64.store
      opcode === 0x3a || // i32.store8
      opcode === 0x3b || // i32.store16
      opcode === 0x38 || // i64.store8
      opcode === 0x39 || // i64.store16
      opcode === 0x3c || // i64.store32
      opcode === 0x3f || // memory.size
      opcode === 0x40    // memory.grow
    ) {

      const a = readULEB(x);
      if (!a) break;

      x = a.next;

      if (
        opcode !== 0x3f &&
        opcode !== 0x40
      ) {
        const b = readULEB(x);
        if (!b) break;
        x = b.next;
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Global get/set
     */
    if (
      opcode === 0x23 ||
      opcode === 0x24
    ) {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;
      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Most remaining numeric/simple opcodes
     * have no immediate.
     */
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("CODE I32.CONST HITS :", totalHits);
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const target = 0x19534c;
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function readS32(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v |= (-1 << (s + 7));

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN RAW CONST 0x19534C");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (let p = 0; p < b.length; p++) {

  if (b[p] !== 0x41)
    continue;

  const x = readS32(p + 1);

  if ((x.value >>> 0) !== target)
    continue;

  hits++;

  console.log("");
  console.log("############################################################");
  console.log(`HIT #${hits} @ ${hx(p)}`);
  console.log("############################################################");

  const from = Math.max(0, p - 32);
  const to = Math.min(b.length, p + 48);

  for (let q = from; q < to; q += 16) {
    const e = Math.min(to, q + 16);
    const bytes: string[] = [];

    for (let k = q; k < e; k++)
      bytes.push(b[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));

    console.log(`${hx(q)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TOTAL HITS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x195354;

function ulebBytes(v: number) {
    const out: number[] = [];
    do {
        let x = v & 0x7f;
        v >>>= 7;
        if (v) x |= 0x80;
        out.push(x);
    } while (v);
    return out;
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const forms = [
    {
        name: "i32.const TARGET",
        bytes: [0x41, ...ulebBytes(TARGET)]
    },
    {
        name: "TARGET little-endian 32-bit",
        bytes: [
            TARGET & 0xff,
            (TARGET >>> 8) & 0xff,
            (TARGET >>> 16) & 0xff,
            (TARGET >>> 24) & 0xff
        ]
    }
];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN 0x195354 EVERYWHERE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET = " + hex(TARGET));
console.log("");

for (const form of forms) {
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(form.name);
    console.log("BYTES = " + form.bytes.map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(" "));

    let count = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i <= b.length - form.bytes.length; i++) {
        let ok = true;

        for (let j = 0; j < form.bytes.length; j++) {
            if (b[i + j] !== form.bytes[j]) {
                ok = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (!ok) continue;

        count++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("HIT #" + count + " @ " + hex(i));

        const s = Math.max(0, i - 20);
        const e = Math.min(b.length, i + form.bytes.length + 20);

        for (let p = s; p < e; p++) {
            console.log(
                hex(p) + "  " +
                b[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("COUNT = " + count);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8, code = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [n, q] = u(p);
  p = q;
  if (id === 10) code = [p, p + n];
  p += n;
}

p = code[0];
const [count, q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

const TARGET = 0x195354;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {

    // i32/i64/f32/f64 store families
    if (![0x36,0x37,0x38,0x39,0x3a,0x3b].includes(b[x]))
      continue;

    for (let z = Math.max(start, x - 100); z < x; z++) {
      if (b[z] !== 0x41) continue;

      const [v, qv] = u(z + 1);

      // exact target or target adjusted by a small store offset
      if (v < TARGET - 16 || v > TARGET + 16)
        continue;

      let y = qv;

      // allow address arithmetic before store
      while (y < x && y < qv + 20) {
        if (b[y] === 0x6a) { // i32.add
          console.log(
            `FUNC ${func} STORE @ 0x${x.toString(16)} ` +
            `CONST=0x${v.toString(16)} ADD-before-store`
          );
          console.log(
            [...b.subarray(Math.max(start,z-12),Math.min(end,x+8))]
              .map(a=>a.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join(" ")
          );
          console.log("--------------------------------------------");
          break;
        }

        y++;
      }
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

FILE: scripts\scan1953cRegion.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const LO = 0x00195000;
const HI = 0x00196000;

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= -1 << shift;
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function getSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (sid === id) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`section ${id} not found`);
}

function getBodies() {
  const sec = getSection(10);

  let p = sec.start;

  const c = readUleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    bodies.push({
      func: 83 + i,
      start: p,
      end: p + s.value,
    });

    p += s.value;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function decode(pos: number) {
  const addr = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readSleb(pos);
    return {
      addr,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "const",
      value: x.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    return {
      addr,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "local.get",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x21) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    return {
      addr,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "local.set",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    return {
      addr,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "local.tee",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x23) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    return {
      addr,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "global.get",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x24) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    return {
      addr,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "global.set",
      index: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 || op === 0x2d) {
    const a = readUleb(pos);
    const o = readUleb(a.next);

    return {
      addr,
      next: o.next,
      kind: op === 0x2d ? "load8" : "load",
      offset: o.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 || op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b) {
    const a = readUleb(pos);
    const o = readUleb(a.next);

    return {
      addr,
      next: o.next,
      kind:
        op === 0x3a
          ? "store8"
          : op === 0x3b
          ? "store16"
          : "store",
      offset: o.value >>> 0,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x6a) return { addr, next: pos, kind: "add" };
  if (op === 0x6b) return { addr, next: pos, kind: "sub" };

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    return {
      addr,
      next: x.next,
      kind: "call",
      func: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    return {
      addr,
      next: x.next,
      kind: op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if",
      depth: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    const x = readSleb(pos);

    return {
      addr,
      next: x.next,
      kind:
        op === 0x02
          ? "block"
          : op === 0x03
          ? "loop"
          : "if",
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    return {
      addr,
      next: pos,
      kind: "end",
    };
  }

  return {
    addr,
    next: pos,
    kind: `unknown_${hex(op, 2)}`,
  };
}

function show(x: any) {
  let s = `${hex(x.addr)} ${x.kind}`;

  if (x.value !== undefined) {
    s += ` ${hex(x.value)}`;
  }

  if (x.offset !== undefined) {
    s += ` offset=${hex(x.offset)}`;
  }

  if (x.index !== undefined) {
    s += ` index=${x.index}`;
  }

  if (x.func !== undefined) {
    s += ` func=${x.func}`;
  }

  return s;
}

console.log("SCAN REGION 0x195000 .. 0x196000");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let total = 0;

for (const body of getBodies()) {
  let p = body.start;
  const ins: any[] = [];

  while (p < body.end) {
    const x = decode(p);
    ins.push(x);

    p = x.next;

    if (x.kind.startsWith("unknown_")) {
      break;
    }
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
    const x = ins[i];

    /*
     * Absolute memory access:
     *
     * i32.const C
     * i32.load/store
     *
     * The memory offset may be zero or non-zero.
     */
    if (
      x.kind === "const" &&
      x.value >= LO &&
      x.value < HI
    ) {
      console.log("");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log(
        `FUNC ${body.func} | CONST ${hex(x.value)} @ ${hex(x.addr)}`
      );
      console.log("============================================================");

      for (
        let j = Math.max(0, i - 3);
        j < Math.min(ins.length, i + 10);
        j++
      ) {
        console.log(show(ins[j]));
      }

      total++;
    }

    /*
     * Direct store with an offset inside the target region.
     *
     * This catches cases where the base is zero/implicit and the
     * encoded memarg itself lands in the region.
     */
    if (
      (x.kind === "store" ||
        x.kind === "store8" ||
        x.kind === "store16") &&
      x.offset >= LO &&
      x.offset < HI
    ) {
      console.log("");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log(
        `FUNC ${body.func} | STORE OFFSET ${hex(x.offset)} @ ${hex(x.addr)}`
      );
      console.log("============================================================");

      for (
        let j = Math.max(0, i - 5);
        j <= i;
        j++
      ) {
        console.log(show(ins[j]));
      }

      total++;
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL REGION HITS = ${total}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET OF SPECIAL INTEREST = 0x0019534C");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

FILE: scripts\scan1953FamilyStores.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = new Set([
  0x195348,
  0x19534c,
  0x195350,
  0x195354,
  0x195358
]);

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }

  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function codeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const r = uleb(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    if (id === 10)
      return { start: p, end: p + size };

    p += size;
  }

  throw new Error("code section not found");
}

function bodies() {
  const sec = codeSection();
  let p = sec.start;

  const c = uleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  const out = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    out.push({
      func: 83 + i,
      start: p,
      end: p + size
    });

    p += size;
  }

  return out;
}

function decodeConst(pos) {
  if (wasm[pos] !== 0x41) return null;

  const r = uleb(pos + 1);

  return {
    value: r.value,
    next: r.next
  };
}

function decodeLoadStore(pos) {
  if (wasm[pos] !== 0x28 && wasm[pos] !== 0x36)
    return null;

  const a = uleb(pos + 1);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    op: wasm[pos] === 0x28 ? "LOAD" : "STORE",
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next
  };
}

const bs = bodies();

console.log("195348..195358 FAMILY STORE SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("========================================================================");
console.log("TARGETS:");
console.log("  0x195348 = 4787");
console.log("  0x19534C = 4788");
console.log("  0x195350 = 4789");
console.log("  0x195354 = 4790");
console.log("  0x195358 = 4791");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (const body of bs) {

  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    const c = decodeConst(p);

    if (!c || !TARGETS.has(c.value))
      continue;

    console.log(
      `CONST FUNC ${body.func} @ 0x${p.toString(16)} = 0x${c.value.toString(16)}`
    );

    let q = c.next;
    let steps = 0;

    while (q < body.end && steps < 24) {

      const x = decodeLoadStore(q);

      if (x) {
        if (x.op === "STORE") {
          console.log(
            `  STORE @ 0x${q.toString(16)} offset=${x.offset}`
          );
          hits++;
        }

        q = x.next;
      } else {
        q++;
      }

      steps++;
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------------------");
  }
}

console.log("========================================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL STORE CANDIDATES : ${hits}`);
console.log("DONE");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
    { name: "BITMAP", addr: 0x255880 },
    { name: "TABLE", addr: 0x2559ac },

    { name: "STATE_255884", addr: 0x255884 },
    { name: "STATE_255888", addr: 0x255888 },
    { name: "STATE_25588C", addr: 0x25588c },
    { name: "STATE_255890", addr: 0x255890 },
    { name: "STATE_255894", addr: 0x255894 },
    { name: "STATE_255898", addr: 0x255898 },
    { name: "STATE_25589C", addr: 0x25589c },
    { name: "STATE_2558A0", addr: 0x2558a0 },
    { name: "STATE_2558A4", addr: 0x2558a4 },
    { name: "STATE_255A5C", addr: 0x255a5c },
];

const FUNCTIONS = [
    { index: 259, bodyStart: 0x00014308, bodyEnd: 0x000157ce },
    { index: 1021, bodyStart: 0x00045932, bodyEnd: 0x00045d52 },
    { index: 1423, bodyStart: 0x00064dc9, bodyEnd: 0x00065396 },
];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(n: number): number[] {
    const out: number[] = [];

    n >>>= 0;

    while (n >= 0x80) {
        out.push((n & 0x7f) | 0x80);
        n >>>= 7;
    }

    out.push(n);
    return out;
}

function hexByte(b: number): string {
    return b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
}

function hexAddr(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function dump(pos: number, radius = 20) {
    const start = Math.max(0, pos - radius);
    const end = Math.min(wasm.length, pos + radius + 1);

    const bytes: string[] = [];

    for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
        if (i === pos) {
            bytes.push(`[${hexByte(wasm[i])}]`);
        } else {
            bytes.push(hexByte(wasm[i]));
        }
    }

    console.log(`    file ${hexAddr(pos)}`);
    console.log(`    ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
}

function findBytes(
    start: number,
    end: number,
    pattern: number[]
): number[] {
    const hits: number[] = [];

    outer:
    for (let p = start; p + pattern.length <= end; p++) {
        for (let i = 0; i < pattern.length; i++) {
            if (wasm[p + i] !== pattern[i]) {
                continue outer;
            }
        }

        hits.push(p);
    }

    return hits;
}

function classifyNearby(pos: number, target: number): string[] {
    const result: string[] = [];

    const from = Math.max(0, pos - 8);
    const to = Math.min(wasm.length, pos + 16);

    for (let p = from; p < to; p++) {
        const op = wasm[p];

        if (op === 0x41) {
            result.push(`i32.const candidate @ ${hexAddr(p)}`);
        }

        if (op === 0x28) {
            result.push(`i32.load @ ${hexAddr(p)}`);
        }

        if (op === 0x36) {
            result.push(`i32.store @ ${hexAddr(p)}`);
        }

        if (op === 0x22) {
            result.push(`local.tee @ ${hexAddr(p)}`);
        }

        if (op === 0x20) {
            result.push(`local.get @ ${hexAddr(p)}`);
        }

        if (op === 0x74) {
            result.push(`i32.shl @ ${hexAddr(p)}`);
        }

        if (op === 0x77) {
            result.push(`i32.rotl @ ${hexAddr(p)}`);
        }
    }

    return result;
}

FILE: scripts\scan500980MemoryOps.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGETS = [
  0x50098050,
  0x50098054
];

const RANGE_START = 0x50098000;
const RANGE_END   = 0x50098080;

function hex(v, n = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < b.length) {

    const x = b[pos++];

    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

    const more = x & 0x80;

    shift += 7;

    if (!more) {

      // Correct i32 sign extension
      if (
        shift < 32 &&
        (x & 0x40)
      ) {
        value |=
          (-1 << shift);
      }

      return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }

    if (shift > 35) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function readName(pos) {
  const r = readULEB(pos);

  if (!r) return null;

  return {
    name:
      b.subarray(
        r.next,
        r.next + r.value
      ).toString("utf8"),

    next:
      r.next + r.value
  };
}

function skipLimits(pos) {
  const flags = readULEB(pos);

  if (!flags) return null;

  let q = flags.next;

  const min = readULEB(q);

  if (!min) return null;

  q = min.next;

  if (flags.value & 1) {

    const max = readULEB(q);

    if (!max) return null;

    q = max.next;
  }

  return q;
}

// ============================================================
// MODULE STRUCTURE
// ============================================================

let p = 8;

let importedFunctions = 0;

const codeBodies = [];

while (p < b.length) {

  const sectionId = b[p++];

  const sr = readULEB(p);

  if (!sr) break;

  const sectionSize = sr.value;

  p = sr.next;

  const sectionEnd =
    p + sectionSize;

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // IMPORT
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (sectionId === 2) {

    const cr = readULEB(p);

    if (!cr) break;

    const count = cr.value;

    p = cr.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

      const m = readName(p);
      if (!m) break;

      p = m.next;

      const n = readName(p);
      if (!n) break;

      p = n.next;

      const kind = b[p++];

      if (kind === 0) {

        const t = readULEB(p);

        if (!t) break;

        p = t.next;

        importedFunctions++;
      }

      else if (kind === 1) {

        p++;

        p = skipLimits(p);
      }

      else if (kind === 2) {

        p = skipLimits(p);
      }

      else if (kind === 3) {

        p += 2;
      }
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // CODE
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  else if (sectionId === 10) {

    const cr = readULEB(p);

    if (!cr) break;

    const count = cr.value;

    p = cr.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

      const br = readULEB(p);

      if (!br) break;

      const size = br.value;

      p = br.next;

      const start = p;
      const end = p + size;

      codeBodies.push({
        start,
        end
      });

      p = end;
    }
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

// ============================================================
// REPORT
// ============================================================

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN 0x50098000 MEMORY OPERATIONS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  `FILE SIZE : ${b.length}`
);

console.log(
  `IMPORTS   : ${importedFunctions}`
);

console.log(
  `DEFINED   : ${codeBodies.length}`
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let total = 0;

// ============================================================
// SEARCH MEMORY OPERANDS
// ============================================================

for (
  let definedIndex = 0;
  definedIndex < codeBodies.length;
  definedIndex++
) {

  const func =
    importedFunctions + definedIndex;

  const body =
    codeBodies[definedIndex];

  let i = body.start;

  while (i < body.end) {

    const at = i;
    const op = b[i++];

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i32.load / i64.load
    // i32.load8_u / i32.load16_u
    // i32.store / i64.store
    // i32.store8 / i32.store16
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const memoryOps = [
      0x28, // i32.load
      0x29, // i64.load
      0x2c, // i32.load8_s
      0x2d, // i32.load8_u
      0x2e, // i32.load16_s
      0x2f, // i32.load16_u
      0x36, // i32.store
      0x37, // i64.store
      0x38, // f32.store
      0x39, // f64.store
      0x3a, // i32.store8
      0x3b  // i32.store16
    ];

    if (memoryOps.includes(op)) {

      const align =
        readULEB(i);

      if (!align) break;

      i = align.next;

      const off =
        readULEB(i);

      if (!off) break;

      i = off.next;

      const offset =
        off.value >>> 0;

      // We cannot know the runtime base of the
      // address from a memory instruction alone.
      // But if the offset itself lies in target range,
      // report it.

      if (
        offset >= RANGE_START &&
        offset < RANGE_END
      ) {

        total++;

        const kind =
          (
            op >= 0x36
              ? "STORE"
              : "LOAD"
          );

        console.log(
          `FUNC ${func} ` +
          `${kind} @ ${hex(at)} ` +
          `offset=${hex(offset)}`
        );
      }

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i32.const
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x41) {

      const r =
        readSLEB(i);

      if (!r) break;

      i = r.next;

      const u =
        r.value >>> 0;

      if (
        u >= RANGE_START &&
        u < RANGE_END
      ) {

        total++;

        console.log(
          `FUNC ${func} ` +
          `I32.CONST @ ${hex(at)} ` +
          `value=${hex(u)}`
        );
      }

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // CALL
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x10) {

      const r =
        readULEB(i);

      if (!r) break;

      i = r.next;

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // CALL INDIRECT
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x11) {

      const t =
        readULEB(i);

      if (!t) break;

      i = t.next;

      const table =
        readULEB(i);

      if (!table) break;

      i = table.next;

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // LOCAL
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22
    ) {

      const r =
        readULEB(i);

      if (!r) break;

      i = r.next;

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // GLOBAL
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (
      op === 0x23 ||
      op === 0x24
    ) {

      const r =
        readULEB(i);

      if (!r) break;

      i = r.next;

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // BLOCK / LOOP / IF
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (
      op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04
    ) {

      i++;

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // BR / BR_IF
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d
    ) {

      const r =
        readULEB(i);

      if (!r) break;

      i = r.next;

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // BR_TABLE
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x0e) {

      const r =
        readULEB(i);

      if (!r) break;

      let q = r.next;

      for (
        let k = 0;
        k < r.value;
        k++
      ) {

        const x =
          readULEB(q);

        if (!x) break;

        q = x.next;
      }

      const def =
        readULEB(q);

      if (!def) break;

      i = def.next;

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // PREFIX FC
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0xfc) {

      const r =
        readULEB(i);

      if (!r) break;

      i = r.next;

      continue;
    }
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

console.log(
  `TOTAL RANGE HITS : ${total}`
);

console.log("");
console.log("NOTE:");
console.log(
  "Memory operands using runtime addresses are not inferred."
);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\scan7006BulkMemoryOps.mjs
