import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const decoderPath = path.resolve("scripts/disassembleAddressUsers.mjs");
const source = fs.readFileSync(decoderPath, "utf8");

const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const markerIndex = source.indexOf(marker);

if (markerIndex < 0) {
    throw new Error("Could not locate decoder bootstrap marker.");
}

const tempPath = path.join(
    os.tmpdir(),
    `rooster_bulk_memory_${process.pid}.mjs`
);

fs.writeFileSync(
    tempPath,
    source.slice(0, markerIndex) +
        "\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction };\n",
    "utf8"
);

let decoder;
try {
    decoder = await import(pathToFileURL(tempPath).href);
} finally {
    try {
        fs.unlinkSync(tempPath);
    } catch {}
}

const { bodies, decodeFunction } = decoder;

if (!(bodies instanceof Map) || typeof decodeFunction !== "function") {
    throw new Error("Could not initialize decoder exports.");
}

const CONTEXT = 6;
const MAX_REPORTS = 250;
const TARGET = 0x256aac;

const counts = {
    functions: bodies.size,
    decoded: 0,
    errors: 0,
    memoryInit: 0,
    memoryCopy: 0,
    memoryFill: 0,
    reports: 0
};

const output = [];

function log(s = "") {
    output.push(String(s));
}

function hex(n) {
    return `0x${n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`;
}

function isTargetOp(text) {
    if (/\bmemory\.init\b/.test(text)) return "memory.init";
    if (/\bmemory\.copy\b/.test(text)) return "memory.copy";
    if (/\bmemory\.fill\b/.test(text)) return "memory.fill";
    return null;
}

log("ROOSTER WASM — BULK MEMORY OPERATION SCAN");
log("READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION");
log(`REFERENCE ADDRESS: ${hex(TARGET)}`);
log(`CONTEXT: ${CONTEXT} instructions before/after`);
log("These are candidates, not proof of a write to the target.");
log("=".repeat(78));

for (const [func] of bodies) {
    let result;

    try {
        result = decodeFunction(func);
        counts.decoded++;
    } catch {
        counts.errors++;
        continue;
    }

    const instructions = result?.instructions;
    if (!Array.isArray(instructions)) {
        counts.errors++;
        continue;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const ins = instructions[i];
        const kind = isTargetOp(ins.text);

        if (!kind) continue;
        counts[kind === "memory.init" ? "memoryInit" :
               kind === "memory.copy" ? "memoryCopy" : "memoryFill"]++;

        if (counts.reports >= MAX_REPORTS) continue;
        counts.reports++;

        log("\n" + "-".repeat(78));
        log(`${kind} | FUNC ${func} | INSTRUCTION #${i} | ${hex(ins.start)}`);

        const from = Math.max(0, i - CONTEXT);
        const to = Math.min(instructions.length - 1, i + CONTEXT);

        for (let j = from; j <= to; j++) {
            const item = instructions[j];
            const mark = j === i ? " >>> " : "     ";
            log(`${mark}#${j} ${hex(item.start)} ${item.text}`);
        }
    }
}

log("\n" + "=".repeat(78));
log("SUMMARY");
for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(counts)) {
    log(`${key}: ${value}`);
}
log("Note: report cap applies to printed contexts, not operation counts.");

const outputPath = path.resolve("scripts/scan7006BulkMemoryOps_output.txt");
fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, output.join("\n") + "\n", "utf8");

console.log(output.slice(0, 90).join("\n"));
console.log(`\nFull report: ${outputPath}`);

FILE: scripts\scan8951.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 8951;

// i32.const 8951
// opcode 0x41
// signed LEB128(8951) = f7 c5 00
const PATTERN = Buffer.from([
  0x41,
  0xf7,
  0xc5,
  0x00
]);

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(p) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {

    if (p >= bytes.length)
      throw new Error("ULEB out of range");

    const b = bytes[p++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value,
        next: p
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  throw new Error(`ULEB too long at 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
}

function readString(p) {

  const x = readULEB(p);
  const start = x.next;
  const end = start + x.value;

  return {
    value: bytes.toString("utf8", start, end),
    next: end
  };
}

function parseSections() {

  let p = 8;
  const sections = [];

  while (p < bytes.length) {

    const id = bytes[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start,
      end
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return sections;
}

function parseImports(section) {

  let p = section.start;

  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  p = countInfo.next;

  let importedFunctions = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {

    const mod = readString(p);
    p = mod.next;

    const name = readString(p);
    p = name.next;

    const kind = bytes[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {

      const typeInfo = readULEB(p);
      p = typeInfo.next;

      importedFunctions++;
    }

    else if (kind === 1) {

      p++; // element type

      const flags = bytes[p++];

      const min = readULEB(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const max = readULEB(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 2) {

      const flags = bytes[p++];

      const min = readULEB(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const max = readULEB(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 3) {

      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return importedFunctions;
}

function parseFunctionSection(section) {

  let p = section.start;

  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  p = countInfo.next;

  const types = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {

    const typeInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = typeInfo.next;

    types.push(typeInfo.value);
  }

  return types;
}

function parseCodeSection(
  section,
  functionTypes,
  importedFunctions
) {

  let p = section.start;

  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  p = countInfo.next;

  const functions = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sizeInfo.value;

    functions.push({
      index: importedFunctions + i,
      type: functionTypes[i],
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return functions;
}

function hex(n) {

  return (
    "0x" +
    n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
  );
}

function context(pos) {

  const start =
    Math.max(0, pos - 12);

  const end =
    Math.min(bytes.length, pos + 20);

  return bytes
    .subarray(start, end)
    .toString("hex");
}

function findPattern(start, end) {

  const hits = [];

  for (
    let p = start;
    p <= end - PATTERN.length;
    p++
  ) {

    let match = true;

    for (
      let i = 0;
      i < PATTERN.length;
      i++
    ) {

      if (bytes[p + i] !== PATTERN[i]) {
        match = false;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (match)
      hits.push(p);
  }

  return hits;
}

function main() {

  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );

  console.log(
    "SCAN i32.const 8951"
  );

  console.log(
    "READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
  );

  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );

  console.log(
    `WASM SIZE : ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`
  );

  console.log(
    `TARGET    : ${TARGET} (0x${TARGET.toString(16)})`
  );

  console.log(
    `PATTERN   : ${PATTERN.toString("hex")}`
  );

  console.log(
    "------------------------------------------------------------"
  );

  const sections = parseSections();

  const importSection =
    sections.find(s => s.id === 2);

  const functionSection =
    sections.find(s => s.id === 3);

  const codeSection =
    sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

  if (
    !importSection ||
    !functionSection ||
    !codeSection
  ) {

    throw new Error(
      "Required WASM sections not found"
    );
  }

  const importedFunctions =
    parseImports(importSection);

  const functionTypes =
    parseFunctionSection(functionSection);

  const functions =
    parseCodeSection(
      codeSection,
      functionTypes,
      importedFunctions
    );

  let totalHits = 0;

  for (const fn of functions) {

    const hits =
      findPattern(
        fn.start,
        fn.end
      );

    for (const pos of hits) {

      totalHits++;

      console.log("");

      console.log(
        `HIT #${totalHits}`
      );

      console.log(
        `FUNC      : ${fn.index}`
      );

      console.log(
        `TYPE      : ${fn.type}`
      );

      console.log(
        `OFFSET    : ${hex(pos)}`
      );

      console.log(
        `CONST     : ${TARGET} (${hex(TARGET)})`
      );

      console.log(
        `NEXT BYTES: ${
          bytes
            .subarray(pos, Math.min(fn.end, pos + 16))
            .toString("hex")
        }`
      );

      console.log(
        `CONTEXT   : ${context(pos)}`
      );

      // بررسی مستقیم دستور بعد از const
      const next =
        pos + PATTERN.length;

      if (next < fn.end) {

        const opcode =
          bytes[next];

        if (opcode === 0x10) {

          try {

            const call =
              readULEB(next + 1);

            console.log(
              `NEXT INST : call ${call.value}`
            );

          } catch {

            console.log(
              "NEXT INST : call <decode-error>"
            );
          }

        }

        else {

          console.log(
            `NEXT OPCODE: 0x${opcode
              .toString(16)
              .padStart(2, "0")}`
          );
        }
      }
    }
  }

  console.log("");

  console.log(
    "------------------------------------------------------------"
  );

  console.log(
    `TOTAL HITS : ${totalHits}`
  );

  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );
}

main();

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(p:number){
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v |= (x&0x7f)<<s;
    if(!(x&0x80)) return {v:v>>>0,p};
    s+=7;
  }
}

function str(p:number){
  const x=uleb(p);
  return x.p+x.v;
}

let imported=0;
const funcs:{fn:number,code:number,end:number}[]=[];

let p=8;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const z=uleb(p);
  const start=z.p;
  const end=start+z.v;

  if(id===2){
    let q=start;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.p;

    for(let i=0;i<n.v;i++){
      q=str(q);
      q=str(q);

      const kind=b[q++];

      if(kind===0){
        q=uleb(q).p;
        imported++;
      }else if(kind===1){
        q++;
        const f=uleb(q); q=f.p;
        q=uleb(q).p;
        if(f.v&1) q=uleb(q).p;
      }else if(kind===2){
        q=uleb(q).p;
      }else if(kind===3){
        q+=2;
      }
    }
  }

  if(id===10){
    let q=start;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.p;

    for(let i=0;i<n.v;i++){
      const bs=uleb(q);
      const body=bs.p;
      const bend=body+bs.v;

      let x=body;
      const nl=uleb(x);
      x=nl.p;

      for(let j=0;j<nl.v;j++){
        const c=uleb(x);
        x=c.p+1;
      }

      funcs.push({
        fn:imported+i,
        code:x,
        end:bend
      });

      q=bend;
    }
  }

  p=end;
}

function decodeOne(p:number){
  const start=p;
  const op=b[p++];

  try{
    if(op===0x20){
      const x=uleb(p);
      return {next:x.p,s:`local.get ${x.v}`};
    }

    if(op===0x21){
      const x=uleb(p);
      return {next:x.p,s:`local.set ${x.v}`};
    }

    if(op===0x22){
      const x=uleb(p);
      return {next:x.p,s:`local.tee ${x.v}`};
    }

    if(op===0x41){
      const x=uleb(p);
      return {next:x.p,s:`i32.const ${x.v}`};
    }

    if(op===0x10){
      const x=uleb(p);
      return {next:x.p,s:`call ${x.v}`};
    }

    if(op===0x11){
      const x=uleb(p);
      p=x.p;
      const y=uleb(p);
      return {next:y.p,s:`call_indirect type=${x.v} table=${y.v}`};
    }

    if(op===0x28){
      p=uleb(p).p;
      const x=uleb(p);
      return {next:x.p,s:`i32.load offset=${x.v}`};
    }

    if(op===0x29){
      p=uleb(p).p;
      const x=uleb(p);
      return {next:x.p,s:`i64.load offset=${x.v}`};
    }

    if(op===0x36){
      p=uleb(p).p;
      const x=uleb(p);
      return {next:x.p,s:`i32.store offset=${x.v}`};
    }

    if(op===0x37){
      p=uleb(p).p;
      const x=uleb(p);
      return {next:x.p,s:`i64.store offset=${x.v}`};
    }

    const names:any = {
      0x6a:"i32.add",
      0x6b:"i32.sub",
      0x6c:"i32.mul",
      0x6d:"i32.div_s",
      0x45:"i32.eqz",
      0x47:"i32.lt_u",
      0x48:"i32.lt_s",
      0x46:"i32.eq",
      0x1a:"drop",
      0x84:"i64.mul",
      0x50:"i64.eqz",
      0x0b:"end"
    };

    return {
      next:p,
      s:names[op] ?? `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
    };

  }catch{
    return {next:p,s:`opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`};
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ALL CALL 113 CONTEXT");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits=0;

for(const f of funcs){

  const arr:{off:number,s:string}[]=[];
  let q=f.code;

  while(q<f.end-1){

    const off=q;
    const d=decodeOne(q);

    if(d.next<=q) break;

    arr.push({off,s:d.s});

    if(d.s==="call 113"){

      hits++;

      console.log("");
      console.log(
        `FUNC ${f.fn}  CALL 113 @ 0x${off.toString(16)}`
      );

      for(const x of arr.slice(-16)){
        console.log(
          `  0x${x.off.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ${x.s}`
        );
      }

      console.log(
        "  --------------------------------------------------------"
      );
    }

    q=d.next;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log(`TOTAL CALL113 : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCS = new Set([480, 1907]);

const TARGET_START = 0x19534c;
const TARGET_END   = 0x195358 + 4;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, pos: number): { value: number, next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function readSLEB(buf: Buffer, pos: number): { value: number, next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value -= Math.pow(2, shift);

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function readSectionHeader(pos: number) {
    const id = bytes[pos++];
    const r = readULEB(bytes, pos);
    return {
        id,
        size: r.value,
        payload: r.next,
        next: r.next + r.value
    };
}

function getCodeSection() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const s = readSectionHeader(p);

        if (s.id === 10) {
            return {
                start: s.payload,
                end: s.next
            };
        }

        p = s.next;
    }

    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

const code = getCodeSection();

let p = code.start;

const functionCount = readULEB(bytes, p);
p = functionCount.next;

const bodies: {
    globalFunc: number;
    bodyStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
    codeStart: number;
    codeEnd: number;
}[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < functionCount.value; ordinal++) {
    const bodySize = readULEB(bytes, p);
    const bodyStart = bodySize.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

    let q = bodyStart;

    const localDecl = readULEB(bytes, q);
    q = localDecl.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(bytes, q);
        q = n.next;

        q++; // value type
    }

    bodies.push({
        globalFunc: ordinal + 83,
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        codeStart: q,
        codeEnd: bodyEnd
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
}

function u32At(pos: number) {
    return (
        bytes[pos] |
        (bytes[pos + 1] << 8) |
        (bytes[pos + 2] << 16) |
        (bytes[pos + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function decodeImmediate(pc: number) {
    const op = bytes[pc];

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readSLEB(bytes, pc + 1);
        return {
            kind: "i32.const",
            value: r.value,
            end: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const r = readSLEB(bytes, pc + 1);
        return {
            kind: "i64.const",
            value: r.value,
            end: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = readULEB(bytes, pc + 1);
        return {
            kind: `local.get ${r.value}`,
            value: null,
            end: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = readULEB(bytes, pc + 1);
        return {
            kind: `local.set ${r.value}`,
            value: null,
            end: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(bytes, pc + 1);
        return {
            kind: `local.tee ${r.value}`,
            value: null,
            end: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x6a)
        return { kind: "i32.add", value: null, end: pc + 1 };

    if (op === 0x6b)
        return { kind: "i32.sub", value: null, end: pc + 1 };

    if (op === 0x45)
        return { kind: "i32.eqz", value: null, end: pc + 1 };

    if (op === 0x47)
        return { kind: "i32.ne", value: null, end: pc + 1 };

    if (op === 0x28) {
        const a = bytes[pc + 1];
        const r = readULEB(bytes, pc + 2);

        return {
            kind: `i32.load offset=${hex(r.value)}`,
            value: null,
            end: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
        const a = bytes[pc + 1];
        const r = readULEB(bytes, pc + 2);

        return {
            kind: `i32.store offset=${hex(r.value)}`,
            value: null,
            end: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0xfc) {
        const sub = readULEB(bytes, pc + 1);

        let kind = `0xfc/${sub.value}`;

        if (sub.value === 8) kind = "memory.init";
        if (sub.value === 9) kind = "data.drop";
        if (sub.value === 10) kind = "memory.copy";
        if (sub.value === 11) kind = "memory.fill";

        return {
            kind,
            value: null,
            end: sub.next
        };
    }

    return {
        kind: `opcode ${hex(op, 2)}`,
        value: null,
        end: pc + 1
    };
}

function findFunction(pc: number) {
    return bodies.find(
        b => pc >= b.codeStart && pc < b.codeEnd
    );
}

function formatOperand(kind: string, value: number | null) {
    if (value !== null)
        return `${kind} = ${value} (${hex(value)})`;

    return kind;
}

];

console.log(`TARGET BULK OPS = ${bulkTargets.length}`);

function staticRangeOverlap(dst: number | null, len: number | null) {
    if (dst === null || len === null)
        return "UNKNOWN";

    const end = dst + len - 1;

    return (
        dst <= TARGET_END - 1 &&
        end >= TARGET_START
    ) ? "YES" : "NO";
}

for (const t of bulkTargets) {
    const f = findFunction(t.pc);

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    if (!f) {

    console.log(
        `BODY = ${hex(f.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(f.bodyEnd)}`

        console.log(`ERROR: opcode=${hex(op, 2)}`);
        continue;

    const sub = readULEB(bytes, t.pc + 1);

    let operation = `0xfc/${sub.value}`;

    if (sub.value === 8) operation = "memory.init";
    if (sub.value === 9) operation = "data.drop";
    if (sub.value === 10) operation = "memory.copy";
    if (sub.value === 11) operation = "memory.fill";

    console.log(`OPERATION = ${operation}`);

    const start = Math.max(f.codeStart, t.pc - 80);

    const decoded: {

        console.log(

    }

    console.log("");

    if (sub.value === 10) {
        console.log("OPERAND ORDER = destination, source, length");
    }
    else if (sub.value === 11) {
        console.log("OPERAND ORDER = destination, value, length");
    }

    const constants = recent
        .filter(x => x.kind === "i32.const")
        .map(x => x.value as number);

    if (sub.value === 10 && constants.length >= 3) {
        const dst = constants[constants.length - 3];
        const src = constants[constants.length - 2];

        console.log(`STATIC SOURCE      = ${hex(src)}`);
        console.log(`STATIC LENGTH      = ${len} (${hex(len)})`);
        console.log(
            `TARGET OVERLAP     = ${staticRangeOverlap(dst, len)}`

    else if (sub.value === 11 && constants.length >= 3) {
        const dst = constants[constants.length - 3];
        const value = constants[constants.length - 2];
        const len = constants[constants.length - 1];

        console.log(`STATIC DESTINATION = ${hex(dst)}`);
        console.log(`STATIC VALUE       = ${value} (${hex(value)})`);
        console.log(`STATIC LENGTH      = ${len} (${hex(len)})`);
        console.log(
            `TARGET OVERLAP     = ${staticRangeOverlap(dst, len)}`
        );
    }
    else {
        console.log("STATIC OPERANDS = NOT FULLY RESOLVED");
        console.log("TARGET OVERLAP  = UNKNOWN");
    }
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < b.length - 3; i++) {

  if (b[i] !== 0x11)
    continue;

  try {

    const typeR = uleb(i + 1);
    const tableR = uleb(typeR.p);

    /*
       Standard call_indirect:
         0x11
         typeidx : ULEB
         tableidx: ULEB
    */

    hits++;

    console.log(
      `#${hits} @ ${hex(i)}  type=${typeR.v}  table=${tableR.v}`
    );

  } catch {
    // Ignore malformed/non-code byte patterns.
  }
}

FILE: scripts\scanAllOffset4Stores.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const IMPORTED = 83;
const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];
        v += (b & 127) * 2 ** s;
        if (!(b & 128)) return { value: v, next: p };
        s += 7;
    }
}

function findCode() {
    let p = 8;
    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const id = bytes[p++];
        const z = uleb(p);
        const start = z.next;
        const end = start + z.value;
        if (id === 10) return { start, end };
        p = end;
    }
    throw Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function decode(p) {
    const start = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `local.get ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `local.set ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `local.tee ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `i32.const ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x36) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        const name = op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" : "i32.store";
        return [o.next, `${name} align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x37) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        return [o.next, `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x2d) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        return [o.next, `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) return [p, "i32.add"];
    if (op === 0x6b) return [p, "i32.sub"];
    if (op === 0x46) return [p, "i32.eq"];
    if (op === 0x47) return [p, "i32.ne"];
    if (op === 0x45) return [p, "i32.eqz"];

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `call ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x0b) return [p, "end"];

    return [p, `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`];
}

const code = findCode();

let p = code.start;
const n = uleb(p);
p = n.next;

const hits = [];

for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

    const z = uleb(p);
    const bodyStart = z.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + z.value;
    const func = IMPORTED + i;

    let q = bodyStart;

    const groups = uleb(q);
    q = groups.next;

    for (let g = 0; g < groups.value; g++) {
        const cnt = uleb(q);
        q = cnt.next + 1;
    }

    const ins = [];

    while (q < bodyEnd) {
        const addr = q;
        const [next, text] = decode(q);

        ins.push({ addr, text });
        q = next;

        if (text === "end") break;
    }

    for (let j = 0; j < ins.length; j++) {

        if (ins[j].text === "i32.store align=2 offset=4" ||
            ins[j].text === "i32.store align=0 offset=4" ||
            ins[j].text === "i64.store align=2 offset=4") {

            hits.push({
                func,
                bodyStart,
                bodyEnd,
                index: j,
                ins
            });
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ALL OFFSET+4 STORE PRODUCERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log("TOTAL HITS :", hits.length);
console.log("");

for (const h of hits) {

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(
        "FUNC", h.func,
        "BODY", "0x" + h.bodyStart.toString(16),
        "..", "0x" + h.bodyEnd.toString(16)
    );
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const a = Math.max(0, h.index - 5);
    const b = Math.min(h.ins.length - 1, h.index + 3);

    for (let i = a; i <= b; i++) {
        console.log(
            i === h.index ? ">>" : "  ",
            "0x" + h.ins[i].addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
            h.ins[i].text
        );
    }

    console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\scanBulkMemory195354.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8, code = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [n, q] = u(p);
  p = q;
  if (id === 10) code = [p, p + n];
  p += n;
}

p = code[0];
const [count, q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  for (let x = start; x < end - 1; x++) {
    // 0xfc prefix: bulk memory/table instructions
    if (b[x] !== 0xfc) continue;

    const [sub, q2] = u(x + 1);

    // memory.init, data.drop, memory.copy, memory.fill
    if (sub < 8 || sub === 10 || sub === 11) {
      console.log(
        `FUNC ${func} BULK @ 0x${x.toString(16)} SUB=0x${sub.toString(16)}`
      );

      const from = Math.max(start, x - 40);
      const to = Math.min(end, x + 20);

      console.log(
        [...b.subarray(from, to)]
          .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")
      );
      console.log("--------------------------------------------");
    }
  }

  p = end;
}
